`src/game/xwb.c`:

```c
#include "game/xwb.h"

#include "common.h"
#include "mem/heap.h"

#include <string.h>

#define SF_XWB_COMPACT 0x00020000u

static int span(uint32_t size, uint32_t off, uint32_t n)
{
    return n <= size && off <= size - n;
}

static uint32_t u32le(const uint8_t *p)
{
    return (uint32_t)p[0] | ((uint32_t)p[1] << 8) | ((uint32_t)p[2] << 16) | ((uint32_t)p[3] << 24);
}

static int xwb_map(const uint8_t *file, uint32_t size, uint32_t *entry_off, uint32_t *elem, uint32_t *count, uint32_t *data_off, uint32_t *data_size, char *err, size_t err_n)
{
    uint32_t version;
    uint32_t base;
    uint32_t base_size;
    uint32_t flags;
    if (!file || size < 0x50u || u32le(file) != 0x444E4257u) {
        sf_set_err(err, err_n, "xwb header");
        return -1;
    }
    version = u32le(file + 4);
    if (version < 2 || version > 3) {
        sf_set_err(err, err_n, "xwb version");
        return -1;
    }
    base = u32le(file + 8);
    base_size = u32le(file + 12);
    *entry_off = u32le(file + 16);
    *data_off = u32le(file + 32);
    *data_size = u32le(file + 36);
    if (base_size < 0x28u || !span(size, base, base_size) || !span(size, *data_off, *data_size)) {
        sf_set_err(err, err_n, "xwb header");
        return -1;
    }
    flags = u32le(file + base);
    *count = u32le(file + base + 4);
    *elem = u32le(file + base + 0x18);
    if ((flags & SF_XWB_COMPACT) || *elem < 0x18u || *elem > size || *count > 4096u || *count > (size / *elem) || !span(size, *entry_off, *elem * *count)) {
        sf_set_err(err, err_n, "xwb header");
        return -1;
    }
    return 0;
}

/* Xbox ADPCM: IMA ADPCM in 36-byte blocks per channel, 64 samples each.
 * A block starts with each channel's 4-byte header (the predictor as a
 * little-endian s16, then the step index and a zero byte); the nibbles
 * follow in 4-byte words per channel, low nibble first. */
#define XBOX_ADPCM_BLOCK 36u
#define XBOX_ADPCM_SAMPLES 64u

static const int16_t k_ima_step[89] = {
    7,     8,     9,     10,    11,    12,    13,    14,    16,    17,    19,    21,    23,    25,    28,
    31,    34,    37,    41,    45,    50,    55,    60,    66,    73,    80,    88,    97,    107,   118,
    130,   143,   157,   173,   190,   209,   230,   253,   279,   307,   337,   371,   408,   449,   494,
    544,   598,   658,   724,   796,   876,   963,   1060,  1166,  1282,  1411,  1552,  1707,  1878,  2066,
    2272,  2499,  2749,  3024,  3327,  3660,  4026,  4428,  4871,  5358,  5894,  6484,  7132,  7845,  8630,
    9493,  10442, 11487, 12635, 13899, 15289, 16818, 18500, 20350, 22385, 24623, 27086, 29794, 32767,
};
static const int8_t k_ima_index[16] = { -1, -1, -1, -1, 2, 4, 6, 8, -1, -1, -1, -1, 2, 4, 6, 8 };

static int16_t ima_step(int *pred, int *index, int nib)
{
    int step = k_ima_step[*index];
    int diff = step >> 3;
    if (nib & 1) {
        diff += step >> 2;
    }
    if (nib & 2) {
        diff += step >> 1;
    }
    if (nib & 4) {
        diff += step;
    }
    *pred += (nib & 8) ? -diff : diff;
    if (*pred > 32767) {
        *pred = 32767;
    } else if (*pred < -32768) {
        *pred = -32768;
    }
    *index += k_ima_index[nib];
    if (*index < 0) {
        *index = 0;
    } else if (*index > 88) {
        *index = 88;
    }
    return (int16_t)*pred;
}

static void xbox_adpcm(const uint8_t *src, uint32_t blocks, int channels, int16_t *out)
{
    uint32_t b;
    int c;
    for (b = 0; b < blocks; b++) {
        const uint8_t *blk = src + b * XBOX_ADPCM_BLOCK * (uint32_t)channels;
        int16_t *dst = out + (size_t)b * XBOX_ADPCM_SAMPLES * (size_t)channels;
        for (c = 0; c < channels; c++) {
            int pred = (int16_t)(blk[c * 4] | (blk[c * 4 + 1] << 8));
            int index = blk[c * 4 + 2] > 88 ? 88 : blk[c * 4 + 2];
            uint32_t w;
            for (w = 0; w < XBOX_ADPCM_SAMPLES / 8u; w++) {
                const uint8_t *word = blk + 4 * channels + (w * (uint32_t)channels + (uint32_t)c) * 4u;
                int k;
                for (k = 0; k < 8; k++) {
                    int nib = (word[k >> 1] >> ((k & 1) * 4)) & 15;
                    dst[(w * 8u + (uint32_t)k) * (uint32_t)channels + (uint32_t)c] = ima_step(&pred, &index, nib);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

int sf_xwb_name(const uint8_t *file, uint32_t size, char *out)
{
    uint32_t entry_off;
    uint32_t elem;
    uint32_t count;
    uint32_t data_off;
    uint32_t data_size;
    uint32_t base;
    out[0] = 0;
    if (xwb_map(file, size, &entry_off, &elem, &count, &data_off, &data_size, NULL, 0) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    base = u32le(file + 8);
    memcpy(out, file + base + 8, 16);
    out[16] = 0;
    return 0;
}

int sf_xwb_count(const uint8_t *file, uint32_t size, int *count, char *err, size_t err_n)
{
    uint32_t entry_off;
    uint32_t elem;
    uint32_t n;
    uint32_t data_off;
    uint32_t data_size;
    if (!count) {
        sf_set_err(err, err_n, "xwb header");
        return -1;
    }
    *count = 0;
    if (xwb_map(file, size, &entry_off, &elem, &n, &data_off, &data_size, err, err_n) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    *count = (int)n;
    return 0;
}

int sf_xwb_entry(const uint8_t *file, uint32_t size, int index, SfWave *out, char *err, size_t err_n)
{
    uint32_t entry_off;
    uint32_t elem;
    uint32_t count;
    uint32_t data_off;
    uint32_t data_size;
    uint32_t format;
    uint32_t rel;
    uint32_t bytes;
    uint32_t frames;
    int tag;
    int channels;
    int rate;
    int wide;
    int16_t *pcm;
    uint32_t i;
    const uint8_t *src;
    if (out) {
        memset(out, 0, sizeof *out);
    }
    if (!out || index < 0 || xwb_map(file, size, &entry_off, &elem, &count, &data_off, &data_size, err, err_n) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    if ((uint32_t)index >= count) {
        sf_set_err(err, err_n, "xwb entry");
        return -1;
    }
    format = u32le(file + entry_off + (uint32_t)index * elem + 4);
    rel = u32le(file + entry_off + (uint32_t)index * elem + 8);
    bytes = u32le(file + entry_off + (uint32_t)index * elem + 12);
    tag = (int)(format & 3u);
    channels = (int)((format >> 2) & 7u);
    rate = (int)((format >> 5) & 0x3FFFFFFu);
    wide = (format & 0x80000000u) != 0;
    if (tag > 1 || (channels != 1 && channels != 2) || rate <= 0 || bytes == 0 || rel > data_size || bytes > data_size - rel) {
        sf_set_err(err, err_n, tag <= 1 ? "xwb entry" : "xwb codec");
        return -1;
    }
    if (tag == 1) {
        if ((bytes % (XBOX_ADPCM_BLOCK * (uint32_t)channels)) != 0) {
            sf_set_err(err, err_n, "xwb entry");
            return -1;
        }
        frames = bytes / (XBOX_ADPCM_BLOCK * (uint32_t)channels) * XBOX_ADPCM_SAMPLES;
    } else if (wide) {
        if ((bytes % (uint32_t)(channels * 2)) != 0) {
            sf_set_err(err, err_n, "xwb entry");
            return -1;
        }
        frames = bytes / (uint32_t)(channels * 2);
    } else {
        if ((bytes % (uint32_t)channels) != 0) {
            sf_set_err(err, err_n, "xwb entry");
            return -1;
        }
        frames = bytes / (uint32_t)channels;
    }
    if (frames == 0 || frames > 16u * 1024u * 1024u) {
        sf_set_err(err, err_n, "xwb entry");
        return -1;
    }
    pcm = sf_alloc((size_t)frames * (size_t)channels * sizeof(int16_t));
    if (!pcm) {
        sf_set_err(err, err_n, "xwb memory");
        return -1;
    }
    src = file + data_off + rel;
    if (tag == 1) {
        xbox_adpcm(src, bytes / (XBOX_ADPCM_BLOCK * (uint32_t)channels), channels, pcm);
    } else if (wide) {
        for (i = 0; i < frames * (uint32_t)channels; i++) {
            pcm[i] = (int16_t)(src[i * 2] | (src[i * 2 + 1] << 8));
        }
    } else {
        for (i = 0; i < frames * (uint32_t)channels; i++) {
            pcm[i] = (int16_t)(((int)src[i] - 128) << 8);
        }
    }
    out->channels = channels;
    out->rate = rate;
    out->samples = (int32_t)frames;
    out->pcm = pcm;
    {
        /* the loop region, in bytes of this entry's data */
        uint32_t loop_off = u32le(file + entry_off + (uint32_t)index * elem + 16);
        uint32_t loop_bytes = u32le(file + entry_off + (uint32_t)index * elem + 20);
        uint32_t frame_bytes = tag == 1 ? 0 : (uint32_t)channels * (wide ? 2u : 1u);
        if (tag == 1 && loop_bytes != 0) {
            uint32_t blk = XBOX_ADPCM_BLOCK * (uint32_t)channels;
            out->loop_start = (int32_t)(loop_off / blk * XBOX_ADPCM_SAMPLES);
            out->loop_len = (int32_t)(loop_bytes / blk * XBOX_ADPCM_SAMPLES);
        } else if (loop_bytes != 0) {
            out->loop_start = (int32_t)(loop_off / frame_bytes);
            out->loop_len = (int32_t)(loop_bytes / frame_bytes);
        }
        if (out->loop_start < 0 || out->loop_start >= out->samples || out->loop_len <= 0) {
            out->loop_start = 0;
            out->loop_len = 0;
        } else if (out->loop_len > out->samples - out->loop_start) {
            out->loop_len = out->samples - out->loop_start;
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

void sf_wave_free(SfWave *wave)
{
    if (!wave) {
        return;
    }
    sf_free(wave->pcm);
    memset(wave, 0, sizeof *wave);
}

```

`src/game/xwb.h`:

```h
#ifndef SF_XWB_H
#define SF_XWB_H

#include <stddef.h>
#include <stdint.h>

/* One wave from an XACT 1 wave bank (WBND version 2 or 3). pcm is
   interleaved s16. This reads 8-bit and 16-bit PCM and Xbox ADPCM
   entries; WMA is refused. loop_len is 0 when the entry has no loop
   region; otherwise the region is loop_start and loop_len frames. */

typedef struct SfWave {
    int channels;
    int rate;
    int32_t samples;
    int16_t *pcm;
    int32_t loop_start;
    int32_t loop_len;
} SfWave;

int sf_xwb_count(const uint8_t *file, uint32_t size, int *count, char *err, size_t err_n);
int sf_xwb_entry(const uint8_t *file, uint32_t size, int index, SfWave *out, char *err, size_t err_n);
void sf_wave_free(SfWave *wave);

/* The bank's name (its WAVEBANKDATA szBankName), which sound banks use to
   refer to it. out holds 17 bytes. */
int sf_xwb_name(const uint8_t *file, uint32_t size, char *out);

#endif
```

`src/host/compat.c`:

```c
/* The halves of src/host/compat.h that are code. */

#include "host/compat.h"

#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <unistd.h>

#define FD_PATH "/proc/self/fd/"

/* "/proc/self/fd/N" is a descriptor the program holds (Android's file
 * picker gives one, not a path, and the file may not be openable by its
 * path): a copy of it, rewound. Anything else is opened. */
int sf_open_source(const char *path)
{
    if (strncmp(path, FD_PATH, sizeof FD_PATH - 1) == 0) {
        int fd = dup(atoi(path + sizeof FD_PATH - 1));
        if (fd >= 0 && lseek(fd, 0, SEEK_SET) < 0) {
            close(fd);
            return -1;
        }
        return fd;
    }
    return open(path, O_RDONLY | O_BINARY);
}

int sf_stat_source(const char *path, struct stat *st)
{
    if (strncmp(path, FD_PATH, sizeof FD_PATH - 1) == 0) {
        return fstat(atoi(path + sizeof FD_PATH - 1), st);
    }
    return stat(path, st);
}

#ifdef _WIN32
#include <windows.h>

long sf_pread(int fd, void *buf, size_t n, long long off)
{
    HANDLE h = (HANDLE)_get_osfhandle(fd);
    OVERLAPPED at;
    DWORD got = 0;
    if (h == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) {
        return -1;
    }
    memset(&at, 0, sizeof at);
    at.Offset = (DWORD)(unsigned long long)off;
    at.OffsetHigh = (DWORD)((unsigned long long)off >> 32);
    if (!ReadFile(h, buf, (DWORD)n, &got, &at)) {
        return GetLastError() == ERROR_HANDLE_EOF ? 0 : -1;
    }
    return (long)got;
}
#endif

```

```h
#ifndef SF_HOST_COMPAT_H
#define SF_HOST_COMPAT_H

/* The few POSIX calls the runtime uses that Windows (MinGW-w64) spells
 * differently. On Linux this is the fsync wrapper. */

#include <fcntl.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <sys/types.h>

#ifndef O_BINARY
#define O_BINARY 0 /* POSIX opens are binary already */
#endif

/* The importer's source: a path, or "/proc/self/fd/N" for a descriptor
 * the program already holds (src/host/compat.c). sf_open_source gives a
 * descriptor of its own, at the start. */
int sf_open_source(const char *path);
int sf_stat_source(const char *path, struct stat *st);

#ifdef _WIN32
#include <direct.h>
#include <io.h>
#include <stdlib.h>

#define mkdir(path, mode) _mkdir(path)
#define realpath(path, resolved) _fullpath((resolved), (path), 4096)
#define lstat stat
#define S_ISLNK(m) 0

/* pread: a read at an offset that leaves the file's position alone, so
 * threads can share one descriptor (src/host/compat.c) */
long sf_pread(int fd, void *buf, size_t n, long long off);
#define pread(fd, buf, n, off) sf_pread((fd), (buf), (n), (long long)(off))

#include <time.h>
/* the signal's number: Windows has no strsignal */
#define strsignal(sig) "signal"

static inline struct tm *sf_localtime_r(const time_t *t, struct tm *out)
{
    return localtime_s(out, t) == 0 ? out : NULL;
}
#define localtime_r(t, tm) sf_localtime_r((t), (tm))

/* Flush a file to disk. Windows names the call differently. */
static inline int sf_file_sync(int fd)
{
    return _commit(fd);
}
#else
#include <unistd.h>

static inline int sf_file_sync(int fd)
{
    return fsync(fd);
}
#endif

#endif
```

`src/host/paths.c`:

```c
#include "host/paths.h"

#include "common.h"

#include <SDL3/SDL.h>

#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>

int sf_exe_dir(char *out, size_t n)
{
#ifdef __ANDROID__
    /* the app's own storage (Android/data/APP/files, which a computer
     * sees over USB), or its internal storage: the install is read-only */
    const char *base = SDL_GetAndroidExternalStoragePath();
    if (!base) {
        base = SDL_GetAndroidInternalStoragePath();
    }
#else
    /* SDL3 owns and caches this string; it is not freed */
    const char *base = SDL_GetBasePath();
#endif
    size_t len;
    if (!base || !out || n == 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    if (snprintf(out, n, "%s", base) >= (int)n) {
        return -1;
    }
    len = strlen(out);
    while (len > 1 && out[len - 1] == '/') {
        out[--len] = 0;
    }
    return 0;
}

int sf_find_shader_tool(const char *start_dir, char *out, size_t n)
{
    char dir[4096];
    int up;
    if (!start_dir || !out) {
        return -1;
    }
    if (strlen(start_dir) >= sizeof dir) {
        return -1;
    }
    memcpy(dir, start_dir, strlen(start_dir) + 1);
    for (up = 0; up < 5; up++) {
        char path[4096];
        struct stat st;
        char *slash;
        if (sf_join(path, sizeof path, dir, "tools/spirv.py") == 0 && stat(path, &st) == 0 && S_ISREG(st.st_mode)) {
            if (strlen(path) >= n) {
                return -1;
            }
            memcpy(out, path, strlen(path) + 1);
            return 0;
        }
        slash = strrchr(dir, '/');
        if (!slash || slash == dir) {
            break;
        }
        *slash = 0;
    }
    return -1;
}
```

`src/host/settings.c`:

```c
#include "host/settings.h"

#include "common.h"
#include "iso/archive.h"

#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

const SfResPreset sf_res_presets[SF_RES_COUNT] = {
    {640, 480, "640x480"},
    {1280, 720, "1280x720"},
    {1920, 1080, "1920x1080"},
    {2560, 1440, "2560x1440"},
    {0, 0, "desktop"},
};

void sf_settings_default(SfSettings *settings)
{
    settings->width = 1280;
    settings->height = 720;
    settings->renderer = SF_RENDER_VULKAN;
}

int sf_settings_load(SfSettings *settings, const char *install_dir)
{
    char path[4096];
    FILE *fp;
    char line[256];
    sf_settings_default(settings);
    if (!install_dir || sf_join(path, sizeof path, install_dir, "config/settings.txt") != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    fp = fopen(path, "rb");
    if (!fp) {
        return 1;
    }
    while (fgets(line, sizeof line, fp)) {
        char key[64];
        char val[64];
        if (sscanf(line, "%63s %63s", key, val) != 2) {
            continue;
        }
        if (strcmp(key, "width") == 0) {
            settings->width = atoi(val);
        } else if (strcmp(key, "height") == 0) {
            settings->height = atoi(val);
        } else if (strcmp(key, "renderer") == 0) {
            settings->renderer = strcmp(val, "opengl") == 0 ? SF_RENDER_OPENGL : SF_RENDER_VULKAN;
        }
    }
    fclose(fp);
    if (settings->width < 0 || settings->height < 0) {
        sf_settings_default(settings);
    }
    return 0;
}

int sf_settings_save(const SfSettings *settings, const char *install_dir)
{
    char dir[4096];
    char path[4096];
    FILE *fp;
    char err[128];
    if (!settings || !install_dir) {
        return -1;
    }
    if (sf_join(dir, sizeof dir, install_dir, "config") != 0 || sf_mkdir_p(dir, err, sizeof err) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    if (sf_join(path, sizeof path, install_dir, "config/settings.txt") != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    fp = fopen(path, "wb");
    if (!fp) {
        return -1;
    }
    fprintf(fp, "width %d\nheight %d\nrenderer %s\n", settings->width, settings->height,
            settings->renderer == SF_RENDER_OPENGL ? "opengl" : "vulkan");
    if (fclose(fp) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    return 0;
}
```

`src/iso/afs.c`:

```c
#include "iso/afs.h"

#include "common.h"
#include "iso/archive.h"

#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <unistd.h>

static uint32_t ru32(const unsigned char *p)
{
    return (uint32_t)p[0] | ((uint32_t)p[1] << 8) | ((uint32_t)p[2] << 16) | ((uint32_t)p[3] << 24);
}

static int clean_name(const unsigned char *raw, char *out, size_t n)
{
    size_t i;
    for (i = 0; i < 32 && raw[i] != 0; i++) {
        unsigned char c = raw[i];
        if (c < 0x20 || c == 0x7F || c == '/' || c == '\\' || c == ':') {
            return -1;
        }
    }
    if (i == 0 || i >= n || (i == 1 && raw[0] == '.') || (i == 2 && raw[0] == '.' && raw[1] == '.')) {
        return -1;
    }
    memcpy(out, raw, i);
    out[i] = 0;
    return 0;
}

static int copy_span(FILE *in, FILE *out, uint32_t left, char *err, size_t err_n)
{
    char buf[8192];
    while (left) {
        size_t chunk = left > sizeof buf ? sizeof buf : left;
        if (fread(buf, 1, chunk, in) != chunk) {
            sf_set_err(err, err_n, "AFS read ended early");
            return -1;
        }
        if (fwrite(buf, 1, chunk, out) != chunk) {
            sf_set_err(err, err_n, "cannot write an AFS file");
            return -1;
        }
        left -= (uint32_t)chunk;
    }
    return 0;
}

int sf_afs_unpack(const char *afs_path, const char *dest_dir, const char *index_path,
                  void (*progress)(const char *name, void *user), void *user,
                  int *written, char *err, size_t err_n)
{
    FILE *in;
    FILE *index = NULL;
    unsigned char hdr[8];
    struct stat st;
    uint64_t file_size;
    uint32_t count;
    uint32_t i;
    unsigned char toc_tail[8];
    uint32_t name_off;
    uint32_t name_size;
    if (written) {
        *written = 0;
    }
    if (!afs_path || !dest_dir || !index_path) {
        sf_set_err(err, err_n, "missing AFS path");
        return -1;
    }
    in = fopen(afs_path, "rb");
    if (!in) {
        sf_set_err(err, err_n, "cannot open the AFS");
        return -1;
    }
    if (fstat(fileno(in), &st) != 0 || st.st_size < 16) {
        fclose(in);
        return 1;
    }
    file_size = (uint64_t)st.st_size;
    if (fread(hdr, 1, 8, in) != 8 || memcmp(hdr, "AFS", 3) != 0 || hdr[3] != 0) {
        fclose(in);
        return 1;
    }
    count = ru32(hdr + 4);
    if (count == 0 || count > 8192 || file_size > 0xFFFFFFFFu || (uint64_t)8 + (uint64_t)count * 8 + 8 > file_size) {
        fclose(in);
        sf_set_err(err, err_n, "AFS directory is not usable");
        return -1;
    }
    if (fseek(in, 8 + (long)count * 8, SEEK_SET) != 0 || fread(toc_tail, 1, 8, in) != 8) {
        fclose(in);
        sf_set_err(err, err_n, "AFS directory is not usable");
        return -1;
    }
    name_off = ru32(toc_tail);
    name_size = ru32(toc_tail + 4);
    if ((uint64_t)name_off + (uint64_t)count * 48 > file_size || name_size < count * 48) {
        fclose(in);
        sf_set_err(err, err_n, "AFS filename table is not usable");
        return -1;
    }
    if (sf_mkdir_p(dest_dir, err, err_n) != 0) {
        fclose(in);
        return -1;
    }
    index = fopen(index_path, "wb");
    if (!index) {
        fclose(in);
        sf_set_err(err, err_n, "cannot write the AFS index");
        return -1;
    }
    if (fprintf(index, "sf33rd-afs 1\nfiles %u\n", count) < 0) {
        fclose(index);
        unlink(index_path);
        fclose(in);
        sf_set_err(err, err_n, "cannot write the AFS index");
        return -1;
    }
    for (i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        unsigned char ent[8];
        unsigned char raw[32];
        char name[40];
        char dest[4096];
        uint32_t offset;
        uint32_t size;
        FILE *out;
        if (fseek(in, 8 + (long)i * 8, SEEK_SET) != 0 || fread(ent, 1, 8, in) != 8) {
            sf_set_err(err, err_n, "AFS directory is not usable");
            goto bad;
        }
        offset = ru32(ent);
        size = ru32(ent + 4);
        if ((uint64_t)offset + size > file_size) {
            sf_set_err(err, err_n, "AFS file %u is outside the archive", i);
            goto bad;
        }
        if (fseek(in, (long)name_off + (long)i * 48, SEEK_SET) != 0 || fread(raw, 1, 32, in) != 32 || clean_name(raw, name, sizeof name) != 0) {
            sf_set_err(err, err_n, "AFS file %u has no usable name", i);
            goto bad;
        }
        if (sf_join(dest, sizeof dest, dest_dir, name) != 0) {
            sf_set_err(err, err_n, "AFS path is too long");
            goto bad;
        }
        out = fopen(dest, "wb");
        if (!out) {
            sf_set_err(err, err_n, "cannot write %s", name);
            goto bad;
        }
        if (fseek(in, (long)offset, SEEK_SET) != 0 || copy_span(in, out, size, err, err_n) != 0) {
            fclose(out);
            unlink(dest);
            goto bad;
        }
        if (fclose(out) != 0) {
            unlink(dest);
            sf_set_err(err, err_n, "cannot write %s", name);
            goto bad;
        }
        if (fprintf(index, "%u %s %u\n", i, name, size) < 0) {
            unlink(dest);
            sf_set_err(err, err_n, "cannot write the AFS index");
            goto bad;
        }
        if (written) {
            (*written)++;
        }
        if (progress) {
            progress(name, user);
        }
    }
    if (fclose(index) != 0) {
        unlink(index_path);
        fclose(in);
        sf_set_err(err, err_n, "cannot write the AFS index");
        return -1;
    }
    fclose(in);
    return 0;

bad:
    if (index) {
        fclose(index);
        unlink(index_path);
    }
    fclose(in);
    return -1;
}

```

```h
#ifndef SF_AFS_H
#define SF_AFS_H

#include <stddef.h>

/* 0 when the members were written. 1 when the file is not an AFS.
   -1 on a broken archive or a write error. */
int sf_afs_unpack(const char *afs_path, const char *dest_dir, const char *index_path,
                  void (*progress)(const char *name, void *user), void *user,
                  int *written, char *err, size_t err_n);

#endif
```

`src/iso/archive.c`:

```c
#include "iso/archive.h"
#include "host/compat.h"

#include "common.h"

#include <dirent.h>
#include <errno.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <unistd.h>

#ifdef SF_HAVE_LIBARCHIVE
#include <archive.h>
#include <archive_entry.h>
#endif

int sf_mkdir_p(const char *path, char *err, size_t err_n)
{
    char buf[4096];
    size_t len;
    size_t i;
    if (!path || !path[0]) {
        sf_set_err(err, err_n, "missing directory");
        return -1;
    }
    len = strlen(path);
    if (len >= sizeof buf) {
        sf_set_err(err, err_n, "directory path is too long");
        return -1;
    }
    memcpy(buf, path, len + 1);
    for (i = 1; i < len; i++) {
        if (buf[i] != '/') {
            continue;
        }
        buf[i] = 0;
        if (mkdir(buf, 0755) != 0 && errno != EEXIST) {
            sf_set_err(err, err_n, "cannot create %s", buf);
            return -1;
        }
        buf[i] = '/';
    }
    if (mkdir(buf, 0755) != 0 && errno != EEXIST) {
        sf_set_err(err, err_n, "cannot create %s", buf);
        return -1;
    }
    return 0;
}

static int rm_one(const char *path)
{
    struct stat st;
    if (lstat(path, &st) != 0) {
        return 0;
    }
    if (S_ISLNK(st.st_mode) || !S_ISDIR(st.st_mode)) {
        return unlink(path);
    }
    DIR *dir = opendir(path);
    if (!dir) {
        return -1;
    }
    struct dirent *de;
    while ((de = readdir(dir)) != NULL) {
        char child[4096];
        if (strcmp(de->d_name, ".") == 0 || strcmp(de->d_name, "..") == 0) {
            continue;
        }
        if (sf_join(child, sizeof child, path, de->d_name) != 0) {
            closedir(dir);
            return -1;
        }
        if (rm_one(child) != 0) {
            closedir(dir);
            return -1;
        }
    }
    closedir(dir);
    return rmdir(path);
}

int sf_rm_rf(const char *path)
{
    char resolved[4096];
    if (!path || path[0] != '/') {
        return -1;
    }
    if (!realpath(path, resolved)) {
        return -1;
    }
    if (strcmp(resolved, "/") == 0 || strcmp(resolved, "/tmp") == 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    if (strncmp(resolved, "/tmp/", 5) != 0 && !strstr(resolved, "/.import-tmp")) {
        return -1;
    }
    return rm_one(resolved);
}

static int normalize_rel(const char *in, char *out, size_t n)
{
    size_t o = 0;
    const char *p = in;
    if (!in) {
        return -1;
    }
    while (*p == '/' || *p == '\\') {
        return -1;
    }
    while (*p) {
        size_t len;
        size_t i;
        const char *start;
        while (*p == '/' || *p == '\\') {
            p++;
        }
        if (!*p) {
            break;
        }
        start = p;
        while (*p && *p != '/' && *p != '\\') {
            p++;
        }
        len = (size_t)(p - start);
        if (len == 1 && start[0] == '.') {
            continue;
        }
        if (len == 2 && start[0] == '.' && start[1] == '.') {
            return -1;
        }
        for (i = 0; i < len; i++) {
            unsigned char c = (unsigned char)start[i];
            if (c < 0x20 || c == 0x7f || c == ':') {
                return -1;
            }
        }
        if (o != 0) {
            if (o + 1 >= n) {
                return -1;
            }
            out[o++] = '/';
        }
        if (o + len >= n) {
            return -1;
        }
        memcpy(out + o, start, len);
        o += len;
    }
    if (o == 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    out[o] = 0;
    return 0;
}

static int mkdir_parent(const char *file, char *err, size_t err_n)
{
    char dir[4096];
    char *slash;
    size_t n = strlen(file);
    if (n >= sizeof dir) {
        sf_set_err(err, err_n, "path is too long");
        return -1;
    }
    memcpy(dir, file, n + 1);
    slash = strrchr(dir, '/');
    if (!slash || slash == dir) {
        return 0;
    }
    *slash = 0;
    return sf_mkdir_p(dir, err, err_n);
}

#ifdef SF_HAVE_LIBARCHIVE

/* The archive read through a descriptor (sf_open_source: a path, or one
 * Android's file picker gave), which the archive closes when it is freed. */
typedef struct Source {
    int fd;
    char buf[65536];
} Source;

#ifdef _WIN32
#define src_lseek _lseeki64 /* past 2 GB */
#else
#define src_lseek lseek
#endif

static la_ssize_t src_read(struct archive *a, void *user, const void **buf)
{
    Source *s = user;
    ssize_t n = read(s->fd, s->buf, sizeof s->buf);
    *buf = s->buf;
    if (n < 0) {
        archive_set_error(a, errno, "read failed");
        return -1;
    }
    return n;
}

static la_int64_t src_seek(struct archive *a, void *user, la_int64_t offset, int whence)
{
    Source *s = user;
    la_int64_t at = (la_int64_t)src_lseek(s->fd, offset, whence);
    (void)a;
    return at < 0 ? ARCHIVE_FATAL : at;
}

static la_int64_t src_skip(struct archive *a, void *user, la_int64_t n)
{
    Source *s = user;
    (void)a;
    return src_lseek(s->fd, n, SEEK_CUR) < 0 ? 0 : n;
}

static int src_close(struct archive *a, void *user)
{
    Source *s = user;
    (void)a;
    close(s->fd);
    free(s);
    return ARCHIVE_OK;
}

static int open_archive(const char *archive, struct archive **out, char *err, size_t err_n)
{
    struct archive *a = archive_read_new();
    Source *s;
    if (!a) {
        sf_set_err(err, err_n, "out of memory");
        return -1;
    }
    archive_read_support_filter_all(a);
    archive_read_support_format_all(a);
    s = malloc(sizeof *s);
    if (!s || (s->fd = sf_open_source(archive)) < 0) {
        free(s);
        sf_set_err(err, err_n, "cannot open %s", archive);
        archive_read_free(a);
        return -1;
    }
    archive_read_set_read_callback(a, src_read);
    archive_read_set_seek_callback(a, src_seek);
    archive_read_set_skip_callback(a, src_skip);
    archive_read_set_close_callback(a, src_close);
    archive_read_set_callback_data(a, s);
    if (archive_read_open1(a) != ARCHIVE_OK) {
        sf_set_err(err, err_n, "cannot read archive (%s)", archive_error_string(a));
        archive_read_free(a);
        return -1;
    }
    *out = a;
    return 0;
}

int sf_archive_visit(const char *archive, SfArchiveVisitFn fn, void *user, char *err, size_t err_n)
{
    struct archive *a = NULL;
    struct archive_entry *ent;
    int rc;
    if (open_archive(archive, &a, err, err_n) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    while ((rc = archive_read_next_header(a, &ent)) == ARCHIVE_OK) {
        char norm[1024];
        const char *raw = archive_entry_pathname(ent);
        int is_dir;
        int stop;
        mode_t mode = archive_entry_filetype(ent);
        if (normalize_rel(raw, norm, sizeof norm) != 0) {
            archive_read_free(a);
            sf_set_err(err, err_n, "archive path refused");
            return -1;
        }
        is_dir = mode == AE_IFDIR;
        stop = fn ? fn(norm, is_dir, (uint64_t)archive_entry_size(ent), user) : 0;
        archive_read_data_skip(a);
        if (stop != 0) {
            break;
        }
    }
    if (rc != ARCHIVE_EOF && rc != ARCHIVE_OK) {
        sf_set_err(err, err_n, "cannot list archive (%s)", archive_error_string(a));
        archive_read_free(a);
        return -1;
    }
    archive_read_free(a);
    return 0;
}

int sf_archive_export(const char *archive, SfArchiveMapFn map, void *user,
                      void (*progress)(const char *name, void *progress_user), void *progress_user,
                      int *written, char *err, size_t err_n)
{
    struct archive *a = NULL;
    struct archive_entry *ent;
    int rc;
    uint64_t total = 0;
    if (written) {
        *written = 0;
    }
    if (open_archive(archive, &a, err, err_n) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    while ((rc = archive_read_next_header(a, &ent)) == ARCHIVE_OK) {
        char norm[1024];
        char dest[4096];
        const char *raw = archive_entry_pathname(ent);
        mode_t mode = archive_entry_filetype(ent);
        int is_dir;
        int decision;
        la_int64_t size;
        if (mode == AE_IFLNK) {
            archive_read_data_skip(a);
            continue;
        }
        if (normalize_rel(raw, norm, sizeof norm) != 0) {
            archive_read_free(a);
            sf_set_err(err, err_n, "archive path refused");
            return -1;
        }
        is_dir = mode == AE_IFDIR;
        dest[0] = 0;
        decision = map ? map(norm, is_dir, dest, sizeof dest, user) : 0;
        if (decision < 0) {
            archive_read_free(a);
            if (err && err_n && err[0] == 0) {
                sf_set_err(err, err_n, "archive extract aborted");
            }
            return -1;
        }
        if (decision == 0 || is_dir) {
            archive_read_data_skip(a);
            continue;
        }
        size = archive_entry_size(ent);
        if (size < 0 || (uint64_t)size > (16ull << 30)) { /* an Xbox DVD-9 image is up to 7.6 GB */
            archive_read_free(a);
            sf_set_err(err, err_n, "archive member is too large");
            return -1;
        }
        total += (uint64_t)size;
        if (total > (4ull << 30)) {
            archive_read_free(a);
            sf_set_err(err, err_n, "refusing to extract more than the Xbox 3rd Strike files");
            return -1;
        }
        if (mkdir_parent(dest, err, err_n) != 0) {
            archive_read_free(a);
            return -1;
        }
        FILE *out = fopen(dest, "wb");
        if (!out) {
            archive_read_free(a);
            sf_set_err(err, err_n, "cannot write %s", dest);
            return -1;
        }
        for (;;) {
            char block[65536];
            la_ssize_t got = archive_read_data(a, block, sizeof block);
            if (got == 0) {
                break;
            }
            if (got < 0 || fwrite(block, 1, (size_t)got, out) != (size_t)got) {
                fclose(out);
                archive_read_free(a);
                sf_set_err(err, err_n, "failed while writing %s", dest);
                return -1;
            }
        }
        if (fclose(out) != 0) {
            archive_read_free(a);
            sf_set_err(err, err_n, "failed to finish %s", dest);
            return -1;
        }
        if (written) {
            (*written)++;
        }
        if (progress) {
            progress(norm, progress_user);
        }
    }
    if (rc != ARCHIVE_EOF && rc != ARCHIVE_OK) {
        sf_set_err(err, err_n, "cannot extract archive (%s)", archive_error_string(a));
        archive_read_free(a);
        return -1;
    }
    archive_read_free(a);
    return 0;
}

#else

int sf_archive_visit(const char *archive, SfArchiveVisitFn fn, void *user, char *err, size_t err_n)
{
    (void)archive;
    (void)fn;
    (void)user;
    sf_set_err(err, err_n, "libarchive is required to read a zip or 7z of the Xbox disc");
    return -1;
}

int sf_archive_export(const char *archive, SfArchiveMapFn map, void *user,
                      void (*progress)(const char *name, void *progress_user), void *progress_user,
                      int *written, char *err, size_t err_n)
{
    (void)archive;
    (void)map;
    (void)user;
    (void)progress;
    (void)progress_user;
    (void)written;
    sf_set_err(err, err_n, "libarchive is required to read a zip or 7z of the Xbox disc");
    return -1;
}

#endif

```

```h
#ifndef SF_ARCHIVE_H
#define SF_ARCHIVE_H

#include <stddef.h>
#include <stdint.h>

int sf_mkdir_p(const char *path, char *err, size_t err_n);
int sf_rm_rf(const char *path);

typedef int (*SfArchiveVisitFn)(const char *name, int is_dir, uint64_t size, void *user);

int sf_archive_visit(const char *archive, SfArchiveVisitFn fn, void *user, char *err, size_t err_n);

/* map returns 1 and fills dest to write the member, 0 to skip, -1 to abort. */
typedef int (*SfArchiveMapFn)(const char *name, int is_dir, char *dest, size_t dest_n, void *user);

int sf_archive_export(const char *archive, SfArchiveMapFn map, void *user,
                      void (*progress)(const char *name, void *progress_user), void *progress_user,
                      int *written, char *err, size_t err_n);
```

`src/iso/extract.c`:

```c
#include "iso/extract.h"

#include "common.h"
#include "iso/afs.h"
#include "iso/archive.h"
#include "iso/xbe.h"
#include "iso/xdvdfs.h"

#include <ctype.h>
#include <dirent.h>
#include <errno.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#ifdef _WIN32
#include <process.h>
#else
#include <spawn.h>
#include <sys/wait.h>

extern char **environ;
#endif

static int ends_ci(const char *s, const char *suf)
{
    size_t n = strlen(s);
    size_t m = strlen(suf);
    if (n < m) {
        return 0;
    }
    return strncasecmp(s + n - m, suf, m) == 0;
}

static int eq_ci(const char *a, const char *b)
{
    return strcasecmp(a, b) == 0;
}

static void add_note(SfExtractResult *result, const char *msg)
{
    size_t len;
    if (!result || !msg) {
        return;
    }
    if (result->note[0] == 0) {
        snprintf(result->note, sizeof result->note, "%s", msg);
        return;
    }
    len = strlen(result->note);
    snprintf(result->note + len, sizeof result->note - len, " %s", msg);
}

static int mkdir_parent(const char *file, char *err, size_t err_n)
{
    char dir[4096];
    char *slash;
    if (strlen(file) >= sizeof dir) {
        sf_set_err(err, err_n, "path is too long");
        return -1;
    }
    memcpy(dir, file, strlen(file) + 1);
    slash = strrchr(dir, '/');
    if (!slash || slash == dir) {
        return 0;
    }
    *slash = 0;
    return sf_mkdir_p(dir, err, err_n);
}

static int copy_stream(const char *src, const char *dest, char *err, size_t err_n)
{
    FILE *in;
    FILE *out;
    uint8_t buf[65536];
    size_t n;
    if (mkdir_parent(dest, err, err_n) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    in = fopen(src, "rb");
    out = fopen(dest, "wb");
    if (!in || !out) {
        if (in) {
            fclose(in);
        }
        if (out) {
            fclose(out);
        }
        sf_set_err(err, err_n, "cannot copy %s", dest);
        return -1;
    }
    while ((n = fread(buf, 1, sizeof buf, in)) > 0) {
        if (fwrite(buf, 1, n, out) != n) {
            fclose(in);
            fclose(out);
            sf_set_err(err, err_n, "failed while writing %s", dest);
            return -1;
        }
    }
    if (ferror(in)) {
        fclose(in);
        fclose(out);
        sf_set_err(err, err_n, "failed while reading %s", src);
        return -1;
    }
    fclose(in);
    if (fclose(out) != 0) {
        sf_set_err(err, err_n, "failed to finish %s", dest);
        return -1;
    }
    return 0;
}

/* The archive is in place (the import's own check, before the sound
 * banks are read). */
static int afs_ready(const char *install_dir)
{
    char path[4096];
    struct stat st;
    if (!install_dir || sf_join(path, sizeof path, install_dir, "data/afs/SF33RDXB.AFS") != 0) {
        return 0;
    }
    return stat(path, &st) == 0 && S_ISREG(st.st_mode) && st.st_size > 0;
}

int sf_data_ready(const char *install_dir)
{
    char path[4096];
    struct stat st;
    if (!install_dir || sf_join(path, sizeof path, install_dir, "data/afs/SF33RDXB.AFS") != 0) {
        return 0;
    }
    if (stat(path, &st) != 0 || !S_ISREG(st.st_mode) || st.st_size <= 0) {
        return 0;
    }
    return sf_sound_banks_ready(install_dir);
}

/* The 21 sound banks the import reads out of sf33rd.xbe (the sound
 * effects' cue tables): data imported before they were has to be
 * imported again. */
int sf_sound_banks_ready(const char *install_dir)
{
    char path[4096];
    char name[32];
    struct stat st;
    int i;
    for (i = 0; i < SF_SOUND_BANKS; i++) {
        snprintf(name, sizeof name, "data/audio/sdbk/%02d.sdbk", i);
        if (sf_join(path, sizeof path, install_dir, name) != 0 || stat(path, &st) != 0 || !S_ISREG(st.st_mode) ||
            st.st_size <= 0) {
            return 0;
        }
    }
    return 1;
}

static int has_cvs(const char *rel)
{
    const char *p = rel;
    while (*p) {
        const char *start = p;
        size_t n = 0;
        while (p[n] && p[n] != '/') {
            n++;
        }
        if (n == 3 && strncasecmp(start, "CVS", 3) == 0) {
            return 1;
        }
        p += n;
        if (*p == '/') {
            p++;
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

static int run_convert(const char *tool, const char *src, const char *dst)
{
#if defined(__ANDROID__)
    /* no Python on a phone: the shaders stay as they are */
    (void)tool;
    (void)src;
    (void)dst;
    return -1;
#elif defined(_WIN32)
    /* Windows: python, if it is on the PATH */
    const char *argv[6];
    intptr_t rc;
    argv[0] = "python";
    argv[1] = tool;
    argv[2] = src;
    argv[3] = "-o";
    argv[4] = dst;
    argv[5] = NULL;
    rc = _spawnvp(_P_WAIT, "python", argv);
    return rc == 0 ? 0 : -1;
#else
    char *argv[6];
    pid_t pid;
    int status = 0;
    argv[0] = "python3";
    argv[1] = (char *)tool;
    argv[2] = (char *)src;
    argv[3] = "-o";
    argv[4] = (char *)dst;
    argv[5] = NULL;
    if (posix_spawnp(&pid, "python3", NULL, NULL, argv, environ) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    for (;;) {
        pid_t w = waitpid(pid, &status, 0);
        if (w < 0 && errno == EINTR) {
            continue;
        }
        if (w < 0) {
            return -1;
        }
        break;
    }
    if (!WIFEXITED(status) || WEXITSTATUS(status) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    return 0;
#endif
}

static int convert_dir(const char *dir, const char *rel, const char *spirv_root, const char *tool, SfExtractResult *result)
{
    char full[4096];
    DIR *d;
    struct dirent *de;
    if (rel[0] == 0) {
        snprintf(full, sizeof full, "%s", dir);
    } else if (sf_join(full, sizeof full, dir, rel) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    d = opendir(full);
    if (!d) {
        return 0;
    }
    while ((de = readdir(d)) != NULL) {
        char child_rel[1024];
        char child_full[4096];
        struct stat st;
        int wrote;
        if (strcmp(de->d_name, ".") == 0 || strcmp(de->d_name, "..") == 0) {
            continue;
        }
        wrote = rel[0] == 0 ? snprintf(child_rel, sizeof child_rel, "%s", de->d_name)
                            : snprintf(child_rel, sizeof child_rel, "%s/%s", rel, de->d_name);
        if (wrote < 0 || (size_t)wrote >= sizeof child_rel || sf_join(child_full, sizeof child_full, dir, child_rel) != 0) {
            closedir(d);
            return -1;
        }
        if (lstat(child_full, &st) != 0 || S_ISLNK(st.st_mode)) {
            continue;
        }
        if (S_ISDIR(st.st_mode)) {
            if (convert_dir(dir, child_rel, spirv_root, tool, result) != 0) {
                closedir(d);
                return -1;
            }
            continue;
        }
        if (!S_ISREG(st.st_mode) || has_cvs(child_rel)) {
            continue;
        }
        if (!ends_ci(de->d_name, ".xvu") && !ends_ci(de->d_name, ".xpu")) {
            continue;
        }
        result->shaders++;
        if (!tool) {
            continue;
        }
        {
            char spv_rel[1024];
            char spv_path[4096];
            size_t len = strlen(child_rel);
            if (len < 4 || len >= sizeof spv_rel) {
                result->spirv_failed++;
                continue;
            }
            memcpy(spv_rel, child_rel, len - 3);
            memcpy(spv_rel + len - 3, "spv", 4);
            if (sf_join(spv_path, sizeof spv_path, spirv_root, spv_rel) != 0 || mkdir_parent(spv_path, result->error, sizeof result->error) != 0) {
                closedir(d);
                return -1;
            }
            if (run_convert(tool, child_full, spv_path) == 0) {
                result->spirv++;
            } else {
                result->spirv_failed++;
                unlink(spv_path);
            }
        }
    }
    closedir(d);
    return 0;
}

static int count_ext(const char *dir, const char *rel, int *count)
{
    char full[4096];
    DIR *d;
    struct dirent *de;
    if (rel[0] == 0) {
        snprintf(full, sizeof full, "%s", dir);
    } else if (sf_join(full, sizeof full, dir, rel) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    d = opendir(full);
    if (!d) {
        return 0;
    }
    while ((de = readdir(d)) != NULL) {
        char child_rel[1024];
        char child_full[4096];
        struct stat st;
        int wrote;
        if (strcmp(de->d_name, ".") == 0 || strcmp(de->d_name, "..") == 0) {
            continue;
        }
        wrote = rel[0] == 0 ? snprintf(child_rel, sizeof child_rel, "%s", de->d_name)
                            : snprintf(child_rel, sizeof child_rel, "%s/%s", rel, de->d_name);
        if (wrote < 0 || (size_t)wrote >= sizeof child_rel) {
            closedir(d);
            return -1;
        }
        if (sf_join(child_full, sizeof child_full, dir, child_rel) != 0) {
            closedir(d);
            return -1;
        }
        if (lstat(child_full, &st) != 0 || S_ISLNK(st.st_mode)) {
            continue;
        }
        if (S_ISDIR(st.st_mode)) {
            if (count_ext(dir, child_rel, count) != 0) {
                closedir(d);
                return -1;
            }
        } else if (S_ISREG(st.st_mode) && ends_ci(de->d_name, ".spv")) {
            (*count)++;
        }
    }
    closedir(d);
    return 0;
}

static int write_manifest(const char *install, const SfExtractResult *result)
{
    char path[4096];
    FILE *fp;
    if (sf_join(path, sizeof path, install, "data/manifest") != 0 || mkdir_parent(path, NULL, 0) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    fp = fopen(path, "wb");
    if (!fp) {
        return -1;
    }
    fprintf(fp, "sf33rd-data 1\nafs yes\nafs_files %d\nshaders %d\nspirv %d\nspirv_failed %d\nshader_api vulkan opengl\n",
            result->afs_files, result->shaders, result->spirv, result->spirv_failed);
    if (fclose(fp) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    return 0;
}

static int finish_shaders(const char *install, const char *tool, SfExtractResult *result)
{
    char nv2a[4096];
    char spirv[4096];
    if (sf_join(nv2a, sizeof nv2a, install, "data/shaders/nv2a") != 0 || sf_join(spirv, sizeof spirv, install, "data/shaders/spirv") != 0) {
        sf_set_err(result->error, sizeof result->error, "shader path is too long");
        return -1;
    }
    result->shaders = 0;
    if (convert_dir(nv2a, "", spirv, tool, result) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    /* without the tool the disc's shaders stay as they are: the game
     * draws with its own */
    if (write_manifest(install, result) != 0) {
        add_note(result, "The data manifest was not written.");
    }
    return 0;
}

static int ci_child(const char *dir, const char *name, char *out, size_t n)
{
    DIR *d = opendir(dir);
    struct dirent *de;
    if (!d) {
        return 1;
    }
    while ((de = readdir(d)) != NULL) {
        if (strcasecmp(de->d_name, name) == 0) {
            int rc = sf_join(out, n, dir, de->d_name);
            closedir(d);
            return rc == 0 ? 0 : -1;
        }
    }
    closedir(d);
    return 1;
}

static int ci_resolve(const char *root, const char *rel, char *out, size_t n)
{
    char cur[4096];
    const char *p = rel;
    if (strlen(root) >= sizeof cur) {
        return -1;
    }
    memcpy(cur, root, strlen(root) + 1);
    while (*p) {
        char name[256];
        char next[4096];
        size_t nlen = 0;
        int rc;
        while (*p == '/') {
            p++;
        }
        if (!*p) {
            break;
        }
        while (p[nlen] && p[nlen] != '/') {
            if (nlen + 1 >= sizeof name) {
                return -1;
            }
            name[nlen] = p[nlen];
            nlen++;
        }
        name[nlen] = 0;
        p += nlen;
        rc = ci_child(cur, name, next, sizeof next);
        if (rc != 0) {
            return rc;
        }
        if (strlen(next) >= sizeof cur) {
            return -1;
        }
        memcpy(cur, next, strlen(next) + 1);
    }
    if (strlen(cur) >= n) {
        return -1;
    }
    memcpy(out, cur, strlen(cur) + 1);
    return 0;
}

static int copy_tree(const char *src_dir, const char *rel, const char *dest_root, int shaders, int *files, SfExtractProgress progress, void *user, char *err, size_t err_n)
{
    char full[4096];
    DIR *d;
    struct dirent *de;
    if (rel[0] == 0) {
        snprintf(full, sizeof full, "%s", src_dir);
    } else if (sf_join(full, sizeof full, src_dir, rel) != 0) {
        sf_set_err(err, err_n, "path is too long");
        return -1;
    }
    d = opendir(full);
    if (!d) {
        return 0;
    }
    while ((de = readdir(d)) != NULL) {
        char child_rel[1024];
        char child_full[4096];
        char dest[4096];
        struct stat st;
        int wrote;
        if (strcmp(de->d_name, ".") == 0 || strcmp(de->d_name, "..") == 0) {
            continue;
        }
        wrote = rel[0] == 0 ? snprintf(child_rel, sizeof child_rel, "%s", de->d_name)
                            : snprintf(child_rel, sizeof child_rel, "%s/%s", rel, de->d_name);
        if (wrote < 0 || (size_t)wrote >= sizeof child_rel || sf_join(child_full, sizeof child_full, src_dir, child_rel) != 0) {
            closedir(d);
            sf_set_err(err, err_n, "path is too long");
            return -1;
        }
        if (lstat(child_full, &st) != 0 || S_ISLNK(st.st_mode) || has_cvs(child_rel)) {
            continue;
        }
        if (S_ISDIR(st.st_mode)) {
            if (copy_tree(src_dir, child_rel, dest_root, shaders, files, progress, user, err, err_n) != 0) {
                closedir(d);
                return -1;
            }
            continue;
        }
        if (!S_ISREG(st.st_mode)) {
            continue;
        }
        if (shaders && !ends_ci(de->d_name, ".xvu") && !ends_ci(de->d_name, ".xpu")) {
            continue;
        }
        if (sf_join(dest, sizeof dest, dest_root, child_rel) != 0 || copy_stream(child_full, dest, err, err_n) != 0) {
            closedir(d);
            return -1;
        }
        (*files)++;
        if (progress) {
            progress(child_rel, user);
        }
    }
    closedir(d);
    return 0;
}

/* The XBE is copied to data/.import-tmp only for its sound banks (the
 * cue tables the sound effects play from), which go to data/audio/sdbk.
 * The XBE itself is not kept. */
#define XBE_TMP "data/.import-tmp/sf33rd.xbe"

/* The sound effects' cue tables, out of the disc's sf33rd.xbe (copied to
 * XBE_TMP by the reader and gone after): the game needs them. */
static int import_sound_banks(const char *install, SfExtractResult *result)
{
    char xbe[4096];
    char out[4096];
    char err[256];
    struct stat st;
    if (sf_join(xbe, sizeof xbe, install, XBE_TMP) != 0 || sf_join(out, sizeof out, install, "data/audio/sdbk") != 0) {
        sf_set_err(result->error, sizeof result->error, "the install path is too long");
        return -1;
    }
    if (stat(xbe, &st) != 0) {
        sf_set_err(result->error, sizeof result->error, "sf33rd.xbe is not on the disc: it holds the sound effects");
        return -1;
    }
    err[0] = 0;
    if (sf_mkdir_p(out, err, sizeof err) != 0 || sf_xbe_sound_banks(xbe, out, err, sizeof err) != 0) {
        sf_set_err(result->error, sizeof result->error, "the sound effects were not read from sf33rd.xbe: %s", err);
        unlink(xbe);
        return -1;
    }
    result->files += SF_SOUND_BANKS;
    unlink(xbe);
    {
        char tmp[4096];
        if (sf_join(tmp, sizeof tmp, install, "data/.import-tmp") == 0) {
            rmdir(tmp); /* empty now */
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

static int finish_import(const char *install, const char *tool, SfExtractProgress progress, void *user, SfExtractResult *result)
{
    char afs[4096];
    char dir[4096];
    char index[4096];
    int written = 0;
    int rc;
    if (sf_join(afs, sizeof afs, install, "data/afs/SF33RDXB.AFS") != 0 || sf_join(dir, sizeof dir, install, "data/afs/files") != 0 || sf_join(index, sizeof index, install, "data/afs/index") != 0) {
        sf_set_err(result->error, sizeof result->error, "AFS path is too long");
        return -1;
    }
    if (import_sound_banks(install, result) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    rc = sf_afs_unpack(afs, dir, index, progress, user, &written, result->error, sizeof result->error);
    if (rc < 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    if (rc > 0) {
        add_note(result, "SF33RDXB.AFS was copied. It is not an AFS archive, so its files were not unpacked.");
    } else {
        result->files += written;
        result->afs_files = written;
    }
    return finish_shaders(install, tool, result);
}

static int import_files_from_dir(const char *root, const char *install, const char *tool, SfExtractProgress progress, void *user, SfExtractResult *result)
{
    char src[4096];
    char dest[4096];
    char tree[4096];
    if (ci_resolve(root, "XB_HostPC/SF33RDXB.AFS", src, sizeof src) != 0) {
        sf_set_err(result->error, sizeof result->error, "The Xbox data has no XB_HostPC/SF33RDXB.AFS.");
        return -1;
    }
    if (sf_join(dest, sizeof dest, install, "data/afs/SF33RDXB.AFS") != 0 || copy_stream(src, dest, result->error, sizeof result->error) != 0) {
        unlink(dest);
        return -1;
    }
    result->files++;
    if (progress) {
        progress("XB_HostPC/SF33RDXB.AFS", user);
    }
    if (ci_resolve(root, "XB_HostPC/ST3_3_OP.aix", src, sizeof src) == 0) {
        if (sf_join(dest, sizeof dest, install, "data/audio/ST3_3_OP.aix") != 0 || copy_stream(src, dest, result->error, sizeof result->error) != 0) {
            return -1;
        }
        result->files++;
        if (progress) {
            progress("XB_HostPC/ST3_3_OP.aix", user);
        }
    }
    if (ci_resolve(root, "sf33rd.xbe", src, sizeof src) == 0) {
        if (sf_join(dest, sizeof dest, install, XBE_TMP) != 0 || mkdir_parent(dest, result->error, sizeof result->error) != 0 || copy_stream(src, dest, result->error, sizeof result->error) != 0) {
            return -1;
        }
    }
    if (ci_resolve(root, "media/Fonts", tree, sizeof tree) == 0) {
        char font_dest[4096];
        if (sf_join(font_dest, sizeof font_dest, install, "data/fonts") != 0 || copy_tree(tree, "", font_dest, 0, &result->files, progress, user, result->error, sizeof result->error) != 0) {
            return -1;
        }
    } else {
        add_note(result, "Fonts were not in the Xbox data and were skipped.");
    }
    if (ci_resolve(root, "media/Shaders", tree, sizeof tree) == 0) {
        char shader_dest[4096];
        if (sf_join(shader_dest, sizeof shader_dest, install, "data/shaders/nv2a") != 0 || copy_tree(tree, "", shader_dest, 1, &result->files, progress, user, result->error, sizeof result->error) != 0) {
            return -1;
        }
    }
    return finish_import(install, tool, progress, user, result);
}

struct WalkCopy {
    SfXdvdfs *img;
    const char *install;
    const char *dest_rel;
    int shaders;
    int *files;
    SfExtractProgress progress;
    void *user;
    char *err;
    size_t err_n;
    int failed;
};

static int on_iso_file(const char *path, const SfXdvdfsEnt *ent, void *user)
{
    struct WalkCopy *w = user;
    const char *base;
    char dest[4096];
    char rel[1024];
    if (!ent || ent->is_dir || w->failed) {
        return 0;
    }
    if (has_cvs(path)) {
        return 0;
    }
    if (strncasecmp(path, "media/Fonts/", 12) == 0) {
        base = path + 12;
    } else if (strncasecmp(path, "media/Shaders/", 14) == 0) {
        base = path + 14;
    } else {
        base = strrchr(path, '/');
        base = base ? base + 1 : path;
    }
    if (w->shaders && !ends_ci(base, ".xvu") && !ends_ci(base, ".xpu")) {
        return 0;
    }
    if (snprintf(rel, sizeof rel, "%s", base) >= (int)sizeof rel) {
        return 0;
    }
    {
        char root[4096];
        if (sf_join(root, sizeof root, w->install, w->dest_rel) != 0 || sf_join(dest, sizeof dest, root, rel) != 0) {
            w->failed = 1;
            sf_set_err(w->err, w->err_n, "path is too long");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    if (mkdir_parent(dest, w->err, w->err_n) != 0 || sf_xdvdfs_save(w->img, ent, dest, w->err, w->err_n) != 0) {
        w->failed = 1;
        return 1;
    }
    (*w->files)++;
    if (w->progress) {
        w->progress(path, w->user);
    }
    return 0;
}

static int import_iso(const char *source, const char *install, const char *tool, SfExtractProgress progress, void *user, SfExtractResult *result)
{
    SfXdvdfs *img;
    SfXdvdfsEnt ent;
    char dest[4096];
    int rc;
    struct WalkCopy walk;
    img = sf_xdvdfs_open(source, result->error, sizeof result->error);
    if (!img) {
        return -1;
    }
    rc = sf_xdvdfs_find(img, "XB_HostPC/SF33RDXB.AFS", &ent, result->error, sizeof result->error);
    if (rc != 0) {
        sf_xdvdfs_close(img);
        if (rc == 1) {
            sf_set_err(result->error, sizeof result->error, "This Xbox image has no XB_HostPC/SF33RDXB.AFS.");
        }
        return -1;
    }
    if (sf_join(dest, sizeof dest, install, "data/afs/SF33RDXB.AFS") != 0 || mkdir_parent(dest, result->error, sizeof result->error) != 0 || sf_xdvdfs_save(img, &ent, dest, result->error, sizeof result->error) != 0) {
        unlink(dest);
        sf_xdvdfs_close(img);
        return -1;
    }
    result->files++;
    if (progress) {
        progress("XB_HostPC/SF33RDXB.AFS", user);
    }
    if (sf_xdvdfs_find(img, "XB_HostPC/ST3_3_OP.aix", &ent, result->error, sizeof result->error) == 0) {
        if (sf_join(dest, sizeof dest, install, "data/audio/ST3_3_OP.aix") != 0 || mkdir_parent(dest, result->error, sizeof result->error) != 0 || sf_xdvdfs_save(img, &ent, dest, result->error, sizeof result->error) != 0) {
            sf_xdvdfs_close(img);
            return -1;
        }
        result->files++;
        if (progress) {
            progress("XB_HostPC/ST3_3_OP.aix", user);
        }
    }
    if (sf_xdvdfs_find(img, "sf33rd.xbe", &ent, result->error, sizeof result->error) == 0) {
        if (sf_join(dest, sizeof dest, install, XBE_TMP) != 0 || mkdir_parent(dest, result->error, sizeof result->error) != 0 || sf_xdvdfs_save(img, &ent, dest, result->error, sizeof result->error) != 0) {
            sf_xdvdfs_close(img);
            return -1;
        }
    }
    result->error[0] = 0;
    memset(&walk, 0, sizeof walk);
    walk.img = img;
    walk.install = install;
    walk.files = &result->files;
    walk.progress = progress;
    walk.user = user;
    walk.err = result->error;
    walk.err_n = sizeof result->error;
    walk.dest_rel = "data/fonts";
    if (sf_xdvdfs_walk(img, "media/Fonts", on_iso_file, &walk, result->error, sizeof result->error) < 0 || walk.failed) {
        sf_xdvdfs_close(img);
        return -1;
    }
    walk.shaders = 1;
    walk.dest_rel = "data/shaders/nv2a";
    if (sf_xdvdfs_walk(img, "media/Shaders", on_iso_file, &walk, result->error, sizeof result->error) < 0 || walk.failed) {
        sf_xdvdfs_close(img);
        return -1;
    }
    sf_xdvdfs_close(img);
    return finish_import(install, tool, progress, user, result);
}

static int find_disc_root(const char *dir, char *out, size_t n, int depth, int *ps2)
{
    char probe[4096];
    DIR *d;
    struct dirent *de;
    struct stat st;
    if (ci_resolve(dir, "XB_HostPC/SF33RDXB.AFS", probe, sizeof probe) == 0 && stat(probe, &st) == 0 && S_ISREG(st.st_mode) && st.st_size > 0) {
        if (strlen(dir) >= n) {
            return -1;
        }
        memcpy(out, dir, strlen(dir) + 1);
        return 0;
    }
    if (ci_resolve(dir, "SYSTEM.CNF", probe, sizeof probe) == 0) {
        *ps2 = 1;
    }
    if (depth >= 5) {
        return 1;
    }
    d = opendir(dir);
    if (!d) {
        return 1;
    }
    while ((de = readdir(d)) != NULL) {
        char child[4096];
        if (strcmp(de->d_name, ".") == 0 || strcmp(de->d_name, "..") == 0) {
            continue;
        }
        if (sf_join(child, sizeof child, dir, de->d_name) != 0) {
            continue;
        }
        if (lstat(child, &st) != 0 || !S_ISDIR(st.st_mode) || S_ISLNK(st.st_mode)) {
            continue;
        }
        if (find_disc_root(child, out, n, depth + 1, ps2) == 0) {
            closedir(d);
            return 0;
        }
    }
    closedir(d);
    return 1;
}

static int find_payload(const char *dir, char *out, size_t n, int depth, int *left)
{
    DIR *d;
    struct dirent *de;
    if (*left <= 0 || depth > 5) {
        return 1;
    }
    d = opendir(dir);
    if (!d) {
        return 1;
    }
    while ((de = readdir(d)) != NULL && *left > 0) {
        char child[4096];
        struct stat st;
        if (strcmp(de->d_name, ".") == 0 || strcmp(de->d_name, "..") == 0) {
            continue;
        }
        if (sf_join(child, sizeof child, dir, de->d_name) != 0) {
            continue;
        }
        if (lstat(child, &st) != 0 || S_ISLNK(st.st_mode)) {
            continue;
        }
        if (S_ISDIR(st.st_mode)) {
            if (find_payload(child, out, n, depth + 1, left) == 0) {
                closedir(d);
                return 0;
            }
            continue;
        }
        if (!S_ISREG(st.st_mode)) {
            continue;
        }
        if (ends_ci(de->d_name, ".iso") || ends_ci(de->d_name, ".zip") || ends_ci(de->d_name, ".7z")) {
            if (strlen(child) >= n) {
                continue;
            }
            memcpy(out, child, strlen(child) + 1);
            (*left)--;
            closedir(d);
            return 0;
        }
    }
    closedir(d);
    return 1;
}

static int import_any(const char *source, const char *install, const char *tool, SfExtractProgress progress, void *user, SfExtractResult *result, int depth);

static int suffix_prefix(const char *path, const char *suffix, char *prefix, size_t n)
{
    size_t pl = strlen(path);
    size_t sl = strlen(suffix);
    size_t pre;
    if (pl < sl || strcasecmp(path + pl - sl, suffix) != 0) {
        return 0;
    }
    if (pl == sl) {
        prefix[0] = 0;
        return 1;
    }
    if (path[pl - sl - 1] != '/') {
        return 0;
    }
    pre = pl - sl - 1;
    if (pre >= n) {
        return 0;
    }
    memcpy(prefix, path, pre);
    prefix[pre] = 0;
    return 1;
}

struct ArchivePlan {
    int afs;
    int ps2;
    char prefix[1024];
    char iso_name[1024];
    const char *install;
    char error[512];
};

static const char *rel_under(const char *name, const char *prefix)
{
    size_t n;
    if (!prefix[0]) {
        return name;
    }
    n = strlen(prefix);
    if (strncasecmp(name, prefix, n) != 0 || name[n] != '/') {
        return NULL;
    }
    return name + n + 1;
}

static int visit_plan(const char *name, int is_dir, uint64_t size, void *user)
{
    struct ArchivePlan *plan = user;
    (void)size;
    if (is_dir) {
        return 0;
    }
    if (suffix_prefix(name, "XB_HostPC/SF33RDXB.AFS", plan->prefix, sizeof plan->prefix)) {
        plan->afs = 1;
    }
    if (eq_ci(name, "SYSTEM.CNF") || ends_ci(name, "/SYSTEM.CNF") || ends_ci(name, "/THIRD/SF33RD.AFS")) {
        plan->ps2 = 1;
    }
    if (plan->iso_name[0] == 0 && ends_ci(name, ".iso")) {
        snprintf(plan->iso_name, sizeof plan->iso_name, "%s", name);
    }
    return 0;
}

static int map_xbox(const char *name, int is_dir, char *dest, size_t dest_n, void *user)
{
    struct ArchivePlan *plan = user;
    const char *rel;
    if (is_dir || !plan->afs) {
        return 0;
    }
    rel = rel_under(name, plan->prefix);
    if (!rel || has_cvs(rel)) {
        return 0;
    }
    if (eq_ci(rel, "XB_HostPC/SF33RDXB.AFS")) {
        return sf_join(dest, dest_n, plan->install, "data/afs/SF33RDXB.AFS") == 0 ? 1 : -1;
    }
    if (eq_ci(rel, "XB_HostPC/ST3_3_OP.aix")) {
        return sf_join(dest, dest_n, plan->install, "data/audio/ST3_3_OP.aix") == 0 ? 1 : -1;
    }
    if (eq_ci(rel, "sf33rd.xbe")) {
        return sf_join(dest, dest_n, plan->install, XBE_TMP) == 0 ? 1 : -1;
    }
    if (strncasecmp(rel, "media/Fonts/", 12) == 0) {
        char rest[4096];
        if (sf_join(rest, sizeof rest, plan->install, "data/fonts") != 0) {
            return -1;
        }
        return sf_join(dest, dest_n, rest, rel + 12) == 0 ? 1 : -1;
    }
    if (strncasecmp(rel, "media/Shaders/", 14) == 0 && (ends_ci(rel, ".xvu") || ends_ci(rel, ".xpu"))) {
        char rest[4096];
        if (sf_join(rest, sizeof rest, plan->install, "data/shaders/nv2a") != 0) {
            return -1;
        }
        return sf_join(dest, dest_n, rest, rel + 14) == 0 ? 1 : -1;
    }
    return 0;
}

static int map_one_iso(const char *name, int is_dir, char *dest, size_t dest_n, void *user)
{
    struct ArchivePlan *plan = user;
    if (is_dir || strcmp(name, plan->iso_name) != 0) {
        return 0;
    }
    return sf_join(dest, dest_n, plan->install, "data/.import-tmp/disc.iso") == 0 ? 1 : -1;
}

static int is_archive_file(const char *path)
{
    int fd = sf_open_source(path);
    uint8_t b[8];
    ssize_t n;
    if (fd < 0) {
        return 0;
    }
    n = read(fd, b, sizeof b);
    close(fd);
    if (n >= 2 && b[0] == 'P' && b[1] == 'K') {
        return 1;
    }
    if (n >= 6 && b[0] == 0x37 && b[1] == 0x7a && b[2] == 0xbc && b[3] == 0xaf && b[4] == 0x27 && b[5] == 0x1c) {
        return 1;
    }
    if (ends_ci(path, ".zip") || ends_ci(path, ".7z") || ends_ci(path, ".7zip")) {
        return 1;
    }
    return 0;
}

static int import_archive(const char *source, const char *install, const char *tool, SfExtractProgress progress, void *user, SfExtractResult *result, int depth)
{
    struct ArchivePlan plan;
    int written = 0;
    memset(&plan, 0, sizeof plan);
    plan.install = install;
    if (sf_archive_visit(source, visit_plan, &plan, result->error, sizeof result->error) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    if (!plan.afs && plan.iso_name[0] && depth < 2) {
        char iso_path[4096];
        char temp[4096];
        int rc;
        if (sf_join(temp, sizeof temp, install, "data/.import-tmp") != 0 || sf_mkdir_p(temp, result->error, sizeof result->error) != 0) {
            return -1;
        }
        if (sf_archive_export(source, map_one_iso, &plan, progress, user, &written, result->error, sizeof result->error) != 0) {
            sf_rm_rf(temp);
            return -1;
        }
        if (sf_join(iso_path, sizeof iso_path, install, "data/.import-tmp/disc.iso") != 0) {
            sf_rm_rf(temp);
            return -1;
        }
        rc = import_any(iso_path, install, tool, progress, user, result, depth + 1);
        sf_rm_rf(temp);
        return rc;
    }
    if (!plan.afs) {
        if (plan.ps2) {
            sf_set_err(result->error, sizeof result->error, "This is not the Xbox version of the game.");
        } else {
            sf_set_err(result->error, sizeof result->error, "No Xbox disc data was found. Choose an Xbox XDVDFS image, or a zip or 7z of that Xbox disc.");
        }
        return -1;
    }
    if (sf_archive_export(source, map_xbox, &plan, progress, user, &written, result->error, sizeof result->error) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    result->files += written;
    if (!afs_ready(install)) {
        sf_set_err(result->error, sizeof result->error, "The archive did not contain the Xbox 3rd Strike files.");
        return -1;
    }
    return finish_import(install, tool, progress, user, result);
}

static int import_any(const char *source, const char *install, const char *tool, SfExtractProgress progress, void *user, SfExtractResult *result, int depth)
{
    struct stat st;
    char root[4096];
    int ps2 = 0;
    if (sf_stat_source(source, &st) != 0) {
        sf_set_err(result->error, sizeof result->error, "cannot read %s", source);
        return -1;
    }
    if (S_ISDIR(st.st_mode)) {
        if (find_disc_root(source, root, sizeof root, 0, &ps2) == 0) {
            return import_files_from_dir(root, install, tool, progress, user, result);
        }
        if (depth < 2) {
            char payload[4096];
            int left = 8;
            if (find_payload(source, payload, sizeof payload, 0, &left) == 0) {
                return import_any(payload, install, tool, progress, user, result, depth + 1);
            }
        }
        if (ps2) {
            sf_set_err(result->error, sizeof result->error, "This is not the Xbox version of the game.");
        } else {
            sf_set_err(result->error, sizeof result->error, "No Xbox disc data was found. Choose an Xbox XDVDFS image, a zip or 7z of that Xbox disc, or the extracted Xbox folder.");
        }
        return -1;
    }
    if (!S_ISREG(st.st_mode)) {
        sf_set_err(result->error, sizeof result->error, "not an Xbox disc");
        return -1;
    }
    if (is_archive_file(source)) {
        return import_archive(source, install, tool, progress, user, result, depth);
    }
    return import_iso(source, install, tool, progress, user, result);
}

int sf_extract(const char *source, const char *install_dir, const char *shader_tool, SfExtractProgress progress, void *user, SfExtractResult *result)
{
    char data[4096];
    if (!result) {
        return -1;
    }
    memset(result, 0, sizeof *result);
    if (!source || !install_dir) {
        sf_set_err(result->error, sizeof result->error, "missing source or install directory");
        return -1;
    }
    if (sf_join(data, sizeof data, install_dir, "data") != 0 || sf_mkdir_p(data, result->error, sizeof result->error) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    return import_any(source, install_dir, shader_tool, progress, user, result, 0);
}

int sf_retry_shaders(const char *install_dir, const char *shader_tool, SfExtractResult *result)
{
    char spirv[4096];
    int existing = 0;
    if (!result) {
        return -1;
    }
    memset(result, 0, sizeof *result);
    if (!install_dir || !shader_tool || !sf_data_ready(install_dir)) {
        return 0;
    }
    if (sf_join(spirv, sizeof spirv, install_dir, "data/shaders/spirv") != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    if (count_ext(spirv, "", &existing) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    if (existing > 0) {
        return 0;
    }
    if (finish_shaders(install_dir, shader_tool, result) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    return 0;
}
```

```h
#ifndef SF_EXTRACT_H
#define SF_EXTRACT_H

#include <stddef.h>

typedef struct SfExtractResult {
    int files;
    int shaders;
    int spirv;
    int spirv_failed;
    int afs_files;
    char error[512];
    char note[512];
} SfExtractResult;

typedef void (*SfExtractProgress)(const char *path, void *user);

/* 1 when data/ holds the game: the archive and the 21 sound banks. */
int sf_data_ready(const char *install_dir);
int sf_sound_banks_ready(const char *install_dir);

/* source is an Xbox XDVDFS image, a zip or 7z of that Xbox disc, or an extracted Xbox folder. */
int sf_extract(const char *source, const char *install_dir, const char *shader_tool,
               SfExtractProgress progress, void *user, SfExtractResult *result);

int sf_retry_shaders(const char *install_dir, const char *shader_tool, SfExtractResult *result);

#endif
```
