`src/port/input.c`:

```c
/* The game's pads, from SDL3.
 *
 * Retail's pad layer reads each Xbox gamepad (a button word, eight analog
 * buttons, two sticks) and turns it into the library's TARPAD records
 * through two fixed tables; keyConvert then joins each port as the next
 * player the first time it is pressed and turns the pads into the game's
 * switch words. This keeps that conversion and fills the gamepad record
 * from SDL3: port 0 is the keyboard, ports 1 to 3 the first three
 * gamepads. The reference is 3s-decomp-ogxb, Source/XBOX/xb_012146.c
 * (xb_PADRead, xb_PADReadOne, xb_PADConvertStick) and xb_061267.c
 * (keyConvert).
 *
 * Keyboard (the defaults; BUTTON CONFIG's keyboard page changes them and
 * config/host.sqlite keeps them): arrows are the d-pad, Z A, X B, A X,
 * S Y, Q White, E Black, W the left trigger, R the right trigger, Enter
 * Start, Backspace Back. A missing key table is filled from these. */

#include "host/paths.h"
#include "host/compat.h"
#include "port/port.h"
#include "port/store.h"

#include "types.h"
#include "structs.h"

#include <SDL3/SDL.h>

#include <errno.h>
#include <math.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>

#define PORTS 4

/* An Xbox gamepad as the XDK's input sample reads it. */
enum { XB_DPAD_UP = 1, XB_DPAD_DOWN = 2, XB_DPAD_LEFT = 4, XB_DPAD_RIGHT = 8, XB_START = 0x10, XB_BACK = 0x20,
       XB_LTHUMB = 0x40, XB_RTHUMB = 0x80 };
enum { XB_A, XB_B, XB_X, XB_Y, XB_BLACK, XB_WHITE, XB_LTRIG, XB_RTRIG };

typedef struct XbGamepad {
    int connected;
    u16 buttons;
    u8 analog[8];
    f32 lx;
    f32 ly; /* up is positive */
    f32 rx;
    f32 ry;
} XbGamepad;

TARPAD tarpad_root[PORTS];

extern MPP mpp_w;
extern FLPAD *flpad_adr[2];
extern IO io_w;
extern u8 Interface_Type[2];
extern struct _SAVE_W save_w[6];
extern u8 Present_Mode;
extern u8 Game_pause;
extern struct _TASK task[11];
extern u16 p1sw_buff, p2sw_buff, p3sw_buff, p4sw_buff;
extern const u32 flpad_io_map[25];
void flPADSetRepeatSw(FLPAD *pad, u32 IOdata, u8 ctr, u8 times);

/* each analog button past 0x1F at half scale sets a bit above the word */
static const u16 k_analog_bit[8][2] = {
    { 0, 0x100 }, { 1, 0x200 }, { 2, 0x400 }, { 3, 0x800 }, { 4, 0x1000 }, { 5, 0x2000 }, { 6, 0x4000 }, { 7, 0x8000 },
};

/* the 16 library buttons: the gamepad bit, and 1 + the analog index that
 * gives its strength (0 for a digital button) */
static const u32 k_library_button[16][2] = {
    { 1, 0 },      { 2, 0 },      { 4, 0 },      { 8, 0 },      { 0x100, 1 }, { 0x200, 2 }, { 0x1000, 5 }, { 0x4000, 7 },
    { 0x400, 3 }, { 0x800, 4 }, { 0x2000, 6 }, { 0x8000, 8 }, { 64, 0 },   { 128, 0 },   { 32, 0 },     { 16, 0 },
};

/* the d-pad nibble with opposite directions cancelled */
static const u8 k_dpad_fix[16] = { 0, 1, 2, 0, 4, 5, 6, 4, 8, 9, 10, 8, 0, 1, 2, 0 };

/* a stick's direction as d-pad bits, by 22.5 degree sector from the right */
static const u8 k_stick_dir[17] = { 8, 9, 9, 1, 1, 5, 5, 4, 4, 6, 6, 2, 2, 10, 10, 8, 1 };

/* the game's thresholds per port: analog button, left and right stick */
static s16 s_threshold[PORTS][3];
static SDL_Gamepad *s_pads[PORTS];

/* the game's switch bits from the library's, Xbox values */
static const u32 k_ioconv[24][2] = {
    { 0x1, 0x1 },     { 0x2, 0x2 },     { 0x4, 0x4 },      { 0x8, 0x8 },      { 0x100, 0x10 },  { 0x200, 0x20 },
    { 0x400, 0x40 },  { 0x400, 0x80 },  { 0x10, 0x100 },   { 0x20, 0x200 },   { 0x40, 0x400 },  { 0x40, 0x800 },
    { 0x0, 0x1000 },  { 0x0, 0x2000 },  { 0x8000, 0x4000 }, { 0x4000, 0x8000 }, { 0x0, 0x10000 }, { 0x0, 0x20000 },
    { 0x0, 0x40000 }, { 0x0, 0x80000 }, { 0x0, 0x100000 }, { 0x0, 0x200000 }, { 0x0, 0x400000 }, { 0x0, 0x800000 },
};

static void keys_load(void);

s32 xb_PADInit(void)
{
    /* pads keep working when another window has the focus */
    SDL_SetHint(SDL_HINT_JOYSTICK_ALLOW_BACKGROUND_EVENTS, "1");
    SDL_InitSubSystem(SDL_INIT_GAMEPAD);
    memset(s_pads, 0, sizeof s_pads);
    keys_load();
    return 1;
}

void xb_PADSetConfig(s32 i, s16 a, s16 b, s16 c)
{
    if (i >= 0 && i < PORTS) {
        s_threshold[i][0] = a;
        s_threshold[i][1] = b;
        s_threshold[i][2] = c;
    }
}

/* level 0..7 for time frames */
s32 xb_PADSetVibration(s32 pad, s32 level, u32 time)
{
    u32 v = ((u32)level << 13) + 0x1FFF;
    if (v > 0xFFFF) {
        v = 0xFFFF;
    }
    if (pad < 0 || pad >= PORTS) {
        return 0;
    }
    if (s_pads[pad]) {
        SDL_RumbleGamepad(s_pads[pad], (Uint16)v, (Uint16)v, time * 1000 / 60);
    }
    return 1;
}

static void refresh_gamepads(void)
{
    SDL_JoystickID *ids;
    int n = 0;
    int i;
    int port = 1;
    if (getenv("SF_NO_GAMEPADS")) {
        return; /* tests: only the keyboard port and scripted presses */
    }
    for (i = 1; i < PORTS; i++) {
        if (s_pads[i] && !SDL_GamepadConnected(s_pads[i])) {
            SDL_CloseGamepad(s_pads[i]);
            s_pads[i] = NULL;
        }
    }
    ids = SDL_GetGamepads(&n);
    for (i = 0; ids && i < n && port < PORTS; i++) {
        SDL_Gamepad *g = SDL_GetGamepadFromID(ids[i]);
        int j;
        int open = 0;
        for (j = 1; j < PORTS; j++) {
            if (s_pads[j] && s_pads[j] == g) {
                open = 1;
            }
        }
        if (open) {
            continue;
        }
        while (port < PORTS && s_pads[port]) {
            port++;
        }
        if (port < PORTS) {
            s_pads[port] = SDL_OpenGamepad(ids[i]);
            if (s_pads[port]) {
                fprintf(stderr, "input: port %d is %s\n", port, SDL_GetGamepadName(s_pads[port]));
            } else {
                fprintf(stderr, "input: gamepad %u: %s\n", (unsigned)ids[i], SDL_GetError());
            }
        }
    }
    SDL_free(ids);
}

static f32 axis(SDL_Gamepad *g, SDL_GamepadAxis a)
{
    return (f32)SDL_GetGamepadAxis(g, a) / 32767.0f;
}

/* The keyboard as a gamepad: one row per binding. A binding is a button
 * bit (XB_*) or an analog button (index | 0x100); name is its word in
 * config/host.sqlite, label what the keyboard page shows. */
typedef struct KeyBinding {
    SDL_Scancode key;
    u16 target;
} KeyBinding;

#define BIND_ANALOG 0x100
#define KEY_COUNT 14

static const char *const k_key_name[KEY_COUNT] = { "up", "down", "left", "right", "start", "back", "a",
                                                   "b", "x", "y", "black", "white", "ltrigger", "rtrigger" };
static const char *const k_key_label[KEY_COUNT] = { "UP",       "DOWN",     "LEFT",     "RIGHT",    "START",
                                                    "BACK",     "A BUTTON", "B BUTTON", "X BUTTON", "Y BUTTON",
                                                    "BLACK",    "WHITE",    "L TRIGGER", "R TRIGGER" };

static const KeyBinding k_keys_default[KEY_COUNT] = {
    { SDL_SCANCODE_UP, XB_DPAD_UP },        { SDL_SCANCODE_DOWN, XB_DPAD_DOWN },
    { SDL_SCANCODE_LEFT, XB_DPAD_LEFT },    { SDL_SCANCODE_RIGHT, XB_DPAD_RIGHT },
    { SDL_SCANCODE_RETURN, XB_START },      { SDL_SCANCODE_BACKSPACE, XB_BACK },
    { SDL_SCANCODE_Z, BIND_ANALOG | XB_A }, { SDL_SCANCODE_X, BIND_ANALOG | XB_B },
    { SDL_SCANCODE_A, BIND_ANALOG | XB_X }, { SDL_SCANCODE_S, BIND_ANALOG | XB_Y },
    { SDL_SCANCODE_E, BIND_ANALOG | XB_BLACK }, { SDL_SCANCODE_Q, BIND_ANALOG | XB_WHITE },
    { SDL_SCANCODE_W, BIND_ANALOG | XB_LTRIG }, { SDL_SCANCODE_R, BIND_ANALOG | XB_RTRIG },
};

static KeyBinding s_keys[KEY_COUNT];
static int s_block; /* the keyboard page is open: the game sees nothing pressed */

/* config/host.sqlite, the same file as the window. */
static int host_db(char *out, size_t n)
{
    char base[4096];
    int wrote;
    if (sf_exe_dir(base, sizeof base) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    wrote = snprintf(out, n, "%s/config", base);
    if (wrote < 0 || (size_t)wrote >= n) {
        return -1;
    }
    if (mkdir(out, 0755) != 0 && errno != EEXIST) {
        fprintf(stderr, "input: %s: %s\n", out, strerror(errno));
        return -1;
    }
    wrote = snprintf(out, n, "%s/config/host.sqlite", base);
    return wrote < 0 || (size_t)wrote >= n ? -1 : 0;
}

void sf_keys_default(void)
{
    memcpy(s_keys, k_keys_default, sizeof s_keys);
}

/* No key rows yet: the original bindings become the file. A name with an
 * empty scancode keeps the binding already in memory. A damaged database
 * is left as it is. */
static void keys_load(void)
{
    char path[4096 + 64];
    char values[KEY_COUNT][64];
    SDL_Scancode sc;
    int r;
    int i;
    sf_keys_default();
    if (host_db(path, sizeof path) != 0) {
        return;
    }
    r = sf_store_read_keys(path, k_key_name, values, KEY_COUNT);
    if (r == 1) {
        sf_keys_save();
        return;
    }
    if (r != 0) {
        return;
    }
    for (i = 0; i < KEY_COUNT; i++) {
        if (values[i][0] == 0) {
            continue;
        }
        sc = SDL_GetScancodeFromName(values[i]);
        if (sc != SDL_SCANCODE_UNKNOWN) {
            s_keys[i].key = sc;
        }
    }
}

int sf_keys_save(void)
{
    char path[4096 + 64];
    const char *values[KEY_COUNT];
    int i;
    if (host_db(path, sizeof path) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    for (i = 0; i < KEY_COUNT; i++) {
        values[i] = SDL_GetScancodeName(s_keys[i].key);
    }
    if (sf_store_write_keys(path, k_key_name, values, KEY_COUNT) != 0) {
        fprintf(stderr, "input: cannot write %s\n", path);
        return -1;
    }
    return 0;
}

int sf_keys_count(void)
{
    return KEY_COUNT;
}

const char *sf_keys_label(int i)
{
    return k_key_label[i];
}

int sf_keys_get(int i)
{
    return (int)s_keys[i].key;
}

/* A key moves to binding i; the binding that had it takes i's old key. */
void sf_keys_set(int i, int key)
{
    int j;
    for (j = 0; j < KEY_COUNT; j++) {
        if (j != i && (int)s_keys[j].key == key) {
            s_keys[j].key = s_keys[i].key;
        }
    }
    s_keys[i].key = (SDL_Scancode)key;
}

void sf_input_block(int on)
{
    s_block = on;
}

static FILE *s_replay;

/* The tape: every frame's pads from a mark, so frames run again after a
 * rollback read the same pads (src/port/state.c). Recording, each read
 * appends; playing, each read takes the next instead of the devices. */
#define TAPE_MAX 1024
static XbGamepad s_tape[TAPE_MAX][PORTS] SF_KEEP;
static int s_tape_n SF_KEEP;
static int s_tape_at SF_KEEP = -1; /* playing from here; -1 recording or off */
static int s_tape_on SF_KEEP;

void sf_input_tape_mark(void)
{
    s_tape_on = 1;
    s_tape_n = 0;
    s_tape_at = -1;
}

void sf_input_tape_rewind(void)
{
    s_tape_at = 0;
}

void sf_input_tape_off(void)
{
    s_tape_on = 0;
    s_tape_at = -1;
}

int sf_input_replaying(void)
{
    return s_replay != NULL;
}

/* SF_AUTOPRESS=F[:BUTTON],...: that button (Start when none is named;
 * up, down, left, right, a, b, x, y, start, back) held on the keyboard
 * port for six frames from frame F (for driving the game in tests). */
static void auto_press(XbGamepad *x)
{
    static int frame;
    const char *env = getenv("SF_AUTOPRESS");
    int now = frame++;
    while (env && *env) {
        char *end;
        char name[16];
        size_t n = 0;
        long f = strtol(env, &end, 10);
        if (end == env) {
            break;
        }
        env = end;
        if (*env == ':') {
            env++;
            while (*env && *env != ',' && n + 1 < sizeof name) {
                name[n++] = *env++;
            }
        }
        name[n] = 0;
        if (*env == ',') {
            env++;
        }
        if (now < f || now >= f + 6) {
            continue;
        }
        if (n == 0 || !strcmp(name, "start")) {
            x->buttons |= XB_START;
        } else if (!strcmp(name, "back")) {
            x->buttons |= XB_BACK;
        } else if (!strcmp(name, "up")) {
            x->buttons |= XB_DPAD_UP;
        } else if (!strcmp(name, "down")) {
            x->buttons |= XB_DPAD_DOWN;
        } else if (!strcmp(name, "left")) {
            x->buttons |= XB_DPAD_LEFT;
        } else if (!strcmp(name, "right")) {
            x->buttons |= XB_DPAD_RIGHT;
        } else if (!strcmp(name, "a")) {
            x->analog[XB_A] = 0xFF;
        } else if (!strcmp(name, "b")) {
            x->analog[XB_B] = 0xFF;
        } else if (!strcmp(name, "x")) {
            x->analog[XB_X] = 0xFF;
        } else if (!strcmp(name, "y")) {
            x->analog[XB_Y] = 0xFF;
        }
    }
}

static void read_keyboard(XbGamepad *x)
{
    const bool *k = SDL_GetKeyboardState(NULL);
    size_t i;
    x->connected = 1;
    auto_press(x);
    for (i = 0; i < sizeof s_keys / sizeof s_keys[0]; i++) {
        if (!k[s_keys[i].key]) {
            continue;
        }
        if (s_keys[i].target & BIND_ANALOG) {
            x->analog[s_keys[i].target & 7] = 0xFF;
        } else {
            x->buttons |= s_keys[i].target;
        }
    }
}

static void read_gamepad(SDL_Gamepad *g, XbGamepad *x)
{
    f32 lt;
    f32 rt;
    if (!g) {
        return;
    }
    x->connected = 1;
    x->buttons = (u16)((SDL_GetGamepadButton(g, SDL_GAMEPAD_BUTTON_DPAD_UP) ? XB_DPAD_UP : 0) |
                       (SDL_GetGamepadButton(g, SDL_GAMEPAD_BUTTON_DPAD_DOWN) ? XB_DPAD_DOWN : 0) |
                       (SDL_GetGamepadButton(g, SDL_GAMEPAD_BUTTON_DPAD_LEFT) ? XB_DPAD_LEFT : 0) |
                       (SDL_GetGamepadButton(g, SDL_GAMEPAD_BUTTON_DPAD_RIGHT) ? XB_DPAD_RIGHT : 0) |
                       (SDL_GetGamepadButton(g, SDL_GAMEPAD_BUTTON_START) ? XB_START : 0) |
                       (SDL_GetGamepadButton(g, SDL_GAMEPAD_BUTTON_BACK) ? XB_BACK : 0) |
                       (SDL_GetGamepadButton(g, SDL_GAMEPAD_BUTTON_LEFT_STICK) ? XB_LTHUMB : 0) |
                       (SDL_GetGamepadButton(g, SDL_GAMEPAD_BUTTON_RIGHT_STICK) ? XB_RTHUMB : 0));
    x->analog[XB_A] = SDL_GetGamepadButton(g, SDL_GAMEPAD_BUTTON_SOUTH) ? 0xFF : 0;
    x->analog[XB_B] = SDL_GetGamepadButton(g, SDL_GAMEPAD_BUTTON_EAST) ? 0xFF : 0;
    x->analog[XB_X] = SDL_GetGamepadButton(g, SDL_GAMEPAD_BUTTON_WEST) ? 0xFF : 0;
    x->analog[XB_Y] = SDL_GetGamepadButton(g, SDL_GAMEPAD_BUTTON_NORTH) ? 0xFF : 0;
    x->analog[XB_BLACK] = SDL_GetGamepadButton(g, SDL_GAMEPAD_BUTTON_RIGHT_SHOULDER) ? 0xFF : 0;
    x->analog[XB_WHITE] = SDL_GetGamepadButton(g, SDL_GAMEPAD_BUTTON_LEFT_SHOULDER) ? 0xFF : 0;
    lt = axis(g, SDL_GAMEPAD_AXIS_LEFT_TRIGGER);
    rt = axis(g, SDL_GAMEPAD_AXIS_RIGHT_TRIGGER);
    x->analog[XB_LTRIG] = (u8)(lt <= 0.f ? 0 : lt >= 1.f ? 255 : lt * 255.f);
    x->analog[XB_RTRIG] = (u8)(rt <= 0.f ? 0 : rt >= 1.f ? 255 : rt * 255.f);
    x->lx = axis(g, SDL_GAMEPAD_AXIS_LEFTX);
    x->ly = -axis(g, SDL_GAMEPAD_AXIS_LEFTY);
    x->rx = axis(g, SDL_GAMEPAD_AXIS_RIGHTX);
    x->ry = -axis(g, SDL_GAMEPAD_AXIS_RIGHTY);
}

/* retail's xb_PADConvertStick */
static void convert_stick(PAD_STICK *st, s16 depth)
{
    f32 radian = 0.0f;
    if ((st->y | st->x) != 0) {
        radian = (f32)atan2(-st->y, st->x);
        if (radian < 0.0f) {
            radian += 6.2831855f;
        }
    }
    st->rad = radian;
    st->ang = (s16)(360.0f * (st->rad / 6.2831855f));
    st->pow = (s16)sqrt((double)(st->y * st->y) + (double)(st->x * st->x));
    if (st->pow > 0x7F) {
        st->pow = 0x7F;
    }
    if (st->pow < depth) {
        st->x = 0;
        st->y = 0;
        st->rad = 0.0f;
        st->ang = 0;
        st->pow = 0;
    }
}

static u32 stick_button(const PAD_STICK *st)
{
    if (st->pow < 0x10) {
        return 0;
    }
    return k_stick_dir[(int)(st->ang / 22.5f)];
}

/* retail's xb_PADReadOne */
static void read_one(int i, const XbGamepad *x)
{
    TARPAD *t = &tarpad_root[i];
    u8 kan[8];
    u32 io;
    u32 sw = 0;
    int j;
    t->state = 1;
    t->kind = x->connected ? 1 : 0;
    t->anstate = 0x63;
    memset(t->anshot.pow, 0, sizeof t->anshot.pow);
    if (t->kind != 1) {
        t->sw = 0;
        memset(t->stick, 0, sizeof t->stick);
        return;
    }
    io = x->buttons & 0xFF;
    for (j = 0; j < 8; j++) {
        kan[j] = (u8)(x->analog[j] >> 1);
    }
    for (j = 0; j < 8; j++) {
        if (kan[k_analog_bit[j][0]] > 0x1F) {
            io |= k_analog_bit[j][1];
        }
    }
    for (j = 0; j < 16; j++) {
        if (io & k_library_button[j][0]) {
            sw |= flpad_io_map[j];
            if (k_library_button[j][1] != 0) {
                u8 v = kan[k_library_button[j][1] - 1];
                t->anshot.pow[j] = (v < s_threshold[i][0]) ? 0 : v;
            } else {
                t->anshot.pow[j] = 0x7F;
            }
        }
    }
    t->stick[0].x = (s16)(x->lx * 127.5f);
    t->stick[1].x = (s16)(x->rx * 127.5f);
    t->stick[0].y = (s16)(x->ly * -127.5f);
    t->sw = sw;
    t->stick[1].y = (s16)(x->ry * -127.5f);
}

/* retail's xb_PADRead */
/* Every session's pads, frame by frame, go to data/last_session.input;
 * SF_REPLAY=PATH plays such a file back in place of the devices. The game
 * is deterministic in its inputs, so a replay reproduces the session. */
#define RECORD_MAGIC "SF3I"

static FILE *s_record;

static void record_open(void)
{
    static int tried;
    char base[4096];
    char path[4096 + 64];
    const char *replay = getenv("SF_REPLAY");
    if (tried) {
        return;
    }
    tried = 1;
    if (replay) {
        char magic[4];
        s_replay = fopen(replay, "rb");
        if (!s_replay || fread(magic, 4, 1, s_replay) != 1 || memcmp(magic, RECORD_MAGIC, 4) != 0) {
            fprintf(stderr, "input: %s is not a session recording\n", replay);
            if (s_replay) {
                fclose(s_replay);
                s_replay = NULL;
            }
        } else {
            fprintf(stderr, "input: replaying %s\n", replay);
        }
        return;
    }
    if (sf_exe_dir(base, sizeof base) != 0) {
        return;
    }
    snprintf(path, sizeof path, "%s/data/last_session.input", base);
    if (getenv("SF_RECORD")) {
        snprintf(path, sizeof path, "%s", getenv("SF_RECORD")); /* tests keep theirs apart */
    }
    s_record = fopen(path, "wb");
    if (s_record) {
        fwrite(RECORD_MAGIC, 4, 1, s_record);
    }
}

void xb_PADRead(void)
{
    XbGamepad x[PORTS];
    int i;
    record_open();
    SDL_PumpEvents();
    refresh_gamepads();
    memset(x, 0, sizeof x);
    read_keyboard(&x[0]);
    for (i = 1; i < PORTS; i++) {
        static u16 last[PORTS];
        read_gamepad(s_pads[i], &x[i]);
        if (getenv("SF_INPUT_DEBUG") && x[i].buttons != last[i]) {
            int b;
            fprintf(stderr, "input: port %d buttons %04X:", i, x[i].buttons);
            for (b = 0; b < SDL_GAMEPAD_BUTTON_COUNT; b++) {
                if (s_pads[i] && SDL_GetGamepadButton(s_pads[i], (SDL_GamepadButton)b)) {
                    fprintf(stderr, " %s", SDL_GetGamepadStringForButton((SDL_GamepadButton)b));
                }
            }
            fputc('\n', stderr);
            last[i] = x[i].buttons;
        }
    }
    if (s_tape_at >= 0) {
        /* frames running again: the pads they had */
        memcpy(x, s_tape[s_tape_at], sizeof x);
        if (++s_tape_at >= s_tape_n) {
            s_tape_at = -1; /* caught up: the devices again, still taped */
        }
        goto have_pads;
    }
    if (s_replay) {
        if (fread(x, sizeof x, 1, s_replay) != 1) {
            /* past the end: the game goes on with nothing pressed */
            fprintf(stderr, "input: the recording ended at frame %d\n", sf_port_frame);
            fclose(s_replay);
            s_replay = NULL;
            memset(x, 0, sizeof x);
            x[0].connected = 1;
        }
    } else {
        if (s_block) {
            /* the keyboard page has the keys; the game, and so the
             * recording, sees nothing pressed */
            for (i = 0; i < PORTS; i++) {
                int c = x[i].connected;
                memset(&x[i], 0, sizeof x[i]);
                x[i].connected = c;
            }
        }
        if (s_record) {
            fwrite(x, sizeof x, 1, s_record);
            fflush(s_record);
        }
    }
    if (s_tape_on && s_tape_n < TAPE_MAX) {
        memcpy(s_tape[s_tape_n++], x, sizeof x);
    }
have_pads:
    for (i = 0; i < PORTS; i++) {
        TARPAD *t = &tarpad_root[i];
        read_one(i, &x[i]);
        t->conn.xbox.handle = x[i].connected ? &tarpad_root[i] : NULL;
        t->sw = (t->sw & 0xFFF0) | k_dpad_fix[t->sw & 0xF];
        convert_stick(&t->stick[0], s_threshold[i][1]);
        convert_stick(&t->stick[1], s_threshold[i][2]);
        t->sw |= ((stick_button(&t->stick[1]) << 4) | stick_button(&t->stick[0])) << 16;
    }
}

/* retail's xb_ResetInputState: no port has joined */
void sf_input_reset(void)
{
    int i;
    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        mpp_w.xb_player_port[i] = -1;
        mpp_w.xb_port_player[i] = -1;
    }
    mpp_w.xb_pad_count = 0;
}

/* retail's keyConvert */
void keyConvert(void)
{
    IOPad *pad;
    FLPAD *fp;
    u32 currSw;
    s32 i;
    s32 j;
    s32 port;
    s32 repeat_on = 0;

    if (mpp_w.xb_pad_count < 4) {
        for (port = 0; port < 4; port++) {
            if ((u8)mpp_w.xb_port_player[port] == 0xFF && flpad_adr[0][port].sw != 0) {
                mpp_w.xb_port_player[port] = (s8)mpp_w.xb_pad_count;
                mpp_w.xb_player_port[mpp_w.xb_pad_count] = (s8)port;
                mpp_w.xb_pad_count++;
                fprintf(stderr, "input: port %d joins as player %d\n", (int)port, (int)mpp_w.xb_pad_count);
            }
        }
    }

    if (io_w.xb_clear_pads > 0) {
        io_w.xb_clear_pads--;
        flpad_adr[0][0].sw = 0;
        flpad_adr[0][0].sw_new = 0;
        flpad_adr[0][0].sw_off = 0;
        flpad_adr[0][0].sw_old = 0;
        flpad_adr[0][0].sw_repeat = 0;
    }

    if (save_w[Present_Mode].extra_option.contents[0][4] && mpp_w.inGame && Game_pause == 0) {
        repeat_on = 1;
        if (task[3].condition == 1 && task[3].r_no[0] != 10) {
            repeat_on = 0;
        }
    }

    io_w.sw[0] = io_w.sw[1] = io_w.sw[2] = io_w.sw[3] = 0;

    for (port = 0; port < 4; port++) {
        i = mpp_w.xb_port_player[port];
        if (i == -1) {
            continue;
        }
        flPADSetRepeatSw(&flpad_adr[0][port], 0xFF000F, 15, 3);
        if (repeat_on) {
            flPADSetRepeatSw(&flpad_adr[0][port], 0x3FF0, 2, 1);
        } else {
            flPADSetRepeatSw(&flpad_adr[0][port], 0x3FF0, 10, 2);
        }
        fp = &flpad_adr[0][port];
        pad = &io_w.data[i];
        pad->state = fp->state;
        pad->anstate = fp->anstate;
        pad->kind = fp->kind;
        pad->sw = fp->sw;
        pad->sw_old = fp->sw_old;
        pad->sw_new = fp->sw_new;
        pad->sw_off = fp->sw_off;
        pad->sw_chg = fp->sw_chg;
        pad->sw_repeat = fp->sw_repeat;
        pad->stick[0] = fp->stick[0];
        pad->stick[1] = fp->stick[1];

        if (mpp_w.useAnalogStickData) {
            for (j = 16; j <= 20; j += 4) {
                if (!(fp->sw & 0xF)) {
                    pad->sw |= (pad->sw >> j) & 0xF;
                    pad->sw_old |= (pad->sw_old >> j) & 0xF;
                    pad->sw_new |= (pad->sw_new >> j) & 0xF;
                    pad->sw_off |= (pad->sw_off >> j) & 0xF;
                    pad->sw_chg |= (pad->sw_chg >> j) & 0xF;
                    pad->sw_repeat |= (pad->sw_repeat >> j) & 0xF;
                }
            }
        }

        /* retail writes all four players; the game's table holds two */
        if (i < 2) {
            Interface_Type[i] = (pad->kind == 0 || pad->kind == 0x8000) ? 0 : 2;
        }

        currSw = pad->sw;
        for (j = 0; j < 4; j++) {
            if (currSw & k_ioconv[j][1]) {
                io_w.sw[i] |= k_ioconv[j][0];
            }
        }
        for (j = 12; j < 16; j++) {
            if (currSw & k_ioconv[j][1]) {
                io_w.sw[i] |= k_ioconv[j][0];
            }
        }
        if (repeat_on) {
            currSw = pad->sw_repeat;
        }
        for (j = 4; j < 12; j++) {
            if (currSw & k_ioconv[j][1]) {
                io_w.sw[i] |= k_ioconv[j][0];
            }
        }
    }

    p1sw_buff = (u16)io_w.sw[0];
    p2sw_buff = (u16)io_w.sw[1];
    p3sw_buff = (u16)io_w.sw[2];
    p4sw_buff = (u16)io_w.sw[3];
}

/* retail's xb_GetJoiningPlayer: the player who just pressed Start (or the
 * button the game counts with it), player 1 first; -1 for none */
extern u16 p1sw_0, p1sw_1, p2sw_0, p2sw_1;

s32 xb_GetJoiningPlayer(void)
{
    s32 r = -1;
    if ((p2sw_0 & 0x4100) & ~p2sw_1) {
        r = 1;
    }
    if ((p1sw_0 & 0x4100) & ~p1sw_1) {
        r = 0;
    }
    return r;
}

/* retail's xb_GetStartBackPress: 1 when the player's port just pressed
 * Start, 0 for Back, -1 for neither */
s32 xb_GetStartBackPress(s32 pl_id)
{
    s32 port;
    if (pl_id < 0 || pl_id >= 4) {
        return -1;
    }
    port = mpp_w.xb_player_port[pl_id];
    if (port < 0 || port >= 4) {
        return -1;
    }
    switch (flpad_adr[0][port].sw_new & 0x30) {
    case 0x10:
        return 1;
    case 0x20:
        return 0;
    }
    return -1;
}

```

`src/port/jobs.c`:

```c
/* Work the game thread hands off: disc reads, music and sound-effect
 * decoding, the first start's import. A few worker threads take jobs in
 * the order they were given.
 *
 * The game keeps its own time (one frame each 1/60 s, and every result it
 * reads lands on the frame it lands on with no threads), so a job never
 * decides when something happens: the game thread asks for a job's result
 * at the point it needs it, and waits there if the job is not done. A job
 * still queued at that point runs on the game thread at once.
 *
 * SF_THREADS=N sets the workers (0 runs every job on the thread that asks
 * for its result, as the program did before it had workers); the default
 * is one fewer than the processor's cores, two at most, as the game
 * thread, the render thread and the audio device's own thread have the
 * rest. */

#include "port/jobs.h"

#include <SDL3/SDL.h>

#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>

#define WORKERS_MAX 4

enum { JOB_IDLE, JOB_QUEUED, JOB_RUNNING, JOB_DONE };

static SDL_Mutex *s_lock;
static SDL_Condition *s_wake; /* a job was queued, or the pool stops */
static SDL_Condition *s_done; /* a job finished */
static SfJob *s_head;
static SfJob *s_tail;
static SDL_Thread *s_thread[WORKERS_MAX];
static int s_workers;
static int s_stop;

static void run(SfJob *j)
{
    j->fn(j->arg);
    SDL_LockMutex(s_lock);
    j->state = JOB_DONE;
    SDL_BroadcastCondition(s_done);
    SDL_UnlockMutex(s_lock);
}

static int worker(void *unused)
{
    (void)unused;
    SDL_LockMutex(s_lock);
    for (;;) {
        SfJob *j;
        while (!s_head && !s_stop) {
            SDL_WaitCondition(s_wake, s_lock);
        }
        if (s_stop) {
            break;
        }
        j = s_head;
        s_head = j->next;
        if (!s_head) {
            s_tail = NULL;
        }
        j->state = JOB_RUNNING;
        SDL_UnlockMutex(s_lock);
        run(j);
        SDL_LockMutex(s_lock);
    }
    SDL_UnlockMutex(s_lock);
    return 0;
}

void sf_jobs_init(void)
{
    const char *env = getenv("SF_THREADS");
    int n;
    int i;
    if (s_lock) {
        return;
    }
    s_lock = SDL_CreateMutex();
    s_wake = SDL_CreateCondition();
    s_done = SDL_CreateCondition();
    if (env) {
        n = atoi(env);
    } else {
        n = SDL_GetNumLogicalCPUCores() - 1;
        n = n > 2 ? 2 : n;
    }
    n = n < 0 ? 0 : n > WORKERS_MAX ? WORKERS_MAX : n;
    for (i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        s_thread[s_workers] = SDL_CreateThread(worker, "sf3 worker", NULL);
        if (s_thread[s_workers]) {
            s_workers++;
        }
    }
}

int sf_jobs_workers(void)
{
    return s_workers;
}

void sf_job_start(SfJob *j, void (*fn)(void *), void *arg)
{
    if (!s_lock) {
        sf_jobs_init();
    }
    j->fn = fn;
    j->arg = arg;
    j->next = NULL;
    if (s_workers == 0) {
        /* no workers: it runs when its result is asked for */
        j->state = JOB_QUEUED;
        return;
    }
    SDL_LockMutex(s_lock);
    j->state = JOB_QUEUED;
    if (s_tail) {
        s_tail->next = j;
    } else {
        s_head = j;
    }
    s_tail = j;
    SDL_SignalCondition(s_wake);
    SDL_UnlockMutex(s_lock);
}

/* Takes a job off the queue if it is still on it; 1 when it was. */
static int unqueue(SfJob *j)
{
    SfJob **p;
    SfJob *prev = NULL;
    for (p = &s_head; *p; prev = *p, p = &(*p)->next) {
        if (*p == j) {
            *p = j->next;
            if (s_tail == j) {
                s_tail = prev;
            }
            j->next = NULL;
            return 1;
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

void sf_job_wait(SfJob *j)
{
    if (j->state == JOB_IDLE) {
        return;
    }
    if (s_workers == 0) {
        if (j->state == JOB_QUEUED) {
            j->state = JOB_RUNNING;
            j->fn(j->arg);
            j->state = JOB_DONE;
        }
        return;
    }
    SDL_LockMutex(s_lock);
    if (j->state == JOB_QUEUED && unqueue(j)) {
        /* nobody has it yet: this thread does it now */
        j->state = JOB_RUNNING;
        SDL_UnlockMutex(s_lock);
        run(j);
        return;
    }
    while (j->state != JOB_DONE) {
        SDL_WaitCondition(s_done, s_lock);
    }
    SDL_UnlockMutex(s_lock);
}

int sf_job_done(SfJob *j)
{
    int done;
    if (j->state == JOB_IDLE) {
        return 1;
    }
    if (s_workers == 0) {
        return j->state == JOB_DONE;
    }
    SDL_LockMutex(s_lock);
    done = j->state == JOB_DONE;
    SDL_UnlockMutex(s_lock);
    return done;
}

void sf_job_reset(SfJob *j)
{
    sf_job_wait(j);
    j->state = JOB_IDLE;
}

/* A job's own progress: the job publishes with sf_job_progress, a waiter
 * waits for a condition on it with sf_job_wait_for. */
void sf_job_progress(void)
{
    if (s_workers == 0) {
        return;
    }
    SDL_LockMutex(s_lock);
    SDL_BroadcastCondition(s_done);
    SDL_UnlockMutex(s_lock);
}

void sf_job_wait_for(SfJob *j, int (*ready)(void *), void *arg)
{
    if (ready(arg) || j->state == JOB_IDLE) {
        return;
    }
    if (s_workers == 0) {
        sf_job_wait(j);
        return;
    }
    SDL_LockMutex(s_lock);
    if (j->state == JOB_QUEUED && unqueue(j)) {
        j->state = JOB_RUNNING;
        SDL_UnlockMutex(s_lock);
        run(j);
        return;
    }
    while (j->state != JOB_DONE && !ready(arg)) {
        SDL_WaitCondition(s_done, s_lock);
    }
    SDL_UnlockMutex(s_lock);
}

void sf_jobs_shutdown(void)
{
    int i;
    if (!s_lock) {
        return;
    }
    SDL_LockMutex(s_lock);
    s_stop = 1;
    SDL_BroadcastCondition(s_wake);
    SDL_UnlockMutex(s_lock);
    for (i = 0; i < s_workers; i++) {
        SDL_WaitThread(s_thread[i], NULL);
    }
    s_workers = 0;
}

```

`src/port/jobs.h`:

```h
#ifndef SF_PORT_JOBS_H
#define SF_PORT_JOBS_H

/* Work for the worker threads (src/port/jobs.c). A job is the caller's
 * own struct, zeroed before first use, and must not move while it runs. */

typedef struct SfJob {
    void (*fn)(void *);
    void *arg;
    int state;
    struct SfJob *next;
} SfJob;

void sf_jobs_init(void);
void sf_jobs_shutdown(void);

/* The worker threads running: 0 when every job runs on the thread that
 * waits for it. */
int sf_jobs_workers(void);

/* Queue fn(arg). A job must be done (or never started) before it is
 * started again. */
void sf_job_start(SfJob *j, void (*fn)(void *), void *arg);

/* Until the job is done; a job no worker has taken yet runs here. */
void sf_job_wait(SfJob *j);
int sf_job_done(SfJob *j);

/* Waits, then marks the job as never started. */
void sf_job_reset(SfJob *j);

/* For a job that publishes part of its result as it goes: it calls
 * sf_job_progress after each part; sf_job_wait_for waits until ready(arg)
 * or the job is done. ready is read under the pool's lock. */
void sf_job_progress(void);
void sf_job_wait_for(SfJob *j, int (*ready)(void *), void *arg);

#endif
```

```c
/* Xbox Live, offline. The port has no Live; the game asks about it at a
 * few points, and these give it the offline answers.
 *
 * Before each match retail runs a settings exchange (Game01 in Game.c:
 * xb_NetSync_Step, xb_NetSync_CheckPlayers) even offline, where both ends
 * are this console: it packs the local settings, parses them back, and
 * writes them to save_w[2]. The parsing is Live code the decomp has no C
 * for (xb_NetSync_ParsePacket), so the values it writes offline are not
 * known from the code; with both ends local they are the settings save_w
 * already holds, and here the exchange finishes at once and leaves them.
 * Whether it also changes permission_player offline is unconfirmed. */

#include "port/port.h"
#include "types.h"

static s32 s_sync_done SF_GAME;

s32 xb_GetOnlineFlag(void)
{
    return 0;
}

 * Live, drawn into the capture texture) and +0x1C4 (start in the Live
 * menu); retail reads 0 from both when it has no Live work, as here. */
s32 xb_LiveWork_Get2330(void)
{
    return 0;
}

void xb_LiveWork_Set2330(s32 v)
{
    (void)v;
}

s32 xb_LiveWork_Get1C4(void)
{
    return 0;
}

/* The screen capture the Live overlay is drawn over (EFF57's wall and the
 * select screen make it, Game.c lets it go); without Live nothing reads it. */

{
}

{
}

    return s_sync_done;
}

    s_sync_done = v;
}
```

`src/port/main.c`:

```c
/* The native entry. The game itself starts in sf_game_init. */

#include "host/compat.h"
#include "port/jobs.h"
#include "port/port.h"

#include <SDL3/SDL.h>

#if !defined(_WIN32) && !defined(__ANDROID__)
#define SF_BACKTRACE 1 /* glibc's; Android's own crash report has one */
#include <execinfo.h>
#endif
#ifdef __ANDROID__
#include <SDL3/SDL_main.h> /* SDL's activity calls SDL_main */
#include <android/log.h>
#include <dlfcn.h>
#include <jni.h>
#include <pthread.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <unwind.h>
#endif
#include <signal.h>
#include <stdarg.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <fcntl.h>
#include "host/paths.h"

/* A crash writes where it happened to stderr and data/crash.log. */
static char s_crash_path[4096 + 32];

#ifdef __ANDROID__
/* This run's log (data/last_run.log), which a crash also writes to. */
static int s_log_fd = -1;

typedef struct Unwound {
    void *pc[32];
    int n;
} Unwound;

static _Unwind_Reason_Code unwind_one(struct _Unwind_Context *ctx, void *arg)
{
    Unwound *u = arg;
    void *pc = (void *)_Unwind_GetIP(ctx);
    if (pc && u->n < 32) {
        u->pc[u->n++] = pc;
    }
    return u->n < 32 ? _URC_NO_REASON : _URC_END_OF_STACK;
}

/* The crash's stack, each frame as library+offset (function+offset). */
static void android_backtrace(int fd)
{
    Unwound u;
    int i;
    u.n = 0;
    _Unwind_Backtrace(unwind_one, &u);
    for (i = 0; i < u.n; i++) {
        Dl_info info;
        char line[512];
        int len;
        if (dladdr(u.pc[i], &info) && info.dli_fname) {
            const char *lib = strrchr(info.dli_fname, '/');
            lib = lib ? lib + 1 : info.dli_fname;
            len = snprintf(line, sizeof line, "  #%02d %s+0x%lx (%s+0x%lx)\n", i, lib,
                           (unsigned long)((char *)u.pc[i] - (char *)info.dli_fbase), info.dli_sname ? info.dli_sname : "?",
                           info.dli_saddr ? (unsigned long)((char *)u.pc[i] - (char *)info.dli_saddr) : 0ul);
        } else {
            len = snprintf(line, sizeof line, "  #%02d %p\n", i, u.pc[i]);
        }
        (void)!write(fd, line, (size_t)len);
    }
}
#endif

static void on_crash(int sig)
{
#ifdef SF_BACKTRACE
    void *frames[64];
    int n = backtrace(frames, 64);
#endif
    char head[128];
    int fd;
    int len = snprintf(head, sizeof head, "crash: signal %d (%s) at game frame %d\n", sig, strsignal(sig), sf_port_frame);
    (void)!write(2, head, (size_t)len);
#ifdef SF_BACKTRACE
    backtrace_symbols_fd(frames, n, 2);
#endif
#ifdef __ANDROID__
    if (s_log_fd >= 0) {
        /* stderr's pipe may not reach the file before the process ends;
         * a new line first, in case the pipe's last line is half written */
        (void)!write(s_log_fd, "\n", 1);
        (void)!write(s_log_fd, head, (size_t)len);
        android_backtrace(s_log_fd);
        fsync(s_log_fd);
    }
#endif
    fd = open(s_crash_path, O_WRONLY | O_CREAT | O_TRUNC | O_BINARY, 0644);
    if (fd >= 0) {
        (void)!write(fd, head, (size_t)len);
#ifdef SF_BACKTRACE
        backtrace_symbols_fd(frames, n, fd);
#endif
        close(fd);
    }
    signal(sig, SIG_DFL);
    raise(sig);
}

static void crash_handler(void)
{
    char base[4096];
    if (sf_exe_dir(base, sizeof base) == 0) {
        snprintf(s_crash_path, sizeof s_crash_path, "%s/data/crash.log", base);
    }
    signal(SIGSEGV, on_crash);
    signal(SIGABRT, on_crash);
    signal(SIGFPE, on_crash);
    signal(SIGILL, on_crash);
#ifdef SIGBUS
    signal(SIGBUS, on_crash);
#endif
}

#ifdef __ANDROID__
/* Android has no terminal: stderr (every message the port writes) goes to
 * the system log, tag sf3, a line at a time (adb logcat -s sf3). */
static void *log_pump(void *arg)
{
    int fd = (int)(intptr_t)arg;
    char buf[1024];
    size_t have = 0;
    for (;;) {
        ssize_t n = read(fd, buf + have, sizeof buf - 1 - have);
        char *line;
        char *nl;
        if (n <= 0) {
            break;
        }
        have += (size_t)n;
        buf[have] = 0;
        line = buf;
        while ((nl = strchr(line, '\n')) != NULL) {
            *nl = 0;
            __android_log_write(ANDROID_LOG_INFO, "sf3", line);
            if (s_log_fd >= 0) {
                *nl = '\n';
                (void)!write(s_log_fd, line, (size_t)(nl - line + 1));
            }
            line = nl + 1;
        }
        have = strlen(line);
        if (have == sizeof buf - 1) {
            __android_log_write(ANDROID_LOG_INFO, "sf3", line);
            have = 0;
        } else {
            memmove(buf, line, have);
        }
    }
    return NULL;
}

/* The phone has no terminal to read: each run's log is kept in
 * data/last_run.log, and a run that did not end cleanly (a crash, or a
 * stop on an error) is shown, from the end of its log, when the game
 * next starts. data/run.marker is there while a run is. */
static char s_report[3072];
static char s_report_full[65536];

/* data/run.marker holds the running process's id while it runs in the
 * foreground; going to the background, where Android may end it, or a
 * clean quit takes it away. */
static char s_marker[4200];
static int s_prev_pid;
static int s_fatal_seen; /* the system log has a fatal line of that run */

static void run_report_mark(int on)
{
    if (!s_marker[0]) {
        return;
    }
    if (on) {
        int fd = open(s_marker, O_WRONLY | O_CREAT | O_TRUNC, 0644);
        if (fd >= 0) {
            char pid[32];
            int n = snprintf(pid, sizeof pid, "%d", (int)getpid());
            (void)!write(fd, pid, (size_t)n);
            close(fd);
        }
    } else {
        unlink(s_marker);
    }
}

/* A line of the report: the stack's frames less the reporter's own. */
static int keep_frame(const char *p)
{
    return !strstr(p, "android_backtrace") && !strstr(p, "on_crash") && !strstr(p, "sigreturn") &&
           !strstr(p, "(abort+") && !strstr(p, "(raise+");
}

static void report_add(const char *line)
{
    size_t at = strlen(s_report);
    snprintf(s_report + at, sizeof s_report - at, "%s\n", line);
}

/* What Android's own C library said as the process stopped (an abort's
 * reason: FORTIFY, fdsan, an uncaught C++ exception) goes to the system
 * log, not to stderr; an app may read its own lines there. */
static void read_system_log(void)
{
    FILE *f = popen("logcat -d -v brief -t 800 '*:W' 2>/dev/null", "r");
    char line[512];
    size_t at = strlen(s_report_full);
    static char fatal[3][512]; /* the newest three */
    int nf = 0;
    int k;
    if (!f) {
        return;
    }
    at += (size_t)snprintf(s_report_full + at, sizeof s_report_full - at, "\n--- system log ---\n");
    while (fgets(line, sizeof line, f)) {
        if (at < sizeof s_report_full - 1) {
            at += (size_t)snprintf(s_report_full + at, sizeof s_report_full - at, "%s", line);
            if (at > sizeof s_report_full - 1) {
                at = sizeof s_report_full - 1;
            }
        }
        char tag[32];
        snprintf(tag, sizeof tag, "(%5d)", s_prev_pid);
        if (s_prev_pid && strstr(line, tag) &&
            (strncmp(line, "F/", 2) == 0 || strstr(line, "FORTIFY") || strstr(line, "fdsan") ||
             strstr(line, "Abort message") || strstr(line, "terminating"))) {
            line[strcspn(line, "\n")] = 0;
            if (nf == 3) {
                memmove(fatal[0], fatal[1], sizeof fatal - sizeof fatal[0]);
                nf--;
            }
            snprintf(fatal[nf++], sizeof fatal[0], "%s", line);
            s_fatal_seen = 1;
        }
    }
    pclose(f);
    for (k = nf - 1; k >= 0; k--) {
        report_add(fatal[k]);
    }
}

static void run_report_begin(void)
{
    char base[4096];
    char path[4200];
    char prev[4200];
    char marker[4200];
    int stopped;
    int fd;
    if (sf_exe_dir(base, sizeof base) != 0) {
        return;
    }
    snprintf(path, sizeof path, "%s/data", base);
    mkdir(path, 0755);
    snprintf(path, sizeof path, "%s/data/last_run.log", base);
    snprintf(prev, sizeof prev, "%s/data/prev_run.log", base);
    snprintf(marker, sizeof marker, "%s/data/run.marker", base);
    stopped = 0;
    s_prev_pid = 0;
    if ((fd = open(marker, O_RDONLY)) >= 0) {
        char pid[32];
        ssize_t n = read(fd, pid, sizeof pid - 1);
        close(fd);
        pid[n > 0 ? n : 0] = 0;
        s_prev_pid = atoi(pid);
        stopped = 1;
    }
    /* the last run's log moves aside before this run's starts */
    rename(path, prev);
    if (stopped && (fd = open(prev, O_RDONLY)) >= 0) {
        off_t end = lseek(fd, 0, SEEK_END);
        off_t from = end > (off_t)sizeof s_report_full / 2 ? end - (off_t)sizeof s_report_full / 2 : 0;
        ssize_t n;
        lseek(fd, from, SEEK_SET);
        n = read(fd, s_report_full, sizeof s_report_full / 2);
        s_report_full[n > 0 ? n : 0] = 0;
        close(fd);
    }
    if (stopped) {
        static char work[sizeof s_report_full];
        char *save = NULL;
        char *p;
        char *crash = strstr(s_report_full, "crash: ");
        s_report[0] = 0;
        read_system_log(); /* its fatal lines first */
        memcpy(work, s_report_full, sizeof work);
        if (crash) {
            /* the crash and its stack */
            int frames = 0;
            int in = 0;
            for (p = strtok_r(work + (crash - s_report_full), "\n", &save); p; p = strtok_r(NULL, "\n", &save)) {
                if (strstr(p, "--- system log ---")) {
                    break;
                }
                if (!in) {
                    report_add(p);
                    in = 1;
                } else if (strncmp(p, "  #", 3) == 0 && keep_frame(p) && frames < 14) {
                    report_add(p);
                    frames++;
                }
            }
        } else {
            /* no crash: the log's last lines, newest first */
            char *lines[14];
            int ln = 0;
            for (p = strtok_r(work, "\n", &save); p; p = strtok_r(NULL, "\n", &save)) {
                if (strstr(p, "--- system log ---")) {
                    break;
                }
                if (strstr(p, "no shim")) {
                    continue;
                }
                if (ln == 14) {
                    memmove(lines, lines + 1, sizeof lines - sizeof lines[0]);
                    ln--;
                }
                lines[ln++] = p;
            }
            while (ln > 0) {
                report_add(lines[--ln]);
            }
        }
        {
            /* eight lines at most: the dialog's buttons stay on the screen */
            char *q = s_report;
            int k;
            for (k = 0; k < 8 && q && *q; k++) {
                q = strchr(q, '\n');
                q = q ? q + 1 : NULL;
            }
            if (q) {
                *q = 0;
            }
        }
        if (!crash && !strstr(s_report_full, "stopped: ") && !s_fatal_seen) {
            /* it was closed, or ended by Android in the background: no stop */
            s_report[0] = 0;
            s_report_full[0] = 0;
        }
    }
    s_log_fd = open(path, O_WRONLY | O_CREAT | O_TRUNC | O_APPEND, 0644);
    snprintf(s_marker, sizeof s_marker, "%s", marker);
    run_report_mark(1);
}

/* SF3Activity.copyText: the text on the clipboard, to paste anywhere. */
static void copy_text(const char *text)
{
    JNIEnv *env = (JNIEnv *)SDL_GetAndroidJNIEnv();
    jobject act = (jobject)SDL_GetAndroidActivity();
    jclass cls;
    jmethodID m;
    jstring s;
    if (!env || !act) {
        return;
    }
    cls = (*env)->GetObjectClass(env, act);
    m = (*env)->GetMethodID(env, cls, "copyText", "(Ljava/lang/String;)V");
    s = (*env)->NewStringUTF(env, text);
    if (m && s) {
        (*env)->CallVoidMethod(env, act, m, s);
    }
    if ((*env)->ExceptionCheck(env)) {
        (*env)->ExceptionClear(env);
    }
    (*env)->DeleteLocalRef(env, s);
    (*env)->DeleteLocalRef(env, cls);
    (*env)->DeleteLocalRef(env, act);
}

static void run_report_show(void)
{
    SDL_MessageBoxButtonData buttons[2];
    SDL_MessageBoxData box;
    int pressed = 0;
    if (!s_report[0]) {
        return;
    }
    memset(buttons, 0, sizeof buttons);
    buttons[0].flags = SDL_MESSAGEBOX_BUTTON_RETURNKEY_DEFAULT;
    buttons[0].buttonID = 0;
    buttons[0].text = "OK";
    buttons[1].buttonID = 1;
    buttons[1].text = "Copy log";
    memset(&box, 0, sizeof box);
    box.flags = SDL_MESSAGEBOX_WARNING;
    box.title = "The last run stopped";
    box.message = s_report;
    box.numbuttons = 2;
    box.buttons = buttons;
    if (SDL_ShowMessageBox(&box, &pressed) && pressed == 1) {
        copy_text(s_report_full);
    }
}


static void log_to_logcat(void)
{
    int p[2];
    pthread_t t;
    run_report_begin();
    setvbuf(stderr, NULL, _IOLBF, 0);
    if (pipe(p) != 0) {
        return;
    }
    dup2(p[1], 2);
    close(p[1]);
    pthread_create(&t, NULL, log_pump, (void *)(intptr_t)p[0]);
    pthread_detach(t);
}
#endif

int main(int argc, char **argv)
{
    (void)argc;
    (void)argv;
#ifdef __ANDROID__
    log_to_logcat();
#endif
    {
        int cli = sf_setup_cli(argc, argv);
        if (cli >= 0) {
            return cli;
        }
    }
    crash_handler();
    SDL_SetHint(SDL_HINT_JOYSTICK_ALLOW_BACKGROUND_EVENTS, "1");
    if (getenv("SF_HEADLESS") && getenv("SF_RENDERER") && strcmp(getenv("SF_RENDERER"), "opengl") == 0) {
        /* a test of the OpenGL screen: nothing on the desktop */
        SDL_SetHint(SDL_HINT_VIDEO_DRIVER, "offscreen");
    }
#ifdef __ANDROID__
    /* landscape, held there (not turned by the sensor) until SCREEN ADJUST's
     * ORIENTATION says otherwise (src/port/display.c): SDL would otherwise
     * let a resizable window turn with the phone */
    SDL_SetHint(SDL_HINT_ORIENTATIONS, "LandscapeLeft");
#endif
    if (!SDL_Init(SDL_INIT_VIDEO | SDL_INIT_GAMEPAD | SDL_INIT_EVENTS)) {
        fprintf(stderr, "SDL_Init: %s\n", SDL_GetError());
        return 1;
    }
#ifdef __ANDROID__
    run_report_show();
    fprintf(stderr, "android: API %d\n", SDL_GetAndroidSDKVersion());
#endif
    sf_game_init();
    while (!sf_port_quitting()) {
        SDL_Event ev;
        while (SDL_PollEvent(&ev)) {
            if (ev.type == SDL_EVENT_QUIT || ev.type == SDL_EVENT_TERMINATING) {
                sf_port_quit();
            } else if (ev.type == SDL_EVENT_WILL_ENTER_BACKGROUND) {
                sf_sound_pause(1); /* Android: SDL holds this thread until the app is back */
#ifdef __ANDROID__
                run_report_mark(0); /* Android may end it there: not a stop */
#endif
            } else if (ev.type == SDL_EVENT_DID_ENTER_FOREGROUND) {
                sf_sound_pause(0);
#ifdef __ANDROID__
                run_report_mark(1);
#endif
            }
        }
        if (!sf_port_quitting()) {
            sf_game_frame();
        }
    }
    /* the sound first, so nothing plays on while the window goes */
    sf_sound_shutdown();
    sf_video_shutdown();
    sf_jobs_shutdown();
#ifdef __ANDROID__
    run_report_mark(0);
#endif
    SDL_Quit();
    return 0;
}

```

`src/port/math.c`:

```c
/* The matrix helpers the game's njScale, njTranslate and njCalcPoint use
 * (DC_Ghost.c), with D3DX's conventions: row vectors, a 4x4 row-major
 * matrix, out = a * b, a point transformed with w = 1 into four floats.
 * The output may be one of the inputs. */

#include "types.h"
#include "structs.h"

#include <string.h>

f32 *D3DXVec3Transform(f32 *out, const Vec3 *v, const MTX *m)
{
    f32 r[4];
    int c;
    for (c = 0; c < 4; c++) {
        r[c] = v->x * m->a[0][c] + v->y * m->a[1][c] + v->z * m->a[2][c] + m->a[3][c];
    }
    memcpy(out, r, sizeof r);
    return out;
}

MTX *D3DXMatrixMultiply(MTX *out, const MTX *a, const MTX *b)
{
    MTX r;
    int i;
    int j;
    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        for (j = 0; j < 4; j++) {
            r.a[i][j] = a->a[i][0] * b->a[0][j] + a->a[i][1] * b->a[1][j] + a->a[i][2] * b->a[2][j] +
                        a->a[i][3] * b->a[3][j];
        }
    }
    *out = r;
    return out;
}

MTX *D3DXMatrixScaling(MTX *out, f32 x, f32 y, f32 z)
{
    memset(out, 0, sizeof *out);
    out->a[0][0] = x;
    out->a[1][1] = y;
    out->a[2][2] = z;
    out->a[3][3] = 1.0f;
    return out;
}

MTX *D3DXMatrixTranslation(MTX *out, f32 x, f32 y, f32 z)
{
    memset(out, 0, sizeof *out);
    out->a[0][0] = 1.0f;
    out->a[1][1] = 1.0f;
    out->a[2][2] = 1.0f;
    out->a[3][0] = x;
    out->a[3][1] = y;
    out->a[3][2] = z;
    out->a[3][3] = 1.0f;
    return out;
}

```

`src/port/menu_ext.c`:

```c
/* The port's additions to the game's menus, drawn with the game's own
 * pieces (its tone-down, the training menu's red band, its menu font and
 * menu sounds) so they look like the rest of the game.
 *
 * - The mode menu's SELECT GAME (retail's way back to the Anniversary
 *   Collection's game list) is EXIT GAME: its prompt asks about quitting,
 *   and Reboot_Program, where retail leaves, quits the port cleanly.
 * - REPLAY's save and load pick one of ten slots on a page of their own
 *   (sf_replay_page, run by SaveMove), read with the game's pad input.
 * - BUTTON CONFIG has a keyboard page: F1 opens it over the screen, each
 *   Xbox button's key is shown and can be changed, and the keys are kept
 *   in config/host.sqlite. While it is open the game sees nothing
 *   pressed, so a session recording stays a faithful replay. */

#include "host/compat.h"
#include "port/port.h"

#include "types.h"
#include "structs.h"

#include <SDL3/SDL.h>

#include <ctype.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdarg.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <time.h>

extern const s8 *Menu_Letter_Data[2][70]; /* 67 to 69: the port's window rows */
extern struct _TASK task[11];
extern u8 No_Trans;
extern const s16 Fade_Pos_tbl[8];
extern f32 PrioBase[];

void njColorBlendingMode(s32 mode, s32 blend);
void njDrawPolygon2D(PAL_CURSOR *poly, s32 n, f32 pri, s32 attr, s32 kind);
void SSPutStr_Bigger(u16 x, u16 y, u8 atr, s8 *str, f32 sc, u8 gr, u16 priority);
void Akaobi(void);
void SE_cursor_move(void);
void SE_selected(void);
void SE_dir_cursor_move(void);

/* The menu font's palettes: 6 white, 4 yellow, 9 the light blue of the
 * game's PRESS START lines. COL_TITLE is 9 with the training menu's
 * title shading. */
enum { COL_TEXT = 6, COL_CURSOR = 4, COL_VALUE = 9, COL_TITLE = 0x109 };

/* The game's 384-pixel-wide screen; the menu font is 8 pixels a letter.
 * COL_TITLE is palette 9 with the training menu's title shading. */
static void put_text(int x, int y, const char *s, float sc, int col, int prio)
{
    char buf[64];
    size_t i;
    for (i = 0; s[i] && i + 1 < sizeof buf; i++) {
        buf[i] = (char)toupper((unsigned char)s[i]);
    }
    buf[i] = 0;
    if (col == COL_TITLE) {
        SSPutStr_Bigger((u16)x, (u16)y, 9, (s8 *)buf, sc, 2, (u16)prio);
    } else {
        SSPutStr_Bigger((u16)x, (u16)y, (u8)col, (s8 *)buf, sc, 0, (u16)prio);
    }
}

static void put_centred(int y, const char *s, float sc, int col, int prio)
{
    put_text((int)(192.0f - (float)strlen(s) * 4.0f * sc), y, s, sc, col, prio);
}

/* ---- retail's tone-down, as the pause and training menus draw it ---- */

/* Darkens the screen: black with alpha a at PrioBase[prio] (retail's
 * xb_ToneDownCopy is the game's ToneDown with polygon kind 2). */
void xb_ToneDownCopy(u8 a, u8 prio)
{
    PAL_CURSOR pc;
    PAL_CURSOR_P p[4];
    PAL_CURSOR_COL c[4];
    int i;
    if (No_Trans) {
        return;
    }
    njColorBlendingMode(0, 1);
    pc.p = p;
    pc.col = c;
    pc.tex = NULL;
    pc.num = 4;
    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        c[i].color = (u32)a << 24;
        p[i].x = (f32)Fade_Pos_tbl[i * 2];
        p[i].y = (f32)Fade_Pos_tbl[i * 2 + 1];
    }
    njDrawPolygon2D(&pc, 4, PrioBase[prio], 0x60, 2);
}

/* ---- the button pictures ---- */

/* The Xbox button pictures (BUTTON CONFIG's rows, the training menu's
 * button list) are cells of the game's screen texture 5. The game hands
 * each to retail's queue (dispButtonImage, xb_DrawScreenShot) and retail
 * draws the queue after the frame's other 2D (xb_DrawButtonImages, in
 * the frame function after CP3toPS2Draw); sf_button_images_draw is that. */

extern u32 D_007A9AA8; /* the pictures' colour (xb_SetScreenShotColor) */
extern const u8 scrnAddTex1UV[9][4];
extern Texture ppgScrTex;
extern Palette ppgScrPalShot;
s32 ppgGetUsingTextureHandle(Texture *tch, s32 ix);
s32 ppgGetUsingPaletteHandle(Palette *pal, s32 ix);
void setFilterMode(s32 mode);
void ps2SeqsRenderQuadInit_A(void);
void ps2SeqsRenderQuad_A2(Sprite *prm, u32 col);
void ps2SeqsRenderQuadEnd(void);
s32 flSetRenderState(s32 state, u32 value);

#define BUTTON_IMAGES 16

static struct {
    Sprite spr;
    u32 col;
} s_btn[BUTTON_IMAGES];
static int s_btn_n;

void xb_QueueButtonImage(void *quad, s32 col, u8 a, u16 b)
{
    (void)a;
    (void)b;
    if (s_btn_n < BUTTON_IMAGES) {
        memcpy(&s_btn[s_btn_n].spr, quad, sizeof(Sprite));
        s_btn[s_btn_n].col = (u32)col;
        s_btn_n++;
    }
}

void xb_ClearButtonImages(void)
{
    s_btn_n = 0;
}

/* Picture uv (scrnAddTex1UV) over w by h at (x, y) on the 640x448
 * screen, a its transparency; a priority past 0x7F is in front of all. */
void xb_DrawScreenShot(s32 x, s32 y, s32 prio, s32 w, s32 h, u8 a, s32 uv)
{
    Sprite spr;
    u32 col;
    f32 z;
    if (No_Trans || uv < 0 || uv > 8) {
        return;
    }
    col = (D_007A9AA8 & 0x00FFFFFFu) | ((u32)(u8)(0xFF - a) << 24);
    memset(&spr, 0, sizeof spr);
    spr.texCode = ((u32)ppgGetUsingPaletteHandle(&ppgScrPalShot, 0) << 16) | (u32)ppgGetUsingTextureHandle(&ppgScrTex, 5);
    spr.v[0].x = (f32)x;
    spr.v[0].y = (f32)y;
    spr.v[3].x = (f32)(x + w);
    spr.v[3].y = (f32)(y + h);
    z = prio <= 0x7F ? PrioBase[prio] : -1.0f;
    spr.v[0].z = spr.v[3].z = z;
    spr.t[0].s = scrnAddTex1UV[uv][0] / 128.0f;
    spr.t[3].s = (scrnAddTex1UV[uv][0] + scrnAddTex1UV[uv][2]) / 128.0f;
    spr.t[0].t = scrnAddTex1UV[uv][1] / 128.0f;
    spr.t[3].t = (scrnAddTex1UV[uv][1] + scrnAddTex1UV[uv][3]) / 128.0f;
    xb_QueueButtonImage(&spr, (s32)col, 1, 0);
}

void sf_button_images_draw(void)
{
    int i;
    if (s_btn_n == 0) {
        return;
    }
    setFilterMode(1);
    ps2SeqsRenderQuadInit_A();
    for (i = 0; i < s_btn_n; i++) {
        flSetRenderState(4, s_btn[i].spr.texCode);
        ps2SeqsRenderQuad_A2(&s_btn[i].spr, s_btn[i].col);
    }
    ps2SeqsRenderQuadEnd();
    s_btn_n = 0;
}

/* ---- retail's debug-font print, in the menu font ---- */

/* Retail prints a few messages (the pause screen's "Please reconnect the
 * controller") with its debug font at 640x448 positions, in cells cw by ch
 * pixels. Here the same text is the menu font on the 384x224 screen. */
static struct {
    int x, y; /* 640x448 */
    int prio;
} s_pr = { 0, 0, 1 };

void xb_PrintSetAttr(s16 attr)
{
    (void)attr;
}

void xb_PrintSetScale(f32 z)
{
    int i;
    s_pr.prio = 1;
    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
        if (PrioBase[i] == z) {
            s_pr.prio = i;
        }
    }
}

void xb_PrintSetColor(s32 cw, s32 ch)
{
    (void)cw;
    (void)ch;
}

void xb_PrintSetPos(s16 x, s16 y)
{
    s_pr.x = x;
    s_pr.y = y;
}

s32 xb_PrintString(const char *fmt, ...)
{
    char buf[128];
    va_list ap;
    float sc;
    va_start(ap, fmt);
    vsnprintf(buf, sizeof buf, fmt, ap);
    va_end(ap);
    /* the menu font, 8 pixels a letter on the 384-wide screen, fits the
     * boxes retail draws round its 22-pixel cells (13 here) */
    sc = 1.0f;
    put_text(s_pr.x * 384 / 640, s_pr.y * 224 / 448, buf, sc, COL_TEXT, s_pr.prio);
    return (s32)strlen(buf);
}

/* ---- EXIT GAME ---- */

/* A key's name as the menu font shows it, at most n - 1 letters. */
static void key_name(int key, char *out, size_t n)
{
    const char *name = SDL_GetScancodeName((SDL_Scancode)key);
    snprintf(out, n, "%s", name && *name ? name : "NONE");
}

/* "YES: [A] (Z)   NO: [B] (X)", centred at y: retail's Yes/No line with
 * the A and B button pictures (scrnAddTex1UV 4 and 5), and the keyboard
 * keys that press them. */
static void draw_yes_no(int y)
{
    char ka[24];
    char kb[24];
    char a[40];
    char b[40];
    int n;
    int x;
    key_name(sf_keys_get(6), ka, sizeof ka);
    key_name(sf_keys_get(7), kb, sizeof kb);
    snprintf(a, sizeof a, "(%s)   NO:", ka);
    snprintf(b, sizeof b, "(%s)", kb);
    /* "YES: " + picture (2 letters wide) + a + " " + picture + b */
    n = 5 + 2 + (int)strlen(a) + 1 + 2 + (int)strlen(b);
    x = 192 - n * 4;
    put_text(x, y, "YES: ", 1.0f, COL_TEXT, 1);
    x += 5 * 8;
    xb_DrawScreenShot(x * 640 / 384, y * 2 - 4, 4, 22, 22, 0, 4);
    x += 2 * 8;
    put_text(x, y, a, 1.0f, COL_TEXT, 1);
    x += ((int)strlen(a) + 1) * 8;
    xb_DrawScreenShot(x * 640 / 384, y * 2 - 4, 4, 22, 22, 0, 5);
    x += 2 * 8;
    put_text(x, y, b, 1.0f, COL_TEXT, 1);
}

/* Retail prints these with its debug font and the A and B button
 * pictures; here they are the menu font, with the same pictures. Type 0 is the mode menu's last
 * entry (EXIT GAME here), 1 the return to the title, 2 a disc error. */
void xb_DrawSystemMessage(s32 type, s32 x, s32 y, s32 cw, s32 ch, s32 lh)
{
    (void)x;
    (void)y;
    (void)cw;
    (void)ch;
    (void)lh;
    switch (type) {
    case 0:
        put_centred(88, "EXIT THE GAME.", 1.0f, COL_TEXT, 1);
        put_centred(102, "IS THIS OK ?", 1.0f, COL_TEXT, 1);
        draw_yes_no(124);
        break;
    case 1:
        put_centred(80, "RETURNING TO TITLE SCREEN.", 1.0f, COL_TEXT, 1);
        put_centred(94, "ALL UNSAVED DATA WILL BE LOST.", 1.0f, COL_TEXT, 1);
        put_centred(108, "IS THIS OK ?", 1.0f, COL_TEXT, 1);
        draw_yes_no(130);
        break;
    default:
        put_centred(90, "THE GAME DATA COULD NOT BE READ.", 1.0f, COL_TEXT, 1);
        put_centred(104, "IMPORT THE DISC AGAIN.", 1.0f, COL_TEXT, 1);
        break;
    }
}

s32 Reboot_Program(const char *path)

    (void)path;
    sf_port_quit();
    return 0;
}

}

}

{

    (void)a;

/* ---- the keyboard page ---- */

/* Laid out as BUTTON CONFIG is, in two columns: the d-pad, START and
 * BACK (bindings 0 to 5) on the left, the eight buttons (6 to 13) on the
 * right, and DEFAULT SETTING and EXIT under them. */
enum { ROW_DEFAULT = 14, ROW_EXIT = 15 };
#define LEFT_ROWS 6
#define RIGHT_ROWS 8
#define ROW_Y(r) (50 + (r) * 16)
#define BOTTOM_Y 186

static int s_open;
static int s_row;
static int s_waiting; /* for the key of s_row */
static int s_hold;    /* blocking on after closing until every key is up */
static bool s_prev[SDL_SCANCODE_COUNT];

/* BUTTON CONFIG from the mode menu's OPTION: the menu task (3) in
 * After_Title, Button_Config, taking input. */
static int on_button_config(void)
{
    const struct _TASK *t = &task[3];
    return t->condition != 0 && t->r_no[0] == 0 && t->r_no[1] == 10 && t->r_no[2] == 3;
}

static int any_key_down(const bool *k)
{
    int i;
    for (i = 0; i < SDL_SCANCODE_COUNT; i++) {
        if (k[i]) {
            return 1;
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

/* The first key pressed this frame, or -1. */
static int key_pressed(const bool *k)
{
    int i;
    for (i = 1; i < SDL_SCANCODE_COUNT; i++) {
        if (k[i] && !s_prev[i]) {
            return i;
        }
    }
    return -1;
}

static void page_close(void)
{
    s_open = 0;
    s_waiting = 0;
    s_hold = 1;
}

/* The cursor's next row for a direction: up and down within a column
 * (the bottom row under each), left and right across. */
static int row_step(int row, SDL_Scancode dir)
{
    int right = row >= LEFT_ROWS && row < ROW_DEFAULT;
    int r = right ? row - LEFT_ROWS : row;
    switch (dir) {
    case SDL_SCANCODE_UP:
        if (row == ROW_DEFAULT) {
            return LEFT_ROWS - 1;
        }
        if (row == ROW_EXIT) {
            return LEFT_ROWS + RIGHT_ROWS - 1;
        }
        if (r == 0) {
            return right ? ROW_EXIT : ROW_DEFAULT;
        }
        return row - 1;
    case SDL_SCANCODE_DOWN:
        if (row == ROW_DEFAULT) {
            return 0;
        }
        if (row == ROW_EXIT) {
            return LEFT_ROWS;
        }
        if (r == (right ? RIGHT_ROWS : LEFT_ROWS) - 1) {
            return right ? ROW_EXIT : ROW_DEFAULT;
        }
        return row + 1;
    case SDL_SCANCODE_LEFT:
    case SDL_SCANCODE_RIGHT:
        if (row == ROW_DEFAULT) {
            return ROW_EXIT;
        }
        if (row == ROW_EXIT) {
            return ROW_DEFAULT;
        }
        return right ? (r < LEFT_ROWS ? r : LEFT_ROWS - 1) : LEFT_ROWS + r;
    default:
        return row;
    }
}

static void page_input(const bool *k)
{
    int key = key_pressed(k);
    if (key < 0) {
        return;
    }
    if (s_waiting) {
        if (key == SDL_SCANCODE_ESCAPE) {
            s_waiting = 0; /* keep the old key */
            SE_dir_cursor_move();
        } else if (key != SDL_SCANCODE_F1) {
            sf_keys_set(s_row, key);
            sf_keys_save();
            s_waiting = 0;
            SE_selected();
        }
        return;
    }
    switch (key) {
    case SDL_SCANCODE_UP:
    case SDL_SCANCODE_DOWN:
    case SDL_SCANCODE_LEFT:
    case SDL_SCANCODE_RIGHT:
        s_row = row_step(s_row, (SDL_Scancode)key);
        SE_cursor_move();
        break;
    case SDL_SCANCODE_RETURN:
    case SDL_SCANCODE_KP_ENTER:
        SE_selected();
        if (s_row == ROW_EXIT) {
            page_close();
        } else if (s_row == ROW_DEFAULT) {
            sf_keys_default();
            sf_keys_save();
        } else {
            s_waiting = 1;
        }
        break;
    case SDL_SCANCODE_ESCAPE:
    case SDL_SCANCODE_F1:
        SE_selected();
        page_close();
        break;
    default:
        break;
    }
}

/* One binding: its label, then its key (or the prompt while it waits). */
static void draw_binding(int i, int x, int y, int key_x, int frame)
{
    char k[16];
    int on = i == s_row;
    put_text(x, y, sf_keys_label(i), 1.0f, on ? COL_CURSOR : COL_TEXT, 1);
    if (on && s_waiting) {
        if ((frame >> 4) & 1) {
            put_text(key_x, y, "PRESS A KEY", 1.0f, COL_CURSOR, 1);
        }
        return;
    }
    key_name(sf_keys_get(i), k, sizeof k);
    put_text(key_x, y, k, 1.0f, on ? COL_CURSOR : COL_VALUE, 1);
}

static void page_draw(int frame)
{
    int i;
    xb_ToneDownCopy(0xE8, 2);
    Akaobi();
    put_text(0x58, 0x16, "KEYBOARD CONFIG", 1.5f, COL_TITLE, 1);
    for (i = 0; i < LEFT_ROWS; i++) {
        draw_binding(i, 24, ROW_Y(i), 80, frame);
    }
    for (i = 0; i < RIGHT_ROWS; i++) {
        draw_binding(LEFT_ROWS + i, 192, ROW_Y(i), 272, frame);
    }
    put_text(24, BOTTOM_Y, "DEFAULT SETTING", 1.0f, s_row == ROW_DEFAULT ? COL_CURSOR : COL_TEXT, 1);
    put_text(192, BOTTOM_Y, "EXIT", 1.0f, s_row == ROW_EXIT ? COL_CURSOR : COL_TEXT, 1);
    put_centred(208, s_waiting ? "ESC: CANCEL" : "ENTER: CHANGE   ESC: BACK", 1.0f, COL_VALUE, 1);
}

void sf_menu_ext_init(void)
{
    Menu_Letter_Data[0][7] = (const s8 *)"EXIT GAME";
    Menu_Letter_Data[1][7] = (const s8 *)"EXIT GAME";
}

/* Each frame, after the game's tasks and before the frame is drawn. */
void sf_menu_ext_frame(void)
{
    static int frame;
    const bool *k = SDL_GetKeyboardState(NULL);
    int here = on_button_config();
    frame++;
    if (s_open && !here) {
        page_close();
    }
    if (s_open) {
        page_input(k);
        if (s_open) {
            page_draw(frame);
        }
    } else if (here) {
        put_centred(214, "F1: KEYBOARD CONFIG", 1.0f, COL_VALUE, 1);
        /* SF_KEYPAGE=FRAME opens it on that frame (tests: no keyboard) */
        const char *test = getenv("SF_KEYPAGE");
        if ((k[SDL_SCANCODE_F1] && !s_prev[SDL_SCANCODE_F1] && !sf_input_replaying()) ||
            (test && atoi(test) == sf_port_frame)) {
            s_open = 1;
            s_row = 0;
            s_waiting = 0;
            SE_selected();
        }
    }
    if (s_hold && !any_key_down(k)) {
        s_hold = 0;
    }
    sf_input_block(s_open || s_hold);
    memcpy(s_prev, k, sizeof s_prev);
}

/* ---- the replay slots ---- */

/* The characters by the game's index (My_char). */
static const char *const k_char_name[20] = { "GILL",  "ALEX", "RYU",  "YUN",   "DUDLEY",  "NECRO",  "HUGO",
                                             "IBUKI", "ELENA", "ORO", "YANG",  "KEN",     "SEAN",   "URIEN",
                                             "AKUMA", "CHUN-LI", "MAKOTO", "Q", "TWELVE", "REMY" };

extern u16 PLsw[2][2];
extern u8 D_007AA5EF;

enum { RP_LIST, RP_CONFIRM, RP_DONE };

static struct {
    int save;
    int step;
    int row;
    int timer;
    int has[SF_REPLAY_SLOTS];
    int chars[SF_REPLAY_SLOTS][2];
    long long when[SF_REPLAY_SLOTS];
    const char *note; /* a line under the list for a while */
    int note_timer;
} s_rp;

static const char *char_name(int c)
{
    return c >= 0 && c < 20 ? k_char_name[c] : "?";
}

static void rp_scan(void)
{
    int i;
    for (i = 0; i < SF_REPLAY_SLOTS; i++) {
        s_rp.has[i] = sf_replay_slot(i, s_rp.chars[i], &s_rp.when[i]);
    }
}

/* The pads' new presses, as the game's menus read them, and who pressed. */
static u16 rp_pressed(int *who)
{
    int p;
    for (p = 0; p < 2; p++) {
        u16 sw = (u16)(~PLsw[p][1] & PLsw[p][0]);
        if (sw & 0x303) {
            *who = p;
            return sw;
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

static void rp_draw(int frame)
{
    int i;
    xb_ToneDownCopy(0xE8, 2);
    Akaobi();
    put_text(0x68, 0x16, s_rp.save ? "SAVE REPLAY" : "LOAD REPLAY", 1.5f, COL_TITLE, 1);
    for (i = 0; i < SF_REPLAY_SLOTS; i++) {
        int y = 50 + i * 15;
        int on = i == s_rp.row;
        int col = on ? COL_CURSOR : COL_TEXT;
        char num[4];
        char line[48];
        snprintf(num, sizeof num, "%02d", i + 1);
        put_text(32, y, num, 1.0f, col, 1);
        if (s_rp.has[i] > 0) {
            char date[24];
            time_t t = (time_t)s_rp.when[i];
            struct tm tm;
            snprintf(line, sizeof line, "%s VS %s", char_name(s_rp.chars[i][0]), char_name(s_rp.chars[i][1]));
            put_text(64, y, line, 1.0f, col, 1);
            if (t && localtime_r(&t, &tm)) {
                strftime(date, sizeof date, "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M", &tm);
                put_text(232, y, date, 1.0f, on ? COL_CURSOR : COL_VALUE, 1);
            }
        } else {
            put_text(64, y, s_rp.has[i] < 0 ? "DAMAGED DATA" : "NO DATA", 1.0f, on ? COL_CURSOR : COL_VALUE, 1);
        }
    }
    if (s_rp.step == RP_CONFIRM) {
        if ((frame >> 4) & 1) {
            put_centred(204, "OVERWRITE THIS REPLAY ?", 1.0f, COL_CURSOR, 1);
        }
        put_centred(214, "YES: A   NO: B", 1.0f, COL_VALUE, 1);
    } else if (s_rp.note_timer > 0) {
        put_centred(208, s_rp.note, 1.0f, COL_CURSOR, 1);
    } else {
        put_centred(208, s_rp.save ? "A: SAVE   B: BACK" : "A: LOAD   B: BACK", 1.0f, COL_VALUE, 1);
    }
}

int sf_replay_page(int start, int save)
{
    static int frame;
    int who = 0;
    u16 sw;
    if (start) {
        memset(&s_rp, 0, sizeof s_rp);
        s_rp.save = save;
        rp_scan();
        /* loading starts on the first replay there is */
        if (!save) {
            int i;
            for (i = 0; i < SF_REPLAY_SLOTS && s_rp.has[i] <= 0; i++) {
            }
            s_rp.row = i < SF_REPLAY_SLOTS ? i : 0;
        }
        return 1;
    }
    frame++;
    if (s_rp.note_timer > 0) {
        s_rp.note_timer--;
    }
    if (s_rp.step == RP_DONE) {
        /* "REPLAY SAVED." stays a moment before the menu goes on */
        rp_draw(frame);
        return --s_rp.timer > 0 ? 1 : 0;
    }
    sw = rp_pressed(&who);
    if (s_rp.step == RP_CONFIRM) {
        if (sw & 0x100) {
            s_rp.step = RP_LIST;
            if (sf_replay_save(s_rp.row) == 0) {
                SE_selected();
                s_rp.note = "REPLAY SAVED.";
                s_rp.step = RP_DONE;
                s_rp.timer = 60;
                rp_scan();
            } else {
                s_rp.note = "THE REPLAY COULD NOT BE SAVED.";
                s_rp.note_timer = 120;
            }
        } else if (sw & 0x200) {
            SE_dir_cursor_move();
            s_rp.step = RP_LIST;
        }
        rp_draw(frame);
        return 1;
    }
    if (sw & 1) {
        s_rp.row = (s_rp.row + SF_REPLAY_SLOTS - 1) % SF_REPLAY_SLOTS;
        SE_cursor_move();
    } else if (sw & 2) {
        s_rp.row = (s_rp.row + 1) % SF_REPLAY_SLOTS;
        SE_cursor_move();
    } else if (sw & 0x200) {
        SE_selected();
        return -1;
    } else if (sw & 0x100) {
        if (save) {
            if (s_rp.has[s_rp.row] != 0) {
                SE_selected();
                s_rp.step = RP_CONFIRM;
            } else if (sf_replay_save(s_rp.row) == 0) {
                SE_selected();
                s_rp.note = "REPLAY SAVED.";
                s_rp.step = RP_DONE;
                s_rp.timer = 60;
                rp_scan();
            } else {
                s_rp.note = "THE REPLAY COULD NOT BE SAVED.";
                s_rp.note_timer = 120;
            }
        } else if (s_rp.has[s_rp.row] > 0 && sf_replay_load(s_rp.row) == 0) {
            SE_selected();
            D_007AA5EF = (u8)who;
            return 0;
        } else {
            SE_dir_cursor_move();
            s_rp.note = s_rp.has[s_rp.row] < 0 ? "THIS REPLAY IS DAMAGED." : "THERE IS NO REPLAY HERE.";
            s_rp.note_timer = 90;
        }
    }
    rp_draw(frame);
    return 1;
}

```

`src/port/missing.c`:

```c
#include "port/port.h"

#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

#define SF_MISSING_MAX 1024

int sf_port_frame;

static const char *s_seen[SF_MISSING_MAX];
static int s_seen_n;

void sf_port_missing(const char *name)
{
    int i;
    for (i = 0; i < s_seen_n; i++) {
        if (s_seen[i] == name) {
            return;
        }
    }
    if (s_seen_n < SF_MISSING_MAX) {
        s_seen[s_seen_n++] = name;
    }
    fprintf(stderr, "port: no shim for %s (frame %d)\n", name, sf_port_frame);
    if (getenv("SF_PORT_STRICT")) {
        abort();
    }
}
```

`src/port/save.c`:

```c
/* The game's saves (its SaveInit and SaveMove), in data/save/game.sqlite.
 *
 * Retail's save layer is an Xbox menu over memory-unit files. What the game
 * needs from it is the data: kind 0 is the system data (the current mode's
 * save_w), kind 1 the system directions (its system_dir), kind 2 a replay;
 * mode 0 loads, mode 1 saves, mode 2 is the load at boot, mode 3 the menu's
 * exit to the dashboard. Replays have ten slots, picked on a page drawn by
 * src/port/menu_ext.c; SaveMove returns 1 while it is open, 0 when the
 * replay is saved or loaded, -1 when the player backs out. Loading applies
 * the data the way retail does (3s-decomp-ogxb, Source/XBOX/xb_121642.c:
 * xb_SaveApplySystem, xb_SaveApplyDirection, xb_SavePrepareSystem).
 *
 * The file is SQLite (src/port/store.c). The whole system row and the
 * directions are loaded when the program starts, once the init task has
 * laid down the factory defaults, so a saved game is already in effect
 * before the logos. A missing database is created from the original
 * defaults: Game_Default_Data and Dir_Default_Data. A damaged database is
 * left as it is. Replay slots stay in the file until a replay screen
 * asks for one. */

#include "host/paths.h"
#include "host/compat.h"
#include "port/port.h"
#include "port/store.h"

#include "types.h"
#include "structs.h"

#include <errno.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>

/* Storage the game declares and retail's platform files defined. */
u8 sound_mode SF_GAME;
s16 bgm_level SF_GAME;
s16 se_level SF_GAME;

extern struct _SAVE_W save_w[6];
extern _REPLAY_W Replay_w;
extern struct _REP_GAME_INFOR Rep_Game_Infor[11];
extern SystemDir system_dir[6];
extern u8 Present_Mode;
extern u8 Forbid_Reset;
extern u8 bgm_type;
extern MPP mpp_w;
extern s32 X_Adjust;
extern s32 Y_Adjust;
extern s16 Correct_X[4];
extern s16 Correct_Y[4];
extern u8 Disp_Size_H;
extern u8 Disp_Size_V;

void setupSoundMode(void);
void SsBgmHalfVolume(s16 flag);
void setSeVolume(void);
void Copy_Save_w(void);
void Copy_Check_w(void);
void Save_Game_Data(void);
void dspwhUnpack(u8 src, u8 *xdsp, u8 *ydsp);
void Setup_Disp_Size(s16 flag);
s16 Check_SysDir_Page(void);

extern const struct _SAVE_W Game_Default_Data;
extern const SystemDir Dir_Default_Data;

/* The player who picked the replay to load (retail's, read by Load_Replay). */
u8 D_007AA5EF SF_GAME;

static int s_kind;
static int s_mode;
static int s_pending;
static s32 s_boot_loaded;

/* Whether the load at boot has run: the title takes Start only after it
 * (retail's flag at 0x7A1808, read by the entry task). */
s32 xb_Get7A1808(void)
{
    return s_boot_loaded;
}

/* SCREEN ADJUST's FILTER, as the menu left it (sys_w.screen_mode):
 * 0 OFF, 1 SMOOTH, 2 CRT CURVED, 3 CRT FLAT. Older saves still read 0 or 1. */
extern struct _SYSTEM_W sys_w;

int sf_save_screen_filter(void)
{
    return sys_w.screen_mode > 3 ? 0 : sys_w.screen_mode;
}

/* What the system file last had for the screen. A damaged file is not
 * rewritten. Opening SCREEN ADJUST does not write; a change does. */
static int s_system_ok;
static int s_screen_known;
static s8 s_screen_x;
static s8 s_screen_y;
static u8 s_screen_size;
static u8 s_screen_mode;

static int game_db(char *out, size_t n);

static void remember_screen(const struct _SAVE_W *sw)
{
    s_screen_x = sw->Adjust_X;
    s_screen_y = sw->Adjust_Y;
    s_screen_size = sw->Screen_Size;
    s_screen_mode = sw->Screen_Mode;
    s_screen_known = 1;
}

void sf_system_commit_screen(void)
{
    struct _SAVE_W *sw = &save_w[1];
    char path[4096 + 64];
    if (!s_system_ok) {
        return;
    }
    if (s_screen_known && sw->Adjust_X == s_screen_x && sw->Adjust_Y == s_screen_y &&
        sw->Screen_Size == s_screen_size && sw->Screen_Mode == s_screen_mode) {
        return;
    }
    if (game_db(path, sizeof path) != 0 || sf_store_write_system(path, sw) != 0) {
        return;
    }
    remember_screen(sw);
}

/* data/save/game.sqlite. Created on the first save. */
static int game_db(char *out, size_t n)
{
    char base[4096];
    int wrote;
    if (sf_exe_dir(base, sizeof base) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    wrote = snprintf(out, n, "%s/data/save", base);
    if (wrote < 0 || (size_t)wrote >= n) {
        return -1;
    }
    if (mkdir(out, 0755) != 0 && errno != EEXIST) {
        fprintf(stderr, "save: %s: %s\n", out, strerror(errno));
        return -1;
    }
    wrote = snprintf(out, n, "%s/data/save/game.sqlite", base);
    return wrote < 0 || (size_t)wrote >= n ? -1 : 0;
}

/* No row yet: write the original default and read that back as the save. */
static int open_system(struct _SAVE_W *sw)
{
    char path[4096 + 64];
    int r;
    if (game_db(path, sizeof path) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    r = sf_store_read_system(path, sw);
    if (r != 1) {
        return r;
    }
    *sw = Game_Default_Data;
    if (sf_store_write_system(path, sw) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    return 0;
}

static int open_directions(SystemDir *dir)
{
    char path[4096 + 64];
    int r;
    if (game_db(path, sizeof path) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    r = sf_store_read_directions(path, dir);
    if (r != 1) {
        return r;
    }
    *dir = Dir_Default_Data;
    if (sf_store_write_directions(path, dir) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    return 0;
}

static int open_replay(int slot, _REPLAY_W *w)
{
    char path[4096 + 64];
    int r;
    if (game_db(path, sizeof path) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    r = sf_store_read_replay(path, slot, w, NULL);
    if (r == 0) {
        w->game_infor.fname = NULL;
    }
    return r;
}

/* The pages of system directions past the first one in use come from mode 0. */
static void fill_directions(void)
{
    s16 page = Check_SysDir_Page();
    int r;
    int i;
    if (page < 9) {
        for (r = page + 1; r < 10; r++) {
            for (i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
                system_dir[Present_Mode].contents[r][i] = system_dir[0].contents[r][i];
            }
        }
    }
}

/* retail's xb_SaveApplySystem */
static void apply_system(const struct _SAVE_W *in)
{
    struct _SAVE_W *sw = &save_w[Present_Mode];
    *sw = *in;
    save_w[4] = *in;
    save_w[5] = *in;
    bgm_type = sw->BgmType;
    sound_mode = sw->SoundMode;
    bgm_level = sw->BGM_Level;
    se_level = sw->SE_Level;
    if (mpp_w.cutAnalogStickData != 0) {
        sw->AnalogStick = 0;
        mpp_w.cutAnalogStickData = 0;
        save_w[4].AnalogStick = 0;
        save_w[5].AnalogStick = 0;
    }
    setupSoundMode();
    SsBgmHalfVolume(0);
    setSeVolume();
    Copy_Save_w();
    Copy_Check_w();
    Y_Adjust = sw->Adjust_Y * 4;
    X_Adjust = sw->Adjust_X * 4;
    sf_video_set_offset(Correct_X[0] + X_Adjust, Correct_Y[0] + Y_Adjust);
    dspwhUnpack(sw->Screen_Size, &Disp_Size_H, &Disp_Size_V);
    Setup_Disp_Size(0);
    sys_w.screen_mode = sw->Screen_Mode > 3 ? 0 : sw->Screen_Mode;
    fill_directions();
    remember_screen(sw);
}

/* The init task writes the factory defaults into save_w and system_dir.
 * This replaces them with the database. Called once that write has
 * finished. A damaged file is left alone and the defaults stay. */
void sf_save_startup(void)
{
    static int done;
    struct _SAVE_W sw;
    SystemDir dir;
    if (done) {
        return;
    }
    done = 1;
    if (open_system(&sw) == 0) {
        s_system_ok = 1;
        apply_system(&sw);
    }
    if (open_directions(&dir) == 0) {
        system_dir[Present_Mode] = dir;
        fill_directions();
    }
}

static void run(void)
{
    struct _SAVE_W sw;
    SystemDir dir;
    char path[4096 + 64];
    int loaded;

    switch (s_kind) {
    case 0:
        if (s_mode == 1) {
            Save_Game_Data();
            if (game_db(path, sizeof path) == 0 &&
                sf_store_write_system(path, &save_w[Present_Mode]) == 0) {
                s_system_ok = 1;
                if (Present_Mode == 1) {
                    remember_screen(&save_w[1]);
                }
            }
        } else {
            loaded = open_system(&sw);
            if (loaded == 0) {
                s_system_ok = 1;
                apply_system(&sw);
            }
        }
        break;
    case 1:
        if (s_mode == 1) {
            if (game_db(path, sizeof path) == 0) {
                sf_store_write_directions(path, &system_dir[Present_Mode]);
            }
        } else if (open_directions(&dir) == 0) {
            system_dir[Present_Mode] = dir;
            fill_directions();
        }
        break;
    default:
        break;
    }
}
```
