```c
/* ---- replays: rows 0 .. 9 of game.sqlite ---- */

/* What the slot holds, for the page: 1 a replay (its two characters and
 * when it was saved), 0 nothing, -1 a damaged file. */
int sf_replay_slot(int slot, int chars[2], long long *when)
{
    static _REPLAY_W w;
    char path[4096 + 64];
    long long saved = 0;
    int r;
    if (slot < 0 || slot >= SF_REPLAY_SLOTS) {
        return 0;
    }
    if (game_db(path, sizeof path) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    r = sf_store_read_replay(path, slot, &w, &saved);
    if (r != 0) {
        return r > 0 ? 0 : -1;
    }
    chars[0] = w.game_infor.player_infor[0].my_char;
    chars[1] = w.game_infor.player_infor[1].my_char;
    *when = saved;
    return 1;
}

/* retail's xb_SavePrepareReplay: the match's characters and stage, the
 * rules and directions it was played with, then the recorded inputs. */
int sf_replay_save(int slot)
{
    struct _SAVE_W *sw = &save_w[Present_Mode];
    char path[4096 + 64];
    Replay_w.game_infor = Rep_Game_Infor[10];
    Replay_w.game_infor.fname = NULL; /* an address, meaningless in a file */
    Replay_w.mini_save_w.Pad_Infor[0] = sw->Pad_Infor[0];
    Replay_w.mini_save_w.Pad_Infor[1] = sw->Pad_Infor[1];
    Replay_w.mini_save_w.Time_Limit = sw->Time_Limit;
    Replay_w.mini_save_w.Battle_Number[0] = sw->Battle_Number[0];
    Replay_w.mini_save_w.Battle_Number[1] = sw->Battle_Number[1];
    Replay_w.mini_save_w.Damage_Level = sw->Damage_Level;
    Replay_w.mini_save_w.extra_option = sw->extra_option;
    Replay_w.system_dir = system_dir[Present_Mode];
    if (game_db(path, sizeof path) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    return sf_store_write_replay(path, slot, &Replay_w);
}

/* retail's xb_SaveApplyReplay */
int sf_replay_load(int slot)
{
    static _REPLAY_W w;
    if (slot < 0 || slot >= SF_REPLAY_SLOTS || open_replay(slot, &w) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    w.game_infor.fname = NULL;
    Replay_w = w;
    return 0;
}

void SaveInit(s32 kind, s32 mode)
{
    s_kind = kind >= 0 && kind < 3 ? kind : 0;
    s_mode = mode;
    s_pending = 1;
    Forbid_Reset = 1;
}

/* 1 while an operation runs, 0 when it is done, -1 when a replay's page
 * was left without one. */
s32 SaveMove(void)
{
    if (s_kind == 2) {
        s32 r = sf_replay_page(s_pending, s_mode == 1);
        s_pending = 0;
        if (r <= 0) {
            Forbid_Reset = 0;
        }
        return r;
    }
    if (s_pending) {
        s_pending = 0;
        if (s_mode == 3) {
            /* the menu's exit to the dashboard: there is none, so quit */
            sf_port_quit();
        }
        if (s_mode == 2) {
            /* the boot load works in mode 1, the console's (retail's
             * boot state sets it before it loads) */
            Present_Mode = 1;
        }
        run();
        if (s_mode == 2) {
            s_boot_loaded = 1;
        }
    }
    Forbid_Reset = 0;
    return 0;
}

```

`src/port/se.c`:

```c
/* The game's sound effects on OpenAL.
 *
 * The game asks for an effect through its sound engine (cseTsbRequest,
 * mlTsbRequest in the decomp's CapSndEng), which on the Xbox queues the
 * request for retail's XACT layer: a request names a slot, a cue, a group
 * and a priority. Slot 0 holds the shared effects (sound bank 0, "SE",
 * over the wave banks SE1, SE2 and NR); slots 1 and 2 hold the two
 * players' character banks (sound banks 1 to 20, one wave bank each).
 * This file is that layer: retail's request queue, its 48 voices, its
 * group and priority rules and its pan tables (3s-decomp-ogxb,
 * Source/XBOX/xb_0F50C9.c, xb_0F4537.c, xb_0F44B2.c, xb_0F501C.c), with
 * XACT's cue playback done here.
 *
 * The cue tables are the XBE's XACT sound banks, written to
 * data/audio/sdbk at import. Their layout is the XDK's
 * (_XACT_SOUNDBANK_FILE_HEADER and the rest, from the type records in
 * xactengd.lib): a cue names a sound; a sound is either one wave (flag 8)
 * or tracks of events. The events these banks use are Play (a wave, maybe
 * looping), AmpEG (an envelope), SetVolume (a fade), StopEG (stop with
 * the envelope's release), SetMixBins (speaker levels, folded to stereo)
 * and PitchLFO, a vibrato (_XACT_TRACK_EVENT_PITCHLFO: wDelay, bDelta,
 * bPitchMod, which the XDK's DSLFODESC page gives units: the delay in
 * 32-sample blocks, a rate of 375 * delta / 16384 Hz, a depth of
 * bPitchMod / 128 octave). Every one in these banks is the same, 3.7 Hz
 * and a quarter octave, on about half of each character's sounds; the
 * wave is taken as a triangle. SF_SE_LFO=0 leaves it out.
 *
 * Voices end by the frame clock, not by the audio device, so a run plays
 * the same requests the same way with or without sound.
 *
 * A wave bank's waves are decoded on a worker (src/port/jobs.c) as soon
 * as the bank is registered, in order; the first play of a wave takes it
 * from there, waiting only if the worker has not reached it yet. */

#include "game/xwb.h"
#include "host/paths.h"
#include "port/jobs.h"
#include "port/port.h"

#include "types.h"

#include <AL/al.h>
#include <AL/alc.h>
#include <AL/alext.h>
#include <SDL3/SDL.h>

#include <math.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

#define SE_VOICES 48
#define SE_QUEUE 0x30
#define SE_SLOTS 3
#define SE_BANKS 21
#define SE_WAVEBANKS 8
#define SE_TRACKS 4
#define SE_FPS 60

/* The pan tables (retail's D_003F3300, D_003F3408): the left and right
 * mix bin volumes in hundredths of a decibel for pan -32 to 32. */
static const s32 k_pan_l[65] = {
    0,    0,    0,    0,    0,    -6,   -13,  -27,  -41,  -63,  -78,  -93,  -108, -123, -139, -147, -155,
    -163, -172, -180, -188, -197, -205, -205, -205, -205, -205, -205, -205, -205, -205, -205, -205, -205,
    -205, -205, -205, -205, -205, -205, -205, -205, -205, -223, -240, -258, -277, -296, -315, -335, -355,
    -376, -397, -419, -441, -464, -487, -511, -536, -562, -588, -615, -643, -672, -702,
};
static const s32 k_pan_r[65] = {
    -702, -672, -643, -615, -588, -562, -536, -511, -487, -464, -441, -419, -397, -376, -355, -335, -315,
    -296, -277, -258, -240, -223, -205, -205, -205, -205, -205, -205, -205, -205, -205, -205, -205, -205,
    -205, -205, -205, -205, -205, -205, -205, -205, -205, -197, -188, -180, -172, -163, -155, -147, -139,
    -123, -108, -93,  -78,  -63,  -41,  -27,  -13,  -6,   0,    0,    0,    0,    0,
};

/* Retail's mono switch (mlSysSetMono writes it): every effect centred. */
s32 D_00797100 SF_GAME;

/* ---- the sound banks (cue tables) ---- */

typedef struct SoundBank {
    uint8_t *data;
    uint32_t size;
    uint16_t sounds;
    uint16_t cues;
    uint16_t wavebanks;
    uint32_t sound_off;
    uint32_t wavebank_off;
} SoundBank;

static SoundBank s_sdbk[SE_BANKS];

static uint16_t rd16(const uint8_t *p)
{
    return (uint16_t)(p[0] | (p[1] << 8));
}

static uint32_t rd32(const uint8_t *p)
{
    return (uint32_t)p[0] | ((uint32_t)p[1] << 8) | ((uint32_t)p[2] << 16) | ((uint32_t)p[3] << 24);
}

static int bank_load(int i, SoundBank *b)
{
    char base[4096];
    char path[4200];
    FILE *fp;
    long n;
    if (sf_exe_dir(base, sizeof base) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    snprintf(path, sizeof path, "%s/data/audio/sdbk/%02d.sdbk", base, i);
    fp = fopen(path, "rb");
    if (!fp) {
        return -1;
    }
    if (fseek(fp, 0, SEEK_END) != 0 || (n = ftell(fp)) < 0x38 || n > 256 * 1024 || fseek(fp, 0, SEEK_SET) != 0) {
        fclose(fp);
        return -1;
    }
    b->data = malloc((size_t)n);
    if (!b->data || fread(b->data, 1, (size_t)n, fp) != (size_t)n) {
        fclose(fp);
        free(b->data);
        b->data = NULL;
        return -1;
    }
    fclose(fp);
    b->size = (uint32_t)n;
    b->sounds = rd16(b->data + 0x1C);
    b->cues = rd16(b->data + 0x1E);
    b->wavebanks = rd16(b->data + 0x22);
    b->wavebank_off = rd32(b->data + 0x08);
    b->sound_off = 0x38u + 20u * b->cues;
    if (memcmp(b->data, "SDBK", 4) != 0 || rd16(b->data + 4) != 11 || b->sound_off + 20u * b->sounds > b->size ||
        b->wavebank_off + 16u * b->wavebanks > b->size) {
        fprintf(stderr, "se: %s is not a sound bank\n", path);
        free(b->data);
        memset(b, 0, sizeof *b);
        return -1;
    }
    return 0;
}

/* ---- the wave banks (in the game's memory) ---- */

typedef struct WaveBank {
    const uint8_t *data;
    uint32_t size;
    char name[17];
    int count;
    ALuint *buffers; /* made on first use */
    int32_t *frames;
    int *rates;
    /* the worker's decode of every wave */
    SfJob job;
    SfWave *dec;
    int *dec_ok;
    SDL_AtomicInt dec_n; /* waves decoded */
    SDL_AtomicInt cancel;
} WaveBank;

static WaveBank s_wb[SE_WAVEBANKS];

/* ---- voices ---- */

typedef struct Track {
    int on;
    ALuint src;
    WaveBank *wb;
    int wave;
    int loop;
    float gain;     /* the sound's and the track's levels, linear */
    float bin[6];   /* SetMixBins' speaker levels, linear: front L/R, centre, LFE, rear L/R */
    float l, r;     /* those folded to stereo */
    float pitch;
    int start;      /* the frame its Play runs */
    int end;        /* the frame it has played out, or -1 while it loops */
    int stop;       /* the frame of its StopEG, or -1 */
    int release;    /* frames of release after a stop */
    int released;   /* the frame the release began, or -1 */
    /* AmpEG, in frames, and its sustain level */
    int eg;
    int attack, hold, decay;
    float sustain;
    /* PitchLFO: Hz, octaves, and frames before it starts */
    float lfo_rate;
    float lfo_depth;
    int lfo_delay;
    /* SetVolume's fade */
    int fade_at, fade_len;
    float fade_to;
} Track;

typedef struct Voice {
    int cue;   /* nonzero while it holds a cue */
    int state; /* 1: stopped, freed at the next frame */
    u16 slot;
    u16 cue_idx;
    u8 grp;
    u8 prio;
    float pan_l, pan_r; /* the source's mix bin volumes, linear */
    Track tr[SE_TRACKS];
} Voice;

typedef struct Request {
    u16 slot;
    u16 cue_idx;
    u8 grp;
    u8 prio;
    u8 e;
    s32 pan;
    u16 g;
} Request;

typedef struct Slot {
    int loaded;
    int bank; /* sound bank */
    WaveBank *own; /* the wave bank this slot registered */
} Slot;

static Voice s_voice[SE_VOICES];
static Request s_queue[SE_QUEUE];
static u32 s_queued;
static Slot s_slot[SE_SLOTS];
static s32 s_entry[SE_SLOTS] = { -1, -1, -1 };
static int s_ready;
static int s_al;
static int s_loop_points;
static float s_master = 1.0f;
static int s_cue_serial;

static float db100(s32 v)
{
    return v <= -9600 ? 0.0f : powf(10.0f, (float)v / 2000.0f);
}

static int frames_of_ms(uint32_t ms)
{
    return (int)((ms * SE_FPS + 999u) / 1000u);
}

/* XACT envelope times count 512-sample periods at 48 kHz. */
static int frames_of_eg(uint32_t t)
{
    return (int)((t * 512u * SE_FPS + 47999u) / 48000u);
}

static int al_ok(void)
{
    if (!s_al && alcGetCurrentContext()) {
        s_al = 1;
        s_loop_points = alIsExtensionPresent("AL_SOFT_loop_points");
    }
    return s_al;
}

static void wb_drop(WaveBank *wb)
{
    int i;
    int t;
    if (!wb->data) {
        return;
    }
    for (i = 0; i < SE_VOICES; i++) {
        for (t = 0; t < SE_TRACKS; t++) {
            Track *tr = &s_voice[i].tr[t];
            if (tr->on && tr->wb == wb) {
                if (tr->src) {
                    alSourceStop(tr->src);
                    alSourcei(tr->src, AL_BUFFER, 0);
                }
                tr->on = 0;
            }
        }
    }
    if (wb->dec) {
        SDL_SetAtomicInt(&wb->cancel, 1);
        sf_job_wait(&wb->job);
        for (i = 0; i < wb->count; i++) {
            sf_wave_free(&wb->dec[i]);
        }
    }
    free(wb->dec);
    free(wb->dec_ok);
    if (wb->buffers) {
        for (i = 0; i < wb->count; i++) {
            if (wb->buffers[i]) {
                alDeleteBuffers(1, &wb->buffers[i]);
            }
        }
    }
    free(wb->buffers);
    free(wb->frames);
    free(wb->rates);
    memset(wb, 0, sizeof *wb);
}

/* The worker's half: each wave of the bank, in order. */
static void wb_decode_run(void *arg)
{
    WaveBank *wb = arg;
    int i;
    for (i = 0; i < wb->count && !SDL_GetAtomicInt(&wb->cancel); i++) {
        char err[64];
        wb->dec_ok[i] = sf_xwb_entry(wb->data, wb->size, i, &wb->dec[i], err, sizeof err) == 0;
        if (!wb->dec_ok[i]) {
            fprintf(stderr, "se: %s wave %d: %s\n", wb->name, i, err);
        }
        SDL_SetAtomicInt(&wb->dec_n, i + 1);
        sf_job_progress();
    }
}

typedef struct WaveNeed {
    WaveBank *wb;
    int idx;
} WaveNeed;

static int wave_decoded(void *arg)
{
    const WaveNeed *n = arg;
    return SDL_GetAtomicInt(&n->wb->dec_n) > n->idx;
}

static WaveBank *wb_register(const uint8_t *data, uint32_t size)
{
    int i;
    int count;
    WaveBank *wb = NULL;
    for (i = 0; i < SE_WAVEBANKS; i++) {
        if (s_wb[i].data == data) {
            /* the game reloaded this memory: what was decoded from it is gone */
            wb_drop(&s_wb[i]);
        }
    }
    for (i = 0; i < SE_WAVEBANKS && !wb; i++) {
        if (!s_wb[i].data) {
            wb = &s_wb[i];
        }
    }
    if (!wb || !data || sf_xwb_count(data, size, &count, NULL, 0) != 0 || count <= 0) {
        return NULL;
    }
    wb->buffers = calloc((size_t)count, sizeof *wb->buffers);
    wb->frames = calloc((size_t)count, sizeof *wb->frames);
    wb->rates = calloc((size_t)count, sizeof *wb->rates);
    if (!wb->buffers || !wb->frames || !wb->rates) {
        free(wb->buffers);
        free(wb->frames);
        free(wb->rates);
        memset(wb, 0, sizeof *wb);
        return NULL;
    }
    wb->data = data;
    wb->size = size;
    wb->count = count;
    sf_xwb_name(data, size, wb->name);
    wb->dec = calloc((size_t)count, sizeof *wb->dec);
    wb->dec_ok = calloc((size_t)count, sizeof *wb->dec_ok);
    if (wb->dec && wb->dec_ok) {
        SDL_SetAtomicInt(&wb->dec_n, 0);
        SDL_SetAtomicInt(&wb->cancel, 0);
        memset(&wb->job, 0, sizeof wb->job);
        sf_job_start(&wb->job, wb_decode_run, wb);
    } else {
        free(wb->dec);
        free(wb->dec_ok);
        wb->dec = NULL;
        wb->dec_ok = NULL;
    }
    return wb;
}

static WaveBank *wb_find(const char *name)
{
    int i;
    for (i = 0; i < SE_WAVEBANKS; i++) {
        if (s_wb[i].data && strncmp(s_wb[i].name, name, 16) == 0) {
            return &s_wb[i];
        }
    }
    return NULL;
}

/* The wave's length in frames and its rate; its AL buffer when sound is on. */
static int wave_prepare(WaveBank *wb, int idx)
{
    SfWave w;
    char err[64];
    if (idx < 0 || idx >= wb->count) {
        return -1;
    }
    if (wb->rates[idx] != 0) {
        return 0;
    }
    if (wb->dec) {
        /* the worker's, once it has got this far */
        WaveNeed n;
        n.wb = wb;
        n.idx = idx;
        sf_job_wait_for(&wb->job, wave_decoded, &n);
        if (!wave_decoded(&n) || !wb->dec_ok[idx]) {
            wb->rates[idx] = -1;
            return -1;
        }
        w = wb->dec[idx];
        memset(&wb->dec[idx], 0, sizeof wb->dec[idx]);
    } else if (sf_xwb_entry(wb->data, wb->size, idx, &w, err, sizeof err) != 0) {
        fprintf(stderr, "se: %s wave %d: %s\n", wb->name, idx, err);
        wb->rates[idx] = -1;
        return -1;
    }
    wb->frames[idx] = w.samples;
    wb->rates[idx] = w.rate;
    if (al_ok()) {
        alGenBuffers(1, &wb->buffers[idx]);
        alBufferData(wb->buffers[idx], w.channels == 2 ? AL_FORMAT_STEREO16 : AL_FORMAT_MONO16, w.pcm,
                     (ALsizei)((size_t)w.samples * (size_t)w.channels * 2), w.rate);
        if (w.loop_len > 0 && s_loop_points) {
            ALint pts[2] = { w.loop_start, w.loop_start + w.loop_len };
            alBufferiv(wb->buffers[idx], AL_LOOP_POINTS_SOFT, pts);
        }
    }
    sf_wave_free(&w);
    return wb->rates[idx] > 0 ? 0 : -1;
}

/* ---- playing a cue ---- */

static void track_reset(Track *tr)
{
    ALuint src = tr->src;
    memset(tr, 0, sizeof *tr);
    tr->src = src;
    tr->gain = 1.0f;
    tr->bin[0] = tr->bin[1] = 1.0f;
    tr->l = tr->r = 1.0f;
    tr->pitch = 1.0f;
    tr->end = -1;
    tr->stop = -1;
    tr->released = -1;
    tr->fade_at = -1;
    tr->sustain = 1.0f;
}

static void track_wave(Track *tr, WaveBank *wb, int wave, int loop, int start)
{
    float secs;
    if (!wb || wave_prepare(wb, wave) != 0) {
        return;
    }
    tr->on = 1;
    tr->wb = wb;
    tr->wave = wave;
    tr->loop = loop;
    tr->start = start;
    secs = (float)wb->frames[wave] / ((float)wb->rates[wave] * tr->pitch);
    tr->end = loop ? -1 : start + (int)ceilf(secs * SE_FPS) + 1;
}

/* A track's events (each an 8-byte _XACT_TRACK_EVENT_HEADER, then bSize
 * bytes of its body). */
static void track_events(const SoundBank *b, uint32_t off, int count, Track *tr, WaveBank *const *wbs, int now)
{
    int e;
    for (e = 0; e < count; e++) {
        const uint8_t *p;
        uint32_t w0;
        int type;
        uint32_t ts;
        int size;
        u16 loops;
        if (off + 8u > b->size) {
            return;
        }
        p = b->data + off;
        w0 = rd32(p);
        type = (int)(w0 & 0xFF);
        ts = w0 >> 8;
        size = p[4];
        loops = rd16(p + 6);
        if (off + 8u + (uint32_t)size > b->size) {
            return;
        }
        switch (type) {
        case 0: /* Play */
            if (size >= 4) {
                u16 wave = rd16(p + 8);
                u16 bank = rd16(p + 10);
                track_wave(tr, bank < b->wavebanks ? wbs[bank] : NULL, wave, loops != 0, now + frames_of_ms(ts));
            }
            break;
        case 2: /* Stop */
        case 3: /* StopAndEGRelease */
            tr->stop = now + frames_of_ms(ts);
            if (type == 2) {
                tr->release = 0;
            }
            break;
        case 5: /* SetVolume: a fade to the level over dwDuration */
            if (size >= 8) {
                tr->fade_at = now + frames_of_ms(ts);
                tr->fade_len = frames_of_ms(rd32(p + 12) >> 8);
                tr->fade_to = db100((s16)rd16(p + 8));
            }
            break;
        case 10: /* AmplitudeEG */
            if (size >= 12) {
                tr->eg = 1;
                tr->attack = frames_of_eg(rd16(p + 10));
                tr->hold = frames_of_eg(rd16(p + 12));
                tr->decay = frames_of_eg(rd16(p + 14));
                tr->release = frames_of_eg(rd16(p + 16));
                tr->sustain = (float)p[18] / 255.0f;
            }
            break;
        case 16: /* SetMixBins: the speaker levels */
        {
            int k;
            for (k = 0; k + 4 <= size && k < 32; k += 4) {
                u16 bin = rd16(p + 8 + k);
                if (bin < 6) {
                    tr->bin[bin] = db100((s16)rd16(p + 10 + k));
                }
            }
            /* Some sounds sit low and lean on the rear speakers (the
             * announcer's second call: -9 dB, front -2 dB, rear 0 dB).
             * Folded to stereo the ITU way: centre and rear at -3 dB,
             * LFE left out. */
            tr->l = tr->bin[0] + 0.70710678f * (tr->bin[2] + tr->bin[4]);
            tr->r = tr->bin[1] + 0.70710678f * (tr->bin[2] + tr->bin[5]);
            break;
        }
        case 8: /* PitchLFO */
            if (size >= 4 && p[10] != 0 && !(getenv("SF_SE_LFO") && atoi(getenv("SF_SE_LFO")) == 0)) {
                tr->lfo_delay = (int)((float)rd16(p + 8) * 32.0f / 48000.0f * SE_FPS + 0.5f);
                tr->lfo_rate = 375.0f * (float)p[10] / 16384.0f;
                tr->lfo_depth = (float)(s8)p[11] / 128.0f;
            }
            break;
        default: /* MixBinPan, markers: not applied */
            break;
        }
        off += 8u + (uint32_t)size;
    }
}

static int voice_cue(Voice *v, int slot, int cue_idx, int now)
{
    const SoundBank *b;
    const uint8_t *cue;
    const uint8_t *snd;
    WaveBank *wbs[8];
    u16 sound;
    uint32_t u;
    float gain;
    float pitch;
    int flags;
    int tracks;
    int i;
    int on = 0;
    if (!s_slot[slot].loaded || s_slot[slot].bank < 0 || s_slot[slot].bank >= SE_BANKS) {
        return 0;
    }
    b = &s_sdbk[s_slot[slot].bank];
    if (!b->data || cue_idx >= b->cues) {
        return 0;
    }
    cue = b->data + 0x38 + 20 * cue_idx;
    sound = rd16(cue + 2);
    if (sound >= b->sounds) {
        return 0;
    }
    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
        wbs[i] = i < b->wavebanks ? wb_find((const char *)b->data + b->wavebank_off + 16 * i) : NULL;
    }
    snd = b->data + b->sound_off + 20u * sound;
    u = rd32(snd);
    gain = db100(-16 * (s32)(rd16(snd + 4) & 0x1FF));
    pitch = powf(2.0f, (float)(s16)rd16(snd + 6) / 4096.0f);
    tracks = snd[8];
    flags = snd[11];
    for (i = 0; i < SE_TRACKS; i++) {
        track_reset(&v->tr[i]);
        v->tr[i].gain = gain;
        v->tr[i].pitch = pitch;
    }
    if (flags & 8) {
        /* one wave: the sound holds its play descriptor */
        u16 bank = (u16)(u >> 16);
        track_wave(&v->tr[0], bank < b->wavebanks && bank < 8 ? wbs[bank] : NULL, (int)(u & 0xFFFF), 0, now);
    } else {
        for (i = 0; i < tracks && i < SE_TRACKS; i++) {
            uint32_t te;
            if (u + 4u * (uint32_t)i + 4u > b->size) {
                break;
            }
            te = rd32(b->data + u + 4u * (uint32_t)i);
            track_events(b, te >> 8, (int)(te & 0xFF), &v->tr[i], wbs, now);
        }
    }
    for (i = 0; i < SE_TRACKS; i++) {
        on |= v->tr[i].on;
    }
    return on;
}

static float track_level(const Track *tr, int now)
{
    float env = 1.0f;
    float g = tr->gain;
    int t = now - tr->start;
    if (tr->eg) {
        if (t < tr->attack) {
            env = (float)(t + 1) / (float)(tr->attack + 1);
        } else if (t < tr->attack + tr->hold) {
            env = 1.0f;
        } else if (t < tr->attack + tr->hold + tr->decay) {
            float k = (float)(t - tr->attack - tr->hold) / (float)tr->decay;
            env = 1.0f + (tr->sustain - 1.0f) * k;
        } else {
            env = tr->sustain;
        }
    }
    if (tr->released >= 0) {
        int r = now - tr->released;
        env *= tr->release > 0 ? 1.0f - (float)r / (float)tr->release : 0.0f;
        if (env < 0.0f) {
            env = 0.0f;
        }
    }
    if (tr->fade_at >= 0 && now >= tr->fade_at) {
        float k = tr->fade_len > 0 ? (float)(now - tr->fade_at) / (float)tr->fade_len : 1.0f;
        g *= 1.0f + (tr->fade_to - 1.0f) * (k > 1.0f ? 1.0f : k);
    }
    return g * env;
}

static void track_al(Voice *v, Track *tr, int now)
{
    float l;
    float r;
    float a;
    float level;
    if (!al_ok()) {
        return;
    }
    if (!tr->src) {
        alGenSources(1, &tr->src);
        if (!tr->src) {
            return;
        }
        alSourcei(tr->src, AL_SOURCE_RELATIVE, AL_TRUE);
        alSourcef(tr->src, AL_ROLLOFF_FACTOR, 0.0f);
        alSourcef(tr->src, AL_MAX_GAIN, 4.0f);
    }
    if (D_00797100) {
        l = r = 1.0f;
    } else {
        l = v->pan_l * tr->l;
        r = v->pan_r * tr->r;
    }
    level = track_level(tr, now) * s_master;
    /* a mono source panned by power between the two speakers */
    a = atan2f(r, l) * 2.0f - 1.5707964f;
    alSource3f(tr->src, AL_POSITION, sinf(a), 0.0f, -cosf(a));
    alSourcef(tr->src, AL_GAIN, level * sqrtf(l * l + r * r));
    if (tr->lfo_rate > 0.0f && now >= tr->start + tr->lfo_delay) {
        /* a triangle from 0, up first, by the frame clock */
        float ph = (float)(now - tr->start - tr->lfo_delay) / SE_FPS * tr->lfo_rate;
        float tri;
        ph -= floorf(ph);
        tri = ph < 0.25f ? 4.0f * ph : ph < 0.75f ? 2.0f - 4.0f * ph : 4.0f * ph - 4.0f;
        alSourcef(tr->src, AL_PITCH, tr->pitch * exp2f(tr->lfo_depth * tri));
    }
    if (now == tr->start) {
        alSourcei(tr->src, AL_BUFFER, (ALint)tr->wb->buffers[tr->wave]);
        alSourcei(tr->src, AL_LOOPING, tr->loop ? AL_TRUE : AL_FALSE);
        alSourcef(tr->src, AL_PITCH, tr->pitch);
        alSourcePlay(tr->src);
    }
}

static void voice_stop_al(Voice *v)
{
    int t;
    for (t = 0; t < SE_TRACKS; t++) {
        if (v->tr[t].src && s_al) {
            alSourceStop(v->tr[t].src);
            alSourcei(v->tr[t].src, AL_BUFFER, 0);
        }
        v->tr[t].on = 0;
    }
}

/* Called a frame: advances every voice and frees those that are done. */
static void voices_frame(int now)
{
    int i;
    int t;
    for (i = 0; i < SE_VOICES; i++) {
        Voice *v = &s_voice[i];
        int live = 0;
        if (!v->cue) {
            continue;
        }
        if (v->state == 1) {
            voice_stop_al(v);
            v->cue = 0;
            continue;
        }
        for (t = 0; t < SE_TRACKS; t++) {
            Track *tr = &v->tr[t];
            if (!tr->on) {
                continue;
            }
            if (tr->stop >= 0 && now >= tr->stop && tr->released < 0) {
                tr->released = now;
            }
            if ((tr->end >= 0 && now >= tr->end) || (tr->released >= 0 && now - tr->released >= tr->release)) {
                if (tr->src && s_al) {
                    alSourceStop(tr->src);
                    alSourcei(tr->src, AL_BUFFER, 0);
                }
                tr->on = 0;
                continue;
            }
            live = 1;
            if (now >= tr->start) {
                track_al(v, tr, now);
            }
        }
        if (!live) {
            /* retail's end notification: the voice is free */
            v->cue = 0;
            v->state = 1;
        }
    }
}

/* ---- retail's layer ---- */

/* xb_SeGroupFree: 0 when a playing voice of the group outranks prio. */
static int group_free(u8 grp, u8 prio)
{
    int i;
    for (i = 0; i < SE_VOICES; i++) {
        if (s_voice[i].cue && s_voice[i].state != 1 && s_voice[i].grp == grp && s_voice[i].prio > prio) {
            return 0;
        }
    }
    return 1;
}

static void stop_group(u8 grp)
{
    int i;
    for (i = 0; i < SE_VOICES; i++) {
        if (s_voice[i].cue && s_voice[i].state != 1 && s_voice[i].grp == grp) {
            voice_stop_al(&s_voice[i]);
            s_voice[i].state = 1;
        }
    }
}

/* xb_StartSoundEntry: 0 keeps the request queued (no voice is free). */
static int start_entry(const Request *q, int now)
{
    Voice *v = NULL;
    int i;
    if (q->slot >= SE_SLOTS || !s_slot[q->slot].loaded || !group_free(q->grp, q->prio)) {
        return 1;
    }
    for (i = 0; i < SE_VOICES; i++) {
        if (!s_voice[i].cue) {
            v = &s_voice[i];
            break;
        }
    }
    if (!v) {
        return 0;
    }
    if (q->pan != 0x100) {
        s32 pan = q->pan < -32 ? -32 : q->pan > 32 ? 32 : q->pan;
        v->pan_l = db100(k_pan_l[pan + 32]);
        v->pan_r = db100(k_pan_r[pan + 32]);
    }
    stop_group(q->grp);
    if (!voice_cue(v, q->slot, q->cue_idx, now)) {
        return 1;
    }
    if (getenv("SF_SE_DEBUG")) {
        int t;
        fprintf(stderr, "se: frame %d slot %u cue %u grp %u prio %u pan %d:", now, q->slot, q->cue_idx, q->grp, q->prio, q->pan);
        for (t = 0; t < SE_TRACKS; t++) {
            const Track *tr = &v->tr[t];
            if (tr->on) {
                fprintf(stderr, " [%s %d %dHz x%.3f%s end %d]", tr->wb->name, tr->wave, tr->wb->rates[tr->wave], tr->pitch,
                        tr->loop ? " loop" : "", tr->end);
            }
        }
        fprintf(stderr, "\n");
    }
    v->cue = ++s_cue_serial ? s_cue_serial : ++s_cue_serial;
    v->state = 0;
    v->slot = q->slot;
    v->cue_idx = q->cue_idx;
    v->grp = q->grp;
    v->prio = q->prio;
    return 1;
}

/* Retail's xb_XactFrame: free finished voices, then start what is queued,
 * in order, until one cannot start. */
void sf_se_frame(void)
{
    int now = sf_port_frame;
    u32 k;
    u32 count;
    if (!s_ready) {
        return;
    }
    voices_frame(now);
    count = s_queued;
    for (k = 0; k < count; k++) {
        if (!start_entry(&s_queue[0], now)) {
            break;
        }
        s_queued--;
        memmove(&s_queue[0], &s_queue[1], s_queued * sizeof s_queue[0]);
    }
    /* what started this frame begins playing now */
    if (s_al) {
        int i;
        int t;
        for (i = 0; i < SE_VOICES; i++) {
            for (t = 0; t < SE_TRACKS; t++) {
                Track *tr = &s_voice[i].tr[t];
                if (s_voice[i].cue && s_voice[i].state != 1 && tr->on && tr->start == now) {
                    track_al(&s_voice[i], tr, now);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

s32 xb_InitSoundBankList(void)
{
    int i;
    int loaded = 0;
    for (i = 0; i < SE_BANKS; i++) {
        if (!s_sdbk[i].data && bank_load(i, &s_sdbk[i]) == 0) {
            loaded++;
        } else if (s_sdbk[i].data) {
            loaded++;
        }
    }
    if (loaded != SE_BANKS) {
        fprintf(stderr, "se: %d of %d sound banks in data/audio/sdbk; no sound effects "
                        "(sf33rdsxb --sound-banks sf33rd.xbe --data DIR writes them)\n",
                loaded, SE_BANKS);
    }
    for (i = 0; i < SE_SLOTS; i++) {
        s_slot[i].loaded = 0;
        s_slot[i].bank = -1;
    }
    for (i = 0; i < SE_VOICES; i++) {
        voice_stop_al(&s_voice[i]);
        s_voice[i].cue = 0;
        s_voice[i].state = 0;
        s_voice[i].pan_l = s_voice[i].pan_r = 1.0f;
    }
    s_queued = 0;
    D_00797100 = 0;
    s_ready = loaded == SE_BANKS;
    return 1;
}

s32 xb_ClearColorRamEntries(void)
{
    s_entry[0] = s_entry[1] = s_entry[2] = -1;
    return 0;
}

/* xb_PurgeListEntry: stops the slot's voices and lets its bank go. */
void xb_PurgeListEntry(u8 id)
{
    int i;
    if (id >= SE_SLOTS) {
        return;
    }
    s_entry[id] = -1;
    if (!s_slot[id].loaded) {
        return;
    }
    for (i = 0; i < SE_VOICES; i++) {
        if (s_voice[i].cue && s_voice[i].state != 1 && s_voice[i].slot == id) {
            voice_stop_al(&s_voice[i]);
            s_voice[i].state = 1;
        }
    }
    if (s_slot[id].own) {
        wb_drop(s_slot[id].own);
        s_slot[id].own = NULL;
    }
    s_slot[id].loaded = 0;
}

/* Retail's resident wave banks (filled by color3rd.c's types 0x50 to 0x52). */
extern u8 D_007C5CE0[];
extern u8 D_00881400[];
extern u8 D_00B84200[];

/* xb_SetColorRamEntry / xb_LoadListEntry: slot 0 is the resident set
 * (sound bank 0 over the three resident wave banks); slots 1 and 2 take
 * sound bank `index` over the wave bank at addr. */
s32 xb_SetColorRamEntry(void *addr, u32 size, s32 id, s32 index)
{
    if (id < 0 || id >= SE_SLOTS) {
        return 0;
    }
    if (id == 0) {
        wb_register(D_007C5CE0, 0xBB6FC);
        wb_register(D_00881400, 0x1490B4);
        wb_register(D_00B84200, 0xC7CC4);
        s_slot[0].bank = 0;
        s_slot[0].loaded = 1;
    } else {
        if (s_slot[id].loaded) {
            xb_PurgeListEntry((u8)id);
        }
        s_slot[id].own = wb_register(addr, size);
        s_slot[id].bank = index;
        s_slot[id].loaded = s_slot[id].own != NULL && index > 0 && index < SE_BANKS;
    }
    s_entry[id] = index;
    return 0;
}

s32 xb_GetColorRamEntry(s32 i)
{
    return i >= 0 && i < SE_SLOTS ? s_entry[i] : -1;
}

/* xb_TsbQueueRequest: slot, cue, group, priority, a flag, the pan
 * (-32..32, 0x100 to keep the voice's), and a value retail passes on. */
s32 xb_TsbQueueRequest(u32 slot, u32 cue_idx, s32 grp, u8 prio, u8 e, s32 pan, s16 g)
{
    Request *q;
    if (s_queued >= SE_QUEUE) {
        return 0;
    }
    q = &s_queue[s_queued++];
    q->slot = (u16)slot;
    q->cue_idx = (u16)cue_idx;
    q->grp = (u8)grp;
    q->prio = prio;
    q->e = e;
    q->pan = pan;
    q->g = (u16)g;
    return 1;
}

s32 xb_StopSeByGroup(u32 bank, u32 code, s32 grp, u8 prio)
{
    int i;
    (void)bank;
    (void)code;
    for (i = 0; i < SE_VOICES; i++) {
        if (s_voice[i].cue && s_voice[i].state != 1 && s_voice[i].grp == (u8)grp && s_voice[i].prio <= prio) {
            voice_stop_al(&s_voice[i]);
            s_voice[i].state = 1;
        }
    }
    return 1;
}

void xb_StopAllSeSlots(void)
{
    int i;
    for (i = 0; i < SE_VOICES; i++) {
        if (s_voice[i].cue && s_voice[i].state != 1) {
            voice_stop_al(&s_voice[i]);
            s_voice[i].state = 1;
        }
    }
}

/* The master effect volume (mlSysSetBankVolume), in hundredths of a dB. */
void IXACTEngine_SetMasterVolume(s32 engine, s32 category, s32 vol)
{
    (void)engine;
    (void)category;
    s_master = db100(vol);
}

/* At exit, before the device closes: the voices stop and the sources and
 * wave buffers go. */
void sf_se_shutdown(void)
{
    int i;
    int t;
    if (!s_al) {
        return;
    }
    for (i = 0; i < SE_VOICES; i++) {
        voice_stop_al(&s_voice[i]);
        s_voice[i].cue = 0;
        for (t = 0; t < SE_TRACKS; t++) {
            if (s_voice[i].tr[t].src) {
                alDeleteSources(1, &s_voice[i].tr[t].src);
                s_voice[i].tr[t].src = 0;
            }
        }
    }
    for (i = 0; i < SE_WAVEBANKS; i++) {
        int w;
        for (w = 0; s_wb[i].buffers && w < s_wb[i].count; w++) {
            if (s_wb[i].buffers[w]) {
                alDeleteBuffers(1, &s_wb[i].buffers[w]);
                s_wb[i].buffers[w] = 0;
            }
        }
    }
    s_al = 0;
    s_ready = 0;
}

```

`src/port/setup.c`:

```c
/* The first start: no game data yet. The window shows where to get it
 * (the Xbox disc as an image, a zip or 7z of it, or the extracted folder:
 * picked with a click or Enter, F for a folder, or dropped on the window),
 * imports it into data/ next to the program (src/iso/extract.c: the
 * archive, the opening theme, the fonts and the 21 sound banks out of
 * sf33rd.xbe), and the game then starts in the same window. The import
 * runs on a worker thread (src/port/jobs.c), so the window keeps drawing
 * and answering the system while it reads.
 *
 * The command line can do the same: --extract SOURCE --data DIR, and
 * --sound-banks sf33rd.xbe --data DIR for data imported before the sound
 * banks were. */

#include "common.h"
#include "host/paths.h"
#include "iso/archive.h"
#include "iso/extract.h"
#include "iso/xbe.h"
#include "port/jobs.h"
#include "port/port.h"
#include "ui/ui.h"

#include <SDL3/SDL.h>
#include <SDL3/SDL_dialog.h>
#ifdef __ANDROID__
#include <jni.h>
#endif

#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#ifdef _WIN32
#include <windows.h>
#endif

#ifndef __ANDROID__
static const SDL_DialogFileFilter k_filters[] = {
    { "Xbox disc or archive", "iso;zip;7z" },
    { "All files", "*" },
};
#endif

static struct {
    SfCanvas canvas;
    SfUi ui;
    char status[512];
    char note[512];
    SDL_Mutex *lock;
    char chosen[4096];
    int chosen_ready;
    int dialog_open;
    /* the import on a worker */
    SfJob job;
    const char *install;
    char source[4096];
    int ok;
    SfExtractResult result;
} s;

#ifdef __ANDROID__
/* The file picker gives a content:// URI, not a path: the activity opens
 * it (SF3Activity.openDocument) and the import reads the descriptor, as
 * /proc/self/fd/N (src/host/compat.c). -1 when it cannot be opened. */
static int open_document(const char *uri)
{
    JNIEnv *env = (JNIEnv *)SDL_GetAndroidJNIEnv();
    jobject act = (jobject)SDL_GetAndroidActivity();
    jclass cls;
    jmethodID m;
    jstring s;
    int fd = -1;
    if (!env || !act) {
        return -1;
    }
    cls = (*env)->GetObjectClass(env, act);
    m = (*env)->GetMethodID(env, cls, "openDocument", "(Ljava/lang/String;)I");
    s = (*env)->NewStringUTF(env, uri);
    if (m && s) {
        fd = (*env)->CallIntMethod(env, act, m, s);
    }
    if ((*env)->ExceptionCheck(env)) {
        (*env)->ExceptionClear(env);
        fd = -1;
    }
    (*env)->DeleteLocalRef(env, s);
    (*env)->DeleteLocalRef(env, cls);
    (*env)->DeleteLocalRef(env, act);
    return fd;
}

static int s_doc_fd = -1;
#endif

static void draw(void)
{
    if (s.lock) {
        SDL_LockMutex(s.lock); /* the status, which the import writes */
    }
    sf_ui_draw(&s.canvas, &s.ui);
    if (s.lock) {
        SDL_UnlockMutex(s.lock);
    }
    sf_video_present_canvas(&s.canvas);
}

static void on_file(void *user, const char *const *files, int filter)
{
    (void)user;
    (void)filter;
    SDL_LockMutex(s.lock);
    s.dialog_open = 0;
    if (files && files[0]) {
        snprintf(s.chosen, sizeof s.chosen, "%s", files[0]);
        s.chosen_ready = 1;
    }
    SDL_UnlockMutex(s.lock);
}

/* each file the import writes: its name on the screen */
static void progress(const char *path, void *user)
{
    const char *base = path ? strrchr(path, '/') : NULL;
    (void)user;
    base = base ? base + 1 : path;
    SDL_LockMutex(s.lock);
    snprintf(s.status, sizeof s.status, "READING %s", base ? base : "");
    SDL_UnlockMutex(s.lock);
    if (sf_jobs_workers() == 0) {
        /* no worker: the import runs here, and the window is kept up */
        draw();
        SDL_PumpEvents();
    }
}

static void import_run(void *unused)
{
    (void)unused;
    s.ok = sf_extract(s.source, s.install, NULL, progress, NULL, &s.result) == 0 && sf_data_ready(s.install);
}

/* Starts the import; 1 while it runs. */
static void import_start(const char *install, const char *source)
{
    snprintf(s.status, sizeof s.status, "READING XBOX DATA");
    s.note[0] = 0;
    s.install = install;
    snprintf(s.source, sizeof s.source, "%s", source);
#ifdef __ANDROID__
    if (strncmp(source, "content://", 10) == 0) {
        if (s_doc_fd >= 0) {
            close(s_doc_fd);
        }
        s_doc_fd = open_document(source);
        snprintf(s.source, sizeof s.source, "/proc/self/fd/%d", s_doc_fd);
    }
#endif
    s.ok = 0;
    memset(&s.result, 0, sizeof s.result);
    draw();
    sf_job_start(&s.job, import_run, NULL);
    if (sf_jobs_workers() == 0) {
        sf_job_wait(&s.job);
    }
}

/* 1 once the import ended well, -1 once it failed, 0 while it runs. */
static int import_poll(void)
{
    if (!s.job.fn || !sf_job_done(&s.job)) {
        return 0;
    }
    sf_job_reset(&s.job);
    s.job.fn = NULL;
    if (s.ok) {
        return 1;
    }
    SDL_LockMutex(s.lock);
    snprintf(s.status, sizeof s.status, "%s", s.result.error[0] ? s.result.error : "XBOX DATA WAS NOT READ");
    SDL_UnlockMutex(s.lock);
    return -1;
}

int sf_setup_run(const char *install)
{
    SDL_Window *win = sf_video_window();
    int done = 0;
    if (!win) {
        /* headless: no window to ask in */
        fprintf(stderr, "setup: no game data in %s/data: sf33rdsxb --extract SOURCE --data %s\n", install, install);
        return -1;
    }
    if (sf_canvas_open(&s.canvas, 960, 540) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    s.lock = SDL_CreateMutex();
    s.ui.status = s.status;
    s.ui.note = s.note;
    if (!sf_sound_banks_ready(install)) {
        char afs[4096];
        if (sf_join(afs, sizeof afs, install, "data/afs/SF33RDXB.AFS") == 0 && access(afs, R_OK) == 0) {
            /* imported before the sound effects were read from the XBE */
            snprintf(s.note, sizeof s.note, "IMPORT THE DISC AGAIN: THE SOUND EFFECTS COME FROM SF33RD.XBE");
        }
    }
    if (getenv("SF_SETUP_SOURCE")) {
        /* a test: that source, as if it were dropped on the window */
        snprintf(s.chosen, sizeof s.chosen, "%s", getenv("SF_SETUP_SOURCE"));
        s.chosen_ready = 1;
    }
    while (!done) {
        SDL_Event ev;
        int busy = s.job.fn != NULL;
        while (SDL_PollEvent(&ev)) {
            if (busy) {
                /* importing: a quit waits for the import to end */
                if (ev.type == SDL_EVENT_QUIT) {
                    sf_job_wait(&s.job);
                    done = -1;
                }
                continue;
            }
            if (ev.type == SDL_EVENT_QUIT ||
                (ev.type == SDL_EVENT_KEY_DOWN && (ev.key.key == SDLK_ESCAPE || ev.key.key == SDLK_AC_BACK) &&
                 !s.dialog_open)) {
                done = -1;
            } else if (!s.dialog_open && ((ev.type == SDL_EVENT_KEY_DOWN && !ev.key.repeat &&
                                           (ev.key.key == SDLK_RETURN || ev.key.key == SDLK_KP_ENTER)) ||
                                          ev.type == SDL_EVENT_MOUSE_BUTTON_UP)) {
                s.dialog_open = 1;
#ifdef __ANDROID__
                /* the picker matches by type, and an .iso or .7z often has none */
                SDL_ShowOpenFileDialog(on_file, NULL, win, NULL, 0, NULL, false);
#else
                SDL_ShowOpenFileDialog(on_file, NULL, win, k_filters, 2, NULL, false);
#endif
            } else if (!s.dialog_open && ev.type == SDL_EVENT_KEY_DOWN && !ev.key.repeat && ev.key.key == SDLK_F) {
                s.dialog_open = 1;
                SDL_ShowOpenFolderDialog(on_file, NULL, win, NULL, false);
            } else if (ev.type == SDL_EVENT_DROP_FILE && ev.drop.data) {
                SDL_LockMutex(s.lock);
                snprintf(s.chosen, sizeof s.chosen, "%s", ev.drop.data);
                s.chosen_ready = 1;
                SDL_UnlockMutex(s.lock);
            }
        }
        if (done) {
            break;
        }
        if (s.job.fn) {
            if (import_poll() > 0) {
                done = 1;
            }
        } else {
            SDL_LockMutex(s.lock);
            if (s.chosen_ready) {
                char path[4096];
                snprintf(path, sizeof path, "%s", s.chosen);
                s.chosen_ready = 0;
                SDL_UnlockMutex(s.lock);
                import_start(install, path);
            } else {
                SDL_UnlockMutex(s.lock);
            }
        }
        if (done) {
            break;
        }
        draw();
        SDL_Delay(16);
    }
    sf_canvas_close(&s.canvas);
    SDL_DestroyMutex(s.lock);
    s.lock = NULL;
    return done > 0 ? 0 : -1;
}

/* --extract SOURCE --data DIR, --sound-banks XBE --data DIR: -1 when the
 * command line asks for neither, else the exit status. */
int sf_setup_cli(int argc, char **argv)
{
    /* SF_SETUP_SHOT=PATH: the first start's screen as a PPM, no window
     * (for the README) */
    if (getenv("SF_SETUP_SHOT")) {
        SfCanvas c;
        SfUi ui;
        FILE *f;
        int i;
        memset(&ui, 0, sizeof ui);
        if (sf_canvas_open(&c, 960, 540) != 0) {
            return 1;
        }
        sf_ui_draw(&c, &ui);
        f = fopen(getenv("SF_SETUP_SHOT"), "wb");
        if (f) {
            fprintf(f, "P6\n%d %d\n255\n", c.width, c.height);
            for (i = 0; i < c.width * c.height; i++) {
                fwrite(&c.rgba[i * 4], 1, 3, f);
            }
            fclose(f);
        }
        sf_canvas_close(&c);
        return f ? 0 : 1;
    }
    const char *source = NULL;
    const char *dir = NULL;
    const char *xbe = NULL;
    SfExtractResult result;
    int i;
    for (i = 1; i < argc; i++) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "--extract") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            source = argv[++i];
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "--sound-banks") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            xbe = argv[++i];
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "--data") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            dir = argv[++i];
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "--help") == 0) {
#ifdef _WIN32
            if (AttachConsole(ATTACH_PARENT_PROCESS)) {
                freopen("CONOUT$", "w", stderr);
            }
#endif
            fprintf(stderr, "usage: sf33rdsxb                         play (the first time, import the disc)\n"
                            "       sf33rdsxb --extract SOURCE --data DIR\n"
                            "       sf33rdsxb --sound-banks sf33rd.xbe --data DIR\n");
            return 0;
        }
    }
    if (!source && !dir && !xbe) {
        return -1;
    }
#ifdef _WIN32
    /* a GUI program: the messages go to the command prompt it was run from */
    if (AttachConsole(ATTACH_PARENT_PROCESS)) {
        freopen("CONOUT$", "w", stdout);
        freopen("CONOUT$", "w", stderr);
    }
#endif
    if (xbe) {
        char out[4096];
        char err[256];
        err[0] = 0;
        if (!dir || sf_join(out, sizeof out, dir, "data/audio/sdbk") != 0) {
            fprintf(stderr, "usage: sf33rdsxb --sound-banks sf33rd.xbe --data DIR\n");
            return 1;
        }
        if (sf_mkdir_p(out, err, sizeof err) != 0 || sf_xbe_sound_banks(xbe, out, err, sizeof err) != 0) {
            fprintf(stderr, "%s\n", err);
            return 1;
        }
        printf("sound banks %d\n", SF_SOUND_BANKS);
        return 0;
    }
    if (!source || !dir) {
        fprintf(stderr, "usage: sf33rdsxb --extract SOURCE --data DIR\n");
        return 1;
    }
    if (sf_extract(source, dir, NULL, NULL, NULL, &result) != 0) {
        fprintf(stderr, "%s\n", result.error);
        return 1;
    }
    printf("files %d\n", result.files);
    if (result.note[0]) {
        printf("%s\n", result.note);
    }
    return sf_data_ready(dir) ? 0 : 1;
}

```

`src/port/sound.c`:

```c
/* The game's music on OpenAL.
 *
 * The game drives its music through CRI's ADXT (an ADX streamed from the
 * archive, a queue of ADX stems played back to back, a short ADX from
 * memory) and AIXP (the opening theme's AIX). This keeps those calls and
 * plays each on an OpenAL source: the file is decoded to PCM with the
 * runtime's ADX and AIX decoders (src/game/adx.c, aix.c).
 *
 * The game waits on these streams (a stem ending, the queue running
 * down), so they keep the game's time, not the device's: sf_sound_pump
 * runs once a frame and takes a frame's worth of samples, 1/60 s, from
 * each playing stream. That decides when a stream ends, and the device
 * plays those samples a few frames behind. A run with sound, without it,
 * or faster than real time (a headless replay) sees the same statuses on
 * the same frames, so a session recording replays exactly. The status
 * codes are CRI's: ADXT 0 stopped, 3 playing, 5 ended; AIXP 2 playing,
 * 3 ended. Volumes are CRI's tenths of a decibel.
 *
 * A stream from the archive is read and decoded on a worker thread
 * (src/port/jobs.c), a part at a time, while it plays: starting one reads
 * only its header, and a stem the game queues starts decoding when it is
 * queued. The opening theme is decoded once, when the device opens. The
 * game's time never waits on the decoder's: a frame takes its samples,
 * and only if the decoder has not got that far yet does the game thread
 * wait for it (a track's first frame on a slow disc). The header's length
 * and loop decide the same as the decoded ones up to any sample already
 * decoded, so the statuses are those of a whole decode. */

#include "game/adx.h"
#include "game/aix.h"
#include "host/paths.h"
#include "port/jobs.h"
#include "port/port.h"

#include "types.h"

#include <AL/al.h>
#include <AL/alc.h>
#include <AL/alext.h>
#include <SDL3/SDL.h>

#include <math.h>
#include <stdarg.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

#define VOICE_BUFFERS 24 /* a frame's samples each */
#define PREROLL 3          /* frames queued before the source starts */
#define FRAME_SAMPLES 1600 /* the most a frame takes: 96 kHz / 60 */
#define ENTRY_MAX 16
#define READ_CHUNK 65536 /* the decoder's read, bytes */
#define HEADER_READ 4096 /* the start's read: the header */

enum { ADXT_STOP = 0, ADXT_PLAYING = 3, ADXT_PLAYEND = 5 };
enum { AIXP_STOP = 0, AIXP_PLAYING = 2, AIXP_PLAYEND = 3 };

struct Decode;

/* Decoded PCM, interleaved s16, with its loop. While dec is set it is
 * still being decoded: total and the loop are the header's (an upper
 * bound), and pcm_need gives the samples. shared PCM (the opening theme's)
 * is not freed with the stream. */
typedef struct Pcm {
    int16_t *pcm;
    int channels;
    int rate;
    int32_t total;
    int loop;
    int32_t loop_start;
    int32_t loop_end;
    struct Decode *dec;
    int shared;
    int no_loop; /* a stem: the queue is its loop */
} Pcm;

/* An archive member read and decoded on a worker. */
typedef struct Decode {
    SfJob job;
    u32 offset; /* in the archive */
    u32 size;
    uint8_t *file; /* the member, read so far */
    u32 file_n;
    SfAdxStream adx;
    int16_t *pcm;
    SDL_AtomicInt ready; /* samples decoded */
    SDL_AtomicInt finished;
    SDL_AtomicInt cancel;
} Decode;

/* One OpenAL source fed from a Pcm, and the stems queued after it. */
typedef struct Voice {
    ALuint source;
    ALuint buffers[VOICE_BUFFERS];
    ALuint free_buf[VOICE_BUFFERS]; /* buffers the source does not hold */
    int free_n;
    Pcm now;
    int32_t pos;
    double due; /* samples owed to the frame clock */
    int started; /* the source was started on what it holds */
    int playing;
    int ended;
    int paused;
    int loop_on; /* AIXP's loop switch; ADX loops follow the file */
    float gain;
    int32_t entries[ENTRY_MAX];
    Pcm entry_pcm[ENTRY_MAX]; /* each queued stem, decoding already */
    int entry_n;
    int seamless;
    /* what now holds, so a rollback can decode it again: an archive
     * member (a stem when stem), an ADX in the game's memory, the AIX */
    int src;
    s32 src_fid;
    const void *src_mem;
    int src_stem;
} Voice;

enum { SRC_NONE, SRC_AFS, SRC_MEM, SRC_AIX };

static ALCdevice *s_dev;
static ALCcontext *s_ctx;
static int s_ready;

/* The handles the game keeps. Static, so a u32 holds their address. */
typedef struct AdxtHandle {
    int used;
    Voice v;
} AdxtHandle;
static AdxtHandle s_adxt[4];
static Voice s_aixp;

/* the opening theme's decode (see aix_get) */
static SfJob s_aix_job;
static Pcm s_aix_cache;
static void aix_cache_run(void *unused);
static void entries_clear(Voice *v);

/* SF_SOUND_DEBUG=1 logs each stream call with the frame it came on. */
static void sdebug(const char *fmt, ...)
{
    static int on = -1;
    va_list ap;
    if (on < 0) {
        on = getenv("SF_SOUND_DEBUG") != NULL;
    }
    if (!on) {
        return;
    }
    fprintf(stderr, "sound: frame %d: ", sf_port_frame);
    va_start(ap, fmt);
    vfprintf(stderr, fmt, ap);
    va_end(ap);
    fputc('\n', stderr);
}

static float gain_of(s32 vol)
{
    if (vol <= -960) {
        return 0.0f;
    }
    return powf(10.0f, (float)vol / 200.0f);
}

static void pcm_free(Pcm *p)
{
    if (p->dec) {
        SDL_SetAtomicInt(&p->dec->cancel, 1);
        sf_job_wait(&p->dec->job);
        free(p->dec->file);
        free(p->dec);
    }
    if (!p->shared) {
        free(p->pcm);
    }
    memset(p, 0, sizeof *p);
}

static int pcm_from_adx(const uint8_t *data, uint32_t size, Pcm *out)
{
    SfAdx adx;
    char err[256];
    if (sf_adx_decode(data, size, &adx, err, sizeof err) != 0) {
        fprintf(stderr, "sound: ADX: %s\n", err);
        return -1;
    }
    out->pcm = adx.pcm;
    out->channels = adx.channels;
    out->rate = adx.rate;
    out->total = adx.total;
    out->loop = adx.loop;
    out->loop_start = adx.loop_start;
    out->loop_end = adx.loop_end;
    return 0;
}

/* The worker's half: the member in parts, each decoded as it comes. */
static void decode_run(void *arg)
{
    Decode *d = arg;
    while (!SDL_GetAtomicInt(&d->cancel)) {
        int final;
        if (d->file_n < d->size) {
            u32 n = d->size - d->file_n < READ_CHUNK ? d->size - d->file_n : READ_CHUNK;
            long r = sf_afs_pread(d->file + d->file_n, n, d->offset + d->file_n);
            if (r <= 0) {
                fprintf(stderr, "sound: archive read failed at %u of %u bytes\n", d->file_n, d->size);
                d->size = d->file_n; /* what was read is the file */
            } else {
                d->file_n += (u32)r;
            }
        }
        final = d->file_n >= d->size;
        sf_adx_step(&d->adx, d->file, d->file_n, final, d->pcm, UINT32_MAX);
        SDL_SetAtomicInt(&d->ready, (int)d->adx.produced);
        if (d->adx.finished) {
            break;
        }
        sf_job_progress();
    }
    SDL_SetAtomicInt(&d->finished, 1);
    sf_job_progress();
}

/* A member as a stream: the header now, the rest on a worker. */
static int pcm_from_afs(s32 fid, Pcm *out)
{
    Decode *d;
    char err[256];
    u32 head;
    memset(out, 0, sizeof *out);
    d = calloc(1, sizeof *d);
    if (!d || sf_afs_member(fid, &d->offset, &d->size) != 0 || d->size == 0 || !(d->file = malloc(d->size))) {
        fprintf(stderr, "sound: archive member %d unreadable\n", fid);
        free(d);
        return -1;
    }
    head = d->size < HEADER_READ ? d->size : HEADER_READ;
    if (sf_afs_pread(d->file, head, d->offset) != (long)head) {
        fprintf(stderr, "sound: archive member %d unreadable\n", fid);
        free(d->file);
        free(d);
        return -1;
    }
    d->file_n = head;
    if (sf_adx_open(d->file, d->file_n, &d->adx, err, sizeof err) != 0) {
        fprintf(stderr, "sound: archive member %d (%u bytes, starts %02X %02X %02X %02X) is not a playable ADX: %s\n",
                fid, d->size, d->file[0], d->file[1], d->file[2], d->file[3], err);
        free(d->file);
        free(d);
        return -1;
    }
    d->pcm = malloc((size_t)d->adx.total * (size_t)d->adx.channels * sizeof(int16_t));
    if (!d->pcm) {
        free(d->file);
        free(d);
        return -1;
    }
    out->pcm = d->pcm;
    out->channels = d->adx.channels;
    out->rate = d->adx.rate;
    out->total = d->adx.total;
    out->loop = d->adx.loop;
    out->loop_start = d->adx.loop_start;
    out->loop_end = d->adx.loop_end;
    out->dec = d;
    sf_job_start(&d->job, decode_run, d);
    return 0;
}

typedef struct Need {
    Decode *d;
    int32_t upto;
} Need;

static int need_ready(void *arg)
{
    const Need *n = arg;
    return SDL_GetAtomicInt(&n->d->finished) || SDL_GetAtomicInt(&n->d->ready) > n->upto;
}

/* Samples up to upto may be read: the decoder is past it, or done (and
 * the PCM's length and loop are then the decoded ones). */
static void pcm_need(Pcm *p, int32_t upto)
{
    Need n;
    Decode *d = p->dec;
    if (!d) {
        return;
    }
    n.d = d;
    n.upto = upto;
    if (!need_ready(&n)) {
        sf_job_wait_for(&d->job, need_ready, &n);
    }
    if (SDL_GetAtomicInt(&d->finished)) {
        sf_job_wait(&d->job);
        sf_adx_final(&d->adx, &p->total, &p->loop, &p->loop_start, &p->loop_end);
        if (p->no_loop) {
            p->loop = 0;
        }
        free(d->file);
        free(d);
        p->dec = NULL;
    }
}

/* An ADX in memory: its size from the header (data offset, frames per
 * channel, and the end frame). */
static uint32_t adx_size(const uint8_t *p)
{
    uint32_t off;
    uint32_t block;
    uint32_t ch;
    uint32_t total;
    if (p[0] != 0x80 || p[1] != 0x00) {
        return 0;
    }
    off = ((uint32_t)p[2] << 8 | p[3]) + 4;
    block = p[5];
    ch = p[7];
    total = (uint32_t)p[12] << 24 | (uint32_t)p[13] << 16 | (uint32_t)p[14] << 8 | p[15];
    if (block == 0 || ch == 0) {
        return 0;
    }
    return off + ((total + 31) / 32 + 1) * block * ch;
}

static void voice_init(Voice *v)
{
    if (v->source || !s_ready) {
        return;
    }
    alGenSources(1, &v->source);
    alGenBuffers(VOICE_BUFFERS, v->buffers);
    memcpy(v->free_buf, v->buffers, sizeof v->buffers);
    v->free_n = VOICE_BUFFERS;
    v->gain = 1.0f;
}

/* Buffers the source has played go back to the free list. A source that
 * is not started counts everything it holds as processed, so its new
 * buffers stay until it starts. */
static void voice_reclaim(Voice *v)
{
    ALint done = 0;
    if (!v->started) {
        return;
    }
    alGetSourcei(v->source, AL_BUFFERS_PROCESSED, &done);
    while (done-- > 0 && v->free_n < VOICE_BUFFERS) {
        alSourceUnqueueBuffers(v->source, 1, &v->free_buf[v->free_n]);
        v->free_n++;
    }
}

static void voice_stop(Voice *v)
{
    ALint queued = 0;
    v->playing = 0;
    v->ended = 0;
    v->paused = 0;
    v->src = SRC_NONE;
    if (!v->source) {
        pcm_free(&v->now);
        return;
    }
    alSourceStop(v->source);
    alGetSourcei(v->source, AL_BUFFERS_QUEUED, &queued);
    while (queued-- > 0 && v->free_n < VOICE_BUFFERS) {
        alSourceUnqueueBuffers(v->source, 1, &v->free_buf[v->free_n]);
        v->free_n++;
    }
    v->started = 0;
    pcm_free(&v->now);
}

/* The next stem in the queue becomes the voice's PCM; 0 when there is none. */
static int voice_next_entry(Voice *v)
{
    s32 fid;
    if (v->entry_n == 0) {
        return 0;
    }
    fid = v->entries[0];
    pcm_free(&v->now);
    v->now = v->entry_pcm[0];
    memmove(v->entries, v->entries + 1, (size_t)(v->entry_n - 1) * sizeof v->entries[0]);
    memmove(v->entry_pcm, v->entry_pcm + 1, (size_t)(v->entry_n - 1) * sizeof v->entry_pcm[0]);
    v->entry_n--;
    memset(&v->entry_pcm[v->entry_n], 0, sizeof v->entry_pcm[0]);
    v->src = SRC_NONE;
    if (!v->now.pcm) {
        return 0; /* it did not read */
    }
    v->now.loop = 0; /* a stem plays once; the queue is the loop */
    v->now.no_loop = 1;
    v->pos = 0;
    v->src = SRC_AFS;
    v->src_fid = fid;
    v->src_stem = 1;
    return 1;
}

/* Up to n samples per channel from the voice's PCM into out, following
 * the file's loop or the stem queue; the count written. */
static int32_t voice_read(Voice *v, int16_t *out, int32_t n)
{
    int32_t done = 0;
    while (done < n && v->now.pcm) {
        int32_t end;
        int32_t take;
        pcm_need(&v->now, v->pos + (n - done));
        end = v->now.loop && v->loop_on ? v->now.loop_end : v->now.total;
        take = end - v->pos;
        if (take > n - done) {
            take = n - done;
        }
        if (take > 0) {
            memcpy(out + (size_t)done * v->now.channels, v->now.pcm + (size_t)v->pos * v->now.channels,
                   (size_t)take * v->now.channels * sizeof *out);
            v->pos += take;
            done += take;
        }
        if (v->pos >= end) {
            if (v->now.loop && v->loop_on) {
                v->pos = v->now.loop_start;
            } else if (v->seamless && voice_next_entry(v)) {
                /* a stem with another layout cannot share the stream */
            } else {
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    return done;
}

static void voice_start(Voice *v)
{
    voice_init(v);
    if (!v->now.pcm) {
        return;
    }
    if (v->now.channels > 2) {
        fprintf(stderr, "sound: %d channels; only mono and stereo play\n", v->now.channels);
        return;
    }
    v->playing = 1;
    v->ended = 0;
    v->due = 0.0;
    if (v->source) {
        alSourcef(v->source, AL_GAIN, v->gain);
    }
}

/* A frame of the stream: its samples are taken now, by the game's clock,
 * and queued on the source if the device has room for them. */
static void voice_pump(Voice *v)
{
    static int16_t tmp[FRAME_SAMPLES * 2];
    ALint queued = 0;
    ALint state = 0;
    int32_t want;
    int32_t n;
    if (!v->playing || v->paused) {
        return;
    }
    v->due += (double)v->now.rate / 60.0;
    want = (int32_t)v->due;
    if (want > FRAME_SAMPLES) {
        want = FRAME_SAMPLES;
    }
    v->due -= want;
    n = voice_read(v, tmp, want);
    if (n < want) {
        v->playing = 0;
        v->ended = 1;
        sdebug("voice %p ended", (void *)v);
    }
    if (!v->source) {
        return;
    }
    alGetSourcei(v->source, AL_SOURCE_STATE, &state);
    if (v->started && state == AL_STOPPED) {
        /* it ran dry: what it holds has played; start again on new samples */
        voice_reclaim(v);
        v->started = 0;
    }
    voice_reclaim(v);
    if (n > 0 && v->free_n > 0) {
        ALuint b = v->free_buf[--v->free_n];
        alBufferData(b, v->now.channels == 1 ? AL_FORMAT_MONO16 : AL_FORMAT_STEREO16, tmp,
                     (ALsizei)((size_t)n * v->now.channels * sizeof(int16_t)), v->now.rate);
        alSourceQueueBuffers(v->source, 1, &b);
    }
    /* a full queue (a run faster than the device) drops the frame */
    alGetSourcei(v->source, AL_BUFFERS_QUEUED, &queued);
    alGetSourcei(v->source, AL_SOURCE_STATE, &state);
    if (!v->started && (queued >= PREROLL || (!v->playing && queued > 0))) {
        alSourcePlay(v->source);
        v->started = 1;
    }
}

void sf_sound_pump(void)
{
    size_t i;
    for (i = 0; i < sizeof s_adxt / sizeof s_adxt[0]; i++) {
        voice_pump(&s_adxt[i].v);
    }
    voice_pump(&s_aixp);
}

/* ---- the device ---- */

s32 xb_CreateDirectSound(const char *path)
{
    (void)path;
    if (s_ready) {
        return 1;
    }
    if (!s_aix_job.fn) {
        sf_job_start(&s_aix_job, aix_cache_run, NULL);
    }
    s_dev = alcOpenDevice(NULL);
    s_ctx = s_dev ? alcCreateContext(s_dev, NULL) : NULL;
    if (!s_ctx || !alcMakeContextCurrent(s_ctx)) {
        /* the streams still keep time, so the game runs as it would */
        fprintf(stderr, "sound: no OpenAL device; the game runs silent\n");
        return 1;
    }
    s_ready = 1;
    return 1;
}

/* The app in the background (Android): the device stops playing until it
 * is back. The game itself is stopped meanwhile (SDL holds its thread). */
void sf_sound_pause(int paused)
{
    LPALCDEVICEPAUSESOFT pause_fn;
    LPALCDEVICERESUMESOFT resume_fn;
    if (!s_ready || !alcIsExtensionPresent(s_dev, "ALC_SOFT_pause_device")) {
        return;
    }
    pause_fn = (LPALCDEVICEPAUSESOFT)alcGetProcAddress(s_dev, "alcDevicePauseSOFT");
    resume_fn = (LPALCDEVICERESUMESOFT)alcGetProcAddress(s_dev, "alcDeviceResumeSOFT");
    if (paused && pause_fn) {
        pause_fn(s_dev);
    } else if (!paused && resume_fn) {
        resume_fn(s_dev);
    }
}

/* At exit: the sources stop and the device closes. */
void sf_sound_shutdown(void)
{
    size_t i;
    if (!s_ready) {
        return;
    }
    for (i = 0; i < sizeof s_adxt / sizeof s_adxt[0]; i++) {
        voice_stop(&s_adxt[i].v);
        entries_clear(&s_adxt[i].v);
    }
    voice_stop(&s_aixp);
    if (s_aix_job.fn) {
        sf_job_wait(&s_aix_job);
        s_aix_cache.shared = 0;
        pcm_free(&s_aix_cache);
    }
    sf_se_shutdown();
    alcMakeContextCurrent(NULL);
    alcDestroyContext(s_ctx);
    alcCloseDevice(s_dev);
    s_ctx = NULL;
    s_dev = NULL;
    s_ready = 0;
}

/* ---- ADXT ---- */

void ADXT_Init(void)
{
}

void ADXT_Finish(void)
{
}

void *ADXT_Create(s32 maxch, void *work, s32 worksize)
{
    size_t i;
    (void)maxch;
    (void)work;
    (void)worksize;
    for (i = 0; i < sizeof s_adxt / sizeof s_adxt[0]; i++) {
        if (!s_adxt[i].used) {
            s_adxt[i].used = 1;
            memset(&s_adxt[i].v.now, 0, sizeof s_adxt[i].v.now);
            voice_init(&s_adxt[i].v);
            s_adxt[i].v.loop_on = 1;
            s_adxt[i].v.gain = 1.0f;
            s_adxt[i].v.entry_n = 0;
            s_adxt[i].v.seamless = 0;
            return &s_adxt[i];
        }
    }
    fprintf(stderr, "sound: no free ADXT handle\n");
    return NULL;
}

void ADXT_Destroy(AdxtHandle *a)
{
    if (!a) {
        return;
    }
    voice_stop(&a->v);
    entries_clear(&a->v);
    a->used = 0;
}

void ADXT_Stop(AdxtHandle *a)
{
    if (a) {
        sdebug("ADXT_Stop %p", (void *)&a->v);
        voice_stop(&a->v);
        a->v.seamless = 0;
    }
}

void ADXT_StartAfs(AdxtHandle *a, s32 patid, s32 fid)
{
    (void)patid;
    if (!a) {
        return;
    }
    sdebug("ADXT_StartAfs %p file %d", (void *)&a->v, fid);
    voice_stop(&a->v);
    a->v.seamless = 0;
    if (pcm_from_afs(fid, &a->v.now) == 0) {
        a->v.pos = 0;
        a->v.src = SRC_AFS;
        a->v.src_fid = fid;
        a->v.src_stem = 0;
        voice_start(&a->v);
    }
}

void ADXT_StartMem(AdxtHandle *a, void *adx)
{
    uint32_t size;
    if (!a || !adx) {
        return;
    }
    sdebug("ADXT_StartMem %p", (void *)&a->v);
    voice_stop(&a->v);
    a->v.seamless = 0;
    size = adx_size(adx);
    if (!size) {
        const uint8_t *p = adx;
        fprintf(stderr, "sound: in-memory ADX starts %02X %02X %02X %02X\n", p[0], p[1], p[2], p[3]);
    }
    if (size && pcm_from_adx(adx, size, &a->v.now) == 0) {
        a->v.pos = 0;
        a->v.src = SRC_MEM;
        a->v.src_mem = adx;
        voice_start(&a->v);
    }
}

void ADXT_EntryAfs(AdxtHandle *a, s32 patid, s32 fid)
{
    (void)patid;
    sdebug("ADXT_EntryAfs %p file %d (%d queued)", (void *)(a ? &a->v : NULL), fid, a ? a->v.entry_n : -1);
    if (a && a->v.entry_n < ENTRY_MAX) {
        /* it starts decoding now, to be ready when its turn comes */
        if (pcm_from_afs(fid, &a->v.entry_pcm[a->v.entry_n]) != 0) {
            memset(&a->v.entry_pcm[a->v.entry_n], 0, sizeof a->v.entry_pcm[0]);
        }
        a->v.entries[a->v.entry_n++] = fid;
    }
}

/* The queued stems go, with their audio. */
static void entries_clear(Voice *v)
{
    int i;
    for (i = 0; i < v->entry_n; i++) {
        pcm_free(&v->entry_pcm[i]);
    }
    v->entry_n = 0;
}

void ADXT_ResetEntry(AdxtHandle *a)
{
    if (a) {
        entries_clear(&a->v);
    }
}

s32 ADXT_GetNumFiles(AdxtHandle *a)
{
    return a ? a->v.entry_n : 0;
}

void ADXT_StartSeamless(AdxtHandle *a)
{
    if (!a) {
        return;
    }
    sdebug("ADXT_StartSeamless %p (%d queued)", (void *)&a->v, a->v.entry_n);
    voice_stop(&a->v);
    a->v.seamless = 1;
    if (voice_next_entry(&a->v)) {
        voice_start(&a->v);
    }
}

s32 ADXT_GetStat(AdxtHandle *a)
{
    if (!a) {
        return ADXT_STOP;
    }
    if (a->v.playing) {
        return ADXT_PLAYING;
    }
    return a->v.ended ? ADXT_PLAYEND : ADXT_STOP;
}

void ADXT_Pause(AdxtHandle *a, s32 sw)
{
    if (!a) {
        return;
    }
    sdebug("ADXT_Pause %p %d", (void *)&a->v, sw);
    a->v.paused = sw != 0;
    if (a->v.source && a->v.started) {
        if (sw) {
            alSourcePause(a->v.source);
        } else {
            alSourcePlay(a->v.source);
        }
    }
}

s32 ADXT_GetStatPause(AdxtHandle *a)
{
    return a ? a->v.paused : 0;
}

void ADXT_SetOutVol(AdxtHandle *a, s32 vol)
{
    if (!a) {
        return;
    }
    sdebug("ADXT_SetOutVol %p %d", (void *)&a->v, vol);
    a->v.gain = gain_of(vol);
    if (a->v.source) {
        alSourcef(a->v.source, AL_GAIN, a->v.gain);
    }
}

/* The sound option's mono; OpenAL plays what the device is set to. */
void ADXT_SetOutputMono(s32 flag)
{
    (void)flag;
}

void ADXT_ClearErrCode(AdxtHandle *a)
{
    (void)a;
}

/* ---- AIXP ---- */

void AIXP_Init(void)
{
}

void AIXP_Finish(void)
{
}

void *AIXP_Create(s32 maxnch, s32 nstm, void *work, s32 worksize)
{
    (void)maxnch;
    (void)nstm;
    (void)work;
    (void)worksize;
    voice_init(&s_aixp);
    s_aixp.loop_on = 1;
    s_aixp.gain = 1.0f;
    return &s_aixp;
}

/* The opening theme, data/audio/NAME: its front stereo pair (the AIX's
 * other pairs are the surround channels). */
static char s_aix_name[64];

static int aix_load(const char *fname, Pcm *out)
{
    char base[4096];
    char path[4096 + 300];
    FILE *f;
    long n;
    uint8_t *data;
    SfAix aix;
    char err[256];
    if (sf_exe_dir(base, sizeof base) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    snprintf(path, sizeof path, "%s/data/audio/%s", base, fname);
    f = fopen(path, "rb");
    if (!f) {
        fprintf(stderr, "sound: %s missing\n", path);
        return -1;
    }
    fseek(f, 0, SEEK_END);
    n = ftell(f);
    fseek(f, 0, SEEK_SET);
    data = malloc((size_t)n);
    if (!data || fread(data, 1, (size_t)n, f) != (size_t)n) {
        fclose(f);
        free(data);
        return -1;
    }
    fclose(f);
    if (sf_aix_decode(data, (uint32_t)n, &aix, err, sizeof err) != 0) {
        fprintf(stderr, "sound: %s: %s\n", fname, err);
        free(data);
        return -1;
    }
    free(data);
    out->pcm = aix.pcm;
    out->channels = aix.channels;
    out->rate = aix.rate;
    out->total = aix.total;
    out->loop = aix.loop;
    out->loop_start = aix.loop_start;
    out->loop_end = aix.loop_end;
    return 0;
}

/* The opening theme, the one AIX the game plays, is decoded once on a
 * worker as soon as the device opens; streams of it share that PCM. */
#define AIX_OPENING "ST3_3_OP.aix"

static int s_aix_cache_ok;

static void aix_cache_run(void *unused)
{
    (void)unused;
    s_aix_cache_ok = aix_load(AIX_OPENING, &s_aix_cache) == 0;
}

static int aix_get(const char *fname, Pcm *out)
{
    if (strcmp(fname, AIX_OPENING) == 0 && s_aix_job.fn) {
        sf_job_wait(&s_aix_job);
        if (!s_aix_cache_ok) {
            return -1;
        }
        *out = s_aix_cache;
        out->shared = 1;
        return 0;
    }
    return aix_load(fname, out);
}

void xb_aixp_StartFnameSurround(void *aixp, const char *fname, void *dir)
{
    (void)aixp;
    (void)dir;
    sdebug("AIXP start %s", fname);
    voice_stop(&s_aixp);
    if (aix_get(fname, &s_aixp.now) != 0) {
        return;
    }
    snprintf(s_aix_name, sizeof s_aix_name, "%s", fname);
    s_aixp.src = SRC_AIX;
    s_aixp.pos = 0;
    voice_start(&s_aixp);
}

void AIXP_Stop(void *aixp)
{
    (void)aixp;
    sdebug("AIXP_Stop");
    voice_stop(&s_aixp);
}

s32 AIXP_GetStat(void *aixp)
{
    (void)aixp;
    if (s_aixp.playing) {
        return AIXP_PLAYING;
    }
    return s_aixp.ended ? AIXP_PLAYEND : AIXP_STOP;
}

void AIXP_SetLpSw(void *aixp, s32 sw)
{
    (void)aixp;
    s_aixp.loop_on = sw != 0;
}

void AIXP_Pause(void *aixp, s32 sw)
{
    (void)aixp;
    s_aixp.paused = sw != 0;
    if (s_aixp.source && s_aixp.started) {
        if (sw) {
            alSourcePause(s_aixp.source);
        } else {
            alSourcePlay(s_aixp.source);
        }
    }
}

/* Channel 0 sets the front pair's volume; the others are surround. */
void AIXP_SetOutVolDolbyDigital(void *aixp, s32 ch, s32 vol)
{
    (void)aixp;
    if (ch != 0) {
        return;
    }
    s_aixp.gain = gain_of(vol);
    if (s_aixp.source) {
        alSourcef(s_aixp.source, AL_GAIN, s_aixp.gain);
    }
}

/* Retail's physical memory, for the AIX player's work area: from the
 * arena, so a rollback snapshot keeps it. */
void *XPhysicalAlloc(u32 size, u32 addr, u32 align, u32 protect)
{
    (void)addr;
    (void)protect;
    return sf_arena_alloc(size, align ? align : 16);
}

/* ---- rollback: the streams' place in the game's time ---- */

/* What the game sees of a stream (its status, its stem queue) moves with
 * the frames, so a rollback puts it back with the game's state. The
 * decoded audio and the device's source are not copied: a stream whose
 * file changed is decoded again, and the source refills from the place. */
typedef struct StreamState {
    int used;
    int src;
    s32 src_fid;
    const void *src_mem;
    int src_stem;
    int32_t pos;
    double due;
    int playing;
    int ended;
    int paused;
    int loop_on;
    float gain;
    int32_t entries[ENTRY_MAX];
    int entry_n;
    int seamless;
} StreamState;

#define STREAMS (sizeof s_adxt / sizeof s_adxt[0] + 1)

typedef struct SoundState {
    StreamState st[STREAMS];
    char aix[64];
} SoundState;

static Voice *stream(size_t i, int **used)
{
    if (i < sizeof s_adxt / sizeof s_adxt[0]) {
        *used = &s_adxt[i].used;
        return &s_adxt[i].v;
    }
    *used = NULL;
    return &s_aixp;
}

size_t sf_sound_state_size(void)
{
    return sizeof(SoundState);
}

void sf_sound_state_save(void *buf)
{
    SoundState *ss = buf;
    size_t i;
    memset(ss, 0, sizeof *ss);
    for (i = 0; i < STREAMS; i++) {
        int *used;
        Voice *v = stream(i, &used);
        StreamState *t = &ss->st[i];
        t->used = used ? *used : 1;
        t->src = v->src;
        t->src_fid = v->src_fid;
        t->src_mem = v->src_mem;
        t->src_stem = v->src_stem;
        t->pos = v->pos;
        t->due = v->due;
        t->playing = v->playing;
        t->ended = v->ended;
        t->paused = v->paused;
        t->loop_on = v->loop_on;
        t->gain = v->gain;
        memcpy(t->entries, v->entries, sizeof t->entries);
        t->entry_n = v->entry_n;
        t->seamless = v->seamless;
    }
    memcpy(ss->aix, s_aix_name, sizeof ss->aix);
}

void sf_sound_state_restore(const void *buf)
{
    const SoundState *ss = buf;
    size_t i;
    for (i = 0; i < STREAMS; i++) {
        int *used;
        Voice *v = stream(i, &used);
        const StreamState *t = &ss->st[i];
        int same = v->src == t->src && v->now.pcm &&
                   (t->src == SRC_AFS ? v->src_fid == t->src_fid && v->src_stem == t->src_stem
                    : t->src == SRC_MEM ? v->src_mem == t->src_mem
                    : t->src == SRC_AIX ? strcmp(s_aix_name, ss->aix) == 0
                                        : 1);
        if (used) {
            *used = t->used;
        }
        if (!same) {
            voice_stop(v); /* frees the audio, empties the source */
            if (t->src == SRC_AFS && pcm_from_afs(t->src_fid, &v->now) == 0) {
                if (t->src_stem) {
                    v->now.loop = 0;
                    v->now.no_loop = 1;
                }
            } else if (t->src == SRC_MEM && t->src_mem) {
                uint32_t size = adx_size(t->src_mem);
                if (!size || pcm_from_adx(t->src_mem, size, &v->now) != 0) {
                    memset(&v->now, 0, sizeof v->now);
                }
            } else if (t->src == SRC_AIX && aix_get(ss->aix, &v->now) == 0) {
                snprintf(s_aix_name, sizeof s_aix_name, "%s", ss->aix);
            }
        } else if (v->source) {
            /* the same audio from another place: the queued samples go */
            ALint queued = 0;
            alSourceStop(v->source);
            alGetSourcei(v->source, AL_BUFFERS_QUEUED, &queued);
            while (queued-- > 0 && v->free_n < VOICE_BUFFERS) {
                alSourceUnqueueBuffers(v->source, 1, &v->free_buf[v->free_n]);
                v->free_n++;
            }
            v->started = 0;
        }
        v->src = v->now.pcm ? t->src : SRC_NONE;
        v->src_fid = t->src_fid;
        v->src_mem = t->src_mem;
        v->src_stem = t->src_stem;
        v->pos = t->pos;
        v->due = t->due;
        v->playing = t->playing && v->now.pcm;
        v->ended = t->ended;
        v->paused = t->paused;
        v->loop_on = t->loop_on;
        v->gain = t->gain;
        if (v->entry_n != t->entry_n || memcmp(v->entries, t->entries, (size_t)t->entry_n * sizeof v->entries[0]) != 0) {
            /* another queue: its stems decode again */
            int k;
            entries_clear(v);
            for (k = 0; k < t->entry_n; k++) {
                if (pcm_from_afs(t->entries[k], &v->entry_pcm[k]) != 0) {
                    memset(&v->entry_pcm[k], 0, sizeof v->entry_pcm[k]);
                }
            }
        }
        memcpy(v->entries, t->entries, sizeof v->entries);
        v->entry_n = t->entry_n;
        v->seamless = t->seamless;
        if (v->source) {
            alSourcef(v->source, AL_GAIN, v->gain);
        }
    }
}

```

`src/port/state.c`:

```c
/* The game's state, as one block that can be saved and put back: what
 * rollback netcode needs to run frames again from an earlier one.
 *
 * The state is the program's writable data (.data and .bss: the decomp's
 * globals, and the generated stubs' blocks) less the port's own sections
 * (sfport_data and sfport_bss: the window, renderer, textures, sound and
 * input devices, renamed at build in cmake/decomp.cmake), and the used
 * part of the arena (the game's heaps). Saving copies them, restoring
 * copies them back.
 *
 * SF_ROLLBACK_TEST=FRAME:K tests that the game runs frames again exactly:
 * at FRAME it saves, runs K frames hashing the state after each (and each
 * 4 KB page of it), puts the state back, runs the same K frames on the
 * same pads (the input tape) and compares. A difference names the frame
 * and the pages, by address, that came out otherwise. */

#include "port/port.h"

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

#ifdef _WIN32
/* PE has no __start_/__stop_ section bounds: the snapshot is Linux-only
 * for now, and on Windows it is empty. */
static char __start_sfport_data[1], __stop_sfport_data[1], __start_sfport_bss[1], __stop_sfport_bss[1];
static char __start_sfkeep[1], __stop_sfkeep[1];
#elif defined(__ANDROID__)
/* Android's libc has no __data_start: the game library's writable
 * segments are read from its program headers instead (data_ranges). */
#include <link.h>
extern char __start_sfport_data[];
extern char __stop_sfport_data[];
extern char __start_sfport_bss[];
extern char __stop_sfport_bss[];
extern char __start_sfkeep[];
extern char __stop_sfkeep[];
#else
extern char __data_start[];
extern char _end[];
extern char __start_sfport_data[];
extern char __stop_sfport_data[];
extern char __start_sfport_bss[];
extern char __stop_sfport_bss[];
extern char __start_sfkeep[];
extern char __stop_sfkeep[];
#endif

void sf_arena_extent(uint8_t **base, size_t *used);

#define REGIONS 32
#define PAGE 4096

typedef struct Region {
    uint8_t *p;
    size_t n;
} Region;

static Region s_reg[REGIONS] SF_KEEP;
static int s_reg_n SF_KEEP;

/* The program's writable data, before the port's sections are cut out:
 * on Linux .data to the end of .bss; on Android each writable segment of
 * the game library, less what the loader makes read-only (RELRO). */
#define BASES 4
static uint8_t *s_base[BASES][2] SF_KEEP;
static int s_base_n SF_KEEP;

#ifdef __ANDROID__
static int phdr_cb(struct dl_phdr_info *info, size_t size, void *data)
{
    uintptr_t me = (uintptr_t)data;
    uintptr_t rlo = 0;
    uintptr_t rhi = 0;
    int mine = 0;
    int i;
    (void)size;
    for (i = 0; i < info->dlpi_phnum; i++) {
        const ElfW(Phdr) *ph = &info->dlpi_phdr[i];
        uintptr_t a = info->dlpi_addr + ph->p_vaddr;
        if (ph->p_type == PT_LOAD && me >= a && me < a + ph->p_memsz) {
            mine = 1;
        }
        if (ph->p_type == PT_GNU_RELRO) {
            rlo = a;
            rhi = a + ph->p_memsz;
        }
    }
    if (!mine) {
        return 0;
    }
    s_base_n = 0;
    for (i = 0; i < info->dlpi_phnum && s_base_n < BASES; i++) {
        const ElfW(Phdr) *ph = &info->dlpi_phdr[i];
        uintptr_t a = info->dlpi_addr + ph->p_vaddr;
        uintptr_t e = a + ph->p_memsz;
        if (ph->p_type != PT_LOAD || !(ph->p_flags & PF_W)) {
            continue;
        }
        if (rhi > a && rlo <= a) {
            a = rhi < e ? rhi : e; /* the read-only start of the segment goes */
        }
        if (e > a) {
            s_base[s_base_n][0] = (uint8_t *)a;
            s_base[s_base_n][1] = (uint8_t *)e;
            s_base_n++;
        }
    }
    return 1;
}
#endif

static void data_ranges(void)
{
    if (s_base_n) {
        return;
    }
#if defined(_WIN32)
    s_base_n = 0;
#elif defined(__ANDROID__)
    dl_iterate_phdr(phdr_cb, (void *)&s_reg);
#else
    s_base[0][0] = (uint8_t *)__data_start;
    s_base[0][1] = (uint8_t *)_end;
    s_base_n = 1;
#endif
}

static void add_region(uint8_t *a, uint8_t *b)
{
    if (b > a && s_reg_n < REGIONS) {
        s_reg[s_reg_n].p = a;
        s_reg[s_reg_n].n = (size_t)(b - a);
        s_reg_n++;
    }
}

/* [lo, hi) less the port's sections, wherever the linker put them (with
 * SF_ROLLBACK_ALL, a test, less only sfkeep: the snapshot's own state) */
static void regions(void)
{
    static int all = -1;
    uint8_t *cut[4][2] = {
        { (uint8_t *)__start_sfport_data, (uint8_t *)__stop_sfport_data },
        { (uint8_t *)__start_sfport_bss, (uint8_t *)__stop_sfport_bss },
        { (uint8_t *)__start_sfkeep, (uint8_t *)__stop_sfkeep },
    };
    int ncut = 3;
    uint8_t *arena;
    size_t used;
    int k;
    int bi;
    s_reg_n = 0;
#ifdef _WIN32
    (void)cut;
    (void)ncut;
    (void)arena;
    (void)used;
    (void)k;
    (void)bi;
    (void)all;
    return;
#endif
    data_ranges();
    if (all < 0) {
        all = getenv("SF_ROLLBACK_ALL") != NULL;
    }
    if (all) {
        cut[0][0] = cut[2][0];
        cut[0][1] = cut[2][1];
        ncut = 1;
    }
    /* the cuts in address order */
    for (k = 0; k < ncut; k++) {
        int j;
```
