```c
        for (j = k + 1; j < ncut; j++) {
            if (cut[j][0] < cut[k][0]) {
                uint8_t *t0 = cut[k][0];
                uint8_t *t1 = cut[k][1];
                cut[k][0] = cut[j][0];
                cut[k][1] = cut[j][1];
                cut[j][0] = t0;
                cut[j][1] = t1;
            }
        }
    }
    for (bi = 0; bi < s_base_n; bi++) {
        uint8_t *at = s_base[bi][0];
        uint8_t *end = s_base[bi][1];
        for (k = 0; k < ncut; k++) {
            if (cut[k][0] >= at && cut[k][1] <= end) {
                add_region(at, cut[k][0]);
                at = cut[k][1];
            }
        }
        add_region(at, end);
    }
    {
        /* SF_ROLLBACK_EXTRA=ADDR:LEN,...: port ranges a test takes in too */
        const char *e = getenv("SF_ROLLBACK_EXTRA");
        while (e && *e) {
            char *c;
            unsigned long long p0 = strtoull(e, &c, 0);
            unsigned long long n0 = *c == ':' ? strtoull(c + 1, &c, 0) : 0;
            add_region((uint8_t *)(uintptr_t)p0, (uint8_t *)(uintptr_t)(p0 + n0));
            e = *c == ',' ? c + 1 : c;
            if (c == e && *e != ',') {
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    sf_arena_extent(&arena, &used);
    add_region(arena, arena + used);
}

/* The memory regions, then the port subsystems the game reads back (the
 * music streams' place: sound.c). */
size_t sf_state_size(void)
{
    size_t n = sf_sound_state_size();
    int i;
    regions();
    for (i = 0; i < s_reg_n; i++) {
        n += s_reg[i].n;
    }
    return n;
}

void sf_state_save(uint8_t *buf)
{
    int i;
    sf_files_sync(); /* no read still landing in the memory copied */
    regions();
    for (i = 0; i < s_reg_n; i++) {
        memcpy(buf, s_reg[i].p, s_reg[i].n);
        buf += s_reg[i].n;
    }
    sf_sound_state_save(buf);
}

void sf_state_restore(const uint8_t *buf)
{
    int i;
    sf_files_sync(); /* no read still landing in the memory copied */
    regions();
    for (i = 0; i < s_reg_n; i++) {
        memcpy(s_reg[i].p, buf, s_reg[i].n);
        buf += s_reg[i].n;
    }
    sf_sound_state_restore(buf);
}

static uint64_t hash_bytes(const uint8_t *p, size_t n, uint64_t h)
{
    size_t i;
    for (i = 0; i + 8 <= n; i += 8) {
        uint64_t w;
        memcpy(&w, p + i, 8);
        h = (h ^ w) * 0x100000001B3ull;
        h ^= h >> 29;
    }
    for (; i < n; i++) {
        h = (h ^ p[i]) * 0x100000001B3ull;
    }
    return h;
}

/* ---- the test ---- */

static struct {
    int on;
    int from;
    int frames;
    int step; /* 0 before, 1 first run, 2 second run, 3 done */
    int at;
    uint8_t *saved;
    size_t size;
    size_t pages;
    uint64_t *page_hash; /* the first run's, frames by pages */
    uint64_t *hash;      /* the first run's, by frame */
    uint8_t *first;      /* the first run's state after its first frame */
    int bad;
} s_t SF_KEEP;

static void page_hashes(uint64_t *out)
{
    size_t k = 0;
    int i;
    for (i = 0; i < s_reg_n; i++) {
        size_t off;
        for (off = 0; off < s_reg[i].n; off += PAGE) {
            size_t n = s_reg[i].n - off < PAGE ? s_reg[i].n - off : PAGE;
            out[k++] = hash_bytes(s_reg[i].p + off, n, 0xCBF29CE484222325ull);
        }
    }
}

static uint64_t fold(const uint64_t *pages)
{
    return hash_bytes((const uint8_t *)pages, s_t.pages * sizeof *pages, 0xCBF29CE484222325ull);
}

/* the page k's address */
static uint8_t *page_addr(size_t k)
{
    int i;
    for (i = 0; i < s_reg_n; i++) {
        size_t n = (s_reg[i].n + PAGE - 1) / PAGE;
        if (k < n) {
            return s_reg[i].p + k * PAGE;
        }
        k -= n;
    }
    return NULL;
}

/* After each game frame. */
void sf_state_test_frame(void)
{
    const char *env;
    uint64_t *now;
    size_t i;
    if (s_t.step == 0) {
        env = getenv("SF_ROLLBACK_TEST");
        if (!env) {
            s_t.step = 3;
            return;
        }
        s_t.from = atoi(env);
        s_t.frames = strchr(env, ':') ? atoi(strchr(env, ':') + 1) : 60;
        if (s_t.frames < 1 || s_t.frames > 600) {
            s_t.frames = 60;
        }
        if (sf_port_frame < s_t.from) {
            return;
        }
        s_t.size = sf_state_size();
        s_t.saved = malloc(s_t.size);
        s_t.pages = 0;
        for (i = 0; i < (size_t)s_reg_n; i++) {
            s_t.pages += (s_reg[i].n + PAGE - 1) / PAGE;
        }
        s_t.page_hash = malloc(s_t.pages * sizeof *s_t.page_hash * (size_t)s_t.frames);
        s_t.hash = malloc(sizeof *s_t.hash * (size_t)s_t.frames);
        s_t.first = malloc(s_t.size);
        if (!s_t.saved || !s_t.page_hash || !s_t.hash || !s_t.first) {
            fprintf(stderr, "rollback test: no memory\n");
            exit(1);
        }
        sf_state_save(s_t.saved);
        sf_input_tape_mark();
        fprintf(stderr, "rollback test: saved %zu bytes (%zu pages) at frame %d\n", s_t.size, s_t.pages,
                sf_port_frame);
        s_t.step = 1;
        s_t.at = 0;
        return;
    }
    if (s_t.step == 3) {
        return;
    }
    now = s_t.page_hash + (size_t)s_t.at * s_t.pages;
    if (s_t.step == 1) {
        page_hashes(now);
        s_t.hash[s_t.at] = fold(now);
        if (s_t.at == 0) {
            sf_state_save(s_t.first);
        }
        if (s_t.at > 0) {
            size_t changed = 0;
            for (i = 0; i < s_t.pages; i++) {
                changed += now[i] != now[i - s_t.pages];
            }
            if (s_t.at == 1 || s_t.at == s_t.frames - 1) {
                fprintf(stderr, "rollback test: frame %d changed %zu pages (%zu KB)\n", s_t.at, changed,
                        changed * PAGE / 1024);
            }
        }
        if (++s_t.at == s_t.frames) {
            sf_state_restore(s_t.saved);
            sf_input_tape_rewind();
            s_t.step = 2;
            s_t.at = 0;
            fprintf(stderr, "rollback test: put back; running the %d frames again\n", s_t.frames);
        }
        return;
    }
    if (s_t.at == 0) {
        /* the bytes that came out otherwise, as address ranges */
        uint8_t *now_state = malloc(s_t.size);
        size_t off = 0;
        int ri;
        int shown = 0;
        sf_state_save(now_state);
        for (ri = 0; ri < s_reg_n && shown < 60; ri++) {
            size_t j = 0;
            while (j < s_reg[ri].n && shown < 60) {
                size_t k;
                if (now_state[off + j] == s_t.first[off + j]) {
                    j++;
                    continue;
                }
                for (k = j; k < s_reg[ri].n && k < j + 64 && now_state[off + k] != s_t.first[off + k]; k++) {
                }
                fprintf(stderr, "rollback test: differs at %p (+%zu bytes)\n", (void *)(s_reg[ri].p + j), k - j);
                shown++;
                j = k + 1;
            }
            off += s_reg[ri].n;
        }
        free(now_state);
    }
    {
        uint64_t *again = malloc(s_t.pages * sizeof *again);
        page_hashes(again);
        if (fold(again) != s_t.hash[s_t.at]) {
            int shown = 0;
            fprintf(stderr, "rollback test: frame %d differs:", s_t.at);
            for (i = 0; i < s_t.pages && shown < 12; i++) {
                if (again[i] != now[i]) {
                    fprintf(stderr, " %p", (void *)page_addr(i));
                    shown++;
                }
            }
            fprintf(stderr, "\n");
            s_t.bad++;
        }
        free(again);
    }
    if (++s_t.at == s_t.frames) {
        fprintf(stderr, "rollback test: %s (%d of %d frames differ)\n", s_t.bad ? "FAILED" : "the same", s_t.bad,
                s_t.frames);
        sf_input_tape_off();
        s_t.step = 3;
        exit(s_t.bad ? 2 : 0);
    }
}

```

`src/port/store.c`:

```c
/* SQLite for the game's saves and the host's settings.
 *
 * game.sqlite: one system row, one directions row, replay slots 0 to 9.
 * host.sqlite: one window row, one row per key. Schema version is 1.
 * A write is a transaction with synchronous=FULL. A file that fails
 * integrity_check is left alone.
 *
 * Not stored: the memory-card header, sega_reserve, free_free, the network
 * record, lag, the pad's free bytes, and fname. A load leaves them zero. */

#include "port/store.h"

#include <sqlite3.h>

#include <errno.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <time.h>

#define STORE_VERSION 1
#define INPUT_FRAMES 7198
#define REPLAY_SLOTS 10

_Static_assert(sizeof(((const _REPLAY_W *)0)->io_unit.key_buff[0]) / sizeof(u16) == INPUT_FRAMES,
               "replay input frames");

static const char k_game_sql[] =
    "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS system ("
    " id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (id = 1),"
    " difficulty INTEGER NOT NULL, time_limit INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " battle0 INTEGER NOT NULL, battle1 INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " damage_level INTEGER NOT NULL, handicap INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " partner0 INTEGER NOT NULL, partner1 INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " adjust_x INTEGER NOT NULL, adjust_y INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " screen_size INTEGER NOT NULL, screen_mode INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " guard_check INTEGER NOT NULL, auto_save INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " analog_stick INTEGER NOT NULL, bgm_type INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " sound_mode INTEGER NOT NULL, bgm_level INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " se_level INTEGER NOT NULL, extra_option INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " sum INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " pad0_vibration INTEGER NOT NULL, pad1_vibration INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " pad0_shot BLOB NOT NULL, pad1_shot BLOB NOT NULL,"
    " color0 BLOB NOT NULL, color1 BLOB NOT NULL,"
    " extra BLOB NOT NULL);"
    "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS rank ("
    " idx INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (idx >= 0 AND idx < 20),"
    " name BLOB NOT NULL, player INTEGER NOT NULL, score INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " cpu_grade INTEGER NOT NULL, grade INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " wins INTEGER NOT NULL, player_color INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " all_clear INTEGER NOT NULL);"
    "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS directions ("
    " id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (id = 1),"
    " rows BLOB NOT NULL, sum INTEGER NOT NULL);"
    "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS replay ("
    " slot INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (slot >= 0 AND slot < 10),"
    " saved_at INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " control_time INTEGER NOT NULL, difficulty INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " monitor_type INTEGER NOT NULL, champion INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " full_data INTEGER NOT NULL, sum INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " p0_char INTEGER NOT NULL, p0_sa INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " p0_color INTEGER NOT NULL, p0_type INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " p1_char INTEGER NOT NULL, p1_sa INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " p1_color INTEGER NOT NULL, p1_type INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " stage INTEGER NOT NULL, direction_working INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " vital0 INTEGER NOT NULL, vital1 INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " random_ix16 INTEGER NOT NULL, random_ix32 INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " random_ix16_ex INTEGER NOT NULL, random_ix32_ex INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " winner INTEGER NOT NULL, play_type INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " players_timer INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " old_mes_no2 INTEGER NOT NULL, old_mes_no3 INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " old_mes_no_pl INTEGER NOT NULL, mes_already INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " mini_time INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " mini_battle0 INTEGER NOT NULL, mini_battle1 INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " mini_damage INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " mini_pad0_vib INTEGER NOT NULL, mini_pad1_vib INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " mini_pad0_shot BLOB NOT NULL, mini_pad1_shot BLOB NOT NULL,"
    " mini_extra BLOB NOT NULL,"
    " directions BLOB NOT NULL, directions_sum INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " inputs BLOB NOT NULL);";

static const char k_host_sql[] =
    "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS window ("
    " id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (id = 1),"
    " mode TEXT NOT NULL, width INTEGER NOT NULL,"
    " height INTEGER NOT NULL, aspect TEXT NOT NULL);"
    "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS key ("
    " name TEXT PRIMARY KEY, scancode TEXT NOT NULL);";

static int file_exists(const char *path)
{
    struct stat st;
    return stat(path, &st) == 0;
}

static int exec_sql(sqlite3 *db, const char *sql)
{
    char *err = NULL;
    int rc = sqlite3_exec(db, sql, NULL, NULL, &err);
    if (rc != SQLITE_OK) {
        fprintf(stderr, "save: %s\n", err ? err : sqlite3_errmsg(db));
        sqlite3_free(err);
        return -1;
    }
    return 0;
}

static int user_version(sqlite3 *db, int *ver)
{
    sqlite3_stmt *st = NULL;
    int rc;
    *ver = -1;
    if (sqlite3_prepare_v2(db, "PRAGMA user_version", -1, &st, NULL) != SQLITE_OK) {
        return -1;
    }
    rc = sqlite3_step(st);
    if (rc == SQLITE_ROW) {
        *ver = sqlite3_column_int(st, 0);
    }
    sqlite3_finalize(st);
    return rc == SQLITE_ROW ? 0 : -1;
}

static int integrity_ok(sqlite3 *db)
{
    sqlite3_stmt *st = NULL;
    const char *msg;
    int rc;
    int ok = 0;
    if (sqlite3_prepare_v2(db, "PRAGMA integrity_check", -1, &st, NULL) != SQLITE_OK) {
        return 0;
    }
    rc = sqlite3_step(st);
    msg = rc == SQLITE_ROW ? (const char *)sqlite3_column_text(st, 0) : NULL;
    ok = msg && strcmp(msg, "ok") == 0;
    sqlite3_finalize(st);
    return ok;
}

/* 0 open, 1 no save yet, -1 damaged. writing creates a missing file. */
static int open_db(const char *path, sqlite3 **out, int writing)
{
    int exists;
    int flags;
    int ver = 0;
    int rc;
    sqlite3 *db = NULL;
    if (!path || !out) {
        return -1;
    }
    exists = file_exists(path);
    if (!exists && !writing) {
        return 1;
    }
    flags = SQLITE_OPEN_READWRITE;
    if (!exists) {
        flags |= SQLITE_OPEN_CREATE;
    }
    if (sqlite3_open_v2(path, &db, flags, NULL) != SQLITE_OK) {
        fprintf(stderr, "save: %s: %s\n", path, db ? sqlite3_errmsg(db) : "open failed");
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    sqlite3_busy_timeout(db, 2000);
    if (exec_sql(db, "PRAGMA journal_mode=DELETE") != 0 || exec_sql(db, "PRAGMA synchronous=FULL") != 0) {
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    if (exists && !integrity_ok(db)) {
        fprintf(stderr, "save: %s is damaged; not loaded\n", path);
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    if (user_version(db, &ver) != 0) {
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    if (ver != 0 && ver != STORE_VERSION) {
        fprintf(stderr, "save: %s is damaged; not loaded\n", path);
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    if (!writing && ver == 0) {
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return 1;
    }
    if (writing && ver == 0) {
        rc = exec_sql(db, "PRAGMA user_version=1");
        if (rc != 0) {
            sqlite3_close(db);
            return -1;
        }
    }
    *out = db;
    return 0;
}

static int ensure(sqlite3 *db, const char *sql)
{
    return exec_sql(db, sql);
}

static int begin(sqlite3 *db)
{
    return exec_sql(db, "BEGIN IMMEDIATE");
}

static int commit(sqlite3 *db)
{
    return exec_sql(db, "COMMIT");
}

static void rollback(sqlite3 *db)
{
    sqlite3_exec(db, "ROLLBACK", NULL, NULL, NULL);
}

static int bind_i(sqlite3_stmt *st, int i, long long v)
{
    return sqlite3_bind_int64(st, i, v) == SQLITE_OK ? 0 : -1;
}

static int bind_blob(sqlite3_stmt *st, int i, const void *p, int n)
{
    const void *src = p ? p : "";
    if (n < 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    return sqlite3_bind_blob(st, i, src, n, SQLITE_TRANSIENT) == SQLITE_OK ? 0 : -1;
}

static int col_i(sqlite3_stmt *st, int i, long long min, long long max, long long *out)
{
    long long v;
    if (sqlite3_column_type(st, i) != SQLITE_INTEGER) {
        return -1;
    }
    v = sqlite3_column_int64(st, i);
    if (v < min || v > max) {
        return -1;
    }
    *out = v;
    return 0;
}

static int col_blob(sqlite3_stmt *st, int i, void *dest, int n)
{
    const void *p;
    if (n < 0 || sqlite3_column_bytes(st, i) != n) {
        return -1;
    }
    if (n == 0) {
        return 0;
    }
    if (sqlite3_column_type(st, i) != SQLITE_BLOB) {
        return -1;
    }
    p = sqlite3_column_blob(st, i);
    if (!p) {
        return -1;
    }
    memcpy(dest, p, (size_t)n);
    return 0;
}

static int step_row(sqlite3_stmt *st)
{
    int rc = sqlite3_step(st);
    if (rc == SQLITE_ROW) {
        return 0;
    }
    if (rc == SQLITE_DONE) {
        return 1;
    }
    return -1;
}

static int prepare(sqlite3 *db, const char *sql, sqlite3_stmt **st)
{
    if (sqlite3_prepare_v2(db, sql, -1, st, NULL) != SQLITE_OK) {
        fprintf(stderr, "save: %s\n", sqlite3_errmsg(db));
        return -1;
    }
    return 0;
}

static const char k_system_ins[] =
    "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO system ("
    "id, difficulty, time_limit, battle0, battle1, damage_level, handicap,"
    "partner0, partner1, adjust_x, adjust_y, screen_size, screen_mode,"
    "guard_check, auto_save, analog_stick, bgm_type, sound_mode,"
    "bgm_level, se_level, extra_option, sum,"
    "pad0_vibration, pad1_vibration, pad0_shot, pad1_shot, color0, color1, extra"
    ") VALUES (1,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)";

static const char k_rank_ins[] =
    "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO rank ("
    "idx, name, player, score, cpu_grade, grade, wins, player_color, all_clear"
    ") VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)";

static int write_system_tx(sqlite3 *db, const struct _SAVE_W *sw)
{
    sqlite3_stmt *st = NULL;
    int i;
    int rc = -1;
    if (begin(db) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    if (prepare(db, k_system_ins, &st) != 0) {
        rollback(db);
        return -1;
    }
    if (bind_i(st, 1, sw->Difficulty) != 0 || bind_i(st, 2, sw->Time_Limit) != 0 ||
        bind_i(st, 3, sw->Battle_Number[0]) != 0 || bind_i(st, 4, sw->Battle_Number[1]) != 0 ||
        bind_i(st, 5, sw->Damage_Level) != 0 || bind_i(st, 6, sw->Handicap) != 0 ||
        bind_i(st, 7, sw->Partner_Type[0]) != 0 || bind_i(st, 8, sw->Partner_Type[1]) != 0 ||
        bind_i(st, 9, sw->Adjust_X) != 0 || bind_i(st, 10, sw->Adjust_Y) != 0 ||
        bind_i(st, 11, sw->Screen_Size) != 0 || bind_i(st, 12, sw->Screen_Mode) != 0 ||
        bind_i(st, 13, sw->GuardCheck) != 0 || bind_i(st, 14, sw->Auto_Save) != 0 ||
        bind_i(st, 15, sw->AnalogStick) != 0 || bind_i(st, 16, sw->BgmType) != 0 ||
        bind_i(st, 17, sw->SoundMode) != 0 || bind_i(st, 18, sw->BGM_Level) != 0 ||
        bind_i(st, 19, sw->SE_Level) != 0 || bind_i(st, 20, sw->Extra_Option) != 0 ||
        bind_i(st, 21, sw->sum) != 0 || bind_i(st, 22, sw->Pad_Infor[0].Vibration) != 0 ||
        bind_i(st, 23, sw->Pad_Infor[1].Vibration) != 0 ||
        bind_blob(st, 24, sw->Pad_Infor[0].Shot, 8) != 0 || bind_blob(st, 25, sw->Pad_Infor[1].Shot, 8) != 0 ||
        bind_blob(st, 26, sw->PL_Color[0], 20) != 0 || bind_blob(st, 27, sw->PL_Color[1], 20) != 0 ||
        bind_blob(st, 28, sw->extra_option.contents, 32) != 0 || sqlite3_step(st) != SQLITE_DONE) {
        sqlite3_finalize(st);
        rollback(db);
        return -1;
    }
    sqlite3_finalize(st);
    st = NULL;
    if (prepare(db, k_rank_ins, &st) != 0) {
        rollback(db);
        return -1;
    }
    for (i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
        const RANK_DATA *r = &sw->Ranking[i];
        sqlite3_reset(st);
        sqlite3_clear_bindings(st);
        if (bind_i(st, 1, i) != 0 || bind_blob(st, 2, r->name, 3) != 0 || bind_i(st, 3, r->player) != 0 ||
            bind_i(st, 4, r->score) != 0 || bind_i(st, 5, r->cpu_grade) != 0 || bind_i(st, 6, r->grade) != 0 ||
            bind_i(st, 7, r->wins) != 0 || bind_i(st, 8, r->player_color) != 0 || bind_i(st, 9, r->all_clear) != 0 ||
            sqlite3_step(st) != SQLITE_DONE) {
            sqlite3_finalize(st);
            rollback(db);
            return -1;
        }
    }
    sqlite3_finalize(st);
    rc = commit(db);
    if (rc != 0) {
        rollback(db);
    }
    return rc;
}

static int read_ranks(sqlite3 *db, struct _SAVE_W *sw)
{
    sqlite3_stmt *st = NULL;
    int n = 0;
    int rc;
    if (prepare(db, "SELECT idx, name, player, score, cpu_grade, grade, wins, player_color, all_clear "
                    "FROM rank ORDER BY idx",
                &st) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    for (;;) {
        long long idx;
        long long player;
        long long score;
        long long cpu;
        long long grade;
        long long wins;
        long long color;
        long long clear;
        RANK_DATA *r;
        rc = step_row(st);
        if (rc == 1) {
            break;
        }
        if (rc != 0 || n >= 20) {
            sqlite3_finalize(st);
            return -1;
        }
        if (col_i(st, 0, 0, 19, &idx) != 0 || idx != n || col_blob(st, 1, sw->Ranking[n].name, 3) != 0 ||
            col_i(st, 2, 0, 65535, &player) != 0 || col_i(st, 3, 0, 4294967295LL, &score) != 0 ||
            col_i(st, 4, -128, 127, &cpu) != 0 || col_i(st, 5, -128, 127, &grade) != 0 ||
            col_i(st, 6, 0, 65535, &wins) != 0 || col_i(st, 7, 0, 255, &color) != 0 ||
            col_i(st, 8, 0, 255, &clear) != 0) {
            sqlite3_finalize(st);
            return -1;
        }
        r = &sw->Ranking[n];
        r->player = (u16)player;
        r->score = (u32)score;
        r->cpu_grade = (s8)cpu;
        r->grade = (s8)grade;
        r->wins = (u16)wins;
        r->player_color = (u8)color;
        r->all_clear = (u8)clear;
        n++;
    }
    sqlite3_finalize(st);
    return n == 20 ? 0 : -1;
}

int sf_store_read_system(const char *path, struct _SAVE_W *sw)
{
    sqlite3 *db = NULL;
    sqlite3_stmt *st = NULL;
    int rc;
    long long v[22];
    int i;
    if (!path || !sw) {
        return -1;
    }
    rc = open_db(path, &db, 0);
    if (rc != 0) {
        return rc;
    }
    if (ensure(db, k_game_sql) != 0) {
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    if (prepare(db, "SELECT difficulty, time_limit, battle0, battle1, damage_level, handicap,"
                    "partner0, partner1, adjust_x, adjust_y, screen_size, screen_mode,"
                    "guard_check, auto_save, analog_stick, bgm_type, sound_mode,"
                    "bgm_level, se_level, extra_option, sum, pad0_vibration, pad1_vibration,"
                    "pad0_shot, pad1_shot, color0, color1, extra FROM system WHERE id=1",
                &st) != 0) {
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    rc = step_row(st);
    if (rc != 0) {
        sqlite3_finalize(st);
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return rc;
    }
    memset(sw, 0, sizeof *sw);
    for (i = 0; i < 22; i++) {
        long long min = 0;
        long long max = 255;
        if (i == 1 || i == 8 || i == 9) {
            min = -128;
            max = 127;
        } else if (i == 20) {
            min = 0;
            max = 4294967295LL;
        }
        if (col_i(st, i, min, max, &v[i]) != 0) {
            sqlite3_finalize(st);
            sqlite3_close(db);
            return -1;
        }
    }
    sw->Difficulty = (u8)v[0];
    sw->Time_Limit = (s8)v[1];
    sw->Battle_Number[0] = (u8)v[2];
    sw->Battle_Number[1] = (u8)v[3];
    sw->Damage_Level = (u8)v[4];
    sw->Handicap = (u8)v[5];
    sw->Partner_Type[0] = (u8)v[6];
    sw->Partner_Type[1] = (u8)v[7];
    sw->Adjust_X = (s8)v[8];
    sw->Adjust_Y = (s8)v[9];
    sw->Screen_Size = (u8)v[10];
    sw->Screen_Mode = (u8)v[11];
    sw->GuardCheck = (u8)v[12];
    sw->Auto_Save = (u8)v[13];
    sw->AnalogStick = (u8)v[14];
    sw->BgmType = (u8)v[15];
    sw->SoundMode = (u8)v[16];
    sw->BGM_Level = (u8)v[17];
    sw->SE_Level = (u8)v[18];
    sw->Extra_Option = (u8)v[19];
    sw->sum = (u32)v[20];
    sw->Pad_Infor[0].Vibration = (u8)v[21];
    if (col_i(st, 22, 0, 255, &v[0]) != 0) {
        sqlite3_finalize(st);
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    sw->Pad_Infor[1].Vibration = (u8)v[0];
    if (col_blob(st, 23, sw->Pad_Infor[0].Shot, 8) != 0 || col_blob(st, 24, sw->Pad_Infor[1].Shot, 8) != 0 ||
        col_blob(st, 25, sw->PL_Color[0], 20) != 0 || col_blob(st, 26, sw->PL_Color[1], 20) != 0 ||
        col_blob(st, 27, sw->extra_option.contents, 32) != 0) {
        sqlite3_finalize(st);
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    sqlite3_finalize(st);
    rc = read_ranks(db, sw);
    sqlite3_close(db);
    return rc;
}

int sf_store_write_system(const char *path, const struct _SAVE_W *sw)
{
    sqlite3 *db = NULL;
    int rc;
    if (!path || !sw) {
        return -1;
    }
    rc = open_db(path, &db, 1);
    if (rc != 0) {
        return rc;
    }
    if (ensure(db, k_game_sql) != 0) {
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    rc = write_system_tx(db, sw);
    sqlite3_close(db);
    if (rc != 0) {
        fprintf(stderr, "save: writing %s failed\n", path);
    }
    return rc;
}

int sf_store_read_directions(const char *path, SystemDir *dir)
{
    sqlite3 *db = NULL;
    sqlite3_stmt *st = NULL;
    long long sum;
    int rc;
    if (!path || !dir) {
        return -1;
    }
    rc = open_db(path, &db, 0);
    if (rc != 0) {
        return rc;
    }
    if (ensure(db, k_game_sql) != 0) {
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    if (prepare(db, "SELECT rows, sum FROM directions WHERE id=1", &st) != 0) {
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    rc = step_row(st);
    if (rc != 0) {
        sqlite3_finalize(st);
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return rc;
    }
    memset(dir, 0, sizeof *dir);
    if (col_blob(st, 0, dir->contents, 70) != 0 || col_i(st, 1, 0, 65535, &sum) != 0) {
        sqlite3_finalize(st);
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    dir->sum = (u16)sum;
    sqlite3_finalize(st);
    sqlite3_close(db);
    return 0;
}

int sf_store_write_directions(const char *path, const SystemDir *dir)
{
    sqlite3 *db = NULL;
    sqlite3_stmt *st = NULL;
    int rc;
    if (!path || !dir) {
        return -1;
    }
    rc = open_db(path, &db, 1);
    if (rc != 0) {
        return rc;
    }
    if (ensure(db, k_game_sql) != 0 || begin(db) != 0) {
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    if (prepare(db, "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO directions (id, rows, sum) VALUES (1, ?, ?)", &st) != 0 ||
        bind_blob(st, 1, dir->contents, 70) != 0 || bind_i(st, 2, dir->sum) != 0 || sqlite3_step(st) != SQLITE_DONE) {
        sqlite3_finalize(st);
        rollback(db);
        sqlite3_close(db);
        fprintf(stderr, "save: writing %s failed\n", path);
        return -1;
    }
    sqlite3_finalize(st);
    rc = commit(db);
    sqlite3_close(db);
    if (rc != 0) {
        fprintf(stderr, "save: writing %s failed\n", path);
    }
    return rc;
}

static int pack_inputs(const _REPLAY_W *w, u8 **out, int *n_out)
{
    int last;
    int i;
    int frames;
    u8 *buf;
    u16 a;
    u16 b;
    last = -1;
    for (i = INPUT_FRAMES - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        if (w->io_unit.key_buff[0][i] != 0 || w->io_unit.key_buff[1][i] != 0) {
            last = i;
            break;
        }
    }
    frames = last + 1;
    *n_out = frames * 4;
    *out = NULL;
    if (frames == 0) {
        return 0;
    }
    buf = malloc((size_t)frames * 4);
    if (!buf) {
        return -1;
    }
    for (i = 0; i < frames; i++) {
        a = w->io_unit.key_buff[0][i];
        b = w->io_unit.key_buff[1][i];
        buf[i * 4] = (u8)a;
        buf[i * 4 + 1] = (u8)(a >> 8);
        buf[i * 4 + 2] = (u8)b;
        buf[i * 4 + 3] = (u8)(b >> 8);
    }
    *out = buf;
    return 0;
}

static int take_inputs(sqlite3_stmt *st, int col, _REPLAY_W *w)
{
    int got;
    int frames;
    int i;
    const u8 *p;
    got = sqlite3_column_bytes(st, col);
    if (got < 0 || (got % 4) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    frames = got / 4;
    if (frames > INPUT_FRAMES) {
        return -1;
    }
    if (frames == 0) {
        return 0;
    }
    if (sqlite3_column_type(st, col) != SQLITE_BLOB) {
        return -1;
    }
    p = sqlite3_column_blob(st, col);
    if (!p) {
        return -1;
    }
    for (i = 0; i < frames; i++) {
        w->io_unit.key_buff[0][i] = (u16)(p[i * 4] | (p[i * 4 + 1] << 8));
        w->io_unit.key_buff[1][i] = (u16)(p[i * 4 + 2] | (p[i * 4 + 3] << 8));
    }
    return 0;
}

static const char k_replay_ins[] =
    "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO replay ("
    "slot, saved_at, control_time, difficulty, monitor_type, champion, full_data, sum,"
    "p0_char, p0_sa, p0_color, p0_type, p1_char, p1_sa, p1_color, p1_type,"
    "stage, direction_working, vital0, vital1,"
    "random_ix16, random_ix32, random_ix16_ex, random_ix32_ex,"
    "winner, play_type, players_timer, old_mes_no2, old_mes_no3, old_mes_no_pl, mes_already,"
    "mini_time, mini_battle0, mini_battle1, mini_damage, mini_pad0_vib, mini_pad1_vib,"
    "mini_pad0_shot, mini_pad1_shot, mini_extra, directions, directions_sum, inputs"
    ") VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)";

int sf_store_write_replay(const char *path, int slot, const _REPLAY_W *w)
{
    sqlite3 *db = NULL;
    sqlite3_stmt *st = NULL;
    u8 *inputs = NULL;
    int n_in = 0;
    int rc;
    const struct _REP_GAME_INFOR *g;
    const struct _MINI_SAVE_W *m;
    if (!path || !w || slot < 0 || slot >= REPLAY_SLOTS) {
        return -1;
    }
    if (pack_inputs(w, &inputs, &n_in) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    rc = open_db(path, &db, 1);
    if (rc != 0) {
        free(inputs);
        return rc;
    }
    g = &w->game_infor;
    m = &w->mini_save_w;
    if (ensure(db, k_game_sql) != 0 || begin(db) != 0 || prepare(db, k_replay_ins, &st) != 0) {
        free(inputs);
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    if (bind_i(st, 1, slot) != 0 || bind_i(st, 2, (long long)time(NULL)) != 0 ||
        bind_i(st, 3, w->Control_Time_Buff) != 0 || bind_i(st, 4, w->Difficulty) != 0 ||
        bind_i(st, 5, w->Monitor_Type) != 0 || bind_i(st, 6, w->champion) != 0 || bind_i(st, 7, w->full_data) != 0 ||
        bind_i(st, 8, w->sum) != 0 || bind_i(st, 9, g->player_infor[0].my_char) != 0 ||
        bind_i(st, 10, g->player_infor[0].sa) != 0 || bind_i(st, 11, g->player_infor[0].color) != 0 ||
        bind_i(st, 12, g->player_infor[0].player_type) != 0 || bind_i(st, 13, g->player_infor[1].my_char) != 0 ||
        bind_i(st, 14, g->player_infor[1].sa) != 0 || bind_i(st, 15, g->player_infor[1].color) != 0 ||
        bind_i(st, 16, g->player_infor[1].player_type) != 0 || bind_i(st, 17, g->stage) != 0 ||
        bind_i(st, 18, g->Direction_Working) != 0 || bind_i(st, 19, g->Vital_Handicap[0]) != 0 ||
        bind_i(st, 20, g->Vital_Handicap[1]) != 0 || bind_i(st, 21, g->Random_ix16) != 0 ||
        bind_i(st, 22, g->Random_ix32) != 0 || bind_i(st, 23, g->Random_ix16_ex) != 0 ||
        bind_i(st, 24, g->Random_ix32_ex) != 0 || bind_i(st, 25, g->winner) != 0 || bind_i(st, 26, g->play_type) != 0 ||
        bind_i(st, 27, g->players_timer) != 0 || bind_i(st, 28, g->old_mes_no2) != 0 ||
        bind_i(st, 29, g->old_mes_no3) != 0 || bind_i(st, 30, g->old_mes_no_pl) != 0 ||
        bind_i(st, 31, g->mes_already) != 0 || bind_i(st, 32, m->Time_Limit) != 0 ||
        bind_i(st, 33, m->Battle_Number[0]) != 0 || bind_i(st, 34, m->Battle_Number[1]) != 0 ||
        bind_i(st, 35, m->Damage_Level) != 0 || bind_i(st, 36, m->Pad_Infor[0].Vibration) != 0 ||
        bind_i(st, 37, m->Pad_Infor[1].Vibration) != 0 || bind_blob(st, 38, m->Pad_Infor[0].Shot, 8) != 0 ||
        bind_blob(st, 39, m->Pad_Infor[1].Shot, 8) != 0 || bind_blob(st, 40, m->extra_option.contents, 32) != 0 ||
        bind_blob(st, 41, w->system_dir.contents, 70) != 0 || bind_i(st, 42, w->system_dir.sum) != 0 ||
        bind_blob(st, 43, inputs, n_in) != 0 || sqlite3_step(st) != SQLITE_DONE) {
        sqlite3_finalize(st);
        rollback(db);
        sqlite3_close(db);
        free(inputs);
        fprintf(stderr, "save: writing %s failed\n", path);
        return -1;
    }
    sqlite3_finalize(st);
    free(inputs);
    rc = commit(db);
    sqlite3_close(db);
    if (rc != 0) {
        fprintf(stderr, "save: writing %s failed\n", path);
    }
    return rc;
}

int sf_store_read_replay(const char *path, int slot, _REPLAY_W *w, long long *saved_at)
{
    sqlite3 *db = NULL;
    sqlite3_stmt *st = NULL;
    int rc;
    int i;
    long long v;
    struct _REP_GAME_INFOR *g;
    struct _MINI_SAVE_W *m;
    /* Columns after saved_at: control_time through mes_already. */
    static const long long k_min[29] = { -32768, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, -128, -128, -128, 0, -128, -128, -128, -128, -128,
                                         -128, -128, -32768, -32768, -32768, -32768, 0, 0, 0, -32768, -32768, -32768,
                                         -32768 };
    static const long long k_max[29] = { 32767, 255, 255, 255, 255, 4294967295LL, 255, 127, 127, 127, 255, 127, 127,
                                         127, 127, 127, 127, 127, 32767, 32767, 32767, 32767, 255, 255, 65535, 32767,
                                         32767, 32767, 32767 };
    if (!path || !w || slot < 0 || slot >= REPLAY_SLOTS) {
        return -1;
    }
    rc = open_db(path, &db, 0);
    if (rc != 0) {
        return rc;
    }
    if (ensure(db, k_game_sql) != 0) {
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    if (prepare(db, "SELECT saved_at, control_time, difficulty, monitor_type, champion, full_data, sum,"
                    "p0_char, p0_sa, p0_color, p0_type, p1_char, p1_sa, p1_color, p1_type,"
                    "stage, direction_working, vital0, vital1,"
                    "random_ix16, random_ix32, random_ix16_ex, random_ix32_ex,"
                    "winner, play_type, players_timer, old_mes_no2, old_mes_no3, old_mes_no_pl, mes_already,"
                    "mini_time, mini_battle0, mini_battle1, mini_damage, mini_pad0_vib, mini_pad1_vib,"
                    "mini_pad0_shot, mini_pad1_shot, mini_extra, directions, directions_sum, inputs "
                    "FROM replay WHERE slot=?",
                &st) != 0 || bind_i(st, 1, slot) != 0) {
        sqlite3_finalize(st);
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    rc = step_row(st);
    if (rc != 0) {
        sqlite3_finalize(st);
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return rc;
    }
    memset(w, 0, sizeof *w);
    w->game_infor.fname = NULL;
    if (col_i(st, 0, 0, 4294967295LL, &v) != 0) {
        sqlite3_finalize(st);
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    if (saved_at) {
        *saved_at = v;
    }
    /* columns 1..29 are the integers between saved_at and the blobs */
    g = &w->game_infor;
    m = &w->mini_save_w;
    for (i = 0; i < 29; i++) {
        if (col_i(st, i + 1, k_min[i], k_max[i], &v) != 0) {
            sqlite3_finalize(st);
            sqlite3_close(db);
            return -1;
        }
        switch (i) {
        case 0:
            w->Control_Time_Buff = (s16)v;
            break;
        case 1:
            w->Difficulty = (u8)v;
            break;
        case 2:
            w->Monitor_Type = (u8)v;
            break;
        case 3:
            w->champion = (u8)v;
            break;
        case 4:
            w->full_data = (u8)v;
            break;
        case 5:
            w->sum = (u32)v;
            break;
        case 6:
            g->player_infor[0].my_char = (u8)v;
            break;
        case 7:
            g->player_infor[0].sa = (s8)v;
            break;
        case 8:
            g->player_infor[0].color = (s8)v;
            break;
        case 9:
            g->player_infor[0].player_type = (s8)v;
            break;
        case 10:
            g->player_infor[1].my_char = (u8)v;
            break;
        case 11:
            g->player_infor[1].sa = (s8)v;
            break;
        case 12:
            g->player_infor[1].color = (s8)v;
            break;
        case 13:
            g->player_infor[1].player_type = (s8)v;
            break;
        case 14:
            g->stage = (s8)v;
            break;
        case 15:
            g->Direction_Working = (s8)v;
            break;
        case 16:
            g->Vital_Handicap[0] = (s8)v;
            break;
        case 17:
            g->Vital_Handicap[1] = (s8)v;
            break;
        case 18:
            g->Random_ix16 = (s16)v;
            break;
        case 19:
            g->Random_ix32 = (s16)v;
            break;
        case 20:
            g->Random_ix16_ex = (s16)v;
            break;
        case 21:
            g->Random_ix32_ex = (s16)v;
            break;
        case 22:
            g->winner = (u8)v;
            break;
        case 23:
            g->play_type = (u8)v;
            break;
        case 24:
            g->players_timer = (u16)v;
            break;
        case 25:
            g->old_mes_no2 = (s16)v;
            break;
        case 26:
            g->old_mes_no3 = (s16)v;
            break;
        case 27:
            g->old_mes_no_pl = (s16)v;
            break;
        default:
            g->mes_already = (s16)v;
            break;
        }
    }
    if (col_i(st, 30, -128, 127, &v) != 0) {
        sqlite3_finalize(st);
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    m->Time_Limit = (s8)v;
    if (col_i(st, 31, 0, 255, &v) != 0) {
        sqlite3_finalize(st);
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    m->Battle_Number[0] = (u8)v;
    if (col_i(st, 32, 0, 255, &v) != 0) {
        sqlite3_finalize(st);
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    m->Battle_Number[1] = (u8)v;
    if (col_i(st, 33, 0, 255, &v) != 0) {
        sqlite3_finalize(st);
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    m->Damage_Level = (u8)v;
    if (col_i(st, 34, 0, 255, &v) != 0) {
        sqlite3_finalize(st);
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    m->Pad_Infor[0].Vibration = (u8)v;
    if (col_i(st, 35, 0, 255, &v) != 0) {
        sqlite3_finalize(st);
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    m->Pad_Infor[1].Vibration = (u8)v;
    if (col_blob(st, 36, m->Pad_Infor[0].Shot, 8) != 0 || col_blob(st, 37, m->Pad_Infor[1].Shot, 8) != 0 ||
        col_blob(st, 38, m->extra_option.contents, 32) != 0 || col_blob(st, 39, w->system_dir.contents, 70) != 0 ||
        col_i(st, 40, 0, 65535, &v) != 0 || take_inputs(st, 41, w) != 0) {
        sqlite3_finalize(st);
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    w->system_dir.sum = (u16)v;
    sqlite3_finalize(st);
    sqlite3_close(db);
    return 0;
}

int sf_store_read_window(const char *path, char *mode, unsigned mode_n, int *width, int *height, char *aspect,
                         unsigned aspect_n)
{
    sqlite3 *db = NULL;
    sqlite3_stmt *st = NULL;
    const char *ms;
    const char *as;
    long long w;
    long long h;
    int rc;
    unsigned n;
    if (!path || !mode || mode_n < 2 || !width || !height || !aspect || aspect_n < 2) {
        return -1;
    }
    rc = open_db(path, &db, 0);
    if (rc != 0) {
        return rc;
    }
    if (ensure(db, k_host_sql) != 0) {
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    if (prepare(db, "SELECT mode, width, height, aspect FROM window WHERE id=1", &st) != 0) {
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    rc = step_row(st);
    if (rc != 0) {
        sqlite3_finalize(st);
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return rc;
    }
    ms = (const char *)sqlite3_column_text(st, 0);
    as = (const char *)sqlite3_column_text(st, 3);
    if (!ms || !as || col_i(st, 1, 320, 100000, &w) != 0 || col_i(st, 2, 240, 100000, &h) != 0) {
        sqlite3_finalize(st);
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    n = (unsigned)strlen(ms);
    if (n == 0 || n >= mode_n || strlen(as) == 0 || strlen(as) >= aspect_n) {
        sqlite3_finalize(st);
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    memcpy(mode, ms, n + 1);
    memcpy(aspect, as, strlen(as) + 1);
    *width = (int)w;
    *height = (int)h;
    sqlite3_finalize(st);
    sqlite3_close(db);
    return 0;
}

int sf_store_write_window(const char *path, const char *mode, int width, int height, const char *aspect)
{
    sqlite3 *db = NULL;
    sqlite3_stmt *st = NULL;
    int rc;
    if (!path || !mode || !mode[0] || !aspect || !aspect[0] || width < 320 || width > 100000 || height < 240 ||
        height > 100000) {
        return -1;
    }
    rc = open_db(path, &db, 1);
    if (rc != 0) {
        return rc;
    }
    if (ensure(db, k_host_sql) != 0 || begin(db) != 0 ||
        prepare(db, "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO window (id, mode, width, height, aspect) VALUES (1, ?, ?, ?, ?)", &st) !=
            0 ||
        sqlite3_bind_text(st, 1, mode, -1, SQLITE_TRANSIENT) != SQLITE_OK || bind_i(st, 2, width) != 0 ||
        bind_i(st, 3, height) != 0 || sqlite3_bind_text(st, 4, aspect, -1, SQLITE_TRANSIENT) != SQLITE_OK ||
        sqlite3_step(st) != SQLITE_DONE) {
        sqlite3_finalize(st);
        rollback(db);
        sqlite3_close(db);
        fprintf(stderr, "save: writing %s failed\n", path);
        return -1;
    }
    sqlite3_finalize(st);
    rc = commit(db);
    sqlite3_close(db);
    return rc;
}

int sf_store_read_keys(const char *path, const char *const *names, char values[][64], int n)
{
    sqlite3 *db = NULL;
    sqlite3_stmt *count = NULL;
    sqlite3_stmt *st = NULL;
    long long rows = 0;
    const char *text;
    int i;
    int rc;
    unsigned nlen;
    if (!path || !names || !values || n < 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    rc = open_db(path, &db, 0);
    if (rc != 0) {
        return rc;
    }
    if (ensure(db, k_host_sql) != 0 || prepare(db, "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM key", &count) != 0) {
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    if (step_row(count) != 0 || col_i(count, 0, 0, 100000, &rows) != 0) {
        sqlite3_finalize(count);
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    sqlite3_finalize(count);
    if (rows == 0) {
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return 1;
    }
    for (i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        values[i][0] = 0;
    }
    if (prepare(db, "SELECT scancode FROM key WHERE name=?", &st) != 0) {
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    for (i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        sqlite3_reset(st);
        sqlite3_clear_bindings(st);
        if (!names[i] || sqlite3_bind_text(st, 1, names[i], -1, SQLITE_TRANSIENT) != SQLITE_OK) {
            sqlite3_finalize(st);
            sqlite3_close(db);
            return -1;
        }
        rc = step_row(st);
        if (rc == 1) {
            continue;
        }
        if (rc != 0) {
            sqlite3_finalize(st);
            sqlite3_close(db);
            return -1;
        }
        text = (const char *)sqlite3_column_text(st, 0);
        if (!text) {
            sqlite3_finalize(st);
            sqlite3_close(db);
            return -1;
        }
        nlen = (unsigned)strlen(text);
        if (nlen >= 64) {
            sqlite3_finalize(st);
            sqlite3_close(db);
            return -1;
        }
        memcpy(values[i], text, nlen + 1);
    }
    sqlite3_finalize(st);
    sqlite3_close(db);
    return 0;
}

int sf_store_write_keys(const char *path, const char *const *names, const char *const *values, int n)
{
    sqlite3 *db = NULL;
    sqlite3_stmt *st = NULL;
    int i;
    int rc;
    if (!path || !names || !values || n < 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    rc = open_db(path, &db, 1);
    if (rc != 0) {
        return rc;
    }
    if (ensure(db, k_host_sql) != 0 || begin(db) != 0 || exec_sql(db, "DELETE FROM key") != 0 ||
        prepare(db, "INSERT INTO key (name, scancode) VALUES (?, ?)", &st) != 0) {
        rollback(db);
        sqlite3_close(db);
        fprintf(stderr, "save: writing %s failed\n", path);
        return -1;
    }
    for (i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        sqlite3_reset(st);
        sqlite3_clear_bindings(st);
        if (!names[i] || !values[i] || sqlite3_bind_text(st, 1, names[i], -1, SQLITE_TRANSIENT) != SQLITE_OK ||
            sqlite3_bind_text(st, 2, values[i], -1, SQLITE_TRANSIENT) != SQLITE_OK || sqlite3_step(st) != SQLITE_DONE) {
            sqlite3_finalize(st);
            rollback(db);
            sqlite3_close(db);
            fprintf(stderr, "save: writing %s failed\n", path);
            return -1;
        }
    }
    sqlite3_finalize(st);
    rc = commit(db);
    sqlite3_close(db);
    if (rc != 0) {
        fprintf(stderr, "save: writing %s failed\n", path);
    }
    return rc;
}

```

`src/port/store.h`:

```h
#ifndef SF_PORT_STORE_H
#define SF_PORT_STORE_H

/* SQLite saves. The game keeps its structs; these files are only storage.
 * data/save/game.sqlite holds the system row, the directions, and ten
 * replay slots. config/host.sqlite holds the window and the keyboard.
 * Schema version is 1. A write is one transaction, with the journal
 * synced. A file that is not a database is left as it is.
 *
 * The memory-card header, the network ids, and the old .sav are not
 * stored. A read of those fields is zero, and a replay's fname is NULL.
 *
 * 0 the row was read, 1 there is no row, -1 the file is damaged or the
 * write failed. Screen_Mode is stored as it is. */

#include "structs.h"

int sf_store_read_system(const char *path, struct _SAVE_W *sw);
int sf_store_write_system(const char *path, const struct _SAVE_W *sw);
int sf_store_read_directions(const char *path, SystemDir *dir);
int sf_store_write_directions(const char *path, const SystemDir *dir);

/* slot is 0 .. 9. saved_at may be NULL. It is the unix time of the write. */
int sf_store_read_replay(const char *path, int slot, _REPLAY_W *w, long long *saved_at);
int sf_store_write_replay(const char *path, int slot, const _REPLAY_W *w);

/* mode and aspect are the short names ("windowed", "4:3"). */
int sf_store_read_window(const char *path, char *mode, unsigned mode_n, int *width, int *height, char *aspect,
                         unsigned aspect_n);
int sf_store_write_window(const char *path, const char *mode, int width, int height, const char *aspect);

/* values[i] is the scancode name, or empty when that name has no row.
 * 1 means the key table has no rows at all. */
int sf_store_read_keys(const char *path, const char *const *names, char values[][64], int n);
int sf_store_write_keys(const char *path, const char *const *names, const char *const *values, int n);

#endif

```

`src/port/system.c`:

```c
/* The game's start-up and its frame of game logic, on the native platform.
 *
 * Retail starts the game in its platform layer: a work pool it carves the
 * game's buffers from, the subsystems' initialisation in a fixed order,
 * the region, the first task. This file keeps that order and those sizes,
 * so the game's heaps are laid out as retail lays them out. What was the
 * Xbox's own (its print layer's font, the Xbox Live work, the pad-removed
 * screen, the region from the console's settings) is left out or comes
 * from the port. The reference is 3s-decomp-ogxb, Source/XBOX/xb_0C8301.c
 * (njUserInit, njUserMain), xb_0C7E80.c (cpLoopTask, the region) and
 * xb_011000.c (the pool). */

#include "game/memman.h"
#include "port/gfx.h"
#include "port/port.h"

#include "types.h"
#include "structs.h"

#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

/* Globals the game logic uses whose storage was in retail's platform
 * files, with the types the game declares them with. */
MPP mpp_w SF_GAME;
PLW plw[2] SF_GAME;
s32 system_init_level SF_GAME;
MemMan rckey_mmobj SF_GAME; /* RAMCNT's heap, on the native memory manager */

/* Types as the defining files have them. */
extern struct _TASK task[11];
extern struct _SYSTEM_W sys_w;
extern u8 CPU_Time_Lag[2];
extern u8 CPU_Rec[2];
extern u8 Replay_Status[2];
extern f32 Frame_Zoom_X;
extern f32 Frame_Zoom_Y;
extern f32 Screen_Zoom_X;
extern f32 Screen_Zoom_Y;
extern f32 SA_Zoom_X;
extern f32 SA_Zoom_Y;
extern u8 Disp_Size_H;
extern u8 Disp_Size_V;
extern u8 Country;
extern u8 Turbo;
extern u8 Turbo_Timer;
extern u8 Screen_PAL;
extern u8 Interface_Type[2];
extern u8 Process_Counter;
extern u8 Game_pause;
extern u8 Mode_Type;
extern u8 Play_Mode;
extern u32 Interrupt_Timer;
extern s16 Correct_X[4];
extern s16 Correct_Y[4];
extern u16 p1sw_0, p1sw_1, p2sw_0, p2sw_1;

void mmSystemInitialize(void);
u32 seqsGetUseMemorySize(void);
void seqsInitialize(void *adrs);
void ppg_Initialize(void *lcmAdrs, s32 lcmSize);
void zlib_Initialize(void *tempAdrs, s32 tempSize);
void Init_ram_control_work(u8 *adrs, s32 size);
void appSetupBasePriority(void);
void appSetupTempPriority(void);
void initRenderState(s32 flag);
void appViewSetItems(VPRM *prm);
void appViewMatrix(void);
void setBackGroundColor(u32 color);
void xb_SetViewportRect(s32 x, s32 y, s32 w, s32 h);
void Setup_Disp_Size(s16 flag);
void Init_sound_system(void);
void Init_bgm_work(void);
s32 Setup_Directory_Record_Data(void);
void sndInitialLoad(void);
void cpReadyTask(u16 num, void (*func_adrs)(void));
void Init_Task(struct _TASK *task_ptr);
void Check_Replay_Status(s16 PL_id, u8 Status);

/* ---- the work pool (retail's xb_PoolInit and xb_PoolAllocAligned) ----
 *
 * 14 MB, aligned to 0x40, taken from the front (or the back for "top"). */

#define SF_POOL_SIZE 0xE00000u
#define SF_POOL_ALIGN 0x40u

/* the work pool's ends move as the game takes and gives back work: game state */
static uint8_t *s_pool_base SF_GAME;
static uintptr_t s_pool_front SF_GAME;
static uintptr_t s_pool_back SF_GAME;

static void pool_init(void)
{
    s_pool_base = sf_arena_alloc(SF_POOL_SIZE, SF_POOL_ALIGN);
    memset(s_pool_base, 0, SF_POOL_SIZE);
    s_pool_front = (uintptr_t)s_pool_base;
    s_pool_back = (uintptr_t)s_pool_base + SF_POOL_SIZE;
}

static void *pool_alloc(u32 size, int top)
{
    uintptr_t at;
    size = (size + SF_POOL_ALIGN - 1) & ~(SF_POOL_ALIGN - 1);
    if (s_pool_front + size > s_pool_back) {
        fprintf(stderr, "port: work pool full (0x%X bytes asked)\n", size);
        return NULL;
    }
    if (top) {
        s_pool_back -= size;
        return (void *)s_pool_back;
    }
    at = s_pool_front;
    s_pool_front += size;
    return (void *)at;
}

/* what is left between the two ends (retail calls it the used size) */
static u32 pool_left(void)
{
    return (u32)(s_pool_back - s_pool_front);
}

/* ---- start-up ---- */

/* The region: retail reads the console's region and language. 1 and 2
 * are the Japanese and Asian builds, 3 Europe, 4 North America (the
 * default); SF_COUNTRY picks another. */
static void setup_region(void)
{
    const char *env = getenv("SF_COUNTRY");
    Turbo = 0;
    Screen_PAL = 0;
    Country = 4;
    if (env && env[0] >= '1' && env[0] <= '4' && env[1] == 0) {
        Country = (u8)(env[0] - '0');
    }
}

/* retail's xb_ResetScreenState, without the three black frames it presents
 * (the window starts black) */
static void reset_screen_state(void)
{
    appSetupBasePriority();
    appSetupTempPriority();
    initRenderState(0);
    mpp_w.vprm.x0 = 0.0f;
    mpp_w.vprm.y0 = 0.0f;
    mpp_w.vprm.x1 = 640.0f;
    mpp_w.vprm.y1 = 448.0f;
    mpp_w.vprm.ne = -1.0f;
    mpp_w.vprm.fa = 1.0f;
    appViewSetItems(&mpp_w.vprm);
    appViewMatrix();
    sf_video_set_offset(0, 0);
    setBackGroundColor(0xFF000000);
    xb_SetViewportRect(0, 0, 640, 448);
}

void sf_input_reset(void);
extern IO io_w;
extern u16 p3sw_0, p3sw_1, p4sw_0, p4sw_1;

/* retail's xb_ResetInputState (xb_0611EA.c): no port has joined and no
 * switch is held. Start-up runs it, and the Capcom logo at the start of
 * each attract loop. */
void xb_ResetInputState(void)
{
    sf_input_reset();
    memset(&io_w, 0, sizeof io_w);
    memset(Interface_Type, 0, sizeof Interface_Type);
    p1sw_0 = 0;
    p1sw_1 = 0;
    p2sw_0 = 0;
    p2sw_1 = 0;
    p3sw_0 = 0;
    p3sw_1 = 0;
    p4sw_0 = 0;
    p4sw_1 = 0;
}

void njUserInit(void)
{
    u32 size;
    int i;

    mpp_w.vprm.x0 = 0.0f;
    mpp_w.vprm.y0 = 0.0f;
    mpp_w.vprm.x1 = 384.0f;
    mpp_w.vprm.y1 = 224.0f;
    mpp_w.vprm.ne = -1.0f;
    mpp_w.sysStop = 0;
    mpp_w.inGame = 0;
    mpp_w.ctrDemo = 0;
    mpp_w.language = 0;
    mpp_w.langload = -1;
    mpp_w.xb_unk35 = 0;
    mpp_w.vprm.fa = 1.0f;
    appViewSetItems(&mpp_w.vprm);
    appViewMatrix();
    mmSystemInitialize();

    pool_init();
    mpp_w.fmsFrame.pFrame = (u8 *)s_pool_front;
    mpp_w.fmsFrame.heapnum = 0;
    seqsInitialize(pool_alloc(seqsGetUseMemorySize(), 0));
    ppg_Initialize(pool_alloc(0x60000, 0), 0x60000);
    zlib_Initialize(pool_alloc(0x10000, 0), 0x10000);
    /* retail's print layer buffer and its Live text buffer, each 0xD400;
     * taken here too so the game's heap is the size it is on retail */
    pool_alloc(0xD400, 0);
    pool_alloc(0xD400, 0);
    size = pool_left();
    mpp_w.ramcntBuff = pool_alloc(size, 0);
    Init_ram_control_work(mpp_w.ramcntBuff, (s32)size);
    reset_screen_state();

    for (i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
        mpp_w.useChar[i] = 0;
    }

    Interrupt_Timer = 0;
    SA_Zoom_X = 0.0f;
    SA_Zoom_Y = 0.0f;
    Disp_Size_H = 100;
    Disp_Size_V = 100;
    mpp_w.ds_h[1] = 100;
    mpp_w.ds_h[0] = 100;
    mpp_w.ds_v[1] = 100;
    mpp_w.ds_v[0] = 100;
    Country = 0;
    setup_region();
    Turbo_Timer = 1;
    Screen_Zoom_X = 1.0f;
    Screen_Zoom_Y = 1.0f;
    Setup_Disp_Size(0);
    Frame_Zoom_X = Screen_Zoom_X + SA_Zoom_X;
    Correct_X[0] = 0;
    Correct_Y[0] = 0;
    Frame_Zoom_Y = Screen_Zoom_Y + SA_Zoom_Y;
    sys_w.disp.new = 1;
    sys_w.disp.now = 1;
    sys_w.pause = 0;
    sys_w.reset = 0;
    xb_ResetInputState();
    Init_sound_system();
    Init_bgm_work();
    Setup_Directory_Record_Data();
    sndInitialLoad();
    memset(task, 0, sizeof task);
    cpReadyTask(0, (void (*)(void))Init_Task);
}

/* ---- a frame of game logic ---- */

/* The task loop. Retail walks 12 slots of 0x14 bytes, one past the 11 its
 * table holds (the slot after it is never armed); this walks the 11. */
void cpLoopTask(void)
{
    u32 i;

    Process_Counter = 1;
    if (Turbo && Game_pause != 0x81 && --Turbo_Timer == 0) {
        Turbo_Timer = Turbo;
        Process_Counter = 2;
    }
    for (i = 0; i < sizeof task / sizeof task[0]; i++) {
        switch (task[i].condition) {
        case 1:
            ((void (*)(struct _TASK *))task[i].func_adrs)(&task[i]);
            break;
        case 2:
            task[i].condition = 1;
            break;
        }
    }
}

s32 njUserMain(void)
{
    CPU_Time_Lag[0] = 0;
    CPU_Time_Lag[1] = 0;
    CPU_Rec[0] = 0;
    CPU_Rec[1] = 0;

    Check_Replay_Status(0, Replay_Status[0]);
    Check_Replay_Status(1, Replay_Status[1]);

    Frame_Zoom_X = Screen_Zoom_X + SA_Zoom_X;
    Frame_Zoom_Y = Screen_Zoom_Y + SA_Zoom_Y;

    if (sys_w.disp.now == sys_w.disp.new) {
        cpLoopTask();
        if (Game_pause != 0x81 && Mode_Type == 1 && Play_Mode == 1) {
            if (plw[0].wu.operator == 0 && CPU_Rec[0] == 0 && Replay_Status[0] == 1) {
                p1sw_0 = 0;
                Check_Replay_Status(0, 1);
            }
            if (plw[1].wu.operator == 0 && CPU_Rec[1] == 0 && Replay_Status[1] == 1) {
                p2sw_0 = 0;
                Check_Replay_Status(1, 1);
            }
        }
    } else {
        sys_w.disp.now = sys_w.disp.new;
    }
    return sys_w.gd_error;
}

/* ---- the PS2-sized picture (retail's CP3toPS2Draw, xb_0F4537.c) ---- */

static int s_cp3_on SF_GAME;

extern struct _SAVE_W save_w[6];
extern u8 Disp_Size_H;
extern u8 Disp_Size_V;
void dspwhUnpack(u8 src, u8 *xdsp, u8 *ydsp);

void CP3toPS2DrawOn(void)
{
    s_cp3_on = 1;
}

void CP3toPS2DrawOff(void)
{
    s_cp3_on = 0;
}

s32 getCP3toFullScreenDrawFlag(void)
{
    return s_cp3_on;
}

/* With the copy on and the game not stopped: the screen's corner the
 * screen-size option leaves (383x223 at 100%) goes onto the output, and
 * what is drawn after it is blended over from the 638x447 area. */
void CP3toPS2Draw(void)
{
    if (mpp_w.sysStop == 2 || !s_cp3_on) {
        return;
    }
    dspwhUnpack(save_w[1].Screen_Size, &Disp_Size_H, &Disp_Size_V);
    sf_gfx_copy_screen(0, 0, 0x247 - Disp_Size_H * 2, 0x1A7 - Disp_Size_V * 2);
    xb_SetViewportRect(0, 0, 0x27E - (s32)((100.0f - Disp_Size_H) * -3.3333333f), 0x34F - Disp_Size_V * 4);
}
```

`src/port/video.c`:

```c
/* The window the game draws in: SDL3, the Vulkan renderer, and one
 * presented frame per game frame. */

#include "port/gfx.h"
#include "port/port.h"
#include "render/renderer.h"

#include <SDL3/SDL.h>

#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

/* a phone's window is the whole screen, past the status and navigation
 * bars (Android's immersive mode) */
#ifdef __ANDROID__
#define SF_PHONE_FULLSCREEN SDL_WINDOW_FULLSCREEN
#else
#define SF_PHONE_FULLSCREEN 0
#endif

static SDL_Window *s_window;
static SfRenderer *s_renderer;

/* ---- the render thread ----
 *
 * The game thread builds a frame's draw list; the render thread draws and
 * presents it (and waits on the display's refresh) while the game thread
 * runs the next frame. At the hand-off the game thread waits for the
 * render thread to finish the frame before, then copies the new frame
 * into the render thread's own list: the vertices and batches, and the
 * textures and palettes whose version changed. Every other use of the
 * renderer (the aspect, the first start's screen, a test's screenshot) is
 * run on the render thread between frames, so the renderer is only ever
 * used from one thread, as OpenGL's context wants. SF_RENDER_THREAD=0
 * draws on the game thread, as does a machine with one core. */

typedef struct RtCall {
    void (*fn)(void *);
    void *arg;
} RtCall;

static struct {
    SDL_Thread *thread;
    SDL_Mutex *lock;
    SDL_Condition *cv;
    int frame; /* list holds a frame to draw */
    RtCall call;
    int quit;
    SfDrawList list;
    int filter;
    char err[512];
    int failed;
} s_rt;

static int rt_main(void *unused)
{
    (void)unused;
    sf_render_make_current(s_renderer, 1);
    SDL_LockMutex(s_rt.lock);
    for (;;) {
        while (!s_rt.frame && !s_rt.call.fn && !s_rt.quit) {
            SDL_WaitCondition(s_rt.cv, s_rt.lock);
        }
        if (s_rt.call.fn) {
            RtCall c = s_rt.call;
            SDL_UnlockMutex(s_rt.lock);
            c.fn(c.arg);
            SDL_LockMutex(s_rt.lock);
            s_rt.call.fn = NULL;
        } else if (s_rt.frame) {
            SDL_UnlockMutex(s_rt.lock);
            sf_render_set_filter(s_renderer, s_rt.filter);
            if (sf_render_present_list(s_renderer, &s_rt.list, s_rt.err, sizeof s_rt.err) != 0) {
                s_rt.failed = 1;
            }
            SDL_LockMutex(s_rt.lock);
            s_rt.frame = 0;
        } else {
            break;
        }
        SDL_BroadcastCondition(s_rt.cv);
    }
    SDL_UnlockMutex(s_rt.lock);
    sf_render_make_current(s_renderer, 0);
    return 0;
}

/* Until the render thread has nothing to do. */
static void rt_idle(void)
{
    SDL_LockMutex(s_rt.lock);
    while (s_rt.frame || s_rt.call.fn) {
        SDL_WaitCondition(s_rt.cv, s_rt.lock);
    }
    SDL_UnlockMutex(s_rt.lock);
}

/* fn(arg) on the thread that owns the renderer, once it is idle. */
static void rt_call(void (*fn)(void *), void *arg)
{
    if (!s_rt.thread) {
        fn(arg);
        return;
    }
    SDL_LockMutex(s_rt.lock);
    while (s_rt.frame || s_rt.call.fn) {
        SDL_WaitCondition(s_rt.cv, s_rt.lock);
    }
    s_rt.call.fn = fn;
    s_rt.call.arg = arg;
    SDL_BroadcastCondition(s_rt.cv);
    while (s_rt.call.fn) {
        SDL_WaitCondition(s_rt.cv, s_rt.lock);
    }
    SDL_UnlockMutex(s_rt.lock);
}

static void rt_start(void)
{
    const char *env = getenv("SF_RENDER_THREAD");
    if (s_rt.thread || (env && strcmp(env, "0") == 0) || SDL_GetNumLogicalCPUCores() < 2) {
        return;
    }
    s_rt.lock = SDL_CreateMutex();
    s_rt.cv = SDL_CreateCondition();
    sf_render_make_current(s_renderer, 0);
    s_rt.thread = SDL_CreateThread(rt_main, "sf3 render", NULL);
    if (!s_rt.thread) {
        sf_render_make_current(s_renderer, 1);
        fprintf(stderr, "video: no render thread: %s\n", SDL_GetError());
    }
}

static void rt_stop(void)
{
    int i;
    if (!s_rt.thread) {
        return;
    }
    rt_idle();
    SDL_LockMutex(s_rt.lock);
    s_rt.quit = 1;
    SDL_BroadcastCondition(s_rt.cv);
    SDL_UnlockMutex(s_rt.lock);
    SDL_WaitThread(s_rt.thread, NULL);
    s_rt.thread = NULL;
    sf_render_make_current(s_renderer, 1);
    for (i = 0; i < SF_TEX_MAX; i++) {
        free(s_rt.list.tex[i].rgba);
    }
    free(s_rt.list.v);
    free(s_rt.list.b);
    memset(&s_rt.list, 0, sizeof s_rt.list);
}

/* The game's frame into the render thread's list (it is idle). */
static void rt_copy(const SfDrawList *l)
{
    SfDrawList *o = &s_rt.list;
    int i;
    for (i = 0; i < SF_TEX_MAX; i++) {
        const SfTexture *t = &l->tex[i];
        SfTexture *d = &o->tex[i];
        if (d->version == t->version && d->w == t->w && d->h == t->h && d->indexed == t->indexed &&
            !d->rgba == !t->rgba) {
            continue;
        }
        if (!t->rgba) {
            free(d->rgba);
            d->rgba = NULL;
        } else {
            size_t n = (size_t)t->w * (size_t)t->h * 4;
            if (!d->rgba || d->w != t->w || d->h != t->h) {
                free(d->rgba);
                d->rgba = malloc(n);
                if (!d->rgba) {
                    sf_fatal("video: no memory for a %dx%d texture", t->w, t->h);
                }
            }
            memcpy(d->rgba, t->rgba, n);
        }
        d->w = t->w;
        d->h = t->h;
        d->indexed = t->indexed;
        d->version = t->version;
    }
    for (i = 0; i < SF_PAL_MAX; i++) {
        if (o->pal[i].version != l->pal[i].version) {
            o->pal[i] = l->pal[i];
        }
    }
    if (o->v_cap < l->v_n) {
        o->v_cap = l->v_cap;
        o->v = realloc(o->v, (size_t)o->v_cap * sizeof *o->v);
    }
    if (o->b_cap < l->b_n) {
        o->b_cap = l->b_cap;
        o->b = realloc(o->b, (size_t)o->b_cap * sizeof *o->b);
    }
    if ((l->v_n && !o->v) || (l->b_n && !o->b)) {
        sf_fatal("video: no memory for the frame");
    }
    if (l->v_n) {
        memcpy(o->v, l->v, (size_t)l->v_n * sizeof *o->v);
    }
    if (l->b_n) {
        memcpy(o->b, l->b, (size_t)l->b_n * sizeof *o->b);
    }
    o->v_n = l->v_n;
    o->b_n = l->b_n;
    o->clear = l->clear;
    memcpy(o->show, l->show, sizeof o->show);
    memcpy(o->show_at, l->show_at, sizeof o->show_at);
    o->cp3 = l->cp3;
    o->cp3_at = l->cp3_at;
    memcpy(o->cp3_rect, l->cp3_rect, sizeof o->cp3_rect);
}

/* The renderer's calls, on its thread. */
typedef struct CanvasCall {
    const SfCanvas *canvas;
} CanvasCall;

static void call_canvas(void *arg)
{
    char err[256];
    sf_render_present(s_renderer, ((CanvasCall *)arg)->canvas, err, sizeof err);
}

static void call_aspect(void *arg)
{
    sf_render_set_aspect(s_renderer, *(float *)arg);
}

typedef struct ReadCall {
    const uint8_t **rgba;
    int *w;
    int *h;
    int r;
} ReadCall;

static void call_read(void *arg)
{
    ReadCall *c = arg;
    c->r = sf_render_read_screen(s_renderer, c->rgba, c->w, c->h);
}

/* sf_render_read_screen, on the render thread once the frame is drawn. */
static int read_screen(const uint8_t **rgba, int *w, int *h)
{
    ReadCall c;
    c.rgba = rgba;
    c.w = w;
    c.h = h;
    c.r = -1;
    rt_call(call_read, &c);
    return c.r;
}

/* SF_RENDERER=opengl asks for the OpenGL screen even where Vulkan works. */
static int want_gl(void)
{
    const char *r = getenv("SF_RENDERER");
    return r && strcmp(r, "opengl") == 0;
}

/* The window with OpenGL 3.3, where Vulkan is missing or not wanted. */
static void open_gl(int w)
{
    char err[512];
    SDL_GL_SetAttribute(SDL_GL_CONTEXT_MAJOR_VERSION, 3);
    SDL_GL_SetAttribute(SDL_GL_CONTEXT_MINOR_VERSION, 3);
    /* compatibility: the first start's screen draws the old way */
    SDL_GL_SetAttribute(SDL_GL_CONTEXT_PROFILE_MASK, SDL_GL_CONTEXT_PROFILE_COMPATIBILITY);
    SDL_GL_SetAttribute(SDL_GL_DOUBLEBUFFER, 1);
    s_window = SDL_CreateWindow("Street Fighter III: 3rd Strike", w * 2, 480 * 2,
                                SDL_WINDOW_RESIZABLE | SDL_WINDOW_HIGH_PIXEL_DENSITY | SDL_WINDOW_OPENGL);
    if (!s_window) {
        sf_fatal("video: window: %s", SDL_GetError());
    }
    if (sf_render_create(&s_renderer, s_window, SF_RENDER_OPENGL, err, sizeof err) != 0) {
        sf_fatal("video: OpenGL: %s", err);
    }
    fprintf(stderr, "video: OpenGL\n");
}

void sf_video_init(int w, int h)
{
    char err[512];
    (void)h;
    if (getenv("SF_HEADLESS") && want_gl()) {
        /* tests of the OpenGL screen: SDL's offscreen driver (main.c) */
        open_gl(w);
        sf_gfx_init();
        sf_display_init();
        return;
    }
    if (getenv("SF_HEADLESS")) {
        /* tests: nothing on the desktop, the screen only read back */
        if (sf_render_create_headless(&s_renderer, err, sizeof err) != 0) {
            sf_fatal("video: headless Vulkan: %s", err);
        }
        sf_gfx_init();
        sf_display_init();
        return;
    }
    if (!want_gl()) {
        s_window = SDL_CreateWindow("Street Fighter III: 3rd Strike", w * 2, 480 * 2,
                                    SDL_WINDOW_RESIZABLE | SDL_WINDOW_HIGH_PIXEL_DENSITY | SDL_WINDOW_VULKAN |
                                        SF_PHONE_FULLSCREEN);
        if (s_window && sf_render_create(&s_renderer, s_window, SF_RENDER_VULKAN, err, sizeof err) == 0) {
            sf_gfx_init();
            sf_display_init();
            return;
        }
        fprintf(stderr, "video: Vulkan: %s; trying OpenGL\n", s_window ? err : SDL_GetError());
        if (s_window) {
            SDL_DestroyWindow(s_window);
            s_window = NULL;
        }
    }
    open_gl(w);
    sf_gfx_init();
    sf_display_init();
}

/* A picture of the whole window (the first start's screen). */
void sf_video_present_canvas(const SfCanvas *canvas)
{
    char err[256];
    CanvasCall c;
    (void)err;
    c.canvas = canvas;
    rt_call(call_canvas, &c);
}

SDL_Window *sf_video_window(void)
{
    return s_window;
}

void sf_video_set_aspect(float aspect)
{
    rt_call(call_aspect, &aspect);
}

/* SF_SHOT=FRAME:PATH writes the screen after that frame as a PPM;
 * SF_SHOT_EVERY=N:DIR writes DIR/NNNNNN.ppm every N frames (from
 * SF_SHOT_FROM to SF_SHOT_TO when they are set). */
static void screenshot(int frame)
{
    const uint8_t *rgba;
    int w;
    int h;
    const char *env = getenv("SF_SHOT");
    const char *every = getenv("SF_SHOT_EVERY");
    char *colon;
    char path[4096];
    long want;
    FILE *f;
    int i;
    if (every) {
        long n = strtol(every, &colon, 10);
        const char *from = getenv("SF_SHOT_FROM");
        const char *to = getenv("SF_SHOT_TO");
        if (*colon != ':' || n <= 0 || (from && frame + 1 < atoi(from)) || (to && frame > atoi(to))) {
            return;
        }
        if ((frame + 1) % n == 0) {
            /* a read asks for the next frame's picture; it comes the frame
             * after. A picture already waiting is an old one: ask again. */
            if (read_screen(&rgba, &w, &h) == 0) {
                read_screen(&rgba, &w, &h);
            }
            return;
        }
        if (frame == 0 || frame % n != 0) {
            return;
        }
        snprintf(path, sizeof path, "%s/%06d.ppm", colon + 1, frame);
    } else {
        if (!env) {
            return;
        }
        want = strtol(env, &colon, 10);
        if (*colon != ':' || (frame != want && frame != want + 1)) {
            return;
        }
        snprintf(path, sizeof path, "%s", colon + 1);
    }
    if (read_screen(&rgba, &w, &h) != 0) {
        return;
    }
    f = fopen(path, "wb");
    if (!f) {
        return;
    }
    fprintf(f, "P6\n%d %d\n255\n", w, h);
    for (i = 0; i < w * h; i++) {
        fwrite(&rgba[i * 4], 1, 3, f);
    }
    fclose(f);
    if (!every) {
        fprintf(stderr, "video: frame %d written to %s\n", frame, path);
    }
}

/* The game steps once per frame and was made for 60 of them a second
 * (NTSC Xbox). The window's vsync follows the monitor, which may be
 * faster, so frames are also paced to 1/60 s here. */
static void pace(void)
{
    static Uint64 next;
    const Uint64 step = SDL_NS_PER_SECOND / 60;
    Uint64 now = SDL_GetTicksNS();
    if (next == 0 || now > next + step * 4) {
        next = now; /* first frame, or far behind (a load): start again */
    }
    if (next > now) {
        Uint64 wait = next - now;
        if (wait > 2000000) {
            SDL_DelayNS(wait - 1000000); /* sleep most of it, then spin */
        }
        while (SDL_GetTicksNS() < next) {
        }
    }
    next += step;
}

void sf_video_shutdown(void)
{
    rt_stop();
    if (s_renderer) {
        sf_render_destroy(s_renderer);
        s_renderer = NULL;
    }
    if (s_window) {
        SDL_DestroyWindow(s_window);
        s_window = NULL;
    }
}

void sf_video_present(void)
{
    static int frame;
    char err[512];
    sf_gfx_frame_ready();
    rt_start();
    if (s_rt.thread) {
        /* the frame before is drawn; this one goes to the render thread */
        rt_idle();
        if (s_rt.failed) {
            sf_fatal("video: %s", s_rt.err);
        }
        rt_copy(sf_gfx_list());
        s_rt.filter = sf_save_screen_filter();
        SDL_LockMutex(s_rt.lock);
        s_rt.frame = 1;
        SDL_BroadcastCondition(s_rt.cv);
        SDL_UnlockMutex(s_rt.lock);
    } else {
        sf_render_set_filter(s_renderer, sf_save_screen_filter());
        if (sf_render_present_list(s_renderer, sf_gfx_list(), err, sizeof err) != 0) {
            sf_fatal("video: %s", err);
        }
    }
    if (getenv("SF_DUMP_QUADS") && atoi(getenv("SF_DUMP_QUADS")) == frame) {
        /* debugging: each quad of the frame, its batch's texture and the
         * texel rectangle it samples */
        const SfDrawList *l = sf_gfx_list();
        uint32_t b;
        for (b = 0; b < l->b_n; b++) {
            const SfBatch *bt = &l->b[b];
            uint32_t q;
            for (q = 0; q < bt->quads; q++) {
                const SfVertex *v = &l->v[bt->first + q * 4];
                const SfTexture *t = &l->tex[bt->shade.tex];
                fprintf(stderr,
                        "quad b%u tex %u (%dx%d) pos %.3f,%.3f-%.3f,%.3f uv %.3f,%.3f-%.3f,%.3f rect %.3f,%.3f-%.3f,%.3f col "
                        "%08X addr %u%s\n",
                        b, bt->shade.tex, t->w, t->h, v[0].x, v[0].y, v[3].x, v[3].y, v[0].u * t->w, v[0].v * t->h,
                        v[3].u * t->w, v[3].v * t->h, v[0].u0 * t->w, v[0].v0 * t->h, v[0].u1 * t->w, v[0].v1 * t->h,
                        v[0].color, (unsigned)bt->shade.address_u, l->cp3 && b >= l->cp3_at ? " (over)" : "");
            }
        }
    }
    if (getenv("SF_DUMP_TEX") && atoi(getenv("SF_DUMP_TEX")) == frame) {
        /* debugging: every texture as a PPM (an indexed one shows its index) */
        const SfDrawList *l = sf_gfx_list();
        int h;
        for (h = 1; h < SF_TEX_MAX; h++) {
            const SfTexture *t = &l->tex[h];
            char path[64];
            FILE *f;
            int i;
            if (!t->rgba) {
                continue;
            }
            snprintf(path, sizeof path, "tex%03d%s.ppm", h, t->indexed ? "i" : "");
            f = fopen(path, "wb");
            if (!f) {
                continue;
            }
            fprintf(f, "P6\n%d %d\n255\n", t->w, t->h);
            for (i = 0; i < t->w * t->h; i++) {
                fwrite(&t->rgba[i * 4], 1, 3, f);
            }
            fclose(f);
        }
    }
    if (!getenv("SF_HEADLESS") || getenv("SF_PACE")) {
        pace(); /* headless tests run as fast as they can, unless SF_PACE */
    }
    screenshot(frame++);
    sf_gfx_frame_done();
}
```
