`tests/test_frame.c`:

```c
#include "game/memman.h"
#include "game/opening.h"
#include "game/task.h"

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdio.h>

static int g_fail;

static void expect(int cond, const char *msg)
{
    if (!cond) {
        fprintf(stderr, "FAIL %s\n", msg);
        g_fail++;
    } else {
        printf("ok %s\n", msg);
    }
}

static int g_calls;
static int g_callbacks;
static int g_seen_index;
static int g_seen_condition;

static void on_task(SfTask *task_ptr)
{
    g_calls++;
    g_seen_index = current_task_num;
    g_seen_condition = task_ptr->condition;
}

static void on_callback(void)
{
    g_callbacks++;
}

static void test_tasks(void)
{
    int i;
    xb_ClearTasks();
    Turbo = 0;
    Turbo_Timer = 0;
    Game_pause = 0;
    g_calls = 0;
    g_callbacks = 0;
    cpReadyTask(3, on_task);
    expect(task[3].condition == 2 && task[3].func_adrs == on_task, "ready arms condition 2");
    expect(task[3].r_no[0] == 0 && task[3].callback_adrs == NULL, "ready clears the slot");
    xb_TurboFrameStep();
    expect(g_calls == 0 && task[3].condition == 1, "first step waits");
    expect(current_task_num == SF_TASK_COUNT && Process_Counter == 1, "step visits 12 slots");
    xb_TurboFrameStep();
    expect(g_calls == 1 && g_seen_index == 3 && g_seen_condition == 1, "second step runs the slot");
    task[3].callback_adrs = on_callback;
    cpExitTask(3);
    expect(task[3].condition == 0 && g_callbacks == 1, "exit clears and calls back");
    cpReadyTask(99, on_task);
    expect(task[0].func_adrs == NULL, "a slot past the table is ignored");
    xb_ClearTasks();
    g_calls = 0;
    for (i = 0; i < SF_TASK_COUNT; i++) {
        cpReadyTask((uint16_t)i, on_task);
    }
    xb_TurboFrameStep();
    xb_TurboFrameStep();
    expect(g_calls == SF_TASK_COUNT, "all 12 slots run");
    Turbo = 3;
    Turbo_Timer = 1;
    Game_pause = 0;
    xb_TurboFrameStep();
    expect(Process_Counter == 2 && Turbo_Timer == 3, "turbo reloads the timer");
    Turbo_Timer = 2;
    xb_TurboFrameStep();
    expect(Process_Counter == 1 && Turbo_Timer == 1, "turbo counts down");
    Game_pause = 0x81;
    Turbo_Timer = 1;
    xb_TurboFrameStep();
    expect(Process_Counter == 1 && Turbo_Timer == 1, "pause 0x81 skips turbo");
    xb_ClearTasks();
    g_callbacks = 0;
    cpReadyTask(1, on_task);
    xb_TurboFrameStep();
    task[1].callback_adrs = on_callback;
    task[1].condition = 1;
    cpReadyTask(2, on_task);
    task[2].callback_adrs = on_callback;
    task[2].condition = 4;
    cpReadyTask(4, on_task);
    task[4].callback_adrs = on_callback;
    task[4].condition = 2;
    xb_ReleaseTasks();
    expect(g_callbacks == 2, "release calls condition 1 and 4");
    expect(task[1].func_adrs == NULL && task[2].condition == 0, "release clears the table");
}

static void test_heap(void)
{
    MemMan heap;
    MemMan other;
    void *a;
    void *b;
    void *c;
    int32_t after_one;
    mmSystemInitialize();
    expect(mmHeapCreate(&heap, 4096, 16) == 0, "heap create");
    expect(heap.owned == 1 && heap.ownUnit == 16 && heap.remainder == 4096 - 32, "heap remainder");
    expect(heap.ownNumber == 0, "first heap number");
    a = mmAlloc(&heap, 16, 0);
    after_one = heap.remainder;
    b = mmAlloc(&heap, 16, 0);
    expect(a && b && a != b, "two blocks");
    expect(heap.remainder == after_one - 32, "remainder tracks the cell");
    mmFree(&heap, a);
    expect(heap.remainder == after_one, "free restores the remainder");
    c = mmAlloc(&heap, 16, 0);
    expect(c == a, "exact hole is reused");
    after_one = heap.remainder;
    mmFree(&heap, NULL);
    expect(heap.remainder == after_one, "free of null is ignored");
    mmHeapDestroy(&heap);
    expect(mmHeapCreate(&heap, 4096, 16) == 0, "second heap");
    expect(heap.ownNumber == 1, "heap numbers increase");
    a = mmAlloc(&heap, 32, 0);
    b = mmAlloc(&heap, 32, 1);
    expect(a && b && (uint8_t *)b > (uint8_t *)a, "end allocation sits higher");
    {
        int n = 0;
        while (n < 200 && mmAlloc(&heap, 64, 0)) {
            n++;
        }
        expect(n < 200 && mmAlloc(&heap, 64, 0) == NULL, "a full heap returns null");
    }
    mmHeapDestroy(&heap);
    expect(mmHeapCreate(&other, 16, 16) != 0, "a block smaller than two units fails");
    expect(mmHeapInitialize(&heap, (void *)(uintptr_t)8, 16, 3, NULL) != 0, "unit must be a power of two");
}

static void test_opening(void)
{
    SfOpening op;
    int i;
    sf_opening_init(&op);
    expect(op.timer == 0 && op.status == 0 && op.r_no_0 == 0 && op.screen == 0, "the opening clock boots clear");
    for (i = 0; i < 11; i++) {
        sf_opening_step(&op);
    }
    expect(op.r_no_0 == 1 && op.r_no_1 == 0 && op.timer == 2 && op.status == 0 && op.pos_offset == 192, "the clock starts at 2");
    expect(op.layer_r[0] == 0 && op.screen == 0, "background 0 is not selected during the countdown");
    sf_opening_step(&op);
    expect(op.timer == 3 && op.status == 0 && op.held == 0 && op.index == 0, "time 3 has not posted 101");
    expect(op.layer_r[0] == 1 && op.layer_r[1] == 1 && op.layer_r[2] == 1 && op.layer_x[2] == 0x02000000, "the first background move arms layer 2");
    sf_opening_step(&op);
    expect(op.timer == 4 && op.status == 101 && op.scene == 1 && op.held == 1 && op.index == 0, "time 4 posts 101 and holds background 0");
    expect(op.screen == 0 && op.layer_x[2] == 0x01FF0000 && op.layer_y[2] == 0, "background 0 leaves the screens off and walks layer 2");
    expect(op.f6_y[0] == 15 && op.f6_y[1] == -224 && op.show_next == 0, "the first sketch reads 15 at time 4");
    for (i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
        sf_opening_step(&op);
    }
    expect(op.timer == 11 && op.status == 2 && op.scene == 2 && op.index == 0 && op.obj_disp == 0 && op.layer_x[2] == 0x01F80000 && op.f6_y[0] == 11, "time 11 posts status 2 and background 0 keeps walking");
    while (op.timer < 79) {
        sf_opening_step(&op);
    }
    expect(op.timer == 79 && op.status == 11 && op.scene == 11 && op.index == 1 && op.obj_disp == 1 && op.r_no_2 == 2 && op.show_next == 0 && op.screen == 0 && op.layer_x[2] == 0x01B50000 && op.layer_y[0] == 0 && op.f6_y[0] == -23, "time 79 posts status 11 and leaves the old picture up for this frame");
    sf_opening_step(&op);
    expect(op.timer == 80 && op.show_next == 1 && op.f6_y[1] == -222 && op.layer_x[2] == 0x02000000 && (op.screen & 1u) == 1u && op.layer_y[0] == -8 && op.status == 11 && op.r_no_1 == 1, "the next frame shows the second sketch and the ground quake starts at -8");
    while (op.timer < 123) {
        sf_opening_step(&op);
    }
    expect(op.timer == 123 && op.f6_y[1] == -136 && op.f6_stop[1] == 1 && op.status == 16 && op.r_no_1 == 1 && op.layer_x[2] == 0x02000000 && op.layer_y[0] == 3, "the second sketch stops at -136 and scene 102 stays unposted");
    sf_opening_step(&op);
    expect(op.timer == 124 && op.status == 102 && op.r_no_1 == 2 && op.r_no_2 == 1 && op.index == 2 && op.show_line == 1 && op.show_next == 0 && op.screen == 0 && op.line_x == 449 && op.layer_x[2] == 0x02000000 && op.layer_y[2] == 0 && op.layer_y[0] == 0, "time 124 posts 102, drops the ground, and steps the next sketch to 449");
    sf_opening_step(&op);
    expect(op.timer == 125 && op.line_x == 451 && op.layer_y[2] == 0x00010000 && op.show_line == 1 && op.index == 2, "the next frame steps the sketch to 451 and walks layer 2 down");
    while (op.timer < 183) {
        sf_opening_step(&op);
    }
    expect(op.timer == 183 && op.line_x == 538 && op.line_stop == 1 && op.status == 8 && op.index == 2 && op.r_no_1 == 2, "the sketch stops at 538 on timer 183");
    sf_opening_step(&op);
    expect(op.timer == 184 && op.status == 8 && op.index == 2 && op.line_x == 538 && op.r_no_1 == 2, "status 9 stays unposted");
}

int main(void)
{
    test_tasks();
    test_heap();
    test_opening();
    if (g_fail) {
        fprintf(stderr, "%d failed\n", g_fail);
        return 1;
    }
    return 0;
}
```

`tests/test_import.c`:

```c
#include "iso/afs.h"
#include "iso/extract.h"
#include "iso/xdvdfs.h"

#include <errno.h>
#include <spawn.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <sys/wait.h>
#include <unistd.h>

extern char **environ;

static int g_fail;

static void expect(int cond, const char *msg)
{
    if (!cond) {
        fprintf(stderr, "FAIL %s\n", msg);
        g_fail++;
    } else {
        printf("ok %s\n", msg);
    }
}

static void wu16(uint8_t *p, uint16_t v)
{
    p[0] = (uint8_t)v;
    p[1] = (uint8_t)(v >> 8);
}

static void wu32(uint8_t *p, uint32_t v)
{
    p[0] = (uint8_t)v;
    p[1] = (uint8_t)(v >> 8);
    p[2] = (uint8_t)(v >> 16);
    p[3] = (uint8_t)(v >> 24);
}

static void put_ent(uint8_t *dir, uint32_t off, uint16_t left, uint16_t right, uint32_t sector, uint32_t size, uint8_t attr, const char *name)
{
    uint8_t *p = dir + off;
    uint8_t nlen = (uint8_t)strlen(name);
    wu16(p, left);
    wu16(p + 2, right);
    wu32(p + 4, sector);
    wu32(p + 8, size);
    p[12] = attr;
    p[13] = nlen;
    memcpy(p + 14, name, nlen);
}

/* A stand-in sf33rd.xbe: the header, then the 21 sound banks the import
 * reads out of it ("SDBK" version 11, named SE and pl00 to pl19, no RPC
 * table, 0x38 bytes each). */
#define FAKE_XBE_N (0x1000u + 21u * 0x38u)

static void fake_xbe(uint8_t *x)
{
    int i;
    memset(x, 0, FAKE_XBE_N);
    memcpy(x, "XBEH", 4);
    for (i = 0; i < 21; i++) {
        uint8_t *b = x + 0x1000u + (size_t)i * 0x38u;
        memcpy(b, "SDBK", 4);
        wu16(b + 4, 11);
        wu32(b + 0x14, 0x38);
        if (i == 0) {
            memcpy(b + 0x28, "SE", 2);
        } else {
            b[0x28] = 'p';
            b[0x29] = 'l';
            b[0x2A] = (uint8_t)('0' + (i - 1) / 10);
            b[0x2B] = (uint8_t)('0' + (i - 1) % 10);
        }
    }
}

static void write_fake_xbe(const char *dir)
{
    static uint8_t x[FAKE_XBE_N];
    char path[4096];
    FILE *fp;
    fake_xbe(x);
    snprintf(path, sizeof path, "%s/sf33rd.xbe", dir);
    fp = fopen(path, "wb");
    if (fp) {
        fwrite(x, 1, sizeof x, fp);
        fclose(fp);
    }
}

static uint8_t *build_iso(size_t *out_n)
{
    const uint32_t base = 4096;
    const uint32_t sectors = 47;
    size_t n = base + (size_t)sectors * 2048u;
    uint8_t *img = calloc(1, n);
    uint8_t *vol;
    uint8_t *root;
    uint8_t *host;
    uint8_t *media;
    uint8_t *shaders;
    uint8_t *fonts;
    if (!img) {
        return NULL;
    }
    memset(img, 0x11, base);
    vol = img + base + 32u * 2048u;
    memcpy(vol, "MICROSOFT*XBOX*MEDIA", 20);
    memcpy(vol + 0x7EC, "MICROSOFT*XBOX*MEDIA", 20);
    wu32(vol + 0x14, 34);
    wu32(vol + 0x18, 2048);
    root = img + base + 34u * 2048u;
    put_ent(root, 0, 6, 11, 35, 2048, 0x10, "XB_HostPC");
    put_ent(root, 24, 0, 16, 36, 2048, 0x10, "media");
    put_ent(root, 44, 0, 0, 42, 3, 0x80, "zz.txt");
    put_ent(root, 64, 0, 0, 44, FAKE_XBE_N, 0x80, "sf33rd.xbe");
    host = img + base + 35u * 2048u;
    put_ent(host, 0, 0, 7, 39, 4, 0x80, "SF33RDXB.AFS");
    put_ent(host, 28, 0, 0, 41, 4, 0x80, "ST3_3_OP.aix");
    media = img + base + 36u * 2048u;
    put_ent(media, 0, 6, 0, 37, 2048, 0x10, "Shaders");
    put_ent(media, 24, 0, 0, 38, 2048, 0x10, "Fonts");
    shaders = img + base + 37u * 2048u;
    put_ent(shaders, 0, 0, 0, 40, 4, 0x80, "dummy.xvu");
    fonts = img + base + 38u * 2048u;
    put_ent(fonts, 0, 0, 0, 43, 4, 0x80, "note.txt");
    memcpy(img + base + 39u * 2048u, "AFS!", 4);
    memcpy(img + base + 40u * 2048u, "XVU!", 4);
    memcpy(img + base + 41u * 2048u, "AIX!", 4);
    memcpy(img + base + 42u * 2048u, "ZZ!", 3);
    memcpy(img + base + 43u * 2048u, "FONT", 4);
    fake_xbe(img + base + 44u * 2048u);
    *out_n = n;
    return img;
}

static uint32_t crc32(const void *data, size_t n)
{
    static uint32_t table[256];
    static int ready;
    const uint8_t *p = data;
    uint32_t c = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
    size_t i;
    if (!ready) {
        uint32_t nibble;
        for (nibble = 0; nibble < 256; nibble++) {
            uint32_t v = nibble;
            int b;
            for (b = 0; b < 8; b++) {
                v = (v & 1u) ? (0xEDB88320u ^ (v >> 1)) : (v >> 1);
            }
            table[nibble] = v;
        }
        ready = 1;
    }
    for (i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        c = table[(c ^ p[i]) & 0xFFu] ^ (c >> 8);
    }
    return c ^ 0xFFFFFFFFu;
}

static void zip_file(FILE *fp, const char *name, const void *data, size_t n, uint32_t *offset, uint8_t *cd, size_t *cd_n, int *count)
{
    uint32_t crc = crc32(data, n);
    uint8_t local[30];
    uint8_t central[46];
    uint16_t nlen = (uint16_t)strlen(name);
    memset(local, 0, sizeof local);
    wu32(local, 0x04034b50u);
    wu16(local + 4, 20);
    wu16(local + 8, 0);
    wu32(local + 14, crc);
    wu32(local + 18, (uint32_t)n);
    wu32(local + 22, (uint32_t)n);
    wu16(local + 26, nlen);
    fwrite(local, 1, sizeof local, fp);
    fwrite(name, 1, nlen, fp);
    fwrite(data, 1, n, fp);
    memset(central, 0, sizeof central);
    wu32(central, 0x02014b50u);
    wu16(central + 4, 20);
    wu16(central + 6, 20);
    wu32(central + 16, crc);
    wu32(central + 20, (uint32_t)n);
    wu32(central + 24, (uint32_t)n);
    wu16(central + 28, nlen);
    wu32(central + 42, *offset);
    memcpy(cd + *cd_n, central, sizeof central);
    memcpy(cd + *cd_n + sizeof central, name, nlen);
    *cd_n += sizeof central + nlen;
    *offset += 30u + nlen + (uint32_t)n;
    (*count)++;
}

static int write_tree_zip(const char *path, const uint8_t *iso, size_t iso_n, int with_iso, int ps2)
{
    FILE *fp = fopen(path, "wb");
    uint8_t cd[4096];
    size_t cd_n = 0;
    uint32_t offset = 0;
    int count = 0;
    uint8_t end[22];
    if (!fp) {
        return -1;
    }
    if (ps2) {
        zip_file(fp, "SYSTEM.CNF", "BOOT2", 5, &offset, cd, &cd_n, &count);
        zip_file(fp, "THIRD/SF33RD.AFS", "PS2!", 4, &offset, cd, &cd_n, &count);
    } else if (with_iso) {
        zip_file(fp, "disc.iso", iso, iso_n, &offset, cd, &cd_n, &count);
    } else {
        static uint8_t x[FAKE_XBE_N];
        fake_xbe(x);
        zip_file(fp, "sf33rd.xbe", x, sizeof x, &offset, cd, &cd_n, &count);
        zip_file(fp, "XB_HostPC/SF33RDXB.AFS", "AFS!", 4, &offset, cd, &cd_n, &count);
        zip_file(fp, "XB_HostPC/ST3_3_OP.aix", "AIX!", 4, &offset, cd, &cd_n, &count);
        zip_file(fp, "media/Fonts/note.txt", "FONT", 4, &offset, cd, &cd_n, &count);
        zip_file(fp, "media/Shaders/dummy.xvu", "XVU!", 4, &offset, cd, &cd_n, &count);
        zip_file(fp, "hyper/movie.sfd", "MOV!", 4, &offset, cd, &cd_n, &count);
    }
    fwrite(cd, 1, cd_n, fp);
    memset(end, 0, sizeof end);
    wu32(end, 0x06054b50u);
    wu16(end + 8, (uint16_t)count);
    wu16(end + 10, (uint16_t)count);
    wu32(end + 12, (uint32_t)cd_n);
    wu32(end + 16, offset);
    fwrite(end, 1, sizeof end, fp);
    fclose(fp);
    return 0;
}

static int read_file(const char *path, char *dst, size_t n)
{
    FILE *fp = fopen(path, "rb");
    size_t got;
    if (!fp) {
        return -1;
    }
    got = fread(dst, 1, n, fp);
    fclose(fp);
    return got == n ? 0 : -1;
}

static char *make_dir(void)
{
    char tmpl[] = "/tmp/sf33rd-test-XXXXXX";
    return mkdtemp(tmpl) ? strdup(tmpl) : NULL;
}

static void check_ready(const char *install, int expect_shader_fail, const char *msg)
{
    char path[4096];
    char buf[8];
    struct stat st;
    snprintf(path, sizeof path, "%s/data/afs/SF33RDXB.AFS", install);
    expect(read_file(path, buf, 4) == 0 && memcmp(buf, "AFS!", 4) == 0, msg);
    snprintf(path, sizeof path, "%s/hyper/movie.sfd", install);
    expect(stat(path, &st) != 0, "movies stay out");
    snprintf(path, sizeof path, "%s/data/shaders/nv2a/dummy.xvu", install);
    expect(read_file(path, buf, 4) == 0 && memcmp(buf, "XVU!", 4) == 0, "shader copied");
    snprintf(path, sizeof path, "%s/data/audio/sdbk/20.sdbk", install);
    expect(read_file(path, buf, 4) == 0 && memcmp(buf, "SDBK", 4) == 0, "sound banks from the xbe");
    snprintf(path, sizeof path, "%s/data/.import-tmp/sf33rd.xbe", install);
    expect(stat(path, &st) != 0, "the xbe is not kept");
    if (expect_shader_fail) {
        snprintf(path, sizeof path, "%s/data/shaders/spirv/dummy.spv", install);
        expect(stat(path, &st) != 0, "bad shader is not spirv");
    }
}

static int run_ok(const char *source, const char *install, const char *tool, int shader_fail, const char *msg)
{
    SfExtractResult result;
    int rc = sf_extract(source, install, tool, NULL, NULL, &result);
    expect(rc == 0 && sf_data_ready(install), msg);
    if (rc == 0) {
        check_ready(install, shader_fail, msg);
        if (shader_fail) {
            expect(result.spirv_failed > 0, "conversion failure is recorded");
        }
    }
    return rc;
}

int main(void)
{
    size_t iso_n = 0;
    uint8_t *iso = build_iso(&iso_n);
    char *dir;
    char path[4096];
    SfXdvdfs *img;
    SfXdvdfsEnt ent;
    char err[512];
    SfExtractResult result;
    expect(iso != NULL, "synthetic xbox image");
    snprintf(path, sizeof path, "/tmp/sf33rd-test-image.iso");
    {
        FILE *fp = fopen(path, "wb");
        expect(fp && fwrite(iso, 1, iso_n, fp) == iso_n, "write iso");
        if (fp) {
            fclose(fp);
        }
    }
    img = sf_xdvdfs_open(path, err, sizeof err);
    expect(img != NULL, "open xbox xdvdfs");
    if (img) {
        expect(sf_xdvdfs_find(img, "XB_HostPC/SF33RDXB.AFS", &ent, err, sizeof err) == 0 && ent.size == 4, "find afs");
        expect(sf_xdvdfs_find(img, "media/Shaders/dummy.xvu", &ent, err, sizeof err) == 0, "find shader");
        expect(sf_xdvdfs_find(img, "zz.txt", &ent, err, sizeof err) == 0 && ent.size == 3, "right child");
        expect(sf_xdvdfs_find(img, "media", &ent, err, sizeof err) == 0 && ent.is_dir, "left child");
        sf_xdvdfs_close(img);
    }
    dir = make_dir();
    expect(dir != NULL, "temp install");
    if (dir) {
        expect(run_ok(path, dir, SF_SHADER_TOOL, 1, "extract xbox iso") == 0, "iso import");
        free(dir);
    }
    dir = make_dir();
    snprintf(path, sizeof path, "/tmp/sf33rd-tree.zip");
    expect(write_tree_zip(path, iso, iso_n, 0, 0) == 0, "write zip");
    if (dir) {
        run_ok(path, dir, NULL, 0, "extract zip of xbox tree");
        free(dir);
    }
    dir = make_dir();
    snprintf(path, sizeof path, "/tmp/sf33rd-iso.zip");
    {
        FILE *fp = fopen("/tmp/sf33rd-test-image.iso", "rb");
        expect(fp != NULL, "reread iso");
        if (fp) {
            fclose(fp);
        }
    }
    expect(write_tree_zip(path, iso, iso_n, 1, 0) == 0, "write iso zip");
    if (dir) {
        run_ok(path, dir, NULL, 0, "extract zip of xbox iso");
        free(dir);
    }
    snprintf(path, sizeof path, "/tmp/sf33rd-ps2.zip");
    expect(write_tree_zip(path, iso, iso_n, 0, 1) == 0, "write ps2 zip");
    dir = make_dir();
    if (dir) {
        int rc = sf_extract(path, dir, NULL, NULL, NULL, &result);
        expect(rc != 0 && strstr(result.error, "Xbox") != NULL, "reject non-xbox archive");
        expect(!sf_data_ready(dir), "ps2 archive writes no afs");
        free(dir);
    }
    dir = make_dir();
    if (dir) {
        FILE *fp;
        snprintf(path, sizeof path, "%s/junk.bin", dir);
        fp = fopen(path, "wb");
        fwrite("nope", 1, 4, fp);
        fclose(fp);
        expect(sf_extract(path, dir, NULL, NULL, NULL, &result) != 0, "reject a file that is not an xbox iso");
        free(dir);
    }
    {
        char src[] = "/tmp/sf33rd-test-XXXXXX";
        char *tree = mkdtemp(src);
        char *install;
        expect(tree != NULL, "extracted folder");
        if (tree) {
            char file[4096];
            write_fake_xbe(tree);
            snprintf(file, sizeof file, "%s/XB_HostPC", tree);
            mkdir(file, 0755);
            snprintf(file, sizeof file, "%s/XB_HostPC/SF33RDXB.AFS", tree);
            {
                FILE *fp = fopen(file, "wb");
                fwrite("AFS!", 1, 4, fp);
                fclose(fp);
            }
            snprintf(file, sizeof file, "%s/media/Shaders", tree);
            mkdir(file, 0755);
            snprintf(file, sizeof file, "%s/media", tree);
            mkdir(file, 0755);
            snprintf(file, sizeof file, "%s/media/Shaders", tree);
            mkdir(file, 0755);
            snprintf(file, sizeof file, "%s/media/Shaders/dummy.xvu", tree);
            {
                FILE *fp = fopen(file, "wb");
                fwrite("XVU!", 1, 4, fp);
                fclose(fp);
            }
            install = make_dir();
            if (install) {
                run_ok(tree, install, NULL, 0, "extract xbox folder");
                free(install);
            }
        }
    }
    {
        char staged[] = "/tmp/sf33rd-test-XXXXXX";
        char *tree = mkdtemp(staged);
        char archive[] = "/tmp/sf33rd-tree.7z";
        if (tree) {
            pid_t pid;
            char *argv[] = {"7z", "a", "-bd", "-y", archive, "XB_HostPC", "media", "sf33rd.xbe", NULL};
            char file[4096];
            int status = 0;
            write_fake_xbe(tree);
            snprintf(file, sizeof file, "%s/XB_HostPC", tree);
            mkdir(file, 0755);
            snprintf(file, sizeof file, "%s/XB_HostPC/SF33RDXB.AFS", tree);
            {
                FILE *fp = fopen(file, "wb");
                fwrite("AFS!", 1, 4, fp);
                fclose(fp);
            }
            snprintf(file, sizeof file, "%s/media/Shaders", tree);
            mkdir(file, 0755);
            snprintf(file, sizeof file, "%s/media", tree);
            mkdir(file, 0755);
            snprintf(file, sizeof file, "%s/media/Shaders", tree);
            mkdir(file, 0755);
            snprintf(file, sizeof file, "%s/media/Shaders/dummy.xvu", tree);
            {
                FILE *fp = fopen(file, "wb");
                fwrite("XVU!", 1, 4, fp);
                fclose(fp);
            }
            if (chdir(tree) == 0 && posix_spawnp(&pid, "7z", NULL, NULL, argv, environ) == 0) {
                waitpid(pid, &status, 0);
                expect(WIFEXITED(status) && WEXITSTATUS(status) == 0, "write 7z");
                dir = make_dir();
                if (dir) {
                    run_ok(archive, dir, NULL, 0, "extract 7z of xbox tree");
                    free(dir);
                }
            } else {
                expect(0, "spawn 7z");
            }
        }
    }
    {
        char tmpl[] = "/tmp/sf33rd-afs-XXXXXX";
        char *dir = mkdtemp(tmpl);
        char afs_path[256];
        char files[256];
        char index[256];
        char got[8];
        unsigned char image[168];
        int written = 0;
        char err[128];
        FILE *fp;
        expect(dir != NULL, "afs temp");
        if (dir) {
            memset(image, 0, sizeof image);
            memcpy(image, "AFS", 3);
            wu32(image + 4, 2);
            wu32(image + 8, 64);
            wu32(image + 12, 4);
            wu32(image + 16, 68);
            wu32(image + 20, 4);
            wu32(image + 24, 72);
            wu32(image + 28, 96);
            memcpy(image + 64, "ONE!", 4);
            memcpy(image + 68, "TWO!", 4);
            memcpy(image + 72, "One.bin", 7);
            memcpy(image + 120, "Two.bin", 7);
            snprintf(afs_path, sizeof afs_path, "%s/pack.afs", dir);
            snprintf(files, sizeof files, "%s/files", dir);
            snprintf(index, sizeof index, "%s/index", dir);
            fp = fopen(afs_path, "wb");
            expect(fp && fwrite(image, 1, sizeof image, fp) == sizeof image, "write tiny afs");
            if (fp) {
                fclose(fp);
            }
            expect(sf_afs_unpack(afs_path, files, index, NULL, NULL, &written, err, sizeof err) == 0 && written == 2, "unpack afs");
            snprintf(afs_path, sizeof afs_path, "%s/files/One.bin", dir);
            expect(read_file(afs_path, got, 4) == 0 && memcmp(got, "ONE!", 4) == 0, "first afs file");
            snprintf(afs_path, sizeof afs_path, "%s/files/Two.bin", dir);
            expect(read_file(afs_path, got, 4) == 0 && memcmp(got, "TWO!", 4) == 0, "second afs file");
        }
    }
    free(iso);
    unlink("/tmp/sf33rd-test-image.iso");
    unlink("/tmp/sf33rd-tree.zip");
    unlink("/tmp/sf33rd-iso.zip");
    unlink("/tmp/sf33rd-ps2.zip");
    unlink("/tmp/sf33rd-tree.7z");
    return g_fail ? 1 : 0;
}

```

`tests/test_picture.c`:

```c
#include "game/fs.h"
#include "game/pieces.h"
#include "game/ppg.h"
#include "game/title.h"
#include "mem/heap.h"

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <zlib.h>

static int g_fail;

static void expect(int cond, const char *msg)
{
    if (!cond) {
        fprintf(stderr, "FAIL %s\n", msg);
        g_fail++;
    } else {
        printf("ok %s\n", msg);
    }
}

static uint16_t texel_at(const SfPpgTex *tex, int i)
{
    return (uint16_t)(tex->texels[i * 2] | (tex->texels[i * 2 + 1] << 8));
}

static uint8_t expand5(unsigned channel)
{
    channel &= 31u;
    return (uint8_t)((channel << 3) | (channel >> 2));
}

static void put_be16(uint8_t *p, uint16_t v)
{
    p[0] = (uint8_t)(v >> 8);
    p[1] = (uint8_t)v;
}

static int write_file(const char *path, const void *data, size_t n)
{
    FILE *fp = fopen(path, "wb");
    if (!fp) {
        return -1;
    }
    if (n > 0 && fwrite(data, 1, n, fp) != n) {
        fclose(fp);
        return -1;
    }
    if (fclose(fp) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    return 0;
}

static int mkdir_at(const char *path)
{
    if (mkdir(path, 0700) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    return 0;
}

/* 16x16 A1R5G5B5, one compressed or raw chunk, one raw chunk, then pEND.
   The next chunk starts on the 4-byte boundary the loader uses. */
static uint8_t *make_ppg(size_t *out_size, int pixel, int compress, const uint16_t *logical, int count)
{
    uint8_t raw[16 * 16 * 2];
    uint8_t *packed;
    uLongf packed_len;
    uint8_t *file;
    uint32_t payload;
    uint32_t chunk;
    uint32_t step;
    uint32_t second;
    uint32_t total;
    int i;
    if (count != 16 * 16) {
        return NULL;
    }
    for (i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        if (pixel & 4) {
            raw[i * 2] = (uint8_t)logical[i];
            raw[i * 2 + 1] = (uint8_t)(logical[i] >> 8);
        } else {
            put_be16(raw + i * 2, logical[i]);
        }
    }
    packed_len = compressBound(sizeof raw);
    packed = sf_alloc(packed_len);
    if (!packed) {
        return NULL;
    }
    if (compress == 2) {
        if (compress2(packed, &packed_len, raw, sizeof raw, Z_DEFAULT_COMPRESSION) != Z_OK) {
            sf_free(packed);
            return NULL;
        }
        payload = (uint32_t)packed_len;
    } else {
        memcpy(packed, raw, sizeof raw);
        payload = (uint32_t)sizeof raw;
    }
    chunk = 16u + payload;
    step = (chunk + 3u) & ~3u;
    second = 16u + (uint32_t)sizeof raw;
    total = step + second + 4u;
    file = sf_alloc(total);
    if (!file) {
        sf_free(packed);
        return NULL;
    }
    memset(file, 0, total);
    memcpy(file, "pTEX", 4);
    file[4] = (uint8_t)(chunk >> 24);
    file[5] = (uint8_t)(chunk >> 16);
    file[6] = (uint8_t)(chunk >> 8);
    file[7] = (uint8_t)chunk;
    file[8] = 1;
    file[9] = 1;
    file[10] = (uint8_t)compress;
    file[11] = (uint8_t)pixel;
    file[12] = 0x15;
    file[13] = 0x55;
    memcpy(file + 16, packed, payload);
    memcpy(file + step, "pTEX", 4);
    file[step + 4] = 0;
    file[step + 5] = 0;
    file[step + 6] = (uint8_t)(second >> 8);
    file[step + 7] = (uint8_t)second;
    file[step + 8] = 1;
    file[step + 9] = 1;
    file[step + 10] = 0;
    file[step + 11] = 0x82;
    file[step + 12] = 0x15;
    file[step + 13] = 0x55;
    for (i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        put_be16(file + step + 16 + i * 2, 0xFFFF);
    }
    memcpy(file + total - 4, "pEND", 4);
    sf_free(packed);
    *out_size = total;
    return file;
}

/* 16x16 8-bit, one rectangle (palette 15, one cell), zlib, then pEND. */
static uint8_t *make_indexed(size_t *out_size)
{
    uint8_t raw[16 * 16];
    uint8_t *packed;
    uLongf packed_len;
    uint8_t *file;
    uint32_t payload;
    uint32_t chunk;
    uint32_t step;
    int i;
    for (i = 0; i < 16 * 16; i++) {
        raw[i] = 1;
    }
    raw[0] = 28;
    raw[1] = 0;
    packed_len = compressBound(sizeof raw);
    packed = sf_alloc(packed_len);
    if (!packed) {
        return NULL;
    }
    if (compress2(packed, &packed_len, raw, sizeof raw, Z_DEFAULT_COMPRESSION) != Z_OK) {
        sf_free(packed);
        return NULL;
    }
    payload = (uint32_t)packed_len;
    chunk = 19u + payload;
    step = (chunk + 3u) & ~3u;
    file = sf_alloc(step + 4u);
    if (!file) {
        sf_free(packed);
        return NULL;
    }
    memset(file, 0, step + 4u);
    memcpy(file, "pTEX", 4);
    file[4] = (uint8_t)(chunk >> 24);
    file[5] = (uint8_t)(chunk >> 16);
    file[6] = (uint8_t)(chunk >> 8);
    file[7] = (uint8_t)chunk;
    file[8] = 1;
    file[9] = 1;
    file[10] = 2;
    file[11] = 0x81;
    file[15] = 1;
    file[16] = 15;
    memcpy(file + 19, packed, payload);
    memcpy(file + step, "pEND", 4);
    sf_free(packed);
    *out_size = step + 4u;
    return file;
}

static void test_indexed(void)
{
    uint8_t *file;
    size_t size = 0;
    SfPpgTex tex;
    uint16_t pal[64];
    char err[128];
    memset(&tex, 0, sizeof tex);
    memset(pal, 0, sizeof pal);
    pal[1] = 0x8000;
    pal[28] = 0xEF7B;
    file = make_indexed(&size);
    expect(file != NULL, "synthetic indexed pTEX builds");
    if (!file) {
        return;
    }
    err[0] = 0;
    expect(sf_ppg_tex(file, (uint32_t)size, 0, &tex, err, sizeof err) == 0, "indexed texture decodes");
    if (tex.texels) {
        expect(tex.width == 16 && tex.height == 16 && tex.form == 0 && tex.raw_size == 256, "indexed size is one cell");
        expect((tex.compress & 3) == 2 && tex.pixel == 0x81 && tex.rects == 1 && tex.pal == 15, "indexed header keeps palette 15");
        expect(tex.rgba == NULL && tex.texels[0] == 28 && tex.texels[1] == 0, "8-bit indices are not expanded yet");
        expect(sf_ppg_expand_ci(&tex, pal, 64, err, sizeof err) == 0 && tex.rgba != NULL, "the palette row expands");
        if (tex.rgba) {
            expect(tex.rgba[0] == 222 && tex.rgba[1] == 222 && tex.rgba[2] == 222 && tex.rgba[3] == 255, "index 28 is 0xEF7B");
            expect(tex.rgba[4] == 0 && tex.rgba[7] == 0, "index 0 stays transparent");
            expect(tex.rgba[11] == 255, "index 1 is opaque black");
        }
        tex.texels[2] = 64;
        expect(sf_ppg_expand_ci(&tex, pal, 64, err, sizeof err) != 0, "an index past the row fails");
        if (tex.rgba) {
            expect(tex.rgba[0] == 222, "a failed expand leaves the previous picture");
        }
    }
    sf_ppg_tex_free(&tex);
    sf_free(file);
}

static void test_synthetic(void)
{
    uint16_t logical[16 * 16];
    uint8_t *file;
    size_t size = 0;
    SfPpgTex tex;
    SfPpgTex second;
    char err[128];
    int i;
    memset(logical, 0, sizeof logical);
    logical[0] = 0x8000;
    logical[1] = 0x7C00;
    logical[2] = 0x03E0;
    logical[3] = 0x001F;
    logical[4] = 0xFFFF;
    memset(&tex, 0, sizeof tex);
    memset(&second, 0, sizeof second);
    file = make_ppg(&size, 0x82, 2, logical, 16 * 16);
    expect(file != NULL, "synthetic zlib pTEX builds");
    if (!file) {
        return;
    }
    err[0] = 0;
    expect(sf_ppg_tex(file, (uint32_t)size, 0, &tex, err, sizeof err) == 0, "synthetic texture decodes");
    if (tex.texels && tex.rgba) {
        expect(tex.width == 16 && tex.height == 16, "synthetic size is one cell");
        expect(tex.form == 0x1555 && tex.raw_size == 512 && (tex.compress & 3) == 2, "synthetic header is A1R5G5B5 inflate");
        expect(texel_at(&tex, 0) == 0x8000 && texel_at(&tex, 1) == 0x7C00, "inflate then byte-swap restores the texels");
        expect(tex.rgba[0] == 0 && tex.rgba[3] == 255, "pixel 0 is opaque black");
        expect(tex.rgba[4] == 255 && tex.rgba[7] == 0, "pixel 1 is red with alpha clear");
        expect(tex.rgba[9] == expand5(31) && tex.rgba[11] == 0, "pixel 2 is green");
        expect(tex.rgba[14] == expand5(31) && tex.rgba[15] == 0, "pixel 3 is blue");
        expect(tex.rgba[16] == 255 && tex.rgba[17] == 255 && tex.rgba[18] == 255 && tex.rgba[19] == 255, "pixel 4 is opaque white");
    }
    expect(sf_ppg_tex(file, (uint32_t)size, 1, &second, err, sizeof err) == 0, "the second chunk decodes");
    if (second.texels) {
        expect(second.width == 16 && texel_at(&second, 0) == 0xFFFF && (second.compress & 3) == 0, "the raw chunk swaps once");
    }
    sf_ppg_tex_free(&tex);
    file[18] ^= 0xFF;
    expect(sf_ppg_tex(file, (uint32_t)size, 0, &tex, err, sizeof err) != 0, "a broken zlib stream fails");
    sf_ppg_tex_free(&tex);
    sf_ppg_tex_free(&second);
    for (i = 0; i < 16 * 16; i++) {
        logical[i] = 0x8000;
    }
    logical[0] = 0x001F;
    sf_free(file);
    file = make_ppg(&size, 0x86, 0, logical, 16 * 16);
    expect(file != NULL && sf_ppg_tex(file, (uint32_t)size, 0, &tex, err, sizeof err) == 0, "pixel bit 2 skips the swap");
    if (tex.texels) {
        expect(texel_at(&tex, 0) == 0x001F, "little-endian texels stay little-endian");
    }
    sf_ppg_tex_free(&tex);
    sf_free(file);
    expect(sf_ppg_tex((const uint8_t *)"pEND", 4, 0, &tex, err, sizeof err) != 0, "pEND alone has no texture");
}

static void test_fs_fixture(void)
{
    char root[] = "/tmp/sf33rd-fsXXXXXX";
    char data[128];
    char afs[160];
    char files[180];
    char index_path[200];
    char member[200];
    char note_path[200];
    const char note[] = "hi";
    char index[128];
    SfFsFile file;
    char err[128];
    uint8_t *ppg;
    size_t ppg_size = 0;
    uint16_t logical[16 * 16];
    int n;
    if (!mkdtemp(root)) {
        expect(0, "temp archive directory");
        return;
    }
    snprintf(data, sizeof data, "%s/data", root);
    snprintf(afs, sizeof afs, "%s/afs", data);
    snprintf(files, sizeof files, "%s/files", afs);
    snprintf(index_path, sizeof index_path, "%s/index", afs);
    expect(mkdir_at(data) == 0 && mkdir_at(afs) == 0 && mkdir_at(files) == 0, "fixture directories");
    memset(logical, 0, sizeof logical);
    logical[0] = 0xFFFF;
    ppg = make_ppg(&ppg_size, 0x82, 2, logical, 16 * 16);
    snprintf(member, sizeof member, "%s/Tiny.ppg", files);
    snprintf(note_path, sizeof note_path, "%s/Note.bin", files);
    n = snprintf(index, sizeof index, "sf33rd-afs 1\nfiles 2\n0 Tiny.ppg %u\n1 Note.bin 2\n", (unsigned)ppg_size);
    expect(ppg && n > 0 && write_file(index_path, index, (size_t)n) == 0, "fixture index");
    expect(write_file(member, ppg, ppg_size) == 0 && write_file(note_path, note, 2) == 0, "fixture members");
    err[0] = 0;
    expect(fsOpen(root, 0, &file, err, sizeof err) == 0, "fsOpen reads file 0");
    expect(strcmp(file.name, "Tiny.ppg") == 0 && file.size == ppg_size, "file 0 name and size");
    expect(file.data && file.data[0] == 'p', "file 0 bytes");
    fsClose(&file);
    expect(fsOpen(root, 1, &file, err, sizeof err) == 0 && file.size == 2 && memcmp(file.data, "hi", 2) == 0, "fsOpen reads file 1");
    fsClose(&file);
    expect(fsOpen(root, 2, &file, err, sizeof err) != 0, "fsOpen rejects a number past the index");
    expect(fsOpen(root, 99, &file, err, sizeof err) != 0, "fsOpen rejects 99");
    unlink(member);
    unlink(note_path);
    unlink(index_path);
    rmdir(files);
    rmdir(afs);
    rmdir(data);
    rmdir(root);
    sf_free(ppg);
}

static void test_rank1(void)
{
    SfFsFile file;
    SfFsFile past;
    SfPpgTex none;
    char err[256];
    unsigned count = 0;
    FILE *fp;
    char line[64];
    int i;
    err[0] = 0;
    memset(&file, 0, sizeof file);
    memset(&past, 0, sizeof past);
    memset(&none, 0, sizeof none);
    fp = fopen("data/afs/index", "r");
    if (!fp) {
        printf("skip Rank1 (no archive index)\n");
        return;
    }
    if (!fgets(line, (int)sizeof line, fp) || !fgets(line, (int)sizeof line, fp) || sscanf(line, "files %u", &count) != 1) {
        fclose(fp);
        expect(0, "archive index count");
        return;
    }
    fclose(fp);
    expect(count == 1531, "the archive index lists 1531 files");
    expect(fsOpen(".", count, &past, err, sizeof err) != 0, "a file number past the archive fails");
    fsClose(&past);
    expect(fsOpen(".", 0, &file, err, sizeof err) == 0, "Rank1 opens as file 0");
    expect(file.data && strcmp(file.name, "Rank1.ppg") == 0 && file.size == 20732, "Rank1 name and size");
    if (file.data && strcmp(file.name, "Rank1.ppg") == 0) {
        for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
            SfPpgTex tex;
            char msg[64];
            int ok;
            memset(&tex, 0, sizeof tex);
            ok = sf_ppg_tex(file.data, file.size, i, &tex, err, sizeof err) == 0;
            snprintf(msg, sizeof msg, "Rank1 texture %d decodes", i);
            expect(ok, msg);
            if (!ok || !tex.texels) {
                sf_ppg_tex_free(&tex);
                continue;
            }
            snprintf(msg, sizeof msg, "Rank1 texture %d is 128x128 A1R5G5B5", i);
            expect(tex.width == 128 && tex.height == 128 && tex.form == 0x1555 && tex.raw_size == 32768, msg);
            snprintf(msg, sizeof msg, "Rank1 texture %d is inflate pixel 0x82", i);
            expect((tex.compress & 3) == 2 && tex.pixel == 0x82, msg);
            if (i == 0 && tex.rgba) {
                expect(texel_at(&tex, 0) == 0 && texel_at(&tex, 100) == 0x80ea && texel_at(&tex, 16383) == 0x8067, "Rank1 texture 0 texels");
                expect(tex.rgba[100 * 4] == 0 && tex.rgba[100 * 4 + 1] == 57 && tex.rgba[100 * 4 + 2] == 82 && tex.rgba[100 * 4 + 3] == 255, "Rank1 texture 0 pixel 100 is opaque teal");
            }
            if (i == 1) {
                expect(texel_at(&tex, 0) == 0x810b, "Rank1 texture 1 starts at 0x810b");
            }
            sf_ppg_tex_free(&tex);
        }
        expect(sf_ppg_tex(file.data, file.size, 8, &none, err, sizeof err) != 0, "Rank1 has eight textures");
        sf_ppg_tex_free(&none);
    }
    fsClose(&file);
}

static void expect_logo(uint32_t fnum, const char *name, uint32_t bytes)
{
    SfFsFile file;
    SfPpgTex tex;
    char err[128];
    char msg[80];
    memset(&file, 0, sizeof file);
    memset(&tex, 0, sizeof tex);
    err[0] = 0;
    snprintf(msg, sizeof msg, "%s opens", name);
    expect(fsOpen(".", fnum, &file, err, sizeof err) == 0, msg);
    snprintf(msg, sizeof msg, "%s name and size", name);
    expect(file.data && strcmp(file.name, name) == 0 && file.size == bytes, msg);
    if (!file.data) {
        fsClose(&file);
        return;
    }
    snprintf(msg, sizeof msg, "%s decodes", name);
    expect(sf_ppg_tex(file.data, file.size, 0, &tex, err, sizeof err) == 0, msg);
    if (tex.texels && tex.rgba) {
        snprintf(msg, sizeof msg, "%s is 512x256 A1R5G5B5", name);
        expect(tex.width == 512 && tex.height == 256 && tex.form == 0x1555 && tex.raw_size == 512u * 256u * 2u, msg);
        snprintf(msg, sizeof msg, "%s is inflate pixel 0x82", name);
        expect((tex.compress & 3) == 2 && tex.pixel == 0x82, msg);
        expect(texel_at(&tex, 0) == 0x8000 && texel_at(&tex, 9902) == 0x8400, "title texel 9902 is 0x8400");
        expect(tex.rgba[9902 * 4] == 8 && tex.rgba[9902 * 4 + 1] == 0 && tex.rgba[9902 * 4 + 3] == 255, "title pixel 9902 is dark red");
    }
    sf_ppg_tex_free(&tex);
    fsClose(&file);
}

static void test_title(void)
{
    FILE *fp;
    expect(sf_title_fnum(0) == 0x53 && sf_title_fnum(3) == 0x53 && sf_title_fnum(8) == 0x53, "other countries load TitleTM");
    expect(sf_title_fnum(1) == 0x54 && sf_title_fnum(2) == 0x54, "countries 1 and 2 load Title_R");
    expect(Country == 0 && sf_title_fnum(Country) == 0x53, "boot Country is 0");
    fp = fopen("data/afs/index", "r");
    if (!fp) {
        printf("skip title logos (no archive index)\n");
        return;
    }
    fclose(fp);
    expect_logo(0x53, "TitleTM.ppg", 38496);
    expect_logo(0x54, "Title_R.ppg", 38524);
}

static void test_opening(void)
{
    SfFsFile image;
    SfFsFile colors;
    SfPpgTex tex;
    uint16_t row[64];
    char err[128];
    const uint8_t *words;
    int i;
    FILE *fp = fopen("data/afs/index", "r");
    if (!fp) {
        printf("skip opening tile (no archive index)\n");
        return;
    }
    fclose(fp);
    memset(&image, 0, sizeof image);
    memset(&colors, 0, sizeof colors);
    memset(&tex, 0, sizeof tex);
    err[0] = 0;
    expect(fsOpen(".", 81, &image, err, sizeof err) == 0, "Opening.ppg opens");
    expect(image.data && strcmp(image.name, "Opening.ppg") == 0 && image.size == 449032, "Opening.ppg name and size");
    if (!image.data) {
        fsClose(&image);
        return;
    }
    expect(sf_ppg_tex(image.data, image.size, 0, &tex, err, sizeof err) == 0, "opening texture 0 decodes");
    if (!tex.texels) {
        sf_ppg_tex_free(&tex);
        fsClose(&image);
        return;
    }
    expect(tex.width == 256 && tex.height == 256 && tex.form == 0 && tex.raw_size == 256u * 256u, "opening texture 0 is 256x256 indexed");
    expect((tex.compress & 3) == 2 && tex.pixel == 0x81 && tex.rects == 1 && tex.pal == 15, "opening texture 0 is inflate palette 15");
    expect(tex.rgba == NULL && tex.texels[0] == 28, "opening pixel 0 is index 28");
    expect(fsOpen(".", 82, &colors, err, sizeof err) == 0, "title.bin opens");
    expect(colors.data && strcmp(colors.name, "title.bin") == 0 && colors.size == 11264, "title.bin name and size");
    if (!colors.data || colors.size < 16u * 128u) {
        sf_ppg_tex_free(&tex);
        fsClose(&image);
        fsClose(&colors);
        return;
    }
    words = colors.data + 15 * 128;
    for (i = 0; i < 64; i++) {
        row[i] = (uint16_t)(words[i * 2] | (words[i * 2 + 1] << 8));
    }
    expect(row[28] == 0xEF7B, "title.bin row 15 word 28 is 0xEF7B");
    expect(sf_ppg_expand_ci(&tex, row, 64, err, sizeof err) == 0 && tex.rgba != NULL, "opening texture 0 takes row 15");
    if (tex.rgba) {
        expect(tex.rgba[0] == 222 && tex.rgba[1] == 222 && tex.rgba[2] == 222 && tex.rgba[3] == 255, "opening pixel 0 is light gray");
    }
    sf_ppg_tex_free(&tex);
    fsClose(&image);
    fsClose(&colors);
}

static void test_pieces(void)
{
    SfFsFile bg;
    SfFsFile colors;
    uint8_t linear[256];
    uint8_t *view;
    const uint8_t *red;
    const uint8_t *ink;
    int side;
    char err[128];
    FILE *fp = fopen("data/afs/index", "r");
    if (!fp) {
        printf("skip opening pieces (no archive index)\n");
        return;
    }
    fclose(fp);
    memset(&bg, 0, sizeof bg);
    memset(&colors, 0, sizeof colors);
    err[0] = 0;
    side = 0;
    expect(fsOpen(".", 79, &bg, err, sizeof err) == 0, "bg20.bin opens");
    expect(bg.data && strcmp(bg.name, "bg20.bin") == 0 && bg.size == 1024980, "bg20.bin name and size");
    expect(fsOpen(".", 82, &colors, err, sizeof err) == 0, "title.bin opens for the pieces");
    if (!bg.data || !colors.data) {
        fsClose(&bg);
        fsClose(&colors);
        return;
    }
    expect(sf_pieces_linear(bg.data, bg.size, 2395, linear, (int)sizeof linear, &side) == 0, "chip 2395 untwiddles");
    expect(side == 16 && linear[100] == 35, "chip 2395 index 100 is 35");
    view = sf_alloc((size_t)SF_PIECE_W * (size_t)SF_PIECE_H * 4u);
    expect(view && sf_pieces_view(bg.data, bg.size, colors.data, colors.size, view, err, sizeof err) == 0, "opening pieces compose");
    if (view) {
        red = view + ((size_t)117 * (size_t)SF_PIECE_W + 2u) * 4u;
        ink = view + ((size_t)40 * (size_t)SF_PIECE_W + 200u) * 4u;
        expect(red[0] == 255 && red[1] == 0 && red[2] == 0 && red[3] == 255, "slogan pixel 2,117 is red");
        expect(ink[0] == 222 && ink[1] == 239 && ink[2] == 231 && ink[3] == 255, "sketch pixel 200,40 is pale green");
    }
    {
        size_t n = (size_t)SF_PIECE_W * (size_t)SF_PIECE_H * 4u;
        uint8_t *sketch = sf_alloc(n);
        uint8_t *slogan = sf_alloc(n);
        uint8_t *moved = sf_alloc(n);
        expect(sketch && slogan && moved && sf_pieces_layers(bg.data, bg.size, colors.data, colors.size, sketch, slogan, err, sizeof err) == 0, "opening pieces split into layers");
        if (sketch && slogan && moved && view) {
            const uint8_t *gone;
            sf_pieces_compose(sketch, slogan, 0, 0, moved);
            expect(memcmp(view, moved, n) == 0, "shift 0 matches the hold");
            expect(sf_slogan_shift(0x01FF0000) == 0, "the hold camera shift is 0");
            expect(sf_slogan_shift(0x01F80000) == 7, "timer 11 shifts the slogan by 7");
            sf_pieces_compose(sketch, slogan, sf_slogan_shift(0x01F80000), 0, moved);
            red = moved + ((size_t)117 * (size_t)SF_PIECE_W + 9u) * 4u;
            ink = moved + ((size_t)40 * (size_t)SF_PIECE_W + 200u) * 4u;
            gone = sketch + ((size_t)117 * (size_t)SF_PIECE_W + 2u) * 4u;
            expect(red[0] == 255 && red[1] == 0 && red[2] == 0 && red[3] == 255, "slogan pixel 9,117 is red after seven steps");
            expect(ink[0] == 222 && ink[1] == 239 && ink[2] == 231 && ink[3] == 255, "the sketch stays at pixel 200,40");
            expect(memcmp(moved + ((size_t)117 * (size_t)SF_PIECE_W + 2u) * 4u, gone, 4) == 0, "pixel 2,117 is the sketch after the slogan moves");
            gone = sketch + ((size_t)40 * (size_t)SF_PIECE_W + 201u) * 4u;
            expect(gone[0] == 222 && gone[1] == 239 && gone[2] == 231 && gone[3] == 255, "sketch pixel 201,40 fills the scale gap");
            gone = sketch + ((size_t)40 * (size_t)SF_PIECE_W + 206u) * 4u;
            expect(gone[0] == 214 && gone[1] == 239 && gone[2] == 222 && gone[3] == 255, "sketch pixel 206,40 keeps the texel on its left");
            sf_pieces_compose(sketch, NULL, 0, 1, moved);
            ink = moved + ((size_t)41 * (size_t)SF_PIECE_W + 200u) * 4u;
            expect(ink[0] == 222 && ink[1] == 239 && ink[2] == 231 && ink[3] == 255, "a sketch shift of 1 moves pixel 200,40 down");
        }
        {
            uint8_t *next = sf_alloc(n);
            const uint8_t *px;
            expect(next && sf_pieces_next(bg.data, bg.size, colors.data, colors.size, next, err, sizeof err) == 0, "the second sketch bakes");
            if (next) {
                px = next;
                expect(px[0] == 140 && px[1] == 115 && px[2] == 74 && px[3] == 255, "second sketch pixel 0,0");
                px = next + 50u * 4u;
                expect(px[0] == 140 && px[1] == 115 && px[2] == 66 && px[3] == 255, "second sketch pixel 50,0");
                if (moved) {
                    sf_pieces_compose(next, NULL, 0, -2, moved);
                    px = moved + 50u * 4u;
                    expect(px[0] == 74 && px[1] == 66 && px[2] == 41 && px[3] == 255, "the first type 1 step lifts pixel 50,2 to 50,0");
                }
            }
            sf_free(next);
        }
        {
            SfFsFile image;
            SfPpgTex cell;
            uint8_t *left = NULL;
            uint8_t *right = NULL;
            uint8_t *ground = sf_alloc(n);
            const uint8_t *word;
            const uint8_t *px;
            memset(&image, 0, sizeof image);
            memset(&cell, 0, sizeof cell);
            word = colors.data + 41 * 128 + 48 * 2;
            expect(colors.size >= 42u * 128u && (uint16_t)(word[0] | (word[1] << 8)) == 0x8000, "title.bin row 41 index 48 is opaque black");
            expect(fsOpen(".", 81, &image, err, sizeof err) == 0 && image.data && strcmp(image.name, "Opening.ppg") == 0, "Opening.ppg opens for the ground");
            if (image.data) {
                expect(sf_ppg_tex(image.data, image.size, 47, &cell, err, sizeof err) == 0 && cell.pal == 41 && cell.rects == 8, "opening texture 47 is row 41");
                sf_ppg_tex_free(&cell);
                expect(sf_ppg_tex(image.data, image.size, 48, &cell, err, sizeof err) == 0 && cell.pal == 41 && cell.rects == 10, "opening texture 48 is row 41");
                sf_ppg_tex_free(&cell);
            }
            fsClose(&image);
            expect(ground && sf_ground_load(".", &left, &right, err, sizeof err) == 0, "the opening ground decodes");
            if (ground && left && right) {
                sf_ground_compose(left, right, -24, ground);
                px = ground;
                expect(px[0] == 0 && px[1] == 0 && px[2] == 0 && px[3] == 255, "ground pixel 0,0 is black on the first quake");
                px = ground + ((size_t)0 * (size_t)SF_PIECE_W + 80u) * 4u;
                expect(px[3] == 0, "ground pixel 80,0 stays clear");
                px = ground + ((size_t)0 * (size_t)SF_PIECE_W + 192u) * 4u;
                expect(px[0] == 0 && px[1] == 0 && px[2] == 0 && px[3] == 255, "ground pixel 192,0 is the right cell");
                px = ground + ((size_t)7 * (size_t)SF_PIECE_W + 39u) * 4u;
                expect(px[3] == 0, "ground pixel 39,7 is clear at quake -8");
                sf_ground_compose(left, right, -22, ground);
                px = ground + ((size_t)7 * (size_t)SF_PIECE_W + 39u) * 4u;
                expect(px[0] == 0 && px[1] == 0 && px[2] == 0 && px[3] == 255, "the next quake step paints ground pixel 39,7");
            }
            sf_free(left);
            sf_free(right);
            sf_free(ground);
        }
        {
            uint8_t *art = sf_alloc(n);
            uint8_t *words = sf_alloc(n);
            uint8_t *scene = sf_alloc(n);
            const uint8_t *px;
            expect(art && words && scene && sf_pieces_line(bg.data, bg.size, colors.data, colors.size, art, words, err, sizeof err) == 0, "scene 102 bakes");
            if (art && words && scene) {
                px = art;
                expect(px[0] == 247 && px[1] == 231 && px[2] == 231 && px[3] == 255, "type 2 pixel 0,0 is the paper");
                px = words + ((size_t)5 * (size_t)SF_PIECE_W + 17u) * 4u;
                expect(px[0] == 255 && px[1] == 0 && px[2] == 0 && px[3] == 255, "the scene 102 letter at 17,5 is red");
                sf_pieces_line_compose(art, words, 1, 0, scene);
                px = scene + 4u;
                expect(px[0] == 247 && px[1] == 231 && px[2] == 231 && px[3] == 255, "the first type 2 step puts the paper at 1,0");
                px = scene;
                expect(px[3] == 0, "the first type 2 step clears pixel 0,0");
                px = scene + ((size_t)5 * (size_t)SF_PIECE_W + 17u) * 4u;
                expect(px[0] == 247 && px[1] == 231 && px[2] == 231 && px[3] == 255, "the sketch covers the letter on the first step");
                sf_pieces_line_compose(art, words, 90, 59, scene);
                px = scene + ((size_t)64 * (size_t)SF_PIECE_W + 17u) * 4u;
                expect(px[0] == 255 && px[1] == 0 && px[2] == 0 && px[3] == 255, "the parked sketch uncovers the letter at 17,64");
                px = scene + 90u * 4u;
                expect(px[0] == 247 && px[1] == 231 && px[2] == 231 && px[3] == 255, "the parked sketch puts the paper at 90,0");
            }
            sf_free(art);
            sf_free(words);
            sf_free(scene);
        }
        sf_free(sketch);
        sf_free(slogan);
        sf_free(moved);
    }
    sf_free(view);
    fsClose(&bg);
    fsClose(&colors);
}

int main(void)
{
    test_synthetic();
    test_indexed();
    test_fs_fixture();
    test_rank1();
    test_title();
    test_opening();
    test_pieces();
    if (g_fail) {
        fprintf(stderr, "%d failed\n", g_fail);
        return 1;
    }
    printf("picture ok\n");
    return 0;
}
```

`tests/test_scene.c`:

```c
/* The game's graphics calls through the Vulkan scene, on a headless device
 * (lavapipe under ctest), read back and checked pixel by pixel. */

#include "port/gfx.h"
#include "render/scene.h"

#include "types.h"
#include "structs.h"
#include "sf33rd/AcrSDK/common/plcommon.h"

#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

s32 flCreateTextureHandle(void *bits, u32 attribute);
s32 flCreatePaletteHandle(void *bits, s32 flag);
s32 flLockPalette(void *rect, u32 h, plContext *out, s32 flag);
s32 flLockTexture(s16 *r, u32 h, plContext *out, s32 mode);
s32 flUnlockTexture(u32 h);
s32 flSetRenderState(s32 func, u32 value);
s32 ps2SeqsRenderQuadInit_A(void);
s32 ps2SeqsRenderQuadInit_B(void);
void ps2SeqsRenderQuadEnd(void);
void ps2SeqsRenderQuad_A(Sprite *spr, u32 col);
void ps2SeqsRenderQuad_B(Quad *q, u32 col);

static VkInstance s_instance;
static VkPhysicalDevice s_physical;
static VkDevice s_device;
static VkQueue s_queue;
static VkCommandPool s_pool;
static VkCommandBuffer s_cmd;
static VkFence s_fence;
static const uint8_t *s_screen;
static int s_fail;

static int open_device(void)
{
    VkApplicationInfo app;
    VkInstanceCreateInfo ii;
    uint32_t n = 0;
    VkPhysicalDevice devs[8];
    uint32_t i;
    float prio = 1.f;
    VkDeviceQueueCreateInfo qi;
    VkDeviceCreateInfo di;
    VkPhysicalDeviceFeatures features;
    VkCommandPoolCreateInfo pi;
    VkCommandBufferAllocateInfo ci;
    VkFenceCreateInfo fi;
    memset(&app, 0, sizeof app);
    app.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_APPLICATION_INFO;
    app.apiVersion = VK_API_VERSION_1_0;
    memset(&ii, 0, sizeof ii);
    ii.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_INSTANCE_CREATE_INFO;
    ii.pApplicationInfo = &app;
    if (vkCreateInstance(&ii, NULL, &s_instance) != VK_SUCCESS) {
        return -1;
    }
    vkEnumeratePhysicalDevices(s_instance, &n, NULL);
    if (n > 8) {
        n = 8;
    }
    vkEnumeratePhysicalDevices(s_instance, &n, devs);
    for (i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        if (sf_scene_supported(devs[i])) {
            s_physical = devs[i];
            break;
        }
    }
    if (!s_physical) {
        return -1;
    }
    memset(&qi, 0, sizeof qi);
    qi.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_DEVICE_QUEUE_CREATE_INFO;
    qi.queueFamilyIndex = 0;
    qi.queueCount = 1;
    qi.pQueuePriorities = &prio;
    memset(&features, 0, sizeof features);
    sf_scene_features(s_physical, &features);
    memset(&di, 0, sizeof di);
    di.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_DEVICE_CREATE_INFO;
    di.queueCreateInfoCount = 1;
    di.pQueueCreateInfos = &qi;
    di.pEnabledFeatures = &features;
    if (vkCreateDevice(s_physical, &di, NULL, &s_device) != VK_SUCCESS) {
        return -1;
    }
    vkGetDeviceQueue(s_device, 0, 0, &s_queue);
    memset(&pi, 0, sizeof pi);
    pi.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_COMMAND_POOL_CREATE_INFO;
    pi.flags = VK_COMMAND_POOL_CREATE_RESET_COMMAND_BUFFER_BIT;
    vkCreateCommandPool(s_device, &pi, NULL, &s_pool);
    memset(&ci, 0, sizeof ci);
    ci.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_COMMAND_BUFFER_ALLOCATE_INFO;
    ci.commandPool = s_pool;
    ci.level = VK_COMMAND_BUFFER_LEVEL_PRIMARY;
    ci.commandBufferCount = 1;
    vkAllocateCommandBuffers(s_device, &ci, &s_cmd);
    memset(&fi, 0, sizeof fi);
    fi.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_FENCE_CREATE_INFO;
    vkCreateFence(s_device, &fi, NULL, &s_fence);
    return 0;
}

static int draw(SfScene *scene)
{
    char err[256];
    VkCommandBufferBeginInfo bi;
    VkSubmitInfo si;
    SfDrawList *l = sf_gfx_list();
    sf_gfx_frame_ready();
    if (sf_scene_prepare(scene, l, err, sizeof err) != 0) {
        fprintf(stderr, "prepare: %s\n", err);
        return -1;
    }
    vkResetCommandBuffer(s_cmd, 0);
    memset(&bi, 0, sizeof bi);
    bi.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_COMMAND_BUFFER_BEGIN_INFO;
    vkBeginCommandBuffer(s_cmd, &bi);
    sf_scene_record(scene, s_cmd, l);
    sf_scene_record_readback(scene, s_cmd, 0);
    vkEndCommandBuffer(s_cmd);
    memset(&si, 0, sizeof si);
    si.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_SUBMIT_INFO;
    si.commandBufferCount = 1;
    si.pCommandBuffers = &s_cmd;
    vkResetFences(s_device, 1, &s_fence);
    vkQueueSubmit(s_queue, 1, &si, s_fence);
    vkWaitForFences(s_device, 1, &s_fence, VK_TRUE, UINT64_MAX);
    {
        int w;
        int h;
        s_screen = sf_scene_read(scene, &w, &h);
    }
    sf_gfx_frame_done();
    return 0;
}

static void expect(const char *what, int x, int y, int r, int g, int b, int tol)
{
    const uint8_t *p = &s_screen[((size_t)y * SF_SCREEN_W + x) * 4];
    if (abs(p[0] - r) > tol || abs(p[1] - g) > tol || abs(p[2] - b) > tol) {
        fprintf(stderr, "%s: pixel (%d, %d) is (%d, %d, %d), expected (%d, %d, %d)\n", what, x, y, p[0], p[1], p[2], r,
                g, b);
        s_fail = 1;
    }
}

static void sprite(Sprite *s, float x0, float y0, float x1, float y1)
{
    memset(s, 0, sizeof *s);
    s->v[0].x = x0;
    s->v[0].y = y0;
    s->v[1].x = x1;
    s->v[1].y = y0;
    s->v[2].x = x0;
    s->v[2].y = y1;
    s->v[3].x = x1;
    s->v[3].y = y1;
    s->t[1].s = 1.f;
    s->t[2].t = 1.f;
    s->t[3].s = 1.f;
    s->t[3].t = 1.f;
}

int main(void)
{
    char err[256];
    SfScene *scene = NULL;
    plContext img;
    plContext lock;
    u8 index[4 * 4];
    u32 colors[16];
    s32 tex;
    s32 pal;
    Sprite spr;
    Quad q;
    int i;

    if (open_device() != 0) {
        fprintf(stderr, "no Vulkan device for the scene; skipped\n");
        return 77;
    }
    if (sf_scene_create(&scene, s_physical, s_device, VK_NULL_HANDLE, err, sizeof err) != 0) {
        fprintf(stderr, "scene: %s\n", err);
        return 1;
    }
    sf_gfx_init();

    /* an empty frame is the start-up clear colour, 0x80808080 */
    draw(scene);
    expect("clear", 0, 0, 0x80, 0x80, 0x80, 0);

    /* a 4x4 8-bit indexed texture through a 16-colour palette */
    for (i = 0; i < 16; i++) {
        index[i] = (u8)i;
        colors[i] = 0xFF000000u | (u32)(i * 16) << 16 | (u32)(255 - i * 16) << 8 | 0x40;
    }
    memset(&img, 0, sizeof img);
    img.desc = 0x44; /* indexed, 8-bit indices */
    img.width = 4;
    img.height = 4;
    img.pitch = 4;
    img.bitdepth = 1;
    img.ptr = index;
    tex = flCreateTextureHandle(&img, 0);
    memset(&img, 0, sizeof img);
    img.width = 16;
    img.height = 1;
    img.pitch = 64;
    img.bitdepth = 4;
    img.pixelformat.rl = 8;
    img.pixelformat.rs = 16;
    img.pixelformat.rm = 0xFF;
    img.pixelformat.gl = 8;
    img.pixelformat.gs = 8;
    img.pixelformat.gm = 0xFF;
    img.pixelformat.bl = 8;
    img.pixelformat.bm = 0xFF;
    img.pixelformat.al = 8;
    img.pixelformat.as = 24;
    img.pixelformat.am = 0xFF;
    img.ptr = colors;
    pal = flCreatePaletteHandle(&img, 0);
    if (tex <= 0 || pal <= 0) {
        fprintf(stderr, "handles: texture %d, palette %d\n", tex, pal);
        return 1;
    }

    /* point filtering is the start-up state (blend word bit 0x10000) */
    flSetRenderState(4, (u32)tex | (u32)pal << 16);
    ps2SeqsRenderQuadInit_A();
    sprite(&spr, 10.f, 20.f, 14.f, 24.f);
    ps2SeqsRenderQuad_A(&spr, 0xFFFFFFFF);
    ps2SeqsRenderQuadEnd();
    draw(scene);
    for (i = 0; i < 16; i++) {
        expect("palette sprite", 10 + i % 4, 20 + i / 4, i * 16, 255 - i * 16, 0x40, 0);
    }
    expect("outside the sprite", 14, 20, 0x80, 0x80, 0x80, 0);

    /* the vertex colour modulates the texel */
    flSetRenderState(4, (u32)tex | (u32)pal << 16);
    ps2SeqsRenderQuadInit_A();
    sprite(&spr, 10.f, 20.f, 14.f, 24.f);
    ps2SeqsRenderQuad_A(&spr, 0xFF808080);
    ps2SeqsRenderQuadEnd();
    draw(scene);
    expect("modulated", 13, 23, 0x78, 0x08, 0x20, 1);

    /* a palette written through flLockPalette shows the next frame */
    flLockPalette(NULL, (u32)pal, &lock, 0);
    ((u32 *)lock.ptr)[0] = 0xFF0000FF;
    flSetRenderState(4, (u32)tex | (u32)pal << 16);
    ps2SeqsRenderQuadInit_A();
    sprite(&spr, 10.f, 20.f, 14.f, 24.f);
    ps2SeqsRenderQuad_A(&spr, 0xFFFFFFFF);
    ps2SeqsRenderQuadEnd();
    draw(scene);
    expect("locked palette", 10, 20, 0, 0, 0xFF, 0);

    /* an untextured quad, half alpha, over the clear colour */
    ps2SeqsRenderQuadInit_B();
    memset(&q, 0, sizeof q);
    q.v[0].x = 100.f;
    q.v[0].y = 100.f;
    q.v[1].x = 110.f;
    q.v[1].y = 100.f;
    q.v[2].x = 100.f;
    q.v[2].y = 110.f;
    q.v[3].x = 110.f;
    q.v[3].y = 110.f;
    ps2SeqsRenderQuad_B(&q, 0x80FF0000);
    ps2SeqsRenderQuadEnd();
    draw(scene);
    expect("blended quad", 105, 105, 0xBF, 0x40, 0x40, 2);
    expect("blended quad edge", 110, 105, 0x80, 0x80, 0x80, 0);

    /* a texture written through a lock in place */
    flLockTexture(NULL, (u32)tex, &lock, 1);
    ((u8 *)lock.ptr)[0] = 15;
    flUnlockTexture((u32)tex);
    flSetRenderState(4, (u32)tex | (u32)pal << 16);
    ps2SeqsRenderQuadInit_A();
    sprite(&spr, 10.f, 20.f, 14.f, 24.f);
    ps2SeqsRenderQuad_A(&spr, 0xFFFFFFFF);
    ps2SeqsRenderQuadEnd();
    draw(scene);
    expect("locked texture", 10, 20, 15 * 16, 255 - 15 * 16, 0x40, 0);

    sf_scene_destroy(scene);
    vkDestroyFence(s_device, s_fence, NULL);
    vkDestroyCommandPool(s_device, s_pool, NULL);
    vkDestroyDevice(s_device, NULL);
    vkDestroyInstance(s_instance, NULL);
    if (!s_fail) {
        printf("scene: ok\n");
    }
    return s_fail;
}
```

`tests/test_shaders.c`:

```c
#include "render/nv2a.h"

#include <SDL3/SDL.h>

#include <errno.h>
#include <spawn.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <sys/wait.h>
#include <unistd.h>

extern char **environ;

static int g_fail;

static void expect(int cond, const char *msg)
{
    if (!cond) {
        fprintf(stderr, "FAIL %s\n", msg);
        g_fail++;
    } else {
        printf("ok %s\n", msg);
    }
}

static int run_cmd(char *const argv[])
{
    pid_t pid;
    int status = 0;
    if (posix_spawnp(&pid, argv[0], NULL, NULL, argv, environ) != 0) {
        fprintf(stderr, "spawn %s: %s\n", argv[0], strerror(errno));
        return -1;
    }
    for (;;) {
        pid_t w = waitpid(pid, &status, 0);
        if (w < 0 && errno == EINTR) {
            continue;
        }
        if (w < 0) {
            return -1;
        }
        break;
    }
    if (!WIFEXITED(status) || WEXITSTATUS(status) != 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    return 0;
}

static void *read_all(const char *path, size_t *size)
{
    FILE *fp = fopen(path, "rb");
    void *buf;
    long len;
    if (!fp) {
        return NULL;
    }
    if (fseek(fp, 0, SEEK_END) != 0) {
        fclose(fp);
        return NULL;
    }
    len = ftell(fp);
    if (len < 0 || fseek(fp, 0, SEEK_SET) != 0) {
        fclose(fp);
        return NULL;
    }
    buf = malloc((size_t)len);
    if (!buf || fread(buf, 1, (size_t)len, fp) != (size_t)len) {
        free(buf);
        fclose(fp);
        return NULL;
    }
    fclose(fp);
    *size = (size_t)len;
    return buf;
}

static int vk_device(VkInstance *instance, VkDevice *device)
{
    VkApplicationInfo app;
    VkInstanceCreateInfo ici;
    VkPhysicalDevice phys[8];
    uint32_t count = 8;
    uint32_t i;
    memset(&app, 0, sizeof app);
    app.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_APPLICATION_INFO;
    app.apiVersion = VK_API_VERSION_1_0;
    memset(&ici, 0, sizeof ici);
    ici.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_INSTANCE_CREATE_INFO;
    ici.pApplicationInfo = &app;
    *instance = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    *device = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    if (vkCreateInstance(&ici, NULL, instance) != VK_SUCCESS) {
        return -1;
    }
    if (vkEnumeratePhysicalDevices(*instance, &count, NULL) != VK_SUCCESS || count == 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    if (count > 8) {
        count = 8;
    }
    if (vkEnumeratePhysicalDevices(*instance, &count, phys) != VK_SUCCESS) {
        return -1;
    }
    for (i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        VkQueueFamilyProperties fam[8];
        uint32_t fam_n = 8;
        uint32_t q;
        vkGetPhysicalDeviceQueueFamilyProperties(phys[i], &fam_n, NULL);
        if (fam_n > 8) {
            fam_n = 8;
        }
        vkGetPhysicalDeviceQueueFamilyProperties(phys[i], &fam_n, fam);
        for (q = 0; q < fam_n; q++) {
            float priority = 1.0f;
            VkDeviceQueueCreateInfo qi;
            VkDeviceCreateInfo di;
            if ((fam[q].queueFlags & VK_QUEUE_GRAPHICS_BIT) == 0) {
                continue;
            }
            memset(&qi, 0, sizeof qi);
            qi.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_DEVICE_QUEUE_CREATE_INFO;
            qi.queueFamilyIndex = q;
            qi.queueCount = 1;
            qi.pQueuePriorities = &priority;
            memset(&di, 0, sizeof di);
            di.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_DEVICE_CREATE_INFO;
            di.queueCreateInfoCount = 1;
            di.pQueueCreateInfos = &qi;
            if (vkCreateDevice(phys[i], &di, NULL, device) == VK_SUCCESS) {
                return 0;
            }
        }
    }
    return -1;
}

static int gl_link(GLuint vert, GLuint frag)
{
    GLuint prog = glCreateProgram();
    GLint status = 0;
    glAttachShader(prog, vert);
    glAttachShader(prog, frag);
    glLinkProgram(prog);
    glGetProgramiv(prog, GL_LINK_STATUS, &status);
    if (!status) {
        char log[2048];
        GLsizei len = 0;
        glGetProgramInfoLog(prog, (GLsizei)sizeof log, &len, log);
        fprintf(stderr, "link: %s\n", log);
    }
    glDeleteProgram(prog);
    return status ? 0 : -1;
}

int main(void)
{
    char dir[] = "/tmp/sf33rd-shader-XXXXXX";
    char xvu[256];
    char xpu[256];
    char vert_path[256];
    char frag_path[256];
    uint8_t nop[20];
    uint8_t zero[244];
    void *vert_bytes = NULL;
    void *frag_bytes = NULL;
    size_t vert_size = 0;
    size_t frag_size = 0;
    SDL_Window *window = NULL;
    SDL_GLContext gl = NULL;
    VkInstance instance = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    VkDevice device = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    VkShaderModule vert_mod = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    VkShaderModule frag_mod = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    GLuint vert = 0;
    GLuint frag = 0;
    char err[512];
    char *mk;
    int i;
    const char *envs[] = {"vulkan1.0", "opengl4.5"};

    memset(nop, 0, sizeof nop);
    nop[0] = 0x78;
    nop[1] = 0x20;
    nop[2] = 1;
    nop[16] = 1;
    memset(zero, 0, sizeof zero);
    memcpy(zero, "PSB0", 4);

    mk = mkdtemp(dir);
    expect(mk != NULL, "temp dir");
    if (!mk) {
        return 1;
    }
    snprintf(xvu, sizeof xvu, "%s/nop.xvu", dir);
    snprintf(xpu, sizeof xpu, "%s/zero.xpu", dir);
    snprintf(vert_path, sizeof vert_path, "%s/nop.spv", dir);
    snprintf(frag_path, sizeof frag_path, "%s/zero.spv", dir);
    {
        FILE *fp = fopen(xvu, "wb");
        expect(fp && fwrite(nop, 1, sizeof nop, fp) == sizeof nop, "write nop.xvu");
        if (fp) {
            fclose(fp);
        }
        fp = fopen(xpu, "wb");
        expect(fp && fwrite(zero, 1, sizeof zero, fp) == sizeof zero, "write zero.xpu");
        if (fp) {
            fclose(fp);
        }
    }
    {
        char *argv[] = {"python3", SF_SHADER_TOOL, xvu, "-o", vert_path, NULL};
        expect(run_cmd(argv) == 0, "convert vertex program");
    }
    {
        char *argv[] = {"python3", SF_SHADER_TOOL, xpu, "-o", frag_path, NULL};
        expect(run_cmd(argv) == 0, "convert combiner");
    }
    for (i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
        char *vargv[] = {"spirv-val", "--target-env", (char *)envs[i], vert_path, NULL};
        char *fargv[] = {"spirv-val", "--target-env", (char *)envs[i], frag_path, NULL};
        expect(run_cmd(vargv) == 0, envs[i]);
        expect(run_cmd(fargv) == 0, envs[i]);
    }
    vert_bytes = read_all(vert_path, &vert_size);
    frag_bytes = read_all(frag_path, &frag_size);
    expect(vert_bytes && frag_bytes, "read SPIR-V");

    expect(SDL_Init(SDL_INIT_VIDEO), "SDL video");
    SDL_GL_SetAttribute(SDL_GL_CONTEXT_MAJOR_VERSION, 4);
    SDL_GL_SetAttribute(SDL_GL_CONTEXT_MINOR_VERSION, 6);
    SDL_GL_SetAttribute(SDL_GL_CONTEXT_PROFILE_MASK, SDL_GL_CONTEXT_PROFILE_COMPATIBILITY);
    window = SDL_CreateWindow("sf33rd shader", 64, 64, SDL_WINDOW_OPENGL | SDL_WINDOW_HIDDEN);
    expect(window != NULL, "OpenGL window");
    if (window) {
        gl = SDL_GL_CreateContext(window);
        expect(gl != NULL, "OpenGL 4.6 context");
    }
    if (gl && vert_bytes && frag_bytes) {
        expect(sf_gl_shader_spirv(GL_VERTEX_SHADER, vert_bytes, vert_size, &vert, err, sizeof err) == 0, "OpenGL vertex SPIR-V");
        if (!vert) {
            fprintf(stderr, "%s\n", err);
        }
        expect(sf_gl_shader_spirv(GL_FRAGMENT_SHADER, frag_bytes, frag_size, &frag, err, sizeof err) == 0, "OpenGL fragment SPIR-V");
        if (!frag) {
            fprintf(stderr, "%s\n", err);
        }
        if (vert && frag) {
            expect(gl_link(vert, frag) == 0, "OpenGL links both stages");
        }
    }
    expect(vk_device(&instance, &device) == 0, "Vulkan device");
    if (device && vert_bytes && frag_bytes) {
        expect(sf_vk_shader_spirv(device, vert_bytes, vert_size, &vert_mod, err, sizeof err) == 0, "Vulkan vertex SPIR-V");
        expect(sf_vk_shader_spirv(device, frag_bytes, frag_size, &frag_mod, err, sizeof err) == 0, "Vulkan fragment SPIR-V");
    }

    if (vert) {
        glDeleteShader(vert);
    }
    if (frag) {
        glDeleteShader(frag);
    }
    if (gl) {
        SDL_GL_DestroyContext(gl);
    }
    if (window) {
        SDL_DestroyWindow(window);
    }
    SDL_Quit();
    if (device) {
        if (vert_mod) {
            vkDestroyShaderModule(device, vert_mod, NULL);
        }
        if (frag_mod) {
            vkDestroyShaderModule(device, frag_mod, NULL);
        }
        vkDestroyDevice(device, NULL);
    }
    if (instance) {
        vkDestroyInstance(instance, NULL);
    }
    free(vert_bytes);
    free(frag_bytes);
    unlink(xvu);
    unlink(xpu);
    unlink(vert_path);
    unlink(frag_path);
    rmdir(dir);
    return g_fail ? 1 : 0;
}
```
