`tests/test_store.c`:

```c
/* The save file is SQLite. A struct written out and read back matches,
 * apart from the memory-card fields the file does not keep. A missing
 * file is not created by a read. A file that is not a database, a wrong
 * schema version, a value outside a field's range, or a system row
 * without its twenty rankings is rejected and left as it is. */

#include "port/store.h"

#include <sqlite3.h>

#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <unistd.h>

static int s_fail;

static void expect(int cond, const char *msg)
{
    if (!cond) {
        fprintf(stderr, "fail: %s\n", msg);
        s_fail = 1;
    }
}

static void expect_mem(const void *a, const void *b, size_t n, const char *msg)
{
    const unsigned char *x = a;
    const unsigned char *y = b;
    size_t i;
    if (memcmp(a, b, n) == 0) {
        return;
    }
    for (i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        if (x[i] != y[i]) {
            fprintf(stderr, "fail: %s at %zu: %02x != %02x\n", msg, i, x[i], y[i]);
            s_fail = 1;
            return;
        }
    }
}

static char *slurp(const char *path)
{
    FILE *f;
    long n;
    char *s;
    f = fopen(path, "rb");
    if (!f) {
        return NULL;
    }
    if (fseek(f, 0, SEEK_END) != 0 || (n = ftell(f)) < 0 || fseek(f, 0, SEEK_SET) != 0) {
        fclose(f);
        return NULL;
    }
    s = malloc((size_t)n + 1);
    if (!s) {
        fclose(f);
        return NULL;
    }
    if (fread(s, 1, (size_t)n, f) != (size_t)n) {
        free(s);
        fclose(f);
        return NULL;
    }
    s[n] = 0;
    fclose(f);
    return s;
}

static int write_text(const char *path, const char *text)
{
    FILE *f = fopen(path, "wb");
    if (!f) {
        return -1;
    }
    if (fputs(text, f) < 0) {
        fclose(f);
        return -1;
    }
    return fclose(f) == 0 ? 0 : -1;
}

static int file_is(const char *path, const char *text)
{
    char *s = slurp(path);
    int ok = s && strcmp(s, text) == 0;
    free(s);
    return ok;
}

static int sql_exec(const char *path, const char *sql)
{
    sqlite3 *db = NULL;
    char *err = NULL;
    int rc;
    if (sqlite3_open(path, &db) != SQLITE_OK) {
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    rc = sqlite3_exec(db, sql, NULL, NULL, &err);
    sqlite3_free(err);
    sqlite3_close(db);
    return rc == SQLITE_OK ? 0 : -1;
}

static int sql_int(const char *path, const char *sql, int *out)
{
    sqlite3 *db = NULL;
    sqlite3_stmt *st = NULL;
    if (sqlite3_open(path, &db) != SQLITE_OK) {
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    if (sqlite3_prepare_v2(db, sql, -1, &st, NULL) != SQLITE_OK || sqlite3_step(st) != SQLITE_ROW) {
        sqlite3_finalize(st);
        sqlite3_close(db);
        return -1;
    }
    *out = sqlite3_column_int(st, 0);
    sqlite3_finalize(st);
    sqlite3_close(db);
    return 0;
}

static void fill_system(struct _SAVE_W *a)
{
    int i;
    int r;
    int c;
    memset(a, 0, sizeof *a);
    a->Difficulty = 4;
    a->Time_Limit = -9;
    a->Battle_Number[0] = 2;
    a->Battle_Number[1] = 3;
    a->Damage_Level = 2;
    a->Handicap = 1;
    a->Partner_Type[0] = 5;
    a->Partner_Type[1] = 6;
    a->Adjust_X = -3;
    a->Adjust_Y = 8;
    a->Screen_Size = 1;
    a->Screen_Mode = 3;
    a->GuardCheck = 1;
    a->Auto_Save = 1;
    a->AnalogStick = 1;
    a->BgmType = 2;
    a->SoundMode = 1;
    a->BGM_Level = 12;
    a->SE_Level = 9;
    a->Extra_Option = 1;
    a->sum = 0xA1B2C3D4u;
    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
        a->Pad_Infor[0].Shot[i] = (u8)(10 + i);
        a->Pad_Infor[1].Shot[i] = (u8)(20 + i);
    }
    a->Pad_Infor[0].Vibration = 1;
    a->Pad_Infor[1].Vibration = 2;
    a->Pad_Infor[0].free[0] = 3;
    a->Pad_Infor[0].free[1] = 4;
    a->Pad_Infor[0].free[2] = 5;
    a->Pad_Infor[1].free[2] = 6;
    for (i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
        a->PL_Color[0][i] = (u8)(i + 1);
        a->PL_Color[1][i] = (u8)(30 + i);
        a->Ranking[i].name[0] = (u8)(1 + i);
        a->Ranking[i].name[1] = 2;
        a->Ranking[i].name[2] = 3;
        a->Ranking[i].player = (u16)(100 + i);
        a->Ranking[i].score = 1000u + (u32)i;
        a->Ranking[i].cpu_grade = (s8)(i - 10);
        a->Ranking[i].grade = (s8)(i - 5);
        a->Ranking[i].wins = (u16)(3 + i);
        a->Ranking[i].player_color = (u8)i;
        a->Ranking[i].all_clear = (u8)(i & 1);
    }
    for (r = 0; r < 4; r++) {
        for (c = 0; c < 8; c++) {
            a->extra_option.contents[r][c] = (s8)(r * 8 + c - 5);
        }
    }
}

static void clear_system_unstored(struct _SAVE_W *a)
{
    memset(a->Pad_Infor[0].free, 0, sizeof a->Pad_Infor[0].free);
    memset(a->Pad_Infor[1].free, 0, sizeof a->Pad_Infor[1].free);
}

static s8 s_fname;

static void fill_replay(_REPLAY_W *w)
{
    int r;
    int c;
    memset(w, 0, sizeof *w);
    w->header[0] = 0xAB;
    w->header[639] = 0xCD;
    w->sega_reserve[0] = 0x11;
    w->sega_reserve[63] = 0x33;
    w->Control_Time_Buff = -2;
    w->Difficulty = 3;
    w->Monitor_Type = 1;
    w->free_free[3] = 0x22;
    w->champion = 1;
    w->full_data = 2;
    w->free = 3;
    w->extra_free = 4;
    w->sum = 0x12345678u;
    w->game_infor.player_infor[0].my_char = 5;
    w->game_infor.player_infor[0].sa = -1;
    w->game_infor.player_infor[0].color = 2;
    w->game_infor.player_infor[0].player_type = -2;
    w->game_infor.player_infor[1].my_char = 8;
    w->game_infor.player_infor[1].sa = 1;
    w->game_infor.player_infor[1].color = 3;
    w->game_infor.player_infor[1].player_type = 4;
    w->game_infor.stage = -1;
    w->game_infor.Direction_Working = 1;
    w->game_infor.Vital_Handicap[0] = -8;
    w->game_infor.Vital_Handicap[1] = 9;
    w->game_infor.Random_ix16 = -100;
    w->game_infor.Random_ix32 = 200;
    w->game_infor.Random_ix16_ex = -300;
    w->game_infor.Random_ix32_ex = 400;
    w->game_infor.fname = &s_fname;
    w->game_infor.winner = 1;
    w->game_infor.play_type = 2;
    w->game_infor.players_timer = 1000;
    w->game_infor.old_mes_no2 = -2;
    w->game_infor.old_mes_no3 = -3;
    w->game_infor.old_mes_no_pl = -4;
    w->game_infor.mes_already = -5;
    w->mini_save_w.Time_Limit = -4;
    w->mini_save_w.Battle_Number[0] = 6;
    w->mini_save_w.Battle_Number[1] = 7;
    w->mini_save_w.Damage_Level = 2;
    w->mini_save_w.Pad_Infor[0].Shot[7] = 9;
    w->mini_save_w.Pad_Infor[0].Vibration = 1;
    w->mini_save_w.Pad_Infor[1].free[2] = 4;
    for (r = 0; r < 4; r++) {
        for (c = 0; c < 8; c++) {
            w->mini_save_w.extra_option.contents[r][c] = (s8)(c - r - 5);
        }
    }
    for (r = 0; r < 10; r++) {
        for (c = 0; c < 7; c++) {
            w->system_dir.contents[r][c] = (s8)(r + c - 9);
        }
    }
    w->system_dir.contents[9][6] = -9;
    w->system_dir.sum = 50;
    w->net_infor.Handle_1P[0] = 'K';
    w->net_infor.Handle_1P[17] = 0x7F;
    w->net_infor.Id_1P[7] = 0x42;
    w->net_infor.Handle_2P[0] = 'R';
    w->net_infor.Id_2P[0] = 0x10;
    w->net_infor.Serial_No[5] = 0x11;
    w->net_infor.Battle_Code[13] = 0x12;
    w->lag[0] = 1;
    w->lag[13] = 0xEE;
    w->io_unit.key_buff[0][0] = 0x0001;
    w->io_unit.key_buff[1][0] = 0x8000;
    w->io_unit.key_buff[0][100] = 0x00FF;
    w->io_unit.key_buff[1][100] = 0x0100;
    w->io_unit.key_buff[0][7197] = 0x0002;
    w->io_unit.key_buff[1][7197] = 0x0003;
}

static void clear_replay_unstored(_REPLAY_W *w)
{
    memset(w->header, 0, sizeof w->header);
    memset(w->sega_reserve, 0, sizeof w->sega_reserve);
    memset(w->free_free, 0, sizeof w->free_free);
    w->game_infor.fname = NULL;
    memset(w->mini_save_w.Pad_Infor[0].free, 0, sizeof w->mini_save_w.Pad_Infor[0].free);
    memset(w->mini_save_w.Pad_Infor[1].free, 0, sizeof w->mini_save_w.Pad_Infor[1].free);
    memset(&w->net_infor, 0, sizeof w->net_infor);
    memset(w->lag, 0, sizeof w->lag);
    w->free = 0;
    w->extra_free = 0;
}

static void test_game(const char *dir)
{
    char path[512];
    char missing[512];
    struct _SAVE_W sys_a;
    struct _SAVE_W sys_b;
    struct _SAVE_W blank;
    SystemDir dir_a;
    SystemDir dir_b;
    _REPLAY_W *rep_a;
    _REPLAY_W *rep_b;
    long long saved = 0;
    int r;
    int c;
    int got = 0;
    snprintf(missing, sizeof missing, "%s/missing.sqlite", dir);
    expect(sf_store_read_system(missing, &sys_b) == 1, "missing system");
    expect(access(missing, F_OK) != 0, "read does not create");

    fill_system(&sys_a);
    snprintf(path, sizeof path, "%s/game.sqlite", dir);
    expect(sf_store_write_system(path, &sys_a) == 0, "write system");
    clear_system_unstored(&sys_a);
    memset(&sys_b, 0x5a, sizeof sys_b);
    expect(sf_store_read_system(path, &sys_b) == 0, "read system");
    expect(sys_b.Screen_Mode == 3, "screen mode 3");
    expect(sys_b.Time_Limit == -9, "time limit");
    expect_mem(&sys_a, &sys_b, sizeof sys_a, "system");

    sys_a.Screen_Mode = 255;
    expect(sf_store_write_system(path, &sys_a) == 0, "write mode 255");
    expect(sf_store_read_system(path, &sys_b) == 0, "read mode 255");
    expect(sys_b.Screen_Mode == 255, "mode not clamped");
    sys_a.Screen_Mode = 3;
    expect(sf_store_write_system(path, &sys_a) == 0, "restore mode 3");

    memset(&dir_a, 0, sizeof dir_a);
    for (r = 0; r < 10; r++) {
        for (c = 0; c < 7; c++) {
            dir_a.contents[r][c] = (s8)(r * 7 + c - 30);
        }
    }
    dir_a.sum = 0xBEEF;
    expect(sf_store_write_directions(path, &dir_a) == 0, "write directions");
    memset(&dir_b, 0x5a, sizeof dir_b);
    expect(sf_store_read_directions(path, &dir_b) == 0, "read directions");
    expect_mem(&dir_a, &dir_b, sizeof dir_a, "directions");
    expect(sf_store_read_system(path, &sys_b) == 0, "system kept");
    expect_mem(&sys_a, &sys_b, sizeof sys_a, "system after directions");

    rep_a = calloc(1, sizeof *rep_a);
    rep_b = calloc(1, sizeof *rep_b);
    expect(rep_a && rep_b, "replay alloc");
    if (rep_a && rep_b) {
        fill_replay(rep_a);
        expect(sf_store_write_replay(path, 3, rep_a) == 0, "write replay 3");
        clear_replay_unstored(rep_a);
        memset(rep_b, 0x5a, sizeof *rep_b);
        saved = 0;
        expect(sf_store_read_replay(path, 3, rep_b, &saved) == 0, "read replay 3");
        expect(rep_b->game_infor.fname == NULL, "fname");
        expect(saved > 0, "saved at");
        expect(rep_b->io_unit.key_buff[0][50] == 0, "gap frame");
        expect(rep_b->io_unit.key_buff[0][7197] == 2, "last frame");
        expect(rep_b->Control_Time_Buff == -2, "control time");
        expect(rep_b->header[0] == 0 && rep_b->lag[0] == 0 && rep_b->net_infor.Handle_1P[0] == 0, "dropped fields");
        expect_mem(rep_a, rep_b, sizeof *rep_a, "replay 3");

        memset(rep_a, 0, sizeof *rep_a);
        rep_a->game_infor.player_infor[0].my_char = 9;
        rep_a->header[0] = 1;
        rep_a->io_unit.key_buff[0][10] = 0x1111;
        expect(sf_store_write_replay(path, 7, rep_a) == 0, "write replay 7");
        clear_replay_unstored(rep_a);
        memset(rep_b, 0x5a, sizeof *rep_b);
        expect(sf_store_read_replay(path, 7, rep_b, NULL) == 0, "read replay 7");
        expect(rep_b->io_unit.key_buff[0][10] == 0x1111, "short frame");
        expect(rep_b->io_unit.key_buff[0][11] == 0, "after last");
        expect(rep_b->io_unit.key_buff[0][7197] == 0, "short tail");
        expect_mem(rep_a, rep_b, sizeof *rep_a, "replay 7");
        expect(sf_store_read_replay(path, 3, rep_b, NULL) == 0, "replay 3 kept");
        expect(rep_b->io_unit.key_buff[0][7197] == 2, "slot 3 last");
        expect(rep_b->game_infor.player_infor[0].my_char == 5, "slot 3 char");
        expect(sf_store_read_replay(path, 0, rep_b, NULL) == 1, "empty slot");
        expect(sf_store_read_replay(path, -1, rep_b, NULL) == -1, "slot low");
        expect(sf_store_read_replay(path, 10, rep_b, NULL) == -1, "slot high");
    }
    free(rep_a);
    free(rep_b);

    expect(sql_exec(path, "UPDATE system SET time_limit=999 WHERE id=1") == 0, "set 999");
    expect(sf_store_read_system(path, &sys_b) == -1, "reject range");
    fill_system(&sys_a);
    clear_system_unstored(&sys_a);
    expect(sf_store_write_system(path, &sys_a) == 0, "rewrite system");
    expect(sql_exec(path, "DELETE FROM rank WHERE idx=19") == 0, "drop a rank");
    expect(sf_store_read_system(path, &sys_b) == -1, "reject ranks");
    expect(sf_store_write_system(path, &sys_a) == 0, "restore ranks");
    expect(sql_exec(path, "PRAGMA user_version=2") == 0, "set version 2");
    memset(&blank, 0, sizeof blank);
    blank.Difficulty = 9;
    expect(sf_store_read_system(path, &sys_b) == -1, "reject version");
    expect(sf_store_write_system(path, &blank) == -1, "version not overwritten");
    expect(sql_int(path, "PRAGMA user_version", &got) == 0 && got == 2, "version stays");
    expect(sql_int(path, "SELECT difficulty FROM system WHERE id=1", &got) == 0 && got == 4, "row stays");
}

static void test_host(const char *dir)
{
    char path[512];
    char mode[32];
    char aspect[32];
    char values[3][64];
    static const char *const names[] = { "up", "down", "left" };
    static const char *const vals[] = { "Up", "Down", "" };
    int width = 0;
    int height = 0;
    snprintf(path, sizeof path, "%s/host.sqlite", dir);
    expect(sf_store_read_window(path, mode, sizeof mode, &width, &height, aspect, sizeof aspect) == 1, "missing window");
    expect(sf_store_read_keys(path, names, values, 3) == 1, "missing keys");
    expect(sf_store_write_window(path, "windowed", 100, 960, "4:3") == -1, "reject width");
    expect(access(path, F_OK) != 0, "width not created");

    expect(sf_store_write_keys(path, names, vals, 3) == 0, "write keys");
    expect(sf_store_read_window(path, mode, sizeof mode, &width, &height, aspect, sizeof aspect) == 1, "no window yet");
    expect(sf_store_read_keys(path, names, values, 3) == 0, "read keys");
    expect(strcmp(values[0], "Up") == 0 && strcmp(values[1], "Down") == 0 && values[2][0] == 0, "key values");

    expect(sf_store_write_window(path, "windowed", 1280, 960, "4:3") == 0, "write window");
    width = 0;
    height = 0;
    mode[0] = 0;
    aspect[0] = 0;
    expect(sf_store_read_window(path, mode, sizeof mode, &width, &height, aspect, sizeof aspect) == 0, "read window");
    expect(strcmp(mode, "windowed") == 0 && width == 1280 && height == 960 && strcmp(aspect, "4:3") == 0, "window");
    expect(sf_store_read_keys(path, names, values, 3) == 0, "keys kept");
    expect(strcmp(values[0], "Up") == 0, "up kept");

    expect(sql_exec(path, "DELETE FROM key WHERE name='up'") == 0, "drop up");
    memset(values, 0x5a, sizeof values);
    expect(sf_store_read_keys(path, names, values, 3) == 0, "keys remain");
    expect(values[0][0] == 0 && strcmp(values[1], "Down") == 0, "missing name");
    expect(sf_store_read_window(path, mode, sizeof mode, &width, &height, aspect, sizeof aspect) == 0, "window kept");
}

static void test_garbage(const char *dir)
{
    char path[512];
    struct _SAVE_W sys;
    static const char text[] = "this is not a database\n";
    snprintf(path, sizeof path, "%s/garbage", dir);
    memset(&sys, 0, sizeof sys);
    expect(write_text(path, text) == 0, "write garbage");
    expect(sf_store_read_system(path, &sys) == -1, "read garbage");
    expect(sf_store_write_system(path, &sys) == -1, "write garbage");
    expect(file_is(path, text), "garbage kept");
}

static void rm(const char *dir, const char *name)
{
    char path[512];
    snprintf(path, sizeof path, "%s/%s", dir, name);
    unlink(path);
}

int main(void)
{
    char dir[] = "/tmp/sf33rd-store-XXXXXX";
    if (!mkdtemp(dir)) {
        perror("mkdtemp");
        return 1;
    }
    test_game(dir);
    test_host(dir);
    test_garbage(dir);
    rm(dir, "missing.sqlite");
    rm(dir, "game.sqlite");
    rm(dir, "game.sqlite-journal");
    rm(dir, "host.sqlite");
    rm(dir, "host.sqlite-journal");
    rm(dir, "garbage");
    rm(dir, "garbage-journal");
    if (rmdir(dir) != 0) {
        perror(dir);
        s_fail = 1;
    }
    return s_fail ? 1 : 0;
}

```

`tools/fold_aliases.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""The linker aliases for calls to folded bodies, from the port's own build.

Retail's linker folded identical functions into one body
(analysis-ps2map/folded.json in 3s-decomp-ogxb). Where the decomp's C
keeps a call by the body's address (sub_ADDR) because several names share
it, the body is defined under one of those names only and sub_ADDR is
left undefined. This lists every such name the archives reference and
none defines, and the defined function at the same address it stands
for, as --defsym lines (tools/gen_stubs.py --aliases reads them). It is
the decomp's tools/fold_aliases.py with the port's archives in place of
the decomp's MSVC objects, so it needs no Xbox build.

    fold_aliases.py [--nm NM] DECOMP_DIR OUT ARCHIVE...
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path


def names_at(decomp: Path) -> dict[int, list[str]]:
    at: dict[int, list[str]] = {}
    funcs = decomp / "config" / "sf33rd" / "symbols" / "funcs.txt"
    for line in funcs.read_text(errors="replace").splitlines():
        m = re.match(r"(\S+) = 0x([0-9A-Fa-f]+);", line)
        if m:
            at.setdefault(int(m[2], 16), []).append(m[1])
    folded = decomp / "analysis-ps2map" / "folded.json"
    if folded.is_file():
        for a, ns in json.loads(folded.read_text()).items():
            at.setdefault(int(a, 16), []).extend(ns)
    return at


def main() -> int:
    args = sys.argv[1:]
    nm = "nm"
    if args[:1] == ["--nm"]:
        nm, args = args[1], args[2:]
    decomp, out, archives = Path(args[0]), args[1], args[2:]
    defined: set[str] = set()
    undef: set[str] = set()
    for a in archives:
        text = subprocess.run([nm, a], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
        for line in text.splitlines():
            p = line.split()
            if len(p) == 2 and p[0] == "U":
                undef.add(p[1])
            elif len(p) == 3 and p[1] in "TtWw":
                defined.add(p[2])
    at = names_at(decomp)
    lines = []
    for n in sorted(undef - defined):
        m = re.fullmatch(r"sub_([0-9A-F]{8})", n)
        if not m:
            continue
        to = [t for t in at.get(int(m[1], 16), []) if t in defined]
        if to:
            lines.append(f"--defsym={n}={to[0]}")
    Path(out).write_text("".join(l + "\n" for l in lines))
    print(f"fold_aliases: {len(lines)} aliases", file=sys.stderr)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

```

`tools/gen_stubs.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Write a stub for every name the game code needs and nothing defines.

Reads the symbol tables of the given archives (the decomp's C and the
port shims), takes every undefined name no archive defines and no host
library (libc, libm, zlib, SDL3) exports, and writes one definition per
name:

  a function   reports the call once (sf_port_missing) and returns 0
  data         a zeroed, 16-byte aligned block of 64 KiB, or of twice
               the retail global's size where that is larger (--sizes: the
               decomp's config/sf33rd/symbols/data.txt): the game writes
               a buffer's whole length, and a short block let it run on
               into the next one

A name is a function when every relocation against it is a call
(R_X86_64_PLT32 or PC32 from a call instruction is what gcc emits); any
other use (an absolute or 32S address load) makes it data.

A name the decomp's fold list gives (tools/fold_aliases.py --format ld:
retail's linker merged identical bodies, and a caller kept the address of
the merged body) is not a stub: it jumps to the body's own name, so the
arguments pass through untouched.

    gen_stubs.py [--aliases FOLD.txt] OUT.c ARCHIVE...

For Android (an NDK cross build), --arch aarch64 (arm64's calls, and a
branch for an alias), --no-system and a --host-so for each of the
platform's libraries.

For Windows (a MinGW-w64 cross build), --coff with --nm and --objdump
naming that toolchain's tools, and --host for each import library whose
names the system gives (in place of the Linux shared libraries). COFF
gives calls and data references the same relocation, so there a name is
a function when every instruction that refers to it is a call or a jump.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
import subprocess
import sys

NM = "nm"
OBJDUMP = "objdump"
HOST_LIBS = ["libc.so.6", "libm.so.6", "libz.so.1", "libSDL3.so.0", "libvulkan.so.1", "libGL.so.1", "libopenal.so.1", "libarchive.so.13"]


def syms(path: str, dynamic: bool = False) -> tuple[set[str], set[str]]:
    args = [NM, "-D", "--defined-only", path] if dynamic else [NM, path]
    out = subprocess.run(args, capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
    d, u = set(), set()
    for line in out.splitlines():
        p = line.split()
        if len(p) == 2 and p[0] == "U":
            u.add(p[1])
        elif len(p) == 3 and (p[1].isupper() or (dynamic and p[1] in "iVW")) and p[1] != "U" and (dynamic or p[1] not in "VW"):
            d.add(p[2].split("@")[0])
    return d, u


# a call's relocation: x86-64's PLT32, arm64's CALL26 (bl) and JUMP26 (b)
CALLS = {"R_X86_64_PLT32", "R_AARCH64_CALL26", "R_AARCH64_JUMP26"}


def call_only(archives: list[str]) -> set[str]:
    """Names whose every relocation is a call's."""
    kinds: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
    for a in archives:
        out = subprocess.run([OBJDUMP, "-r", a], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
        for line in out.splitlines():
            p = line.split()
            if len(p) == 3 and p[1].startswith(("R_X86_64_", "R_AARCH64_")):
                n = re.split(r"[-+]0x", p[2])[0]
                kinds.setdefault(n, set()).add(p[1])
    return {n for n, k in kinds.items() if k <= CALLS}


def call_only_coff(archives: list[str]) -> set[str]:
    """Names every instruction refers to by a call or a jump (COFF)."""
    kinds: dict[str, set[bool]] = {}
    for a in archives:
        out = subprocess.run([OBJDUMP, "-dr", a], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
        last = ""
        for line in out.splitlines():
            m = re.match(r"\s+[0-9a-f]+:\s+IMAGE_REL_AMD64_\w+\s+(\S+)", line)
            if m:
                n = re.split(r"[-+]0x", m[1])[0]
                kinds.setdefault(n, set()).add(last.split()[0].startswith(("call", "jmp")) if last.split() else False)
                continue
            m = re.match(r"\s+[0-9a-f]+:\t(?:[0-9a-f]{2} )+\s*\t(.*)", line)
            if m:
                last = m[1]
    return {n for n, k in kinds.items() if k == {True}}


def host_lib(name: str) -> str | None:
    out = subprocess.run(["gcc", f"-print-file-name={name}"], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()
    if out and out != name:
        return out
    for d in ("/usr/lib", "/usr/lib64", "/lib/x86_64-linux-gnu"):
        p = f"{d}/{name}"
        try:
            open(p, "rb").close()
            return p
        except OSError:
            pass
    return None


def main() -> int:
    global NM, OBJDUMP
    args = sys.argv[1:]
    aliases: dict[str, str] = {}
    coff = False
    arch = "x86_64"
    system = True
    hosts: list[str] = []
    host_so: list[str] = []
    sizes: dict[str, int] = {}
    while args and args[0].startswith("--"):
        if args[0] == "--aliases":
            for line in open(args[1]):
                m = re.fullmatch(r"--defsym=(\w+)=(\w+)\s*", line)
                if m:
                    aliases[m[1]] = m[2]
        elif args[0] == "--nm":
            NM = args[1]
        elif args[0] == "--objdump":
            OBJDUMP = args[1]
        elif args[0] == "--host":
            hosts.append(args[1])
        elif args[0] == "--host-so":
            host_so.append(args[1])
        elif args[0] == "--sizes":
            for line in open(args[1]):
                m = re.match(r"\s*(\w+)\s*=\s*0x[0-9A-Fa-f]+;.*size:0x([0-9A-Fa-f]+)", line)
                if m:
                    sizes[m[1]] = int(m[2], 16)
        elif args[0] == "--coff":
            coff = True
            args = args[1:]
            continue
        elif args[0] == "--no-system":
            # a cross build: only the --host-so libraries, not this machine's
            system = False
            args = args[1:]
            continue
        elif args[0] == "--arch":
            arch = args[1]
        args = args[2:]
    out, archives = args[0], args[1:]
    defined, undef = set(), set()
    for a in archives:
        d, u = syms(a)
        defined |= d
        undef |= u
    host = set()
    if hosts:
        for p in hosts:
            d = syms(p)[0]
            # an import library defines NAME and __imp_NAME
            host |= d | {n[6:] for n in d if n.startswith("__imp_")}
    else:
        # the libraries the build links (wherever they are installed), then
        # the system's
        for p in host_so + ([q for q in map(host_lib, HOST_LIBS) if q] if system else []):
            host |= syms(p, dynamic=True)[0]
    # names the linker itself provides: the section bounds the game-state
    # snapshot reads (src/port/state.c)
    linker = {"__data_start", "_edata", "__bss_start", "_end"}
    need = sorted(
        n
        for n in undef - defined - host - linker
        if not n.startswith(("_GLOBAL_OFFSET_TABLE_", "__start_", "__stop_"))
    )
    # a library's own function stubbed means the library was not found:
    # the program would run with it missing
    lost = [n for n in need if re.match(r"(SDL_|vk[A-Z]|al[A-Z]|alc[A-Z]|gl[A-Z]|archive_|sqlite3_)", n)]
    if lost:
        print(f"gen_stubs: library functions not found in any host library: {' '.join(lost[:12])}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    # an alias whose body is not built yet needs a stub of its own
    need += sorted({aliases[n] for n in need if n in aliases} - defined - set(need))
    lines = [
        "/* stubs.c: written by tools/gen_stubs.py at build time. Do not edit.",
        f" * {len(need)} names the game code needs that no shim defines yet. */",
        "",
        'void sf_port_missing(const char *name);',
        "",
    ]
    funcs = call_only_coff(archives) if coff else call_only(archives)
    for n in need:
        if n in aliases:
            t = aliases[n]
            kind = "" if coff else f"\\n.type {n}, @function"
            jump = "b" if arch == "aarch64" else "jmp"
            lines.append(f'__asm__(".text\\n.globl {n}{kind}\\n{n}:\\n    {jump} {t}\\n");')
        elif n not in funcs:
            # twice the retail size: a global that holds addresses (the
            # effect pool, frw, is uintptr_t rows) takes twice the bytes
            # on a 64-bit target
            size = max(65536, (2 * sizes.get(n, 0) + 15) & ~15)
            lines.append(f"__attribute__((aligned(16))) unsigned char {n}[{size}];")
        else:
            lines.append(f"long {n}(void) {{ sf_port_missing(\"{n}\"); return 0; }}")
    open(out, "w").write("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
    print(f"gen_stubs: {len(need)} stubs", file=sys.stderr)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

```

`tools/lto_type_check.sh`:

```sh
#!/bin/sh
# Every global the game code declares with different types in different
# files. On the 32-bit build two such types often have the same size, and
# on x86-64 they do not (a byte-array row the size of 448 u32 words, a
# partial struct copy with 32-bit padding), so one file reads what another
# never wrote. GCC's LTO link compares every declaration of a name; this
# compiles the files the port builds as LTO objects and prints its report.
#
#     tools/lto_type_check.sh BUILD_DIR > report.txt
set -eu
build=$(cd "${1:?usage: $0 BUILD_DIR}" && pwd)
decomp=$(sed -n 's/^SF_DECOMP_DIR:PATH=//p' "$build/CMakeCache.txt")
work=$(mktemp -d)
trap 'rm -rf "$work"' EXIT
grep -oE '"/[^"]+\.c"' "$build/CMakeFiles/sf_decomp.dir/DependInfo.cmake" | tr -d '"' | sort -u |
    xargs -P"$(nproc)" -I{} sh -c '
        f={}; o='"$work"'/$(echo "$f" | md5sum | cut -c1-16).o
        gcc -m64 -O0 -std=gnu17 -w -fcommon -fno-strict-aliasing -fwrapv -fno-pie -D_GNU_SOURCE \
            -Wno-incompatible-pointer-types -Wno-int-conversion -Wno-implicit-function-declaration \
            -Wno-implicit-int -Wno-return-mismatch -flto \
            -I'"$build"'/decomp/include -I"$(dirname "$f")" -I'"$decomp"'/src/sf33rd/Source/Game \
            -c "$f" -o "$o" || echo "does not compile: $f" >&2'
gcc -m64 -flto -fcommon -r -nostdlib -flinker-output=nolto-rel -Wlto-type-mismatch \
    -o "$work/all.o" "$work"/*.o 2>&1 | sed "s#$decomp/##g"

```

`tools/ogxb/__init__.py`:

"""Tools for the original Xbox sf33rd.xbe."""
